24.	“He (Kulasekhara Deva, King of Pandyan Country) had accumulated much wealth during this long reign so that he had in the treasury of the city of Mardi (Mathuri 1200 crores of gold not counting the accumulation of precious stones such as pearls rubies, turquoises and emeralds”- South India  and her Muhammadan Invaders. K. Krishnaswami Iyengar M.A. (1921) Madras - P. 96
25.	Letters on India - Karl Marx.

3. makkaḷum malarkaḷum
tamiḻ makkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ intiyāvil oru taṉi iṉam. avarkaḷuk keṉṟu oru taṉi moḻiyum taṉip paṇpāṭum taṉi neṟiyum taṉik kalikaḷum taṉi nākarikamum uṇṭu. avarkaḷ piṟa iṉattavarkaḷi ṉiṉṟu uṭiyālum uṇavālum piṟavaṟṟālum vēṟupaṭṭa varkaḷ.
avarkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉavarkaḷ. inta nāṭṭukku urit tāṉavarkaḷ. iṅku nāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum poḻutu tirāviṭarkaḷāṉa maliyāḷikaḷ, āntirarkaḷ, kaṉṉaṭarkaḷ tuḷuvarkaḷ pōṉṟa aṉivariyum uḷḷaṭakkip pēcukiṟēṉ.
tamiḻarkaḷ pallāṇṭāka innāṭṭil vāḻntu inta nāṭṭil oru paṇpāṭṭiyum kaliyiyum nākarikattiyum vaḷarttu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
malarkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻvil nīṇṭa kālamāka, malarkaḷ toṭarpu peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ vāḻvilum tāḻvilum malar iṇintiruppatip pārkkalām.
avarkaḷ vāḻvu arumpiya kuṟiñci nilattiliruntē avar kaḷōṭu malarum toṭarntu varukiṟatu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭē tamiḻarkaḷ ceṭi, koṭi maraṅkaḷōṭum malarkaḷōṭum ilikaḷōṭum neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kuṟiñci nilattilē vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyil īṭupaṭṭa niliyiliruntu mara vaṇakkattiyum malar vaṇakkattiyum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ceṭikaḷilum koṭikaḷilum maraṅkaḷilum kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivil civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, ūtā vaṇṇaṅkaḷil eṇṇilāta malarkaḷ eṅkum kottuk kottāyp pūttuk kuluṅkiṉa. avikaḷiṉiṉṟu naṟumaṇam eṅkum vīciyatu. malarkaḷiṉ maṇamum vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ aḻakum, makkaḷ uḷḷattik kavarntaṉa.
vaḻipāṭu
tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻnta ellā nilattilum ārampattil tāy āṭciyē nilaviyatu. ataṉ palaṉākat tāy vaṇakkamum eḻuntatu. tāy āṭciyil, tāy iṟantatum marattaṭiyil avaḷukku naṭukal nāṭṭi makkaḷ vaṇaṅka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl mikap paḻiya kālattilē tamiḻakattil tāy vaṇakkam, āvuṭiyār uruva vaṇakkam maravaṇakkam mutaliyavi eḻuntaṉa. appāl tāy āṭci kuṉṟic cēy āṭci eḻuntatu. cēy āṭciyilē cēy vaḻipāṭu muruka vaḻipāṭu muḷittatu. cēyk kaṭavuḷukkum cēy iṟantapiṉ marattiṉaṭiyil kalnaṭṭu vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. itaṉit tamiḻp periyār tiru. vi.ka. avarkaḷ mika aḻakāka eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ iyaṟki vaḻi vāḻvic celutti ataṟku aṭippaṭiyāka uḷḷa iṟivaṉuṇmi kaṇṭu, avaṉ, aḻakāl iyaṟki aḻaku peṟuvatu nōkki avviṟivaṉi aḻakaṉ eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl aḻit tārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iyaṟki vāyilāka murukik kaṇṭu nukarnta iṉpattik kalittoki pattuppāṭṭu tirukkōviyār mutaliya nūṟkaḷil kāṇalām.5
pūkkaḷum puṉita nilamum
tamiḻar vāḻvu malarnta iṭam maliyēyākum atāvatu kuṟiñci nilamākum. aṅkē tāṉ avarkaḷiṉ paḻam perum paṇ pāṭu piṟantatu. aṅkētāṉ avarkaḷiṉ kalikaḷum nākarikamum tuḷirttaṉa. aṅkētāṉ avarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ arumpiṉa. avarkaḷ aṅkiruntē piṟa nilaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. avarkaḷ ātiyil vāḻnta nilam kuṟiñci eṉa aḻikkap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pulavarkaḷ ovvoru tiṇiyiṉ karupporuḷiyum naṉku ārāyntu arumperum uvamaṅkaḷi yum  urittirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷāl aintiṇikkum iṭap peṟṟa peyarkaḷ, avarkaḷatu aṟivut tiṟaṉiyum iyaṟkiyiṉ mītu avarkaḷ koṇṭa aṉpiyum veḷippaṭuttuvatākum. tamiḻarkaḷ malikkum malicārnta iṭattiṟkum kuṟiñci eṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉar.
kuṟiñci malināṭṭil niṟkum oru ceṭi. atu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭiṟku orumuṟi pūkkum. atu pūttuk kuluṅki niṟkum kālattu maliccāral muḻuvatum orē nīlaniṟamāka uḷḷaṅkavarum eḻiluṭaṉ oḷirum. tamiḻarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta nilatti aṅkuḷḷa akil, āram, vēṅki mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ peyarāl aḻiyātu kuṟiñci malariṉ peyarāl kuṟiñci nilam eṉak kuṟippiṭṭatu āḻntu cintittaṟkuriyatu. iḵtu maṭṭumiṉṟi avarkaḷ piṉṉar kuṭiyēṟiya ovvoru iṭattiṟkum āṇṭuḷḷa marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ malariṉ peyarālē mulli nilam, pāli nilam, marutanilam, neytal nilam eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷatum kavaṉikkattakkatu. pūkkaḷ mikat toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻarkaḷukku oru iṉṟiyamiyāta mukki yattuvam vāynta poruḷāka iruntu vantuḷḷaṉa. eṉavētāṉ avarkaḷ vāḻvu arumpiya mikap paḻiya kālattilē tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta puṉita nilattiṟku pūkkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi vittuḷḷaṉar.
vāḻvum malarum
tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattilē tam vāḻvi ārampittaṉar. akkālattil avarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpat talivi tāyāka iruntāḷ. mutaṉ mutalāka ap paḻam perum camūkattil tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum eḻuntaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattilē iyaṟki vāḻvik kaṇṭaṉar. atilē iṉpuṟṟut tiḷittu niṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ malikaḷil malarkaḷiṉ maṇatti nukarntu makiḻntu vantaṉar. inta maṇam - atāvatu muruku (vācaṉi) eṅkiruntu evvāṟu taṅkaḷiṭam pōntu taṅkaḷukku iṉpam ūṭṭukiṟatu eṉpati aṉṟu avar kaḷāl aṟiya muṭiyavilli. ētō oru kaṭ pulaṉukku eṭṭāta oru caktitāṉ namakku inta iṉpatti aḷikkiṟatu eṉṟu nampiṉar. muruki (maṇatti) uṇṭākkum anta caktikku murukaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. ati vaḻipaṭavum ārampittaṉar. itu namatu paḻam perum nūlākiya tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟku paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku  muṉpē eḻuntākum. tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ 5000 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṟiñci vāḻkkikku aṭikōliya ārampak kālattilē murukaṉ eḻuntu viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. atāvatu putukkaṟkālattiṉ iṟutiyil eṉṟu kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭē maraṅkaḷōṭum malarka ḷōṭum mānilattōṭum mika neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭavar kaḷāy iruntavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ. ātikālattil maraṅkaḷum ceṭi koṭikaḷum avarkaḷukku uṟiyuḷāka - maṉiyāka malarntiruntaṉa. avikaḷē avarkaḷukku uyirūṭṭum kaṟpakat taruvāka (kāy, kaṉi, kiḻaṅku, ili, malarkaḷit taruvaṉavāka) iruntaṉa. avikaḷē avarkaḷiṉ yākkikku uṟumpiṇikaḷi akaṟṟum māmaruntākat tikaḻntaṉa. avikaḷē, avarkaḷukku makiḻcciyūṭṭum maṇatti yum, aḻaku ceyyum aṇikaḷiyum (pūkkaḷiyum) ariyiṟ piṇikkum āṭikaḷiyum (marappaṭṭikaḷiyum) uyirkākkum āyutattiyum (villiyum) uḷḷaṅkaḷikkum icikkaruvi kaḷiyum (vilyāḻiyum) tōṟṟuvittatu. avarkaḷ kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṇṇamalarkaḷik kaṇṭum, uḷḷaṅkavarntu uvaki ūṭṭum maṇatti nukarntum kaḻipēruvaki eytiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷiṭiyē malar vaṇakkamum maravaṇakkamum tuḷirttaṉa.
pūvum pūviyārum
tamiḻ makkaḷ malarkaḷ mītu koṇṭa aḷavu kaṭanta aṉpāl, pirikka muṭiyāta piṇippāl taṅkaḷiṉ uyiraṉiya makaḷirik koṭi eṉṟaṉar. appāl peṇpālarkkup peṇ koṭi eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiṉar. pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. mēlum avarkaḷip pūviyar, pūviyalār, melliyalār eṉṟel lām kūṟi makiḻntaṉar. peṇ makkaḷukkup picci, kamalam malliki mutaliya malarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum cūṭṭi makiḻntaṉar.
pūṅkoṭiyum koḻu kompum
pūṅkoṭi ciṟitāka irukkumpoḻutu yātoru cārpumiṉṟi tuḷirttu eḻum. atu naṉṟāka vaḷarntu paṭarum paruvam vantatum atu oru koḻu kompi nāṭum. atu aṇittē niṟkum oru valuvāṉa marakkompip paṟṟip paṭarum. atu pōṉṟu paruvam aṭinta pāviyarkaḷ pūppeytiyatum oru valimimikka iḷa vīraṉ tōḷ cērtali eṇṇi āyntu teḷintu peṇṇiṟkup pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. itaṉ mūlam avarkaḷ peṇṇiṟkup pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu iṭṭappeyar ettuṇi ciṟappu vāyntatu eṉpatu naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatallavā?
maṅkiyum malarum
pūṅkoṭi paruvam vantuṟṟa poḻutu koṭi vīci oru koḻu kompip paṟṟip paṭarntu kaḷittuc ceḻittu vaḷarntu pūttu maṇappatip pōṉṟu makaḷirum paruvam vantatum āṭavaṉ tōḷ cērvati maṇattal eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. peṇkaḷ paruvam aṭi talip pūttal eṉṟaṉar.
peṇṇip pūṅkoṭi eṉṟa tamiḻar, avaḷ paruvam eytuvatip pūttal eṉṟum avaḷ oru āṭavaṉōṭu kūṭi vāḻap pōvati maṇattal eṉṟum kūṟiyatu ettuṇi poruttamāṉatu? aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ ettuṇi poruḷ tarum muṟiyil taṅkaḷ moḻiyi amittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu itaṉmūlam naṉkaṟiyalām. mēlum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkil, peṇ kaḻuttil āṇ oru pūmāliyic cūṭṭuvatum piṉ peṇ taṉ kaḻuttil uḷḷa oru pūmāliyi āṇ kaḻuttil cūṭṭuvatum mukkiya caṭaṅkākum. itaṟkut tamiḻar māliyiṭal, māli māṟṟal eṉṟellām kūṟuvar.
maṇam nikaḻum poḻutu maṇamakkaḷ mītu avarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷum naṇparkaḷum pūkkaḷit tūvi vāḻttuvar. tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkō nāṭṭiṟkō varum viruntiṉar kaḷip pūmāli cūṭṭi varavēṟpar. teyva uruvaṅkaḷukkup pūmāli cūṭṭi vaḻipaṭuvar. pūkkaḷ tūvi pukaḻntu pāṭuvar. tirumaṇattil maṭṭumaṉṟi vīṭṭil nikaḻum ellāc caṭaṅkukaḷilum viḻākkaḷilum eṅkum malarkaḷāl alaṅkarippar. muṟkālattil pāvalarum pāṇaṉum, pāṭiṉiyum viṟaliyum kūttarum pārvēntar pāl ceṉṟu tam kalittiṟaṉik kāṭṭiṉāl pūvēntarkaḷ puḷakāṅkitamuṟṟu avarkaḷukkup pūmālikaḷi aṇivippar. piṟkālattil poṉṉāṟ ceyta tāmarip pūvi aḷittum paricilāka ūrum vīṭum kaḷiṟum poṉ vaṭṭilum poṉ aṇi kaḷum piṟavum aḷittum ciṟappuc ceytaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
pūvum iyaṟkiyum
pūṅkoṭiyil paruvam vantapoḻutu muki arumpum; ciṟiya mukikaḷil maṇam eḻātu; aḻakum arumpātu, paruvam eytiṉa piṉṉarē mukikaḷ mutirntu malarāka māṟum. pū malarnta piṉṉarē atil aḻaku oḷirum, maṇam poṅki eḻum, ataṉ aṭiyil tēṉ ūṟum. pūviṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyum aḻakiyum maṇattiyum kaṇṭu vaṇṭukaḷ vantu malar mītu amarntu ataṉi maṇantu ataṉ tēṉiyuṇṭu cuvikka nāṭivarum. vaṇṭukaḷ oru pūvil amarntu ataṉ tēṉiyuṇṭatum aṭutta pūvi nāṭic cellum. ataṉ kālil paṭintuḷḷa makarantap poṭikaḷ aṭuttu amarum malaril paṭintu ataṉ karuppikkuḷ ceṉṟu ammalaric cūluṟac cey kiṟatu. inta vaṇṭu mūlam ceṭi koṭi maram mutaliyavi karuvuṟṟuk kāyttup paḻukkiṉṟaṉa. pakalil malarum malarkaḷ ellām palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil irukkum. āṉāl, iruḷ niṟinta iravil malarum malarkaḷ peritum veṇmiyāka irukkum. iravil veṇmi niṟamalarkaḷ virivatāl enta iruṭṭilum vaṇṭukaḷ eḷitil maṇattiyum niṟattiyum aṟintu ataṉ mītu amarntu maṇantu iṉpattēṉiyuṇṭu cūluṟac ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟki kāṭṭum inta uṇmiyi aṟintē akkāla makaḷir iravil veṇmi niṟamāṉa āṭiyi aṇintu vantaṉar. veḷḷic cēli māli āṭi eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟatu. paruvam vāynta pāviyarkaḷum iḷiñarkaḷum paṇṭik kālattil iravil veṇmiyāṉa āṭikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. vaṇṭukaḷip pōl paṇṭikkālap paruvap peṇkaḷukkut tōḻiyar tuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. iravu nēraṅkaḷil peṇ koṭi kaḷum avarkaḷiṉ kātalarkaḷāṉa kāḷikaḷum iravil kātal maṇam puriya viḻintu niṟpārkaḷām. iravil avarkaḷ cantikka vēṇṭiya iṭattiṟkuk kuṟiyāka malarkaḷi vippārkaḷ eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mukattilum (muttamiṭutalum) maṇattalum (tirumaṇam puritalum) makkaḷ vaṇṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭa iyaṟki neṟi; iṉpaneṟi eṉṟu terikiṟatu. vaṇṭukaḷ pūvi luḷḷa tēṉiyuṇpatippōl āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ muttattāl iṉpuṟṟu avar umiḻ nīri uṟiñci iṉpantuykkak kaṟṟaṉar eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ viḷakkam tarukiṉṟaṉar. namatu vaḷḷuvap perun takiyum itaṉi aṟintē.
	pāloṭu tēṉkalantaṟṟē paṇimoḻi
	 vāleyi ṟūṟiya nīr
eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṇ peṇ kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyum ataṉ palaṉāka eḻum iṉpamum iyaṟkiyiliruntu tamiḻaṉ kaṟṟa pāṭamēyākum.
tamiḻ makkaḷ peṇkaḷip pūṅkoṭiyāka, pūviyākak kaṇṭaṉar. cila pulavarkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉittum tāmari malarākak kaṇṭārkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāka,
	ceṅkamalam nāṭṭam
		ceḻun tāmari vataṉam
	 paṅkayam cevvāy
		patumam pōl ceṅkaraṅkaḷ
	 ampōrukantāṉ aravintam māraṉār
		tampōrukan tāṉ taṉam
eṉṟum,
	tirumukaṅ kamalam iṇiviḻi kamalam 
		ceyyavāy kamalam nittilam kamalam
	 varumuli kamalam tuṇikkaram kamalam
		valampuri unti poṟkamalam
	 perukiya alkul maṇittaṭam kamalam
		piṭit taṭit tāḷkaḷum kamalam
	 uru avaṭ kavvāṟu ātali ṉaṉṟē
		uyarnta pūviṉuṭ kamalam
eṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷi eṭuttiyampalām. ivi tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṉukku aḷitta mukkiyattuvattiyum pūvippaṟṟi naṭattiya āyviyum kāṭṭuvaṉavākum.7
āṉāl kārikiyiṉ kaṇkaḷik karuṅkuvaḷip pūviṟkum kamala itaḻukkum kākaṇa malarukkum oppākavum mūkki eḷḷiṉ pūviṟkum, kumiḻam pūviṟkum, vāyi kumuta malarukkum, palli mulli arumpiṟkum, kāti vaḷḷi malarukkum, kiyik kāntaḷ pūviṟkum, koṅkiyi paṅkaya mukikkum, utaratti makiḻa malarukkum kālkaḷi aṉicca malarukkum, mēṉiyic ceṇpaka malarukkum mukatti muṇṭakap pūviṟkum, uvamāṉamāka oru pulavaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭit taṉatu kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉi ellāp pulavariṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭutti uyarttikkāṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. kampaṉ kārikiyiṉ aḻaki eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatil atika āṟṟal peṟṟa vaṉ. avaṉ ariviyiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi oru ciṟu mayiri yum ataṉ iyalpukkēṟṟa eḻil kuṉṟātu eṭuttukkāṭṭupavaṉ. apperuṅkaviñaṉ,
	ki kāntaḷ vāy kumutam
		kaṇ neytal, kārikiyīr
	 mey vār taḷir koṅki
		meṉ kōṅkam - ivvaṉittum
	 vaṉmi cōrntu āvi
		varuntuvatu mātavam oṉṟu
	iṉmiyō aṉṟō emakku
eṉa maṅkiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉittiyum ovvoru malarukku uvamāṉamāka oppiṭṭu urittiruppatu namatu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil paṭikkap paṭikkat tikaṭṭāta iṉpattip perukkum.
tamiḻarkaḷ, malikaḷil tōṉṟiya maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarnta vaṉaṅkaḷil vāḻntu, malarkaḷiṉ maṇaṅkaḷi nukarntu, avaṟṟiṉ aḻakiya amippik kaṇṭu kaḷittu, vaṇṇaṅkaḷi uṇarntu uvakiyuṟṟu, malarkaḷiluḷḷa tēṉiyuṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu vanta oru toṉmiyāṉa iṉam. eṉavē avarkaḷukku maṅki (pūvi) maṭṭum, malarākak kāṭciyaḷikkavilli. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇṇum, mukamum, mūkkum utaṭum kikaḷum kālkaḷum piṟa melliya uṟuppukaḷum ovvoru malarākak kāṭci aḷittami kaṇṭu ulakam eṉṟum uvantu pōṟṟum.
tamiḻarkaḷum nilamum
tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil ulakatti nāṉilam eṉṟaṉar. appāl ati pū, pūtalam, pūtiyam, pūvalayam pūvulakam, pūmātēvi, pūmakaḷ eṉṟellām aḻittu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṉ peyaril koṇṭa aṉpiṉ mūlamākavē tāṅkaḷ vāḻum periya nilavula kiṟkuk kūṭa ap peyari aḷittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.
maṅkiyarkaḷukku malarkaḷ mītu maṭṭilāta matippuṇṭu, kavarcciyuṇṭu. mara vaṇakkamum malar vaṇakkamum malar aṇiyum māṇpum tamiḻakattil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷāṉa tirāviṭarkaḷ vāḻnta ulakil oppaṟṟa nākarikatti niṟuviya arappā mokañcatārō mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum nilaviyatu eṉṟu car. cāṇ mārcal pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakattil maṅkiyarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kūntalkaḷil naṟumaṇam nāṟum naṉ malarkaḷic cūṭi varuvatu paḻamperum paṇpu. kārikiyarkaḷ kaḻunīr, malliki cevvanti picci mutaliya malarkaḷit taṅkaḷatu piṉṉiviṭap peṟṟa paṉicciyil pāṅkuṟa aṇintati; oru pāṭṭil pulavar oruvar aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭ ṭiyuḷḷār. 8 makaḷir  malarkaḷip pukaḻntu vantaṉar; pōṟṟi vantaṉar; puṉitamāka eṇṇi vaḻipāṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. aḻakup poruḷāka niṉittu aṅkaṅkaḷil aṇintu akamakiḻntu āṭip pāṭi vantaṉar. malarkaḷi maṅkalam malkum mānitiyāka matittu vantaṉar.
tamiḻ makkaḷ akavoḻukkattil malarkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantatik kaṇṭu nām āccariyappaṭuvataṟkilli. āṉāl avarkaḷ puṟa oḻukkattilum pūkkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantati aṟiyap peritum viyappāka irukkiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil paṇṭikkālat til pōrkaḷil ovvoru nilikkum ovvoru pū aṭi yāḷamāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ pōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṟatu. pōr ārampittatum ovvoru aracaṉum taṉ paṭi vīrarkaḷukku, pūkkaḷi aḷippāṉ. veṭci, karanti, vañci, kāñci, uḻiñi, nocci tumpi vāki mutaliya pūkkaḷ aṭiyāḷap pūkkaḷākum. ip pūkkaḷ pōrvīrarkaḷ ceyyum pōrt toḻi liṉ potu vakiyiṉi uṇarttuvaṉavākum. pōril mutalnili, pakivarkaḷiṉ pacukkaḷik kavartal; itu veṭci eṉappaṭum. kavarntu ceṉṟa pacukkaḷi mīṭkap pōriṭuvatu karanti eṉappaṭum. pakivar nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttuc celluvatu vañciyākum. vantavarkaḷi māṟṟaracaṉ etirttut tākkuvatu kāñci eṉappaṭum. pakivar nāṭṭiṉ araṇ arukē paṭikaḷ pōntu nakari muṟṟukiyiṭṭut tākkutal uḻiñi eṉappaṭum. araṇiṟku uḷḷē irukkum aracaṉ araṇik kāttup pōriṭutal nocci eṉappaṭum. tākka vanta aracaṉi māṟṟu maṉṉaṉ etir ceṉṟu kaṭum pōr āṟṟal tumpi eṉappaṭum. veṟṟipeṟutal vāki eṉappaṭum. iṅkuk kūṟiya veṭci, karanti, vañci, kāñci, uḻiñi nocci, tumpi, vāki eṉpaṉa aṭiyāḷap pūkkaḷākum. ovvoru paṭiyum ovvoru niliyilum taṅkaḷ nilikkuriya pūkkaḷi aṇintu koḷḷum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka mutaṉmutalāka pōril pakivarkaḷiṉ ānirikaḷik kavarum niliyil pōr vīrarkaḷ, veṭci malari aṇintu koḷvar; veṟṟiyiṉpōtu vāki malaric cūṭikkoḷvar. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil veṟṟi, vāki cūṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum.9
pūkkaḷiṉ paruvaṅkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ pūkkaḷiṉ mītu atikap paṟṟuṭiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻvu pūvil arumpi, pūvil malarntu pūvōṭu neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭatāka irukkiṟatu. pūkkaḷ makkaḷiṉ piṟappu mutal iṟappuvari ēṉ? - iṟappiṟkup piṉ naṭipeṟum kāṭāṟṟu. kalleṭuppu nīttār niṉivu nāḷ mutaliya aṉittilum iṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
pūkkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camayaṅkaḷil taṉiyiṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. piṟa iṉattavarkaḷiviṭat tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ camayaṅkaḷil pūviṟku mutaliṭam aḷittuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ellām pūviṉ mītē tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru teyvattiṟkum ovvoru malar urittāṉatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ teyvam āṇāyiṉum peṇṇāyiṉum avikaḷiṉ kaḻuttilum taliyilum pūkkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkum. avarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ eṅkum pūvē alaṅkārap poruḷāka irukkum. avarkaḷiṉ maṟikaḷilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum, tōttirap pāṭalkaḷilum ākamaṅkaḷilum malarkaḷ ciṟappāṉa iṭattip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilellām pūkkaḷ ciṟappittup pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṟku ataṉ palvēṟu nili kaḷukkum paruvattiṟkēṟpa palvēṟu peyarkaḷ cūṭṭi pāṅkuṭaṉ aḻittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. arumpu, muki, pōtu, alar, malar ākiya aintu coṟkaḷum pūviṉ palvēṟu paruvaṅkaḷi uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip payiṉṟār naṉkaṟivar. naṟumaṇam kamaḻum nantavaṉattiṟkuc ceṉṟāl aṅkup palatiṟap paṭṭa paruvam aminta malarkaḷik kāṇamuṭiyum. aṅku arum pākavum  mukiyākavum pōtākavum alarākavum malarākavum tikaḻu vatik kaṇṇāl kaṇṭukaḷikkalām. pūviṉ mutaṟ paruvam arumpu;  arumpu mutirum niliyi muki allatu moṭṭu eṉṟu kūṟap paṭum. muki aviḻum paruvam, pōtu eṉappaṭum. pōtu, malarum niliyil alar eṉappaṭum, pōtu naṉṟāka malarnta niliyi malar eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭappeṟukiṟatu.
malarum nilamum
iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakam kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉṉum aintu nilap pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta nilappirivukaḷ iṭampaṟṟi eḻuntālum kāraṇap peyarākavē tōṉṟiṉa. ovvoru nilat tilum peruvāriyākak kāṇappaṭṭa malarkaḷiṉ kāṭciyik kaṇṭē ippeyar cūṭṭappeṟṟatākat terikiṟatu. kuṟiñci malarkaḷ niṟinta iṭam kuṟiñcinilam eṉṟum, mulli malarkaḷ niṟinta iṭam mullinilam eṉṟum, maruta malarkaḷ niṟinta iṭam marutanilam eṉṟum, neytal malarkaḷ niṟinta iṭam  neytal nilam eṉṟum, pāli malarkaḷ niṟinta iṭam pāli nilam eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. inta nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷi nilattiṉ peyarkaḷiyiṭṭē aḻittaṉar. ovvoru nila makkaḷukkum taṉittaṉiyē teyvaṅkaḷum, talivarkaḷum yāḻum, paṇṇum, paṟiyum puḷḷum poḻutum vilaṅkum paṟavikaḷum piṟavum taṉittaṉiyākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru nilaṅ kaḷukkum uriya malarkaḷum taṉittaṉiyē pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuk kuṟiñci kāntaḷ, vēṅki, cuṉikkuvaḷi, pōṉṟavi urittāṉa malarkaḷākum. neytal nilattiṟku veḷḷi taṭkitiyum, neytalum pāli nilattiṟkuk kuravu, pātiri mutaliyaṉavum mulli nilattiṟku mulli, tōṉṟi mutaliyaṉavum maruta nilattiṟku marutam, tāmari, kaḻunīr mutaliyaṉavum urittāṉa malarkaḷākum.
inta nilattil vāḻum makkaḷ nilapētam kulapētam etuvum iṉṟi virumpiya nilattavarkaḷik kātalittu maṇantu koḷvar. namatu paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, uruvilum, tiruvilum, kulatti lum, nalattilum, aṟivilum, parivilum otta taṉmi vāynta talivaṉum taliviyum taṉiyiṭattē cantittu kātal koṇṭu vantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ iravu allatu pakal cantikkum iṭatti uṇartta, kuṟippatu vaḻakkam. ataṉi iravuk kuṟi11 ā allatu pakaṟkuṟi 11a eṉpar. intak kuṟiyit talivi taṉ tōḻi mūlam talivaṉukkuk kūṟi aṉuppuvāḷ. antat tōḻiyiṉ vārttippaṭiyē iravilō allatu pakalilō talivaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa poḻilukku varuvāṉ. aṅkut talivi kuṟippiṭṭa kuṟiyiṭatti aṟintu taliviyiṉ varukikkāka antak kuṟi yiṭattil kāttu niṟpāṉ. antak kuṟi pūvāka irukkum. oru talivi pakaṟkālattil talivaṉic cantikkap pōntāḷ. kuṟiyiṭattil talivaṉ niṟpatu kaṇṭu kaḷittāḷ. appāl avaṉ arukil ceṉṟu kātal uriyāṭi kaḻipēruvaki koṇṭāḷ. itaṉi aḻakuṟa vāḻttic caṅkakkālap pulavar oruvar oru oppaṟṟa ōviyam tīṭṭiyuḷḷār. atil kuṟiyīṭṭumalar pēcappaṭuvatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
nīṇṭu parutta tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya taliviyum yāṉum tiṉippuṉaṅ kākka nāḷic celvōm. aṅkē kuraṅkukaḷum ēṟa muṭiyātavāṟu aṭarnta maraṅkaḷuḷḷa iḷañcōliyil kuṉṟi ṉiṉṟu viḻum aruvikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ kuḷircciyum iṉimiyum poruntiya viruppantarum nīr niliyiṉ arukil oḷi mikka ceṅkāntaḷ pūkkaḷ malarntirukkum iṭattil pāmpiṉ māṇikka maṇiyiṉ oḷi viḷakkattil kariya kūntaliyuṭiya kārikiyiṉ kaḷippup poruntiya kalavi iṉpatti aṭivāyāka eṉṟu akanāṉūṟṟil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.11
ivvāṟu malarkaḷik kuṟiyākavum aṇiyākavum marun tākavum maṅkalap poruḷākavum12 kātalarik kāṇpataṟkuriya kuṟi malarākavum cuviyākavum, maṇamākavum eṇṇi aṉupavittu vantatu paḻam perum tamiḻ nākarikam. iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭaḷavil - atāvatu kaṭiccaṅkak kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ koṭi, ili, taḷir, arumpu mutaliyavikaḷiyum malar, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭi mutaliyavikaḷiyum taṉittaṉiyākavum oṉṟākavum cērttuk kaṭṭit taliyilum kaḻuttilum kikaḷilum aṇintu vantaṉar. iṉṟum ataṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir aṇintuḷḷa poṉ aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē ataṟku nalla cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum tamiḻp peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ poṉ aṇikaḷi malar vaṭivākavum, taḷir vaṭivākavum, taḻi vaṭivākavum arumpu vaṭivākavum malar vaṭivākavum, kāy vaṭivākavum paḻa vaṭivākavum koṭi vaṭivākavum ceytu aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷ taliyil aṇiyum māliyik kaṇṇi eṉavum, kaḻuttil āṭavar aṇiyum māliyit tār eṉavum makaḷir kaḻuttil aṇiyum māliyik kōti eṉavum kūṟivantaṉar. peṇkaḷ pūviṉāl toṭutta pūvāṭikaḷiyum taḻikaḷāl toṭutta taḻi uṭikaḷiyum aṇintu vantaṉar.
toṉṟu toṭṭu tamiḻ makkaḷ pūkkaḷōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl pūkkaḷip palvēṟu vitamākak kōkkavum palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttavum vaḻi kaṇṭaṉar. itaṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ kaṇṭa aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷil pūttoṭuppatu oru kaliyāka iṭam peṟṟatu.
kuṟippukaḷ 
6.	Tree Snake Worship, Frgusson P. 20
	intiyāvil intukkaḷukkup puṉitamāṉa maram aracamaram ākum. aracu civaperumāṉ, tirumāl, nāṉ murukaṉ amcamāṉatu eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. peṇ makkaḷ, makkaḷ celvam vēṇṭi aracamaratti valam varukiṉṟaṉar. ūrtōṟum aracamaramum vēppamaramum vittu vaḷarttu ataṉ aṭiyil cili nāṭṭi makaḷir kuḷittu malarcūṭṭi valam vantu vaṇaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ.
	vilva maram civaperumāṉukku ukanta maramāka eṇṇi makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ ili teyvattiṉ āyutamākiya cūlāyutam pōṉṟatu. vēmpu caktiyiṉ vaṭivam. ati vaṇaṅkiṉāl aṉēka nōykaḷ akalum eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṟatu. vēmpu teyvīka maram eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tuḷaci tirumakaḷ amcam eṉṟum tirumālukku ukantatu eṉṟum vaḻipaṭappaṭukiṟatu. ālamarattiṉ aṭiyil civaperumāṉ uṟintār. avar ālamar celvaṉ eṉṟaḻikkap peṟṟār. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ellām marattiṉ kīḻē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. im maraṅkaḷ talaviruṭcam eṉappaṭum.

irumeṉ kūntal ēm uṟu tuyilē	(akam 92)
11.	(a) iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uri. pak. 18
	iṟantupāṭṭu accattiṉāl iravukkuṟi nērum. nērntāḷ talimakaṟku niṉṉāṭṭār eṉṉa pūviṉar, eṉṉa cāntiṉar, eṉṉa marattiṉ kīḻ viḷiyāṭuvar eṉṉum. atu kēṭṭut talimakaḷ muṉṉellām eṉakku maṟuttāḷaṉṟē, maṟuttāḷ itu colliṟṟu, oru kāraṇam nōkki eṉa uṇarntu, tāṉum niṉṉāṭṭār eṉṉa pūviṉar, eṉṉa cāntiṉar, eṉṉa marattiṉkīḻ viḷiyāṭuvatu eṉṉum, eṉṟakkāl tōḻi, yām kāntaḷum vēṅkiyum cūṭutum; cantaṉac cāntu pūcutum; piṇṭi marattiṉ kīḻ viḷiyāṭuvatu eṉṉum. kuṟiñci nilamākaliṉ ivvakic collum allā nilattukkum avaṟṟukkut takkavāṟu collum.
	(ā) pakaṟkuṟi tāṉē ikappiṉum variyār	(iṟiyaṉār 20)
	(i) iravuk kuṟiyē ilvari yikavātu	(iṟiyaṉār 21)
12.	Tree Worship and Ophiolatry - G. Subramania Pilli, M.A., B.L., P. 2-3.
13.	“Extensive researches pursued after the excavation made at Mohenjo - Daro and Harappa having firmly established this fact that the Tamil race was occupying the whole of India from the Himalayas down to Cape Co morin in Pre - Aryan days. Sir. John Marshall has observed that tree worship was essensially a characteristic of the Pre - Aryan, not of the Aryan population and so the tree spirit has loomed far more important in pre - historic days among the people who originated this worship that it did later in an Aryanized India where Tree - worship inevitably became subordinated to other cults alien or seme - alien.”

4. aṇikalaṉkaḷum 
avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟum
aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyart tōṟṟam
aṇikalaṉ eṉpataṟku āparaṇam. naki, taṭiyam, paṇi, alaṅkāram eṉṟu pala poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātit tamiḻarkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyil pāmpik kaḻuttilum kiyilum talimītum cuṟṟikkoṇṭu āṭippāṭi vantuḷḷārkaḷ. iti avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ pōlavum matittirukkalām. ataṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātip peṟṟōrkaḷāyum teyvaṅkaḷākavum pōṟṟap peṟum koṟṟa viyum civaperumāṉum pāmpi aṇikalaṉkaḷākap pūṇṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ pōlum. koṟṟaviyum avaḷ kaṇavaṉākiya civaperu māṉum paṇṭik kālattip piratipalikkum paḻam perum teyva mākap pāmpu aṇikalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ iṉṟu kāṭci aḷittu varukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. piṟkālattil makkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭu piṭittup poṉ veḷḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu aṇintirunta kālattilkūṭa aṇikalaṉkaṭkup paṇi eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭṭu vantuḷḷatu. paṇi eṉṉum patattiṟku pāmpu eṉpatu oru poruḷ. iṉṟum tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ teyvamākiya koṟṟavikkum civaperumāṉukkum carppakuṇṭalam carppakaṅkaṇam pōṉṟa pāmpu vaṭiva aṇikaḷ ceytu pūṭṭi makiḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
aṇiyap paṭuvatāl ataṟku aṇikalaṉ eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu. pāmpu, nakiyāka eḻuntatāl aṇikalaṉkaḷukku paṇi (pāmpu) eṉṟu peyar vantatu. pūvarumpu aṇiyap peṟṟatāl pūvarumpiṉ peyarākiya nakiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarāka amintatu. taṭiyiṉiṉṟu taṭiyam eṉṟa peyar aṇikalaṉukku vantatu. muṟkālattil tamiḻ makkaḷukku pēy, picācu, nōy noṭikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷukku atika accam. atit taṭi ceyya kiyilum kālilum iṭuppilum oru nūl kaṭṭuvar. itik kaṭṭi ṉāl nōy aṇukātu, pēy neruṅkātu eṉṟu periyōrkaḷ kūṟi vantaṉar. avvāṟē periyōrkaḷ aḷitta nūṟkaḷik kaṭṭi makkaḷ accam aṟṟiruntaṉar.
iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil naṭipeṟum tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu kiyil kurukkaḷ oru nūlik kaṭṭuvār. atu kāppuk kaṭṭutal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. tirumaṇak kālattil nōy aṇukāmalum pēy picācukaḷ neruṅkāmalum iruppataṟkē inta nūl kāppākak kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil inta nūl poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟuk kiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kāppu oru aṇi kalaṉāka māṟiyatu. veḷḷi poṉ mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷil palvēṟu vaṭivil kāppu ceyyap peṟṟu makkaḷāl aṇintu varappeṟṟatu. atē pōl muṟkālattil kaṭṭap peṟṟa taṭi eṉṟa nūlum taṭiyam eṉṟa peyaril nakikaḷip paṟṟik koṇṭatu. vēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil illāviṭṭālum nelli, kumari māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil pēccu vaḻakkil taṭiyam iruntē varukiṉṟatu. poṟ kollarkaḷum nakikaḷit taṭiyam eṉṟu aḻittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
kiyil kāppuk kaṭṭiyatu pōl kālilum muṟkāla makkaḷ taṭi kaṭṭi vantaṉar. attaṭi piṟkālattil veḷḷi aṇiyāka māṟit taṇṭiyāyiṟṟu. aim poṉṉāl kālil taṇṭikaḷ ceytu aṇintāl pēy piṇikaḷ aṇukā eṉṟu muṟkāla makkaḷ nampit taṇṭikaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. atēpōl kaḻut til taṭiyāyk kaṭṭappaṭṭa nūlē piṟkālattil tāliyāyiṟṟu. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil nūl iḻantāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭāl tāli iḻantāḷ - atāvatu kimpeṇṇāṉāḷ eṉpatu poruḷ.
kuḻantikaḷukku aimpaṭit tāli - atāvatu tirumāliṉ aintu paṭik kalaṉkaḷi oru tāḷ allatu takaṭṭil eḻuti nūlil kōttu kaḻuttil aṇintāl pīṭikaḷ aṇukā eṉṟu muṟkālat tamiḻarkaḷ aimpaṭit tāliyi aṇivittu vantaṉar. itaṉāl aimpaṭittālikkum nūl eṉṟu peyar eḻuntatu.
ātiyil aṇikalaṉkaḷ malarākat tōṉṟi appāl nūl vaṭivil kāppākavum taṭiyākavum tāliyākavum aimpaṭit tāliyākavum piṉṉar veṇmiyāṉa caṅkukaḷum cōvikaḷum kal uruṇṭikaḷum palvēṟu vaṇṇa maṇ uruṇṭikaḷum aṇi kalaṉkaḷāka uruppeṟṟaṉa. veṇmiyāṉa caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷum caṅkut tōṭukaḷum caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷum muttumālikaḷum pavaḷamālikaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷāka uruppeṟṟaṉa.
malar aṇiyum vaḻakkam kuṟiñci nilattilum, aimpaṭit tāli aṇiyum vaḻakkam mulli nilattilum vaḷi, cōvi, muttu, pavaḷam aṇiyum vaḻakkam neytal nilattilum marutaṅkāy māli, miḷakumāli, kottamalli māli, maṇṇāṟ ceyta uruṇṭi maṇikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷum  paḷiṅkupōṉṟa pala vaṇṇak kaṟkaḷ kōtta mālikaḷum kāppu, taṭi, tāli pōṉṟa nūl aṇikaḷ vaḻakkam maruta nilattilum arumpiyirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu. oru nilattil arumpiya oru paḻakkam piṟa nilattiṟkum paravuvatuṇṭu. tiruñāṉacampantar, veḷḷi vaḷi cōra eṉ uḷḷam kavar kaḷvaṉ, eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉmūlam 6ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil caṅkuvaḷi aṇiyum  vaḻakkam iruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. caṅkakālap pulavarākiya nakkīraṉār tammic caṅkaṟuppa teṅkaḷ kulam eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉ mūlam ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil caṅku vaḷikaḷum caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu. atōṭu caṅkukaḷ aṟukkum toḻil iyaṟṟivarum oru iṉattavar tamiḻakattil iruntu vantaṉar eṉṟum terikiṟatu. pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamāy  viḷaṅkiya koṟkiyil caṅkaṟukkum toḻil peritāka naṭipeṟṟu vantatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟum koṟkiyil akaḻntu pārttāl aṟukkappaṭṭa caṅkukaḷiṉ tuṇṭu tuṇukkukaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, muṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭa caṅku aṇikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa eṉṟum intiya aracāṅka mīṉ toḻil tuṟi iyakkunarāyirunta ṭākṭar kōrṉal eṉpavar  intiyāviṉ puṉitac caṅkukaḷ eṉṟa nūlil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. oru kālattil tamiḻakattil vāḻnta  ellā nila makkaḷum caṅkiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷi, mōtiram kātaṇi mutaliyavikaḷi aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāvil periya celva makaḷirum caṅku vaḷikaḷiyum mōtiraṅkaḷiyum iṉṟum aṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaṅkam, kucarat mānilaṅkaḷiliruntu varum peṇkaḷiṟ palar, ki niṟiyac caṅku valiyalkaḷum viralil caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷum aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ caṅku vaḷi aṇivatu oru matac caṭaṅku pōl eṇṇi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ātikāla aṇikalaṉkaḷ
ātikālattil mutaṉ mutalāka aṇikalaṉkaḷik kaṇṭa varkaḷ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷēyāvar. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl aṇi ceyyum vaḻakkam mutaṉ mutalāka kuṟiñci nilap peṇka ḷiṭiyē tāṉ malarntatu. paḻaṅ kālattilē avarkaḷ aḻakuk kaliyil ārvaṅ koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. malarkaḷit taliyilum kaḻuttilum aṇiyat talippaṭṭaṉar. nāriṟ kōttu māliyākak kaṭṭiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. arumpukaḷiyum iḷantaḷirkaḷiyum ilikaḷiyum koṭikaḷiyum tali, kaḻuttu, kātu, kikaḷ iṭuppup pōṉṟa uṟuppukkaḷilellām aṇintu vantaṉar. nāḷaṭivil pūkkaḷip palvēṟu vitamāka caramākavum māliyāka vum ceṇṭākavum pūvāṭiyākavum ilikaḷit taḷirāṭi yākavum kaṭṭum kaliyil kitērntu viḷaṅkiṉar.
pūttoṭukkum kali vaḷarcciyaṭintatuṭaṉ nillātu avarkaḷ ciṟu kāy, koṭṭi, paḻamkaḷiyum kōttu ākiyavi aṇiya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ aḻakuk kaliyi naṉku uṇarntu atic cuvikka muṟpaṭṭatum veḷḷi civappākiya vaṇṇak kuṉṟi maṇikaḷik kōttu maliyāka aṇintu vantaṉar. civappuk kuṉṟimaṇiyil nalla kaṟuppu vaṇṇam naṭuvē oḷirvatu mika aḻakāka iruntatu. appāl avarkaḷ kaḻaṟcikkoṭṭi, nellikkāy, miḷakukkāy kottamalli pōṉṟavikaḷik kōttu mālikaḷākavum kātaṇiyākavum puya aṇikaḷākavum kiyaṇiyākavum iṭuppaṇiyākavum kāl aṇiyākavum aṇintu vantaṉar. ivvaḻakkam ellā nilaṅkaḷilum paraviyatu. marutam, neytal mutaliya nilaṅkaḷil vāḻpavarkaḷ āṅkāṅkuk kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu aṇikaḷākac ceytu aṇintu vantā lum malar aṇikaḷum kuṉṟimaṇi, nellikkāy pōṉṟa aṇi kaḷum mikavum kavarcciyaḷittu vantaṉa.
aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟil ulōka uṟpattiyāṉatu oru putiya cakāptam eḻuvataṟku oru ārampa niliyāka iruntatu. vēṭṭip poruḷkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭa uṭampi, ulōkap poruḷkaḷ eḻuntatum avaṟṟuḷ oppaṉi ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam piṟantatu. āṇum peṇṇum taṅkaḷ ki, kāl pakutikaḷiyum kaḻuttiyum, ulōkaṅkaḷāl uruvākkap peṟṟa kaḻuttaṇikaḷālum (mālikaḷālum) kātaṇikaḷālum ariyi arippaṭṭiyālum oppaṉi ceyya muṉ vantaṉar. muṉṉar iṟakkikaḷum ciṟu kuccukaḷum vikkōlum iṭam peṟṟirunta mūkku, kātu, utaṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tuvāraṅkaḷ  ulōkattālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷāl nirampiṉa.
āppirikka nāṭṭu pōṅkō eṉṉum pakutiyil vāḻnta aḻakiya kāṭṭu iṉak kārikiyarkaḷ, mūkkil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpu vaḷiyatti aṇintu vantaṉar. annāṭṭiluḷḷa ceṉi kāmpi iṉappeṇkaḷ kūṭa inta irumpu vaḷiyaṅkaḷiyē virumpiyaṇintaṉar. pōṅkō nāṭṭu āppirikkap peṇkaḷ ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷ kātukaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa paṉṉiraṇṭu tuḷikaḷ iṭṭiruppar. atil irumpu vaḷiyaṅkaḷi māṭṭik koḷvārkaḷ. ivvāṟu avviṉap peṇkaḷ ovvoruvarum uṭampil naki aṇivataṟkeṉṟu ceytu koṇṭa tuvāraṅkaḷ nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭaṉavākum. itu avarkaḷ uyir vāḻvataṟku vēṭṭiyāṭa muṟpaṭṭa ārampakāla vaḻakkamākum. uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷ vaḷarcci peṟṟu uṇavup poruḷkaḷ malinta putiya cakāptattilkūṭa avarkaḷiṉ inta nili māṟavilli. māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṇṇiyatumilli. mēlum itu taṅkaḷ paḻamperum paṇpāṭāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum itil pala tattuvaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum itaṉāl pala nōy akalkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟi taṅkaḷ aṟiviṉ tiṟattik kāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
makaḷir ātikālattil aṇintirunta nakikaḷiṉiṉṟu māṟutal ceyya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta cūḻnili eḻuntatu. intac cūḻnili aṭikkaṭi eḻuntu māṟutali uṇṭākkiyatu. āṉāl āṇkaḷ ati virumpavilli. pūviyarkaḷ putiya cakāptattiṟ kēṟpa māṟutalkaḷi nāṭumpoḻutu āṇkaḷāl atu aṭātu eṉṟu atika nāḷ taṭuttu niṟutta muṭiyavilli. āṇ makkaḷ ātiyil aṇintirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷāṉa iṟakukaḷum elumpukaḷum nakaṅkaḷum kātilum mūkkilum utaṭṭilum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa allavā? anta aṇikalaṉkaḷ āṇ makkaḷiṉ vēṭṭiyāṭiya tiṟaṉiṉ niṉivuk kuṟiyāka avarkaḷiṉ avayavaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ akkālattil vēṭṭiyāṭun toḻilil īṭupaṭavilli. eṉṟālum avarkaḷiṉ aṉutāpattip peṟuvataṟ kāka āṇmakaṉ avarkaḷiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷi iṭam peṟac ceytāṉ. āṉāl peṇkaḷ aṇinta ulōkattālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ āṇiṉ vēṭṭiyāṭun tiṟaṉi viḷakkuvaṉavāka illi. atu āṇkaḷiṉ celvac ciṟappik kāṭṭuvaṉavāka iruntaṉa. ataṟkāka āṇ, peṇkaḷiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷil eṇṇaṟṟa nakikaḷip pūṭṭa muṉvantāṉ. nāḷaṭivil oruvaṉuṭiya maṉivi avaṉuṭiya karuvūlaṅkaḷi vikkum peṭṭiyāka eṇṇappaṭṭāḷ. iṟutiyākap peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷiṉ uṭimi yāka māṟṟap paṭṭaṉar.
kāṭukaḷil vāḻnta muṟkāla makkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuk kiṭitta cempu, veṇkalat tātupporuḷkaḷiyellām urukkit tam maṉivimārkaḷukku aṇikalaṉkaḷākac ceytu avikaḷi avarkaḷukku aṇivittu akamakiḻntu vantaṉar. paḻaṅkālak kāṭṭu maṉitaṉ 10, 20, 30, 40 ēṉ nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa maṉivikaḷik kūṭa vittiruntāṉ. itaṟkumēl maṟu maṉiyāṭṭikaḷiyum vit tiruntāṉ. tacarataṉ 60,000 maṉivikaḷip peṟṟiruntāṉ. kiṟitava maṟi kūṟum cālamōṉ ñāṉi 700 maṉiyāṭṭikaḷiyum 300 maṟumaṉiyāṭṭikaḷiyum peṟṟiruntār. pala mulim maṉṉarkaḷ 4 maṉivikaḷiyum 400 maṟumaṉiyāṭṭikaḷi (curiyatkaḷiyum) vittiruntaṉar. itē pōl āppirikkarkaḷ pala maṉivikaḷi vittiruppatu carva cātāraṇa campavamākum. oru āppirikkak kāṭṭup paḻaṅkāla maṉitaṉ oruvaṉ nāṟpatukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṉivikaḷip peṟṟiruntāṉ. atip paṟṟi avaṉ aṭikkaṭi perumiyākavum pēcik koḷvāṉ. avaṉ ovvoru maṉi vikkum taṉittaṉiyākap pala pittaḷi nakikaḷ ceytu avarkaḷukku aṇivittiruntāṉ. ōr āppirikka nīkirōvarkaḷiṉ  taliva ṉuṭiya maṉivimārkaḷ cākumvari kuṟintatu 800 irāttal pittaḷik kaḻuttaṇikaḷic cumantu varuvārkaḷām. avaṉuṭiya āṟu peṇ makkaḷ 120 irāttal pittaḷi nakikaḷiyum avaṉuṭiya piriyamāṉa aṭimip peṇ 200 irāttal pittaḷi nakikaḷiyum avaṉuṭiya maṉivikaḷum peṇ makkaḷum ovvoruvar 6 irāttal kiyaṇikaḷiyum aṇintiruntārkaḷ. āka mottam avaṉiṭam 1396 irāttal pittaḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntu vantatām.
avaṉiyil arumpiya cila ākkak kūṟukaḷiṉ celvākkiṉ payaṉāka ariviyarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷil apiviruttiyum māṟṟamum eḻuntaṉa. nām itit toṉmiyāṉa kālattiluḷḷa poruḷ uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷil eḻunta ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉākat tuḷirtta ceyalkaḷiṉ oru pakuti eṉpati niṉiviṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum. oru pakuti, maṉita iyaṟkiyiṉ niliyāṉa cāyalkaḷum poruḷātārattiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa cāyalkaḷum poruḷātārattiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa celvākkiṟku vaḻiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka āṇkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya peṇkaḷiṉ mūlam celvac ciṟappik kāṭṭutal taṅkaḷuṭiya paṇap perumiyi aṟivikkum akaṅkārac ceyalāka iruntatu. iṉṉum avarkaḷ tamatu āṉma tattuvat taṉmikaḷiyum piṟavaḻi kaḷil taṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷē veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ulōkap poruḷkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ mītu peṇ makkaḷukkup pēravā, maṉitaṉ ulōkap poruḷkaḷi uṟpatti ceyya ārampitta piṉṉartāṉ eḻuntuḷḷatu eṉṟu nām iṅku meyppikka vēṇṭiyatu avaciyam iṉṟu. muṟkālattil maṉitaṉuṭiya āci, peritum, taṉṉuṭiya celvap perukki ulakiṟku kāṭṭik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu iruntatākat teri kiṟatu. celvam taṉṉic ciṟappittuk kāṭṭum vaḻiyil makkaḷi iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu atu celvavāṉ, taṉṉuṭiya maṉivimār kaḷum maṟumaṉiyāṭṭikaḷum ulōkap poruḷkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruppati ulakiṟku uṇartta vēṇṭum eṉṟu tūṇṭiyatu. itaṉi viḷakka nām pala eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷik kāṭṭa muṭiyum. ivvaḷavu nakikaḷip peṇkaḷippōl āṇkaḷum aṇintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu meyppikka maṉitaṉ pala cāṉṟukaḷik kāṭṭa muṭiyum; nīṅkaḷ cintaṉi ceyya vēṇṭām. mutaṉ mutalāka kikaḷilum kālkaḷilum ulōka aṇikaḷ aṇiyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiyavikaḷi maṟuttu nīṅkaḷ eḻutalām. eṅkāvatu kāṇappaṭum oṉṟiraṇṭu vitivilakkukaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi vivātikkalām. atic ciṟappāka eṭuttuk koṇṭu evarum taṅkaḷ mūḷiyik kuḻappik koṇṭirukka vēṇṭām. ārampat til eḻunta nōkkam piṟkālattil māṟik kikaḷum kālkaḷum aḻakāka iruppatākat tōṉṟiyapiṉ  ati mēlum aḻakuṟac ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattil aṇikaḷ pūṇappaṭṭirukka lām. itu, oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil eḻuntirukkalām eṉpatil evarukkum karuttu vēṟupāṭu irukkalām. allatu illāmalum irukkalām.
nāṭṭiyam tamiḻ makkaḷōṭu mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tōṉṟi apivirutti aṭintuḷḷatu. makkaḷ tam uṇavu uṟpattikkāka mikunta ciramappaṭṭu, tam muyaṟciyil perum veṟṟiyīṭṭiyatum eḻum niṟivāṉa makiḻccip perukkāl arumpum āṭṭamē  nāṭṭiyamāka malarntatu eṉṟu nām muṉṉar viḷakkiyuḷḷōm. nāṭṭiyattil ilayap piramāṇam mika mukkiyam eṉpatum namakkut teriyum. āṭṭattil kikaḷum (ataṅkaḷum) pātak kiramaṅkaḷum mika mukkiyamāṉavikaḷākum. mikap paḻaṅkāla nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷil pūkkaḷum mālikaḷumkūṭa mukkiyattuvam peṟṟiruntaṉa. nāṭṭiyattil, mikap piṟkālattil ulōkaṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uracuvatāl eḻum oli mukkiyamāṉatāka irun tatu. kikaḷilum kālkaḷilum aṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mōti allatu kuluṅki eḻum aṇikaḷiṉ ōci kaḷum maṇikaḷiṉ olikaḷum nāṭṭiyattic ciṟappippatāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu. tamiḻar nāṭṭiyattil aṇikaḷukku mukkiyat tuvam aḷikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu itaṉāl tāṉ eṉpati nām maṟantu viṭuvataṟkilli. kālil kaṭṭiya cataṅkikaḷiṉ ōciyum kāl viralkaḷil aṇintuḷḷa meṭṭi, miñci mutaliyavikaḷil eḻum ōciyum iṉpam payappaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkākavē ivvaṇikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulōka aṇikaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ aṇintu vanta tōlāṭikaḷum elumpu aṇikaḷum caṅku aṇikaḷum maṟiyat toṭaṅkiṉa. muṉ ṉuḷḷa tōlāṭikaḷum nakam, pal, kompu, malar, koṭṭi, elumpu, caṅku aṇikaḷ aṉittiyumviṭa ulōkattāl ceyyap paṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ niṟattālum oliyālum kavarcciyi nalkiṉa. kaviṉpeṟa viḷaṅkiṉa. kaṇkaḷukkum cevikaḷukkum karuttiṟkum viruntāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl muṟkālattilirunta irumpu mōtiraṅ kaḷum vaḷikaḷum oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ piṟpakutivari kāṭci aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻakattil cila tāḻttappaṭṭa makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum irumpu īyam pittaḷi veḷḷi cempu aṇikaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻakattiṉ nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyattilaṉṟi, naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟa makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyamāka navilappeṟum parata nāṭṭiyattilum palavita apinayak kikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil pāmpup paṭakki (carppacīrṣa atam), māṉtalikku (mirukacīrṣa atam), pulikki (viyākkirama atam), arimukakki (cimmamuka atam), tēṉīk ki (piramara atam), kōḻik koṇṭik ki (tāmpiracūṭa atam), aṉṉamukak ki (amcāciya atam), aṉṉattiṉ iṟakkik ki (amcapaṭca atam), karimukakki (kajamuka atam) āmik ki (kūrma atam), tavaḷik ki (maṇṭūka atam) iṭampuri caṅkukki (caṅka atam) puṟākki (kapōta atam), naṇṭuk ki (kaṟkaṭaka atam), mīṉ ki (matsaya atam), paṉṟikki (varāka atam), yāṉik kompuk ki (kajatanta atam), mayil ki (mayūra atam), kattirik kōl alakuk ki (kattirimuka atam), viḷāmpaḻak ki (kapitta atam), ūcik ki (cūci atam), mūvilic cūlakki (tiricūla atam), ampuk ki (pāṇa atam), ampumukak ki (cilimuka atam), kayiṟuk ki (pāca atam), kalayakki (kalaca atam), arumpuk ki (mukuḷa atam), malarnta tāmarik ki (alarpatuma atam), pūk koyyum ki (kāṅkula atam), pūkkūṭik ki (puṣpapuṭa atam), kaṅkaṇakki (kaṭakāmuka atam) pōṉṟak kikaḷ mukkiyamāṉa kikaḷ ākum. apinayak kikaḷil perumpālāṉa kikaḷ paṇṭiyat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uṇavup poruṭkaḷāka irunta paṟavikaḷiyum, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uṟuppukaḷiyum, vilaṅku kaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭuvataṟkuriya kompu, ampu, kayiṟu mutaliyavi kaḷiyum kuṟippatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru cila kikaḷ paḻaṅkaḷiyum malarkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭuvatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭiyat tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poruṭkaḷip peṟṟatum makiḻcciyāl āṭiṉar. anta makiḻccikkuriya āṭṭattil paḻaṅ kaḷiyum, koṭṭikaḷiyum taṅkaḷiṉ veṟṟic ciṉṉamāka uṭalil aṇintu koṇṭaṉar. mayiliṟakukaḷit taliyiṟ cūṭiyum pūkkaḷit taliyilum kaḻuttilum aṇintum koṭikaḷi kikaḷil kaṅkaṇamākak kaṭṭiyum āṭiyum pāṭiyum vantaṉar. makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyam avarkaḷ tam uṇavup poruṭkaḷi niṟi vākap peṟṟatiṉ payaṉāka eḻunta ekkaḷippil arumpiyatu. ici yum inta iṉpa āṭṭattōṭu tuḷirttatutāṉ. uṇavup poruḷ kaḷākiya paḻaṅkaḷum koṭṭikaḷum kāykaḷum ātiyil nāṭṭi yattic ciṟappikkum aṇikaḷāy arumpiṉa. uṇavup poruṭ kaḷāṉa ilikaḷum taḷirkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ āṭikaḷāy amintaṉa. kaṇṇiyum mūkkiyum karuttiyum īrtta, vaṇṇam vāynta kiṇṇam pōṉṟa naṟumaṇa malar aṇikaḷum koṭiyaṇi kaḷum itaṟku muṉṉarē tōṉṟiyavi eṉṟālum avi maṟi yātu ivaṟṟōṭu kāṭci aḷittē vantaṉa. namatu nāṭṭil eḻunta nāṭṭiya aṇikaḷum āṭikaḷum namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ pōtiya uṇavic cēkarittu uṇṭu makiḻnta veṟṟik koṇṭāṭṭattil eḻuntavikaḷē eṉṟu maṉita nākarika ārāycciyil taliciṟanta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. ati nām eḷitil taḷḷi viṭuvataṟkilli. atil uṇmi uḷḷatu. uṇavu uṟpatti kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷukku uyir nāṭiyāka iruntatu. avarkaḷ kaṟkālattil vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ uḻavut toḻili uṇarārkaḷ. uḻutu payiriṭṭu uṇavu uṟpatti ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarvē avarkaḷ uḷḷattil untavilli. iyaṟkiyāy malikaḷil uḷḷa maraṅkaḷil kiṭikkum kāykaṉikaḷiyum, vēṭṭiyil kiṭikkum vilaṅku paṟavi mutaliyavikaḷiṉ ūṉiyum pucittu avarkaḷ kālaṅkaḻittu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kāykaṉikaḷi kallāl vīḻttavum kalleṭuttu vīci vilaṅkukaḷi yum paṟavikaḷiyum koṉṟu tiṉṉa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṭa avarkaḷ piṟkālattiltāṉ aṟintārkaḷ. ataṟkum piṉṉartāṉ villikkaṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ neruppip payaṉpaṭuttavum aṟiyārkaḷ. avarkaḷ piṟkālattilē ūṉiyum kāy kaṉikaḷi yum vēkavittuc cāppiṭat terintaṉar. kaṟkālam māṟi ulōka kālam eḻum poḻutē avarkaḷ arivāḷ maṇiyiyum vāḷi yum kattiyiyum kaṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kāykaṉikaḷ niṟinta kuṟiñci nilattil, vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum niṟinta malikaḷil tāṉ vāḻntārkaḷ. aṅku taṉi uṭimi illi; ellām potu uṭimitāṉ. eṉṟālum cila kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ uṇṇu vataṟkuk kāykaṉiyum ūṉum kiṭiyātu pala nāṭkaḷ tavittaṉar inniliyilum avarkaḷukkut tiṭīreṉṟu vēṭṭip poruḷ kaḷum kāykaṉikaḷum koṭṭikaḷum kiṭittuviṭum. appoḻutu iṉpam poṅkip perukkeṭuttōṭum. kāy kaṉikaḷi vēṇṭiya maṭṭum tiṉṟu paciyit taṇippārkaḷ. eñciya kāykaṉikaḷiyum koṭṭikaḷiyum taḷirkaḷiyum ilikaḷiyum  aṇintu āṭippāṭi makiḻvārkaḷ. ituvē namatu ārampak kāla aṇivaki kaḷiṉ piṟappu varalāṟākum.
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	‘The sacred chank of India - James Hornell. F.L.S. (Madras) 1914 : P. 3
2.	“This fishery is the only one that is carried on systematically and with a definite organization. As detiled else where (P. 43) references in Tamil classics make it clear that this fishery was being prosecuted with vigour under Pandyan rule as long as 1,800 years ago; in those days the head - quarters was at Korki, an important city of traders, Jewerllers, Pearl - fishers and Chank - divers.”
	Ibid . 1. The Antiquity of the Industry P. 42
	“Reference to ancient Tamil classics furnishes evidence scanty but conclusive of the existence of an important chank cutting industry in the ancient Pandyan kingdom in the early centuries of the christian era. Similar evidence is also extant of a widespread use of carved and ornamenented chank bangles in formor days by the women of Pandyan Country.”.
2.	“The Tamil name kuli and todu now applied for ear ornaments made of gold or even stones sugest the very ancient and pre - historic times when leaves and flower were worn. The habit of small girls wearing a leaf shaped pendent made of silver and gold and of women wearing an ornament called mekali round their waist is reminiscent of the times when they wore only leaves to cover their nakednes” - Ancient Jaffna. C. Rasanaygan. P. 169
3.	Marg. (Bombay) Vol XIII No. 1 dated dece. 1959 P. 56
4.	muḻavimuḻu makalāṅkaṇ	
viḻavuniṉṟa viyaṉmaṟukiṉ
