5. cintuveḷi nākarikam
mokañcatārō aṇikalaṉkaḷ
mokañcatārōvil pala muttirikaḷum cuṇṇāmpu, veṇkalam ākivikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṭimaṅkaḷum pāṉi caṭṭikaḷum āyutaṅkaḷum cuṭumaṇ ceṅkallāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa pala māṭikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉ ṉōrkaḷ allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟum paḻam perumakkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam veḷḷik kaḷi maṇ, tantam, elumpu, kal, navamaṇikaḷ, caṅku, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavi kaḷik koṇṭu ceyta eṇṇaṟṟa vitamāṉa ērāḷamāṉa eḻiloḻukum aṇikaḷic ceytu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. ēḻikaḷ kaṟuppuk kaḷimaṇ, civappuk kaḷimaṇ, veḷḷik kaḷimaṇ ākiyavikaḷik koṇṭu ciṟu maṇikaḷ pōl ceytu ataṉ naṭuvē tuḷiyiṭṭu nūlāl kōttu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. cilar cuṭṭakaḷimaṇ maṇikaḷi aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. cilar uyarnta vili matippuḷḷa marakatam, māṇikkam, nīlam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷip palavitamākat tēyttu merukiṭṭu tuḷiyiṭṭu nūlil kōttu aṇintuḷḷaṉar. cilar poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum potintu tuḷiyiṭṭu nūlil kōttu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. caṅkukaḷālāṉa vaḷiyalkaḷum aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. paṉṉiṟac calavik kaṟkaḷ, elumpu, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavikaḷāl ceytuḷḷa vaḷikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷ iṉṟiya āntira makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇintuḷḷa veḷḷik kāl aṇikaḷ pōl kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa mālikaḷil kāṇappaṭum maṇikaḷ cuṇṭik kāy mutal koṭṭippākkuvariyuḷḷa parumaṉil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi taṭṭiyāyum vaṭṭamāyum nīḷamāyum uḷḷaṉa.
iṅkup pala poṉ koṇṭi ūcikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku oru veḷḷip pāttirattil pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷum paḷapaḷappāka oḷi vīcum uyarnta maṇikaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyta tali nāṭākkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
taliyaṇikaḷ
mokañcatārōvil kiṭitta patumikaḷ mūlam aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ paṇṭiyat tamiḻ makkaḷip pōl taliyil oru nāṭāvi cuṟṟik kaṭṭi vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. itu cumēriyariṭamum  iruntu vanta paḻakkamākum. ivi poṉ takaṭṭāl ari aṅkula akalamum patiṉāṟu aṅkula nīḷamum uḷḷaṉa. nuṉi iḻuttuk kaṭṭuvataṟkēṟṟatāy nuṉiyil tuḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tuḷiyil vēṟu aṇikaḷik kaṭṭit toṅkaviṭṭum irukka lām. ivvaṇikaḷi āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇintu vantatāka aṟikiṟōm. iṅku āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum talimayiri vārik koṇṭi iṭuvatum curuṭṭik koḷvatum uṇṭeṉṟu terikiṟatu. talimītu tamiḻ makkaḷippōl taliyaṇikaḷ cila aṇivatum uṇṭu.
tali mayirkaḷic cuṟṟi kaṭṭik koṇṭālum avi nekiḻntu vīḻntuviṭātirukka iṉṟiyat tamiḻakap peṇkaḷ pōṉṟu 5000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ vāḻnta mokañcatārō makaḷirum koṇṭi ūcikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantatāka aṟiki ṟōm. koṇṭi ūcikaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi, veṇkalam, tantam, elumpu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāy iruk kiṉṟaṉa. ik koṇṭi ūcikaḷ palvēṟu vakikaḷākavum iruk kiṉṟaṉa. oru veṇkalak koṇṭi ūci cumār 4¼ aṅkula nīḷam uḷḷatu. ataṉ koṇṭikaḷil tamiḻnāṭṭuk koṇṭi ūci kaḷip pōl āṭu, māṭu, māṉ, puṟā pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. oru koṇṭi ūciyil iru māṉ talikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ammāṉkaḷ vevvēṟu ticiyi nōkkuva tāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ataṉ kompukaḷ aḻakāka muṟukkik koṇṭiruppatu pōlat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. oru ūciyiṉ talippakuti maṭṭum kiṭittuḷḷatu. anta ūciyiṉ talipakkam mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ tōḷkaḷil kiyi vittukkoṇṭu kuntiyiruppatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ ūci marattālāṉatupōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vēṟu cila ūcikaḷ talippuṟam veṇkallāl ceyyappaṭṭatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nutal aṇikaḷ
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum iruntu pala nutal aṇikaḷ - atāvatu neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi taliyaṇikaḷiṉiṉṟu neṟṟiyil toṅkum oru vakip patakkamē yākum. ivi vaṭṭavaṭivil mukkōṇampōl kūmpiyaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭavi. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭu aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavu uṭiyaṉa. ivi poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pīṅkāṉ mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi tali muṭiyil kōttuk kaṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu ceyyappaṭṭut tali nāṭāvil kōppataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ mūṉṟu nāṉku cippikaḷi iṇittuc ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku ērāḷamāṉa kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷ kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. ataṉ nutal aṇikaḷip pōṉṟu ivi kāṇappaṭu vatik koṇṭē ivi neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu tīrmāṉikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ neṟṟiyiliruntu toṭaṅki oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka vittu uccantalivari kūntali aḻaku paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. ivi mēṟpuṟam poṉṉālum aṭippuṟam veḷḷi yālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi perumpālum 1¼ aṅkulam nīḷam uḷḷaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtaṉṟi paṭṭiyākak kāṇappaṭum cila poṟṟakaṭukaḷ arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi neṟṟiyil aṇiyappaṭuvaṉavēyākum; neṟṟippaṭṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. inta aṇikaḷip pōṉṟu neṟṟiyil paṭṭam kaṭṭiya cila patumikaḷ iṅkuk kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. neṟṟiyil paṭṭam kaṭṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iṉṟu illi. muṉṉar iruntu vantatu. nelli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ātittanallūrp puki kuḻikaḷil uḷḷa mutumakkaḷ tāḻiyil uḷḷa elumpuk kūṭu kaḷuṭaṉ poṉṉāl, ceyta inta neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭuc ceṉṉi aracāṅkap paḻam poruṭkāṭci cāliyil vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. neṟṟic cuṭṭi aṇiyum vaḻakkam iṉṟum tamiḻakattil uṇṭu. nāṭṭiyamāṭupavarkaḷ inta aṇiyi viṭṭuviṭuvatē illi.
mūkkaṇikaḷ
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum kiṭitta eṇṇi lāppatumikaḷ oṉṟilkūṭa mūkkaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. mēlum mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇiyum vaḻakkam tirāviṭarkaḷiṭam illi. tamiḻakattil uḷḷa cilikaḷilum cempu, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷi lum mūkkaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. eṉavē muṟkālat tamiḻar kaḷiṭam  mūkkaṇikaḷ pūṇum vaḻakkam kiṭiyātu eṉṟu aṟiñar kaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl mūkkiṉ iru puṟaṅkaḷilum tuḷiyiṭṭu mūkkuttikaḷ eṉṉum aṇikaḷ poṉṉāl maṭṭu miṉṟi maṇikaḷ patittum  aṇivatum, pala pōli maṇikaḷ patitta mūkkuttikaḷ aṇivatum mūkkiṉ naṭuttaṇṭiṉ naṭuvil tuvāra miṭṭu mūkkiṉ kīḻē atāvatu mēl utaṭṭiṉmītu toṅkum pullākku eṉṟa oru vaki naki aṇiyum vaḻakkum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu ārampa kālam vari iruntatu.
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum viḷimpil paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa amippuṭiya cakkaram pōṉṟa poṟṟakaṭṭil ceyyap paṭṭa mūkkutti pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kē. eṉ. tīkcit pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ mokañcatārō makkaḷum arappā makkaḷum mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇintataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭavilli eṉṟum ki.pi. 1200kkup piṉ mūkkaṇi aṇiyum vaḻakka muculim kaḷāl intiyāvil pukuttappaṭṭa paḻakkam eṉṟum kūṟiya piṉṉarumkūṭa, pēraṟiñar ci. ār. irāy pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta muntiya kāla makaḷir mūkkaṇikaḷ pūṇṭirun taṉar; avi kātil aṇinta tōṭukaḷuṭaṉ poṟcaṅkiliyāl iru puṟamum iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. cintuveḷip pakutiyic ciṟappāka ārāya intiya aracāṅkam niyamitta aṟiñar ṭākṭar mekkēy avarkaḷ cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ nīlakkal patitta mūkkaṇikaḷi upayōkittu vantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ivari ātarittu pāṭrik kārliṭaṉ avarkaḷum eḻutiyuḷḷār. aṟiñar em.e. vāṭ eṉpavarum arappāvil akaḻntu ārāynta aṟiñaruḷ oruvarēyāvar. avarum arappā vil vāḻnta aṇaṅkukaḷ aḻakiya mūkkuttikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kātaṇikaḷ
cintu veḷiyil kiṭitta cila kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷ kātaṇi kaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē akkāla makaḷirkaḷ palavita māṉa kātaṇikaḷ aṇintirukkalām eṉa eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila kātaṇikaḷ ciṟu kuḻāykaḷiyuṭiya kumiḻ pōṉṟaṉavākavum, cila taṅkaccuruḷ pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir cumār 30 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kātukaḷil aṇiyum vaṭṭamāṉa vaḷiyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa kaṭukkaṉ mātiri poṉṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭavikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila kātaṇikaḷ tirukāṇi vittuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu terikiṉṟaṉa. intak kūmpiya pakutiyil kuḻāy pōṉṟa uruvam vittu paṟṟavikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ituvum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatēyākum. aṟiñar vāṭ avarkaḷ arappāvil naṭattiya ārāycciyil cila veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷiyum kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār.
kaḻuttaṇikaḷ
1. mokañcatārōvil, oru veḷḷik kalattil putittu vikkappaṭṭirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷ cilavaṟṟi ṭākṭar i.cē.em. mekkēy avarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār. ivaṟṟi kaṇṭeṭut tatil avar perum makiḻcciyaṭintār. māliyoṉṟu poṉ ṉālum pacci niṟamāṉa uyarnta maṇikaḷāl kōkkappaṭṭatāy aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟatu. poṉmaṇikaḷ taṭṭiyākac ceyyap peṟṟu avaṟṟiṉ mītu aḻakuṟak kumiḻkaḷ vikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pīppāy vaṭivamāka kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa paccik kaṟkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru poṉmaṇiyum aṭuttāṟpōṟ paccik kallum ati aṭuttup poṉ maṇiyumāka muṟiyākak kōkkappaṭṭa aḻakiya māliyākat tikaḻkiṟatu. immāliyiṉ naṭuvil ēḻu aḻakiya patakkaṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoṉṟiṉ naṭuvilum oru viṭūriyamum, cūriya kāntak kallum vittuc ceyyappaṭṭatāka terikiṟatu. immāliyil oḷirum paccikkal vaṭaparmāvilum tipēttilum kiṭikkum kaṟkaḷip pōṉṟatākak kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.
2. iḵtaṉṟi maṟṟum iru kaḻuttaṇikaḷāṉa mālikaḷ mokañcatārōvil akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil oru māliyil kuḻavi vaṭivattilum, miḷaku pōṉṟa uruṇṭi vaṭivattilum, ceyyappaṭṭa poṉmaṇikaḷ kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭi iṭiyē nīlappaḷiṅkuk kallāl ceyyap paṭṭa maṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭu eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟatu. nīlap paḷiṅkuk kalmaṇikaḷ puttam putiyaṉapōl poṉṟāp poli vuṭaṉ paḷapaḷeṉa miṉṉik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
maṟṟoru māli poṉṉāl āṉatu. atu taṭṭiyākac ceyyap peṟṟa iraṇṭu maṇikaḷi iṇittup poruttap paṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. inta aḻaku mikka aṇivaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ ariya paṇiyiṉ vēli nuṭpam pōṟṟattakkatākum.
arappāviṉ aḻakiya mālikaḷ
mokañcatārō paṇpāṭṭiṟku muntiya paṇpāṭu arappaṉ paṇpāṭu. arappāvil arumpiya aṇikalaṉkaḷē mokañca tārō aṇikalaṉkaḷiviṭat toṉmiyāṉavi. eṉṟālum nuṭpamāṉa vēlippāṭukaḷil arappā vēlippāṭu mokañca tārōviṉativiṭa evvitattilum kuṟintaṉavalla. arappāvil uḷḷa orumāli nāṉku caraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatāy 240 maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭatāy eḻil mikkatāy ilaṅkukiṉṟatu. maṟṟoru māli 27 poṉmaṇikaḷi uṭiyatāyp paṟpala aḻakiya vaṭivaṅ kaḷi uṭiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭit tavirttu 50 allatu 60 maṇikaḷ kōtta oru aḻakiya māliyum kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta māli poṉmaṇikaḷālum navamaṇika ḷālum kōkkappaṭṭatu. itaṉ vēlippāṭu mika aṟputamāka irukkiṟatu. immālikaḷil aḻakiya patakkaṅkaḷum cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi oṉṟu mutal 13 patakkaṅkaḷ vari kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. poṉmaṇikaḷ aṉṟi, muḻuvatum navamaṇikaḷālāṉa, mālikaḷum uḷa. mokañca tārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa mālikaḷiviṭa arappāvil atikamāṉa mālikaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭa pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷ viyattaku vēlippāṭukaḷ koṇṭavikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. cila māli kaḷil civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, viṭūriyam, viram, māṇikkam, amecāṉ eṉṟa oru vakip paccik kaṟkaḷuṭaṉ cūriyakāntak kaṟkaḷ paṭikak kaṟkaḷ patittavikaḷāyum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. inta maṇikaḷ āpkāṉicuttāṉ, kācumīr, tipēt, parmā piṟa intiya nāṭu kaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭavikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maṇikaḷ nalla vaḻavaḻappākat tēykkappeṟṟu merukiṭappaṭṭiruppatum ivikaḷit tuvāramiṭṭiruppatum  ariya ceyaleṉa aṟiñarkaḷāl matikkappaṭukiṟatu.
iṭuppaṇi
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭup palvēṟu vakiyāṉa iṭup paṇikaḷi (nucuppaṇikaḷi) aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. ippoḻutu iṭiyil aṇivatu oṭṭiyāṇam eṉappaṭum. muṟkālattil mēkali, kalāpam, viriciki pōṉṟa palavaki aṇikaḷ aṇiyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. ittakiya aṇikaḷ arappāvilum mokañca tārōvilum kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. arippaṭṭiki nalla vēlippāṭuṭiyavi. cila 4 caraṅkaḷiyum cila 6 caraṅkaḷiyum uṭiyaṉa. ovvoru carattilum pīppāy pōṉṟa civappuk kalmaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷil cila malarkaḷiṉ arumpukaḷ pōṉṟum cila oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟum cataṅkikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku muttukkaḷ kōtta nucuppaṇikaḷ cilavum kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷil cilavaṟṟil nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ uḷḷa aḻakiya mukappukaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
inta nucuppaṇikaḷ cilavaṟṟil palvēṟu uyarnta navamaṇikaḷ kōkkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. inta maṇikaḷiṟ pala veḷināṭukaḷi liruntu iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa koṟki muttukaḷum iṅku iṭam peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.
cintu veḷi nākarikattik kaṭṭi vaḷartta tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ mutuperuṅ kuravarkaḷ naṉiciṟanta nākarikatti yuṭiya makkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa avarkaḷiṉ navamaṇikaḷ ilaṅkum nucuppaṇi oṉṟē nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. maṇi kaḷi naṉṟākac cōtittu avikaḷil kuṟṟamaṟṟa maṇikaḷip poṟukki eṭuttut tēyttu merukiṭṭu, tuḷiyiṭṭu iruppatu āccariyappaṭattakkatākum. avarkaḷiṭam inta ariya vēli kaḷic ceyya uyarnta karuvikaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. palamaṇikaḷi oṭṭavittu iṇittut tēyttu aḻakiya pūppōlac ceytuḷḷaṉar.
kiyaṇi
tamiḻarkaḷ ulōka kālattiṟku muṉpē atāvatu putiya kaṟkālattilē kāppu eṉṉum nūli aṇiya muṟpaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa kiyaṇi palāppaḻattiṉ mēl tōlip pōṉṟu mēlē muḷ muḷḷāka cūriya katirkaḷip pōl kūriya muṉikaḷiyuṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu. ittakiya kik kāppukaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil malippakutiyil uḷḷa paḻam kuṭi makkaḷiṭamum uḷḷaṉa. iḵtaṉṟi palavitak kāppukaḷum vaḷikaḷum iṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mēlum pala cempu, veḷḷi, poṉ kāppukaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cila melliya takaṭukaḷāl kuḻāy pōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷē meḻuku aṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṅku ippi, kaḷimaṇ, mākkal, caṅku, paḷiṅku mutaliyavi kaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa ki aṇikaḷum uṇṭu.
mokañcatārōvil palavitamāṉa mālikaḷum kāppukaḷum kaṭakaṅkaḷum vaḷikaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi pōṟṟat takkaviyākum. uruṇṭiyāṉa poṉmaṇikaḷik kōttu aḻakiya mukappukaḷ iṇittuc ceyyappeṟṟa kolucu kaḷum uṇṭu.
aṉṟiyum civappu, kaṟuppuk kaṟkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟiya kāppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭitta maṇ kāppukaḷ aṟputa māṉa vēlippāṭuṭiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa ikkāppukaḷukku palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa veṇkala nāṭṭiya maṅkiyiṉ kiyil aṭukkaṭukkāyp pala vaḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatu pārkkat takkatākum.
paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻ makaḷir taṅkaḷ kikaḷil poṉ, veḷḷi, kaṇṇāṭi, meḻuku, caṅku, vaḷikaḷ aṇivatip pōṉṟu cintuveḷi makaḷirum palvēṟu vitamāṉa kāppukaḷum kolukkaḷum vaḷikaḷum aṇintu vantatu ārāyattakkatākum.
kiviral aṇikaḷ
arappāvil poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliyavikaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa kiviral aṇikaḷ - mōtiraṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārōvil cempu, veṇkalam, caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. arappāvil aḻakiya pīṅkāṉ mōtiraṅkaḷ kūṭak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuc cila mōtiraṅkaḷ melliya uruṇṭik kampikaḷālāṉa vaḷiyaṅkaḷākak kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. ivi mōtiraṅkaḷākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṉṟit taṭṭiyākak kampikaḷāl taṭṭappeṟṟa ulōkattālāṉa mōtiraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
arappāvil kiṭitta aḻakiya mōtiram oṉṟu catura mukappu uṭiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mukappiṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukku mākap pala kōṭukaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṅkuḷḷa caṅkuc cippi mōtiraṅkaḷ nalla eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku aṟiñarkaḷ maṇikaḷ patitta mōtiraṅkaḷi evarum kaṇṭatākak kūṟavilli.
kāl aṇikaḷ
kāl aṇikaḷ, taṇṭi, cilampu, kāl kāppu eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum. mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa pommi kaḷil vaḷinta kāl kāppukaḷ - atāvatu taṇṭikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ mūlam iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ uṟutiyākak kāl aṇikaḷ aṇintirukkalām eṉṟu karuta muṭiyum. ittakiya kāl kāppukaḷ tirāviṭa nākarikam paraviyiruppatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum kirīṭ tīvilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. moñcatārōvil kaṇṭa kālaṇikaḷ kirīṭ tīvil maṭṭumaṉṟi āntira nāṭṭilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭintiyāvil cimlā pakutikaḷil vāḻum mali iṉa makkaḷiṟ palar iṉṉum intak kālkāppukaḷi viṭāmal aṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa aṇiyākap pulappaṭum cilampukaḷum iṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mokañca tārōvil veḷḷiyāl ceyyap peṟṟa kāl kāppukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṭivil pala poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
kālviral aṇi
makkaḷ kiviralkaḷil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉi mōtiram eṉpar. cilar viralāḻi, kaṇiyāḻi eṉṟum kūṟuvar. tamiḻarkaḷ ki viralkaḷil aṇiyum mōtiram pōṉṟu kāl viralkaḷilum palavita aṇikaḷ aṇivar. avi perumpālum veḷḷiyāka irukkum. āṉāl mokañcatārōvil tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷirkaḷip pōl peṇkaḷ kālviral mōtiraṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa meṭṭiyi aṇintu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. tamiḻakattip pōṉṟu arappāvilum, mokañcatārōvilum palavitak kāl viralaṇikaḷ kiṭikkavilli. āṉāl niccayamākak kāl viral aṇikaḷ aṇintuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum. ār. ṭi. pāṉarji pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ mokañcatārōvil pīli pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa veḷḷik kālviral mōtiraṅkaḷum kiṭittiruppatākak kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
itara aṇikaḷ
iviyaṉṟi arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum poṉ; veḷḷi, veṇkalam, cempu, mākkal, caṅku, pīṅkāṉ, paṭikam, pavaḷam, maṇikkal, maṇ pōṉṟavikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷiṟ palavaṟṟi iṉṉa aṇi eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. aṅkuk kiṭitta pommikaḷil tīṭṭappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷi vittē iṉṉa aṇi eṉṟu tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. iṅkup pala tōṭukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi pottāṉkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. inta aṇiyil aḻakiya iḷam piṟi vaṭivam kūṭak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ naṭuvil oḷitarum maṇikaḷum cippikaḷum patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷa cumār 2-6 aṅkula nīḷamum, 2-3 aṅkula akalamum uḷḷa patakkaṅkaḷ taṉic ciṟappukaḷ vāyntaṉa. avi mākkallil ākkappeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mītu iḷam piṟivaṭivam oḷirkiṟatu. erutu uruvamum uḷḷatu. itu camaya cammantamāṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.5
kuṟippukaḷ :
1.	(A) The Civilization of Babylonia & Assyria - Jastrowe
	(B) The Excavation Babylon - Rebert Koldway.
2.	Mohenjo - Daro and the Indus Civilization - Sir. John Marshall 
Vol. I & II.
3.	Dravidian Civilisation - Prof. Rakhal Das Benerji, Modern Review Sep. 1927.
	“There are two different theories about the Dravidian invation or migration into India. One class of writers believe that the Dravidian migrated from India into Babylonia through Afganistan and Beluchistan. The similarity of Dravidian and Sumarian ethnic type was recongised by H.R. Hall long before the discoveries of Mohenja - daro and Harappa. It is of opinion that” It is by no means improbable that the Sumarians were an Indian race which passed certinly by land perhaps also by sea through Persia to the Valley of two Rivers?
4.	The wonders that was India - A.L. Basham New York - 1954. P. 24
	“The modern South Indian is usually a blend of Mediterranian and Proto - Australoid, the two Chief ethnic factors in the Harappa culture moreover the Harappa religion seems to show many similarities with these elements of Hinduism which are specially popular in the Dravidian Country.”
5.	(a) cintu veḷi makkaḷ kaṟkaḷic cōtittu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmik kēṟṟa muṟiyil uruvākki vaḻavaḻappākat tēyttu merukiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. ivvēlippāṭṭiṟkut tēvippaṭṭa kaṭical entiraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. - mokañcatārō ṭākṭar. mā. ïuhrkhÂ¡f«, v«.V., ãv¢o.,
	(ā) poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam yāṉittantam, kaṇṇāṭi, ōṭukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷāl cintuveḷiyil āparaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa tamiḻ intiyā. na. ci. kantiyā piḷḷi, ceṉṉi 1949. pak. 27.

6. aṇikaḷum paṇikaḷum
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil pala irumpu āyutaṅkaḷum veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṭkalaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ātitta nallūr nākarikam cumār ki.mu. 1000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉum ki.mu. 2000ām āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum eḻunta nākarikamāka irukkalām eṉṟu puti poruḷ ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam tamiḻakattil mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟalām. atōṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷi ākkum poṟkollarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷāṉa taccar, kollar, kaṉṉār, kaltaccar pōṉṟavarkaḷum iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ piruntē tamiḻakattil nilitta kuṭikaḷāy vāḻntu varukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu uṟutiyāyk kūṟamuṭiyum. arappā nākarikattiṉ mūlam tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa cintu veḷik kammāḷarkaḷ ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē arumperum āyutaṅkaḷi yum aḻakiya poṉ aṇikaḷiyum ariya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇi kalaṉkaḷiyum nāliṟku mēṟpaṭṭa māṭikaḷiyuṭiya oppaṟṟa uṟiyuḷkaḷiyum ariya vēlippāṭukaḷiyuṭiya veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷiyum  cuṇṇāmpiṉālum nurikalliṉālum ceyyap paṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa paṭimaṅkaḷiyum muttirikaḷiyum paṭittiruk kiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu meyppikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
teyva kammiyar 
taṭṭār, taccar, kaṉṉār, kollar, kaltaccar ākiya aintu piriviṉarum vicuvappirammaṉiṉ tirukkaṇṇil utayamāṉavarkaḷ eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ vicuvakarmā, mayaṉ ākiya teyvakkammiyar vaḻiyil vantavarkaḷ eṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṟkollarkaḷ tamiḻakattil kammiyar, kammāḷar, taṭṭār, ācāriyār eṉa aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl akarātikaḷilum nikaṇṭukaḷilum ivarkaḷukku, aḵkacāliyar, aṟivar aṟputar, ōvar, kaṇṇāḷar, kaṇviṉiñar, kammiyar kollar, ciṟpiyar, tapatiyar, tuvaṭṭar, pulavar, puṉivar, yavaṉar, vittakar, vittiyar, viṉiñar eṉṟu pala peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷ aṉivarum tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. ivarkaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻar tolkuṭiyil utittavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil cilar taṅkaḷi vicuvappirāmaṇar eṉṟu kūṟi āriyarkaḷōṭu iṇintu koḷḷa muṉintu iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷi nākar eṉpārum uḷar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ uyir nāṭi eṉṟum nām eṇṇukiṟōm.
inta aintu toḻilāḷarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camūka vāḻ viṟku iṉṟiyamiyātavarkaḷ. taccarkaḷum kollarkaḷum vīṭu kaḷiyum vīṭṭiluḷḷa iṉṟiyamiyāta taṭṭu muṭṭuc cāmāṉ kaḷiyum ceytu utavupavarkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi uḻutal, vitittal, aṟuttal mutaliya makkaḷiṉ uyirōmpum toḻilukku iṉṟiyami yāta kalappi, koḻu, arivāḷ mutaliyavikaḷic ceytu koṭuppavarkaḷ. aṉṟiyum pāṉicaṭṭi, necavu mutaliyaṉa ceyyum toḻilāḷarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷic ceytu koṭuppavarkaḷ. kaṉṉārkaḷ cempu, pittaḷi, veṇ kalam, veḷḷi, īyam, poṉ mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷil cempu, kiṇṇam, tāmpaḷam, kuṭam, caruvam, kumpā, vaṭṭil, koṭṭuk kaṭavi, aṇṭā, pōṇiccaṭṭi, tavali, neyccaṭṭi, veṇ ṇeyccaṭṭi pōṉṟavikaḷiyum kipiṭikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, vaṇṭi kaḷukku vēṇṭiya pala alaṅkārap poruḷ pōṉṟavikaḷiyum ceypavarkaḷ. kal taccarkaḷ, tapatikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ  kal maravikaḷ, kīriccaṭṭi, ammi, ural, kuntāṇi pōṉṟavikaḷi yum kōyilkaḷ, cilikaḷ, cempup paṭimam pōṉṟavikaḷiyum ceypavarkaḷ. poṟkollarkaḷ tām āṭamparap poruḷ kaḷākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷilum maṇikaḷ iḻittum ceypavarkaḷ.2 ivarkaḷ  makkaḷukkum teyvaṅkaḷukkum maṉṉarkaḷukkum aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceypavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ atika viliyuyarnta poṉṉil aṇikalaṉ kaḷic ceyvatālum mika nuṇukkamāṉa vēlippāṭukaḷicceytu varuvatālum atikamāṉa kūli peṟupavarkaḷ. piṟa nāṉku vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷiyum viṭa nalla niliyil iruppavarkaḷ.
poṟkollarkaḷ tamiḻakattil ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukku, piṟantu oru āṇṭu niṟivu peṟumpoḻutum, ēṉ ataṟku muṉpum kātu kuttukiṟārkaḷ. kātil kaṭukkaṉ allatu ciṟu kuccukaḷ ceytu pōṭukiṟārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum kuḻantikaḷukku mūkkuk kuttu vatum uṇṭu. kaḻuttil uruttirākkam patitta kavuṭu, poṉ mali, iṭuppil poṟ koṭi, poṟcataṅki, kālil taṇṭi, kolucu kiyil ciṟu kāppu mutaliyavikaḷum ceytu aṇivippārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷil kātu kuttuvatu oru viḻāvākavē naṭattap peṟukiṟatu. itaṟkup poṟ kollarkaḷukku taṭcaṇikaḷum koṭukkappaṭum.
appāl naṭipeṟum tirumaṇa viḻāvil mutaṟkaṭṭam tālikku poṉṉurukki viṭuvatākum. itu oru nalla nāḷil uṟṟār uṟaviṉar naṇparuṭaṉ akkampakkattu vīṭṭukkārarkaḷum cūḻa iruntu naṭakkum viḻāvākum. itil mukkiya paṅku vakippavarkaḷ tamiḻakap poṟkollarkaḷēyāvar. tirumaṇa viḻāvil atāvatu tirunāṇ pūṭṭal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum maṅkalanāḷil tāli ceytu koṭukkum ācārikku taṭcaṇikaḷ koṭuttut tāliyip peṟṟup peṇ kaḻuttil aṇivikkappaṭukiṟatu.
inta camūkattil, irumpu vēli ceyyum kollarkaḷē ellāt toḻilāḷarkaḷukkum tāy pōṉṟu iruppavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ceytu koṭukkum uḷikaḷum cuttiyalkaḷum, koṭiṟu (kuṟaṭu)kaḷum piṟavum illāviṭṭāl ēṉiya toḻilāḷarkaḷ toḻil iyaṟṟa iyalātu. āṉāl ivarkaḷ tām inta aintu vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷilum poruḷātāra niliyil kīḻē iruppavarkaḷ. ellōrilum kaṣṭamāṉa toḻil puripavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷilum koñcam kuṟintiruppavarkaḷ kaltaccarkaḷum, kaṉṉārkaḷum āvar. maravēli ceyyum taccarkaḷum, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyum tapatikaḷum poruḷātāra niliyil caṟṟu uyarntiruppavarkaḷ.2 āṉāl poṉ vēli ceyyum taṭṭār kaḷē inta camūkattil uyarniliyil iruppavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ maṟṟat toḻilāḷarkaḷiviṭap poruḷātāra niliyil uyarntiruppavarkaḷ. atikak kaṣṭamillāta āṉāl nuṇmiyāṉa toḻil ceytu vāḻpavarkaḷ.
tamiḻakattiṉ camūka vāḻvilum paṇpāṭṭilum inta aintu vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷukkum mukkiya paṅkuṇṭu.3 ivarkaḷil teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷi vaṭikkum tapatikaḷ uruva vārpput tuṟiyilum, avikaḷil uyarnta pāvaṅkaḷi uruvākkuvatilum kitērntavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ciṟpa cāttiraṅ kaḷiyum, kaṭṭiṭak kali nūlkaḷiyum (vāttu cāttiraṅkaḷi yum, civākamaṅkaḷiyum) payiṉṟavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ. camaya muṟippaṭi āccāra aṉuṭṭāṉaṅkaḷic ceypavarkaḷāyt tikaḻkiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ pōṟṟutalukkum matippiṟkum uriyavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ.
āṉāl paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷ aṉittilum poṟ kollarkaḷiṉ māperum kittiṟam pōṟṟak kūṭiyatāka miḷir kiṟatu. atu paḻaṅkālap paṇpi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatāka amintirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ kivaṇṇam meyvanta aṇikalak kaliyiṉ uyarnta - oppaṟṟa apiviruttikku naṟcāṉṟākat tikaḻ kiṉṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ aṇikalakkali, arappāviṉ tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ mūlam 5000ām āṇṭu1 paḻamiyuṭiyatāka matikkap paṭukiṟatu; ātittanallūr tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ mūlam, mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya paḻami vāyntatāy matikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivikaḷ tāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē ciṟanta nākarikam paṭitta pēriṉam eṉṟu perumiyōṭu pēcuvataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭiyiṉ vaḻivanta kollarkaḷ camitta aḻakiya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum pala aṭukkukaḷiyuṭiya vāṉṟōy māṭaṅkaḷum, mukil tavaḻum kōpuraṅkaḷum vimāṉaṅkaḷum āyutaṅkaḷum, veṇkala pāttiraṅ kaḷum paṭimaṅkaḷum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum.
aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kaḻuttaṇi kaḷum, kiyaṇikaḷum, nucuppaṇikaḷum, taliyaṇikaḷum, piṟa vaṇikaḷum iṉṟiya nākarikam  paṭitta aṇikaḷiviṭac ciṟappuṭiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟik kaṇṭu namatu intiya aracāṅkamum ataṉ amiccarum ulakil oppaṟṟa pala aṟiñarkaḷum pōṟṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. pāratanāṭu paḻam perum paṇ pāṭṭip peṟṟa nāṭu eṉap pāriluḷḷa palarum pārāṭṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. itaṉ mūlam pārata tēvikkup pārpukaḻum perum pēṟṟit tēṭikkoṭuttavarkaḷ tamiḻakak kammiyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭiyil vanta arappaṉ makkaḷāy irukkiṉ ṟārkaḷ eṉṟu nām perumiyuṟukiṉṟōm;  tirāviṭak kammiyarkaḷ paṇṭu camitta paḻam perum aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikalaṉkaḷum veḷit tōṟṟattil aḻaku vāyntaṉavāyum, uṟuti vāyntaṉa vāyum nuṇṇiya vēlip pāṭṭil oppaṟṟaṉavāyum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. namatu poṟ kollarkaḷ āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē poṉṉi mayiriḻi pōṉṟa kampiyāka iḻuppatilum poṟkaṭṭiyi veṅkāyaccaruku pōṉṟu melliya takaṭukaḷākat taṭṭuvatilum aṇikalaṉkaḷik kottuvatilum aṟuppu vēlikaḷ ceyvatilum takaṭukaḷi oṭṭip poṭi vittu ūtuvatilum caṅkilikaḷākac camippatilum koṭikaḷākap piṉṉi appāl ati iṟukkuvatilum iṟutiyāka merukiṭuvatilum, ciṟu ciṟu vaḷiyaṅkaḷākac ceytu oṉṟāka iṇittu ūci māli kaḷākavum aṇi vaṭaṅkaḷākavum ceyvatilum tiṟami vāynta varkaḷ. takaṭukaḷic curuṭṭuvatilum  taṅkat takaṭukaḷic ceytu appāl ati accil vittu aṭittu - iṟukkik kottumāṉa vēli(nakā vēli)yiṉ mūlam puṭippu uruvaṅkaḷic ceyvatilum aṟuppumāṉa vēlippāṭṭiṉ mūlam, ceṭi, koṭi, malar, arumpu, kāy, paṟavi, vilaṅku teyva uruvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷip peritākavō allatu oru ciṟu eṟumpuk kaṇpōṉṟa aḷavil ciṟitākavō ceytu cittirap pūvēlikaḷ miḷira eḻiluṟap paṇipuriya vallavarkaḷ. mañcaḷ, civappu niṟaṅkaḷil aṇikaḷukkut tiṟampaṭap paḻuppup pōṭuki ṟārkaḷ. poṉṉil kalanta veḷḷi, cempu pōṉṟavikaḷip puṭam pōṭṭu uyarnta pim poṉṉāyp paḻaṅkālattilē ceytu ataṉ māṟṟum uriyum kuṉṟātirukkac ceytārkaḷ. navamaṇikaḷip poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷil eḷitil kīḻē viḻuntuviṭāmal pūṇpittu vaṇṇa malarkaḷ pōṉṟum aḻakiya paḻaṅkaḷ pōṉṟum vaṭivāṉa ceṭi koṭikaḷ pōṉṟum ceyvatil piṟanāṭṭārkaḷiviṭat tamiḻakap pattarkaḷ (kammiyarkaḷ) kivaṇṇam vāyntavarkaḷāy iṉṟum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ.4
paṇikaḷ
aṇikalaṉkaḷukku, niṟattilum, tarattilum pacumiyilum ēṟṟa tātupporuḷ poṉṉēyākum. cuṭiṉum cempoṉ taṉṉoḷi kuṉṟātu eṉpatu tamiḻar kaṇṭa poṉmoḻi. poṉ eṅkuk kiṭantālum mācēṟātu; veḷḷiyil kaṟiyēṟum, cempil kaḷimpēṟum, irumpil turuppiṭikkum. pacumpoṉ ekkālattum turuvēṟātu; oḷi kuṉṟātu; māṟṟuk kuṟiyātu; ataṉ niṟam uḷḷattik kavarkiṟatu. poṉ vāḻvi vaḷampeṟac ceykiṟatu.
poṉ aṇikaḷ ceyvataṟku ēṟṟa, uyarnta taṉmi vāynta kaṉipporuḷāka irukkiṟatu. oru ciṟu tāṉiya eṭi niṟiyuḷḷa poṉṉi 500 aṭi nīḷam uḷḷa poṉ iḻiyāy nīṭṭi viṭalām. atu māperum karuvūlattiṉ carva viyāpakamāṉa ciṉṉamāka ilaṅkukiṟatu. muṟkālat tamiḻaka makaḷir āyiram poṉpeṟṟa poṉ iḻikaḷālāṉa poṟcarikip paṭṭāṭi kaḷip puṉintu vantuḷḷaṉar. poṉ iḻi vēynta tukilkaḷ mikutiyāka muṟkālat tuṇic cantiyil viṟkap peṟṟaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi iḻikaḷit tūymiyākap pārttu iṇittu vili yuyarnta carikic cēliyāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa; uyarāṭiyākap pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉa. teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ poṉṉāl vārkkap peṟṟaṉa. poṟṟakaṭṭāl capparaṅkaḷum, kūrikaḷum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. citamparattiluḷḷa āṭavallāṉ āṭiya poṉṉampalam (kaṉakacapi) ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟatāyt tikaḻkiṟatu. vimāṉa mukaṭu kaḷil poṟ kalayaṅkaḷ ēṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakattil ērāḷa mākap poṉ kiṭittatu. tamiḻarkaḷil ēḻi mutal paṇakkārar vari poṉ aṇikaḷ pūṇpatil pērārvam koṇṭaṉar. poṉṉit teyvamākap pōṟṟiṉar. poṉṉiṉ mītu koṇṭa uyarvāṉa eṇṇattiṉālē iṟivaṉip poṉ pōṉṟa niṟam vāyntavaṉākak karutip pōṟṟiṉar. tēvārattil poṉṉār mēṉiyaṉē eṉṟum poṉṉē eṉṟum pōṟṟip pukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaka vaṇikarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷukku, uḷnāṭṭup poruḷ kaḷi ēṟṟumati ceytu tamiḻnāṭṭil veḷināṭṭiliruntu ērāḷamākap poṉvantu kuviyumāṟu ceytu vantaṉar. vaṇikarkaḷ veḷināṭu ceṉṟu vaṇikam purintu ērāḷamāṉa poṉṉit tiraṭṭi vantaṉar. tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷi veṉṟu aṅkirunta poṉṉic cūṟiyāṭi vantaṉar. veḷināṭṭu maṉṉar kaḷi veṉṟu avarkaḷiṭamiruntu āṇṭutōṟum ērāḷamāṉa poṉṉit tiṟiyākap peṟṟu vantaṉar. yavaṉarkaḷ tamiḻaka muttukkaḷ, maculiṉ tuṇikaḷ miḷaku pōṉṟavimītu mōkaṅ koṇṭu kōṭikōṭiyāyp poṟkācukaḷiyum poṟkaṭṭi kaḷiyum koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiviṭṭut tamiḻaka muttukkaḷiyum tuṇikaḷiyum piṟapaṇṭaṅkaḷiyum vāṅkic ceṉṟaṉar. tamiḻakat tilum atic cūḻnta nāṭukaḷilum ērāḷamāṉa poṉ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. pala maṇikaḷ curaṅkaṅkaḷiliruntum kaṭaliṉiṉṟum kiṭittaṉa. ataṉāl tamiḻakam poṉ koḻikkum naṉṉāṭāy viḷaṅkiyatu. tamiḻaka makaḷirkaḷ poṉkāykkum maramō eṉṟu piṟanāṭṭār aiyuṟum vaṇṇam uccitoṭṭu uḷḷaṅkāl vari poṉ aṇikaḷip pūṇṭiruntaṉar. ēḻi makkaḷ kūṭa ērāḷa māṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭiruntaṉar.
aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkun toḻil
caṅka kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇiyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. ataṟkup paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum cep pēṭukaḷum teyvat tiru uruvaṅkaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkak kāla maṉṉarkaḷum - ēṉ? teyvaṅkaḷum taliyil muṭikaḷit (poṉṉālum maṇiyālum ceyyappeṟṟa makuṭaṅkaḷit) tarittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ meyyil palvēṟu aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum kiyil maṇikaḷ patitta poṉṉālāṉa ceṅkōliyum pūṇ ṭiruntaṉar. avi mika nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ uṭiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa. cilappatikārattil vēntar muṭimutaṟ kalaṉkaḷ camip pōṉ yāṉeṉa varum ceyyuḷ mūlam akkālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum maṉṉarkaḷ poṉmuṭi puṉintiruntaṉar eṉṟum avikaḷit tamiḻakattiṉ poṟkollarkaḷē camittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm.
urukkut taṭṭār
caṅkakkāla maṉṉarkaḷ pala poṉ aṇikaḷip pūṇṭirun taṉar. aracikaḷ muṭiyiṉiṉṟu aṭivarip pala poṉ aṇikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar. kāvalarkaḷ, pāvalarkkum, pāṇarkaḷukkum pāṭiṉikaḷukkum piṟarkkum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷip paricāka aḷittu vantaṉar. poṟkācukaḷi vārivāri vaḻaṅkiṉar. itaṟ kellām kāraṇam tamiḻakam peṟṟa poruḷātārac ceḻumiyē yākum. ērāḷamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyap peṟṟatāl eṅkum poṟkollarkaḷ perukiṉar. akkālap poṟkollarkaḷ teyvakkammiyar eṉṟu pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil, aṉṟu urukkut taṭṭār, paṇit taṭṭār eṉṟa iru piriviṉar iruntaṉar. urukkut taṭṭār peritum poṉṉālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyvōrāy iruntaṉar. iṉṉum nākācu vēlikkārar, aṟuppu vēlikkārar eṉa iru piriviṉar tōṉṟi nuṇukkamāṉa vēli kaḷic ceytu vantaṉar.
tamiḻakattil toḻilkaḷ pala eḻuntamiyāl toḻil kaḷukku vēṇṭiya karuvikaḷ palavum eḻuntaṉa. ivikaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṉikkut toṭi (vaḷi)yiṭum kollarippaṟṟi perum pāṇāṟṟup paṭiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.5 toḻilāḷar
kaḷiṉ mukkiya karuviyākiya turutti6 koṭiṟu7 uli8 cammaṭṭi9 mutaliya karuvikaḷip paṟṟiyum toḻilāḷikaḷ ceyyum vēli kaḷip paṟṟiyum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṇittaṭṭār 
paṇikaḷ, atāvatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyum poṟ kollar kaḷukkup paṇittaṭṭār eṉṟa peyar uṇṭu. ivarkaḷil maṭṭa vēlikkārar, kottumāṉa vēlikkārar, (nakācu vēlikkārar) aṟuppu vēlikkārar pōṉṟa pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ aṉi varum poṉ allatu  veḷḷik kaṭṭikaḷi taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya aḷavu veṭṭi urukki appāl ati aṭittum taṭṭiyum aṟuttum paṇikaḷ ceytārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṟ palar kāṟṟōṭṭa millāta ciṟu aṟikaḷil taṅkaḷ kikaḷik koṇṭē aṭittut takaṭukaḷākac ceytum kampikaḷāka iḻuttum veṭṭiyum oṭṭiyum poṭi vittup paṟṟavittum arāviyum vāḷāl aṟuttum tamar ūciyāl tuḷiyiṭṭum, uḷiyāl kottiyum aṇi kalaṉkaḷi ākkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṭam iyantiraṅkaḷ etuvum kiṭiyātu. mēlum ivarkaḷiṭam cila karuvikaḷē uḷḷaṉa. āṉā lum avi ivarkaḷuṭiya paṇikaḷi niṟivākac ceyvataṟkup pōtumāṉatāka irunta ivarkaḷuṭiya taḷavāṭaṅkaḷil cūṭu uṇṭākkavum urukkavum ceyvataṟku uriya oru ciṟu ūtu kuḻalum oṉṟākum. ivarkaḷatu ulikkaḷam oru cātāraṇa maṇkuṭattiṉ uṭikkappaṭṭa mēṟpākamēyākum. maṭkuṭatti naṭuvil iraṇ ṭāka uṭittu vāyppakkattik kīḻē vittu, aṭippakutiyuḷḷa uṭpuṟatti akaṉṟa ōṭṭuc cillāl mūṭi ataṉmītu nelumiyi nirappi mēlē marakkarikaḷip parappi neruppu mūṭṭi ataṉmītu marakkariyil ciṟitu paḷḷamākat tōṇṭi vittu atil taṅkam vittu urukki appāl ati eṭuttu nīril pōṭṭu kuḷirntatum cuttiyalāl aṭippārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟi kotikkavittum aṭip pārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟik kuḻalāl ūti urukkuvārkaḷ cilavaṟṟit turuttiyāl ūti urukiyatum ati eṭuttu nīril pōṭṭu eṭuttu aṭippārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kirām eṭiyuḷḷa poṟkaṭṭikaḷiyum urukki eṇṇaṟṟa nakikaḷic ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. atikamāṉa poṉ tuṇṭukaḷ iruntāl avikaḷi yellām kuki eṉṉum uṟutiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru kalavi maṇpāttirattil pōṭṭu urukkiviṭuvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ peritum ūtu kuḻaliṉ tūṇṭutalālē poṉṉi urukkukiṉṟaṉar. periya aḷavil poṉ iruntāltāṉ turuttiyi upayōkippar. turuttiyi upayōkikkum poṟkollarkaḷ atikamāka aṇi kalaṉkaḷ ceypavarkaḷāyiruppārkaḷ.
āyutaṅkaḷ
poṟkollarkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷi urukkumpoḻutu peritum koṭiṟi upayōkippārkaḷ. ikkoṭiṟu kukikaḷi eḷitil eṭuttu uliyil vikkavum eṭukkavum upayōkappaṭum. koṭiṟu nīṇṭa kippiṭiyum muṉpakkam vaḷintum irukkum irumpālāṉa oru karuviyākum. ulōkaṅkaḷi urukki uli mukattiṉiṉṟu eṭuttatum nīril pōṭṭu kuḷiracceytu tēvik kēṟṟāṟ pōla veṭṭirumpu eṉṉum āyutattāl veṭṭic cuttiyāl taṭṭiyum vaḷittum aṭittum kampiyākavō, takaṭākavō iḻuttum taṭṭiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyya ārampippārkaḷ. ulimukattiliruntu eṭutta poṟkaṭṭiyi veṭṭuvataṟku mutalil nīṇṭa, kaṉatta marakkaṭṭi mītu caturamāṉa mēṟpakkam uḷḷa oru irumpuk kaṭṭiyāl ceyta oru paṭṭaṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttuvārkaḷ. ippaṭṭaṟi caturamāyum mēṟpakkam 10 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum kīḻppakkam 7 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum uḷḷa oru irumpuk kaṭṭiyēyākum. itu eppoḻutum poṟkollarkaḷ muṉṉar irukkum. ivarkaḷ oru ciṟu tātup poruḷik kotikka vikkavum kotikka vittapiṉ eṭukkavum nīṇṭu vaḷinta melliya takaṭṭiṉālāṉa ulikkatirip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. ulōkap poruḷi ulikkatirāl eṭuttup paṭṭaṟi mītu vittuc cuttiyāl taṭṭuvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ arukē eppoḻutum ulikkatirum ūtu kuḻalum irukkum. iḵtaṉṟi peritum ciṟitu māṉa cāmaṇaṅkaḷum poṭiveṭṭiyum araṅkaḷum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa muṉikkatir, paṟṟuk koṭiṟu, tiṇṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi kaḷum irukkum. poṟkollarkaḷukku oru mukkiyamāṉa karuvi kampi iḻukkum nūl caṭṭamākum. itu oru takaṭṭuvaṭivāṉa karuviyākum. ittakaṭu perumpālum urukkut takaṭṭiṉāl allatu paḻiya araṅkaḷiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatāka irukkum. it takaṭṭiṉmītu ciṟitum peritumāka varici variciyāka tuḷi ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. tuḷikaḷ peritiṉiṉṟu varicik kiramamākac ciṟuttuk koṇṭē pōkum. poṟkollarkaḷ ulōkak kampiyi periya tuvārattiṟ pōṭṭu paṟṟuk koṭiṟiṉāl mutal mutalāka iḻuppārkaḷ. appaṭi varicik kiramamāka iḻuppatāl kampi ciṟuttu nīṇṭu koṇṭē pōkum. taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya nīḷamō allatu parumaṉō vantatum kampi iḻuppati niṟuttik koḷvārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam mika nuṇṇiya mayiriḻi pōṉṟa kampikaḷik kūṭa eḷitil ceytuviṭukiṟārkaḷ. eḷitāka 3 aṅkula muḷḷa oru poṉ allatu veḷḷik kaṭṭiyi 300 aṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kampikaḷāka iḻuttu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi melliya kampikaḷ ceyya, virac caṭṭattiyum payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. itu oru rūpāy allatu ari rūpāy vaṭivil irukkum.
poṟkollarkaḷiṭam pala taramāṉa accukaḷ irukkum. avi uṟutiyāṉa kalavip poruḷkaḷiṉāl ceyyap peṟṟaṉa vākum. avikaḷil mēṟpakuti kīḻppakutiyeṉa iruvaki accukaḷ irukkum. inta accukaḷ oru kaṉatta takaṭṭil paḷḷamākat tōṇṭappaṭṭirukkum. appaḷḷattil taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya uruva vaṭivaṅkaḷ tōṇṭappaṭṭirukkum. anta acciṉmītu melliya poṉ allatu veḷḷit takaṭukaḷi vittu ataṉmēl oru irumput takaṭṭi vittup piṭittuc cuttiyalāl metu vākat taṭṭuvārkaḷ.  takaṭṭil avarkaḷ virumpum uruvam, puṭi ciṟpampōl uruppeṟum. pukalākac ceyya vēṇṭiya aṇikala ṉukku melliya takaṭṭi acciṉmītu vittu, ataṉmītu cempāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvattāl aḻuttuvārkaḷ. uruvam takaṭṭil patintu viṭum. oru pāti uruvāṉatum atē pōṉṟu maṟṟumoru pātiyiyum accil vittu muṉpōl aḻuttiyum aṭittum maṟu pātiyiyum uruvākkuvar. appāl iraṇṭiyum iṇittō allatu iṇiyāmalō meḻukaṭittut tiṇṇam pōṭṭu nakā paṇṇi merukiṭṭu vippārkaḷ. accukaḷil meḻuku accukaḷ eṉṟum, veṇkala accukaḷ eṉṟum, urukku accukaḷ eṉṟum, pala vakikaḷ uṇṭu. aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ taṉmikku ēṟṟavāṟu palavitamāṉa uruvamuḷḷa accukaḷuṇṭu. takaṭukaḷi acciṉmītu vittu aḻutti oḻuṅkupeṟat  taṭṭum tiṇṇaṅ kaḷum irukkum. aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟkuriya paṭiva accukaḷi muṉpu vittuk koṇṭu ulōkat takaṭukaḷiyō kaṭṭi kaḷiyō neruppiliṭṭu cenniṟam varumvari paḻukkak kāycci ulik karutiṉāl eṭuttu acciṉmītu vittuc cuttiyāl aṭittu aṇikalaṉkaḷi uruvākkuvārkaḷ. nakā pōṭa veṇkalam allatu urukku accum upayōkappaṭuttappaṭum. niṟka, cūṭuṇṭāṉa tātup poruḷkaḷ acciṟ patippikka eḷitāṉa kuḻivu uṭiya tāka irukkum. aṇikalaṉkaḷi uruvākkum paṭiva accukaḷ aṉēkam uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ ellām atu uruvākkum aṇikalaṉ kaḷiṉiṉṟu piṟantavikaḷākavē irukkum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka nākar accu, muruku accu, viciṟi muruku accu pōṉṟavi kaḷākum.
potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ *(pattar allatu ācāri) payaṉpaṭuttivarum mukkiyamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷiṉ vivaram aṭiyil varumāṟu.
1.	periya paṟṟuk koṭiṟu (paṟṟukkuṟaṭu - kampi iḻukkum periya paṟṟukkuṟaṭu)
2.	ciṟiya paṟṟuk koṭiṟu (pattukkuṟaṭu - kampi iḻukkum ciṟiya pattukkuṟaṭu)
3.	vāḷ caṭṭam
4.	āṭu tamar (cuḻal tamar)
5.	piṭittarāvi (puṭuccurāvi - arāvuvataṟku uriya poruḷip piṭittuk koḷḷum)
6.	nūl caṭṭam (kampi iḻukkum tuvāramuḷḷa takaṭu)
7.	tiṇṇam (pūvēlippāṭu ceyyum irumpu uḷi)
8.	paṉṟi muṭi (paṉṟi muṭiyāl ceyta kit tūriki)
9.	pittaḷit tūriki (piraṣ - nuṇukkamāṉa miṉukku vēlippāṭṭiṟkāka mikkāt takaṭṭiṉ mītu kampikaḷ poruttic ceyyappaṭṭa tūriki)
10.	poṭiveṭṭi (ciṟu poṉ veḷḷik kampikaḷi veṭṭum irumpu kattirikkōl)
11.	vaḷinta kuḻal (itu perumpālum caṅkilikaḷip paṟṟa vikkappayaṉpaṭuvatu)
12.	vaḷinta nākam (turutti vittu ūtutal)
13.	metukatir (meruku katir)
14.	veṭṭirumpu (irumpiṉālāṉa uḷi.  itu veḷḷi, taṅkam mutaliyavikaḷi veṭṭa upayōkappaṭum)
15.	aram (ulōkaṅkaḷi arāvap payaṉpaṭum)
16.	vāḷ (ulōkaṅkaḷi aṟukkum karuvi)
17.	cāṇi (tiṇṇaṅkaḷit tīṭṭum kal)
inta  āyutaṅkaḷ, tamiḻakattil palvēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷa īḻakam, kāḻakam, cāvakam pōṉṟa pakuti kaḷilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ oṉṟupōl kāṇappaṭṭālum cila iṭaṅkaḷil peyarkaḷ māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vētiyal poruḷkaḷ
tamiḻakattiṉ taṉic ciṟappu vāynta poṟkollarkaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷic camippataṟku oru cila ayalnāṭṭu irācayaṉap poruḷkaḷi upayōkittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, muṟkālat til ivaṟṟiṟkup patilāka uḷnāṭṭil tayāritta poruṭkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantārkaḷ. ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum iṉṟu poṉṉi urukkak kaṭṭikkārattip payaṉpaṭutti (Borax) varukiṟārkaḷ.
aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu muṭittapiṉ, avikaḷit tuppuravu ceyyavum  oḷiyuṇṭākkavum māṅkāy  pēriṅ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum, oru karuviyip perumpālum upayōkappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉpiṉ uppiyum upayōkappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. piṉ, cālṭ ammōṉiyā (uppu navaccāra āvikkuriyatu), paṭikāram mutaliyavi kaḷi mēlē taṭavi ataṉmītu civapput tūḷit tūvi merukiṭu kiṟārkaḷ. kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ kaḷimpip  pōkkavum turuvi akaṟṟavum kantakakkāṭiyi (calprik āciṭi)p payaṉpaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. poṉ pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷil kalantuḷḷa vēṟu tātup poruḷkaḷi akaṟṟip poṉṉit tūymippaṭuttu vataṟku veṭiyakkāṭiyi (niṭrik āciṭṭi) upayōkappaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālattil poṉṉic cuttappaṭutta atip puṭammiṭṭu vantaṉar.
puṭamiṭal 
māṟṟuk kuṟinta poṉṉic cuttikarikka mutalil atit takaṭāka aṭittu appāl ati 3/8 x 2 aṅkulat takaṭukaḷāka aṉittiyum oṉṟupōl veṭṭa vēṇṭum. vīṭṭuk kūṟi mēlē pōṭum ōṭṭit tūḷ ceytu atil upput tūḷik kalakka vēṇṭum. piṉ puḷiyik karittu atil takaṭukaḷi naṉṟāka naṉittu eṭuttu ōṭṭut tūḷil puraṭṭi variciyāka oṉṟaṉmītu oṉṟāka aṭukkip pāṉi ōṭṭil 2½ x 4 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa iru ōṭṭi mēlum kīḻum vittuk kavacam ceytu (potintu) periya pāṉiyil eruvi (varaṭṭiyi) aṭukkip puṭam iṭa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu mūṉṟu allatu nālu puṭam pōṭṭāl poṉ tūyatāki viṭum. appoḻutu ataṉic cokkap poṉ allatu cokkat taṅkam eṉpārkaḷ.
paḻuppiṭal 
vēli muṭinta aṇikalaṉkaḷi, paḻuppiṭa, turucu, uppu, veṭiyuppu ākiyavikaḷi arittu atil aṇikalaṉkaḷip pōṭṭup puraṭṭi neruppil vittuc ciṟitu cūṭēṟṟi taṇṇīril naṉitteṭuppar. aṇikalaṉkaḷ ellām nalla mañcaḷ niṟamāka ilaṅkum. iḵtaṉṟip palavitap paḻuppukaḷum pōṭuvārkaḷ. paḻuppup pōṭṭa piṉṉarē nakikaḷukku merukiṭuvārkaḷ.
merukiṭal
merukiṭuvataṟkup paḻuppup pōṭṭa nakikaḷ mītu oruvitac civapput tūḷit taṭavi appāl nāyttōlāl tuṭippārkaḷ. ataṉāl aṇikalaṉkaḷukku nalla miṉumiṉuppu eḻum. merukiṭa merukuk kampiyāl naṉṟākat tēyppatu uṇṭu.
kāram
aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyya ārampikkumpoḻutu mutalil poṉ takaṭṭiyō kampiyiyō nakiyiṉ aḷaviṟkēṟpa veṭṭi oṉṟōṭoṉṟip porutti ati ilēcāka oṭṭuvārkaḷ. oṭṭuvataṟku upayōkikkappaṭum paciyik kāram eṉpārkaḷ. kāram, kaṭṭikkāram eṉpaṉa tōl nīkkiya kuṉṟiṉ maṇittūḷ mutaliyavi cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭatākum. itaṉic cempu ṭappiyil vittiruppārkaḷ.
poṉṉāl aṇikaḷ ceyyumpoḻutu irutakaṭukaḷi allatu kampikaḷik kārattiṉāl maṭṭum oṭṭiviṭa muṭiyātu. atu uliyil vippataṟkuc cariyāṉa muṟiyil amittup pārppataṟkē payaṉpaṭum. āṉāl, palamāka ētēṉum oru poru ḷāl tākkappaṭṭāl iṇippup pirintuviṭum. aciyāmal irutakaṭukaḷ allatu kampikaḷ oṭṭuvataṟkup poṭi vittal eṉpārkaḷ. iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷukkum iṭiyē kāram taṭavi poṭi vittu ūtiviṭṭāl iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷum oṉṟāka iṇintu viṭum. intap poṭikkuc cuttamāṉa veḷḷi 1 paṅkum atē eṭi cempum cērttu urukki vittuk koḷvārkaḷ. itaṉi maṭṭam eṉpārkaḷ. taṅkam 3 paṅkum maṭṭum 1 paṅkum cērttu urukkap paṭṭataṟkup poṭi allatu caṉṉam eṉpārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭutalākavum kuṟitalākavum cērppatumuṇṭu.
iraṇṭu poṉ takaṭukaḷiyō poṉ kampikaḷiyō poṉ tūḷkaḷik koṇṭu oṭṭiviṭa muṭiyātu. oṭṭuvataṟku mēlē coṉṉa poṭi allatu caṉṉaṅkaḷē vēṇṭum. ōṭṭa vēṇṭiya iraṇṭu poṉ takaṭukaḷ mītum poruttil caṉṉatti vittu uliyiliṭṭu ūtiṉāl caṉṉam uruki iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷi yum oṉṟāka iṇittuviṭukiṟatu. appāl caṉṉatti arāviyō allatu cīviyō vittuviṭṭāl inta oṭṭu ciṟitum teriyātu. aṇikalaṉkaḷ vārppuppōl aḻakāy viḷaṅkum. itaṟkup poṭi vittal allatu caṉṉam vittal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ eṅkum orē vitamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷiyē payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ. 30 āṇṭukaḷāka oru cilar takaṭukaḷi arippatu kampikaḷi iḻuppatu pōṉṟa kāriyaṅ kaḷukku entiraṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
imayantoṭṭuk kumarivari niṟuppataṟku muḻu rūpāyum ēṉiya veḷḷik kācukaḷiyum paṭikkallāka upayōkappaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. namatu nāṭṭil ovvoru pakutiyilum ovvoru paṭik kaṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakam, pampāy, ākkirā pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil oru rūpāy oru tōlāvākap payaṉ paṭutti vantārkaḷ. iṉṉum palaviṭaṅkaḷil kaḷañcikkoṭṭi, mañcāṭi, kuṉṟimaṇi arici pōṉṟavikaḷi upayōkittup poṉ, eṭiyip pārkkiṉṟārkaḷ. cila iṭaṅkil oru mañ cāṭikku eṭṭu arici eṭiyākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṟatu. ip poḻutu eṅkum orē vitamāṉa eṭi naṭimuṟikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉippoṟkollarkaḷ poṉṉiṉ māṟṟuk kāṇpa taṟkup poṉṉi urittup pārkkap perumpālum eṅkum upayōkappaṭuttuvatu urikallēyākum. itu oru ciṟiya metu vāṉa kaṟuppuk kallāka irukkiṟatu. itil poṉ allatu veḷḷiyi urittāl atil ataṉ niṟampaṭiyum. ati vittup poṉ allatu veḷḷiyiṉ māṟṟut tīrmāṉikkappeṟukiṟatu. poṉṉil atikamāṉa cempō veḷḷiyō kalantiruntāl aḷaviṟku ēṟpap poṉ veṇmiyāṉa niṟaṅkāṭṭum. itaṉ māṟṟit tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kaṇakkiṭa maccam allatu mātirip poṉkuccum vittiruppārkaḷ. ati arukil urittup pārttu māṟṟi uṟutip paṭuttuvārkaḷ.
poṉ aṇikaḷ ceyvataṟkuc cuttamāṉa taṉip poṉ payaṉpaṭātu. eṉavē atōṭu cempu allatu veḷḷi, allatu nākam kalappārkaḷ. evvaḷavu kalappatu eṉpatu ovvoru mānilattiṟkum, ēṉ? - ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum aḷavil vēṟṟumi uṇṭu.
cila nāṭukaḷil ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷukku mātiri vit tiruppārkaḷ. cila iṭaṅkaḷil vēṟu tātup poruḷkaḷil mātiri naki ceytu kāṭṭi, atu aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭa piṉṉarē poṉ aṇi ceyvārkaḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷiṭam  mātiri nakikaḷō, mātirippaṭaṅkaḷō kiṭiyā; niṉivilē vittuk koṇṭē aḻakāka aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ.
pala nakikaḷ kaṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭuvatilli. pukalāka atāvatu takaṭākac ceytu ati accil vittu aḻutti appāl kottumāṉa vēlikaḷ ceykiṟārkaḷ. itaṉ uḷḷē meḻuku irukkum. piṉpakkam perumpālum poṟṟakaṭu potiyamāṭ ṭārkaḷ; veḷḷit takaṭē potikiṉṟārkaḷ. pala poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷic ceytu muṭittuk koṇṭu oru ciṟu tuvārattiṉ mūlam uṭpakkam meḻukic celutti viṭukiṟārkaḷ.
vaṭa intiyāvilum ilaṅkiyilum poṉ mulām  pūciya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ eṅkirun tālum poṉ mulām pūciya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇiya virumpuvatē illi.
āṉāl irupatu āṇṭukaḷāyp poṉ aṇikalaṉ mītu aḻakiya civappu, pacci, nīlam, mañcaḷ, karuppu, ūtā vaṇṇaṅ kaḷil mēl pūccu (eṉāmal) vēli naṭipeṟukiṟatu. ati makkaḷiṟ palar virumpukiṉṟārkaḷ. perumpālum inta eṉāmal pūccu atikamāka mōtiraṅkaḷukkē payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻakap poṟ kollarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟku mikap paḻamiyāṉa, ceppamuṟāta karuvikaḷiyē iṉṟu varip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷiṉ ki vaṇṇattāl ulakil enta nāṭṭut toḻilāḷarkaḷ ceyyum aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyumviṭa mikac ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakap poṟkollarkkoppa, ēṉ? avarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyil niṟpataṟkukkūṭa ulakil evarum illi. avarkaḷ entira utaviyiṉṟiyē veṅkāyac carukupōṉṟa poṉ takaṭukaḷālum, cilanti valiyiṉ nūlpōṉṟa poṉ kampikaḷālum aṇikalaṉkaḷ camittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. inta paṇpaṭṭa naya nākarikamuṭiya muṟpaṭṭa kāla muṟiyil ceyyappaṭṭa nakikaḷiṉ tiṉucukaḷ naṭi nayattil ataṉuṭiya ucca niliyi eṭṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟiyat tamiḻakap poṟ kollarkaḷ, niṉivuk keṭṭāta paḻaṅkālattic cērnta marapiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ. taṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa muṟikaḷiyum, taṉit tiṟattiyum marapurimiyākap peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.12
poṟkollarkaḷ uḻippāḷar iṉattavarkaḷēyāvar. āṉāl camūkattil oru kuravamāṉa uṟuppiṉarāka iruntu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tōḷil pūṇūl pōṭṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ. taṭṭār (poṟkollar) eṉṟa oru iṉam pallāṇṭākat tamiḻakattil iruntu varukiṟatu. āṉāl tamiḻnāṭu campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil vēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ ittoḻilil atikam īṭupaṭṭiruppa tākat teriyavilli. vēṟu mānilaṅkaḷil piṟa iṉattavarkaḷ iti oru matippāṉa toḻilāka eṇṇi itil īṭupaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
poṟkollarkaḷ vaḻakkamākap paṇṭutoṭṭu ittoḻi lil nāṇayamāka naṭappatilli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. namnāṭṭil itippaṟṟip pala paḻamoḻikaḷum uṇṭu. taṭṭāṉuk kañciyaṉṟō aṇintāṉ civaṉ carppattiyē eṉṟa pulavar vākkum eḻuntuḷḷatu. taṭṭāṉ tāyp poṉṉilum māp poṉ eṭuppāṉ eṉṟum kammāḷaṉ kaṭāvik kātaṟuttup pārttālum cevvarakkup pāyntirukkum, eṉṟellām paḻa moḻikaḷ uṇṭu. itip pōṉṟa paḻamoḻikaḷ vaṭa intiyā vilum āṅkila nāṭṭilum uṇṭu. iṉṟu inta nili māṟi varukiṟatu eṉṟa kūṟalām. poṟ kollarkaḷ kuṟitta kālattil aṇikalaṉ kaḷic ceytu koṭukkātu nāḷkaṭattip pukal colli varuvatum poṉṉōṭu aḷaviṟkatikamākac cempik kalantu makkaḷi ēmāṟṟuvatum, cētārattiṉ pērāl, niṟuttal aḷaviyiṉ pērāl makkaḷi ēyppatum paṟṟip pala katikaḷ nāṭeṅkum  kūṟappaṭuva tuṇṭu. ivikaḷ kālap pōkkil māṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. ittoḻi lāḷikaḷ mītu pala kuṟṟañcāṭṭap paṭṭiruppatu meytāṉ. āṉāl avarkaḷ iṉṟum vaṟumiyilēyē vāṭi vataṅkukiṟārkaḷ; avarkaḷ nili uyaravilli. taṭṭiṉāl taṭṭāṉ taṭṭāviṭil keṭṭāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
poṟkollarkaḷiṉ viṉinuṭpam
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē atāvatu kiṟittava ūḻi arumpuvataṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟ kollarkaḷ mikunta nuṭpamāṉa toḻil tiṟaṉ vāyntavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ pacum poṉṉit tamatu kūriya uḷiyālum, katirālum koṭi, ili, pū, naṇṭu, makaram, tavaḷi pōṉṟa nēriya palvēṟu vaṭivam peṟṟilaṅkumāṟu ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ aṇikaḷi aṭittum arāviyum aṟuttum  kottu māṉaṅkaḷ ceytum aḻakuṟac ceytu muṭittaṉar. muttukaḷiyum, maṇikaḷiyum tuḷiyiṭṭaṉar. viram pōṉṟa uṟuti vāynta maṇiyilum ciṟiya tuḷikaḷiyiṭṭaṉar. cāṇipiṭittaṉar. palvēṟu vaṇṇam peṟappaṭṭi tīṭṭiṉar. maṇikaḷiṉ mācu nīkkiṉar. muttukaḷum maṇikaḷum tuḷiyiṭap peṟṟatiyum, cāṇikkal payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatiyum,
	tōḷā maṇikuvittāṟ pōṉṟilaṅku tolkulattu
eṉṟa cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷ naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. atōṭu maṇikaḷ ciṟanta uruvattiyum vaṇṇattiyum peṟac cāṇi piṭikkappaṭṭatu. toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻakattil cāṇi piṭikkum kal iruntatu eṉpati
	ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya
	 kalpōl piriyalam 	             (akam : 1 : 5-6; 356 : 9- 10)
eṉṟa akanāṉūṟṟu aṭikaḷ meyppikkiṉṟaṉa. aṇikalaṉkaḷi mācu nīkkak kolli eṉṟa karuvi payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟatu. itaṉip peruṅkati,
	matimācu kaḻīiya vaṇṇam pōlak
	 katirmēl ilaṅkak kiviṉi muṭittapiṉ
	 aṭiviṉik kammiyar veṭipaṭa aṭukkiya
	 uyarnalak kōlattu oḷḷoḷi tikaḻa
	 vakiyami kolliyiṉ vaciyaṟat tuṭittu 									           (peruṅ. 2:4:181-185)
paḻantamiḻakattup poṟkollarkaḷ oru aricip paruma ṉuḷḷa poṉṉil civaperumāṉiṉ tiru uruvattic cemmiyuṟac cetukki eḻil peṟac ceytuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ toḻil nuṭpam maṉṉarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum makkaḷum kaṇṭu viyantu pārāṭṭaṟ kuriyatāy iruntatu.
poṟ kaṭṭiyi melliya cilanti valiyiṉ iḻi pōl - mayiriṉum melliyatāyc ceytu aṇikaḷ ceytaṉar. poṟṟa kaṭṭi aṭittu īruḷḷic carukiṉum melliyatākac ceytu atil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkiṉar. oru ciṟu irākkuṭiyil civaperumāṉ tiripuram erinta katiyi ciṟitum piṉṉamiṉṟit tīṭṭiṉar.
mācillāta maṇikaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttu avaṟṟip poṉ aṇiyil patippittu palvēṟu ilikaḷum pūkkaḷum, kāykaḷum ilaṅkumpaṭi ceytu vantaṉar. ivikaḷil naṉku avarkaḷ toḻil nuṭpam viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṉāl, ivarkaḷ ōraḷavutāṉ piṟitoṉṟip pārttup piṉpaṟṟāta muṟiyil ceyya muṭikiṟatu. ivarkaḷiṭam putiya mātirikaḷi uruvākkikkāṭṭum entak kaliñarkaḷum iruppa tākat teriyavilli. airōppāvippōl kaliñarkaḷ ivarkaḷ mātirikaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷiyum varintō uruvam amittō koṭuppatilli. airōppāvil poṉ aṇiyil ceyya vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷukku, kaliñarkaḷ, paṭam, uruvam, eṭi, māṟṟu, vilikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiyellām pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka amittuk koṭuttu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. airōppāvil kaliñarkaḷē putiya putiya pāṇiyil ellā naki kaḷiyum uruvākki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṅkuḷḷa poṟkollarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ tiṭṭappaṭi entirampōl aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu koṭuttu varukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakattil aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, paṭimaṅ kaḷiyum uruvākkat  tiṭṭamiṭum kaliñarkaḷum uruvākkum toḻilāḷikaḷum oruvarēyākum. tamiḻakap poṉ aṇikala vēli enta nāṭṭiyum viṭa, cari etirppuk kūṟiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭṭatāy piṟar kaṇkaḷāl kavaṉikkum poḻutu uḷḷattiṉ niṉiviṉiṉṟu veḷivarac ceyyum kaliyuṇarcciyuṭiya paṭṭaṟi vāka miḷirkiṟatu. tamiḻk kaliyiṉ uṭkaruttum kuṟikkōḷum taṉippaṭṭaṉavākum. muṟiyum  vēṟāṉatākum. nōkkamum tamiḻakak kaliyum, pāṇiyum aḻaku uṇarcci cārnta aṉupavattōṭu karuttuk koṇṭirukkavilli. mikac ciṟanta uṭkaruttu koṇṭa tāka eṇṇappaṭṭa mokalāya pāṇiyikkūṭa tamiḻakap paṇi taḻuva maṟuttuviṭṭatu. āriyap pāṇiyikkūṭa utariviṭṭatu.
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	“The objects found comprised gold, bronze, and iron articles and pottery. The gold articls which were probably used as diadems, vary in size and are oval in shape; some have a stripe extending beyond the two extremities with a small hole for a wire or string at each end. They are thin plates ornamented with triangular and linear dotted designs and all were found folded up in a manner which suggest that some symbolical meaning may have been attached to the practice” - Catalogue of Prehistoric Antiquities, Adichanallur and Prumbir by Alexander Rea. F.S.A. (Scot) P. 3-4
2.	arumippāṭuṭiya viṉittiṟam amiyap poṉṉiṉāl palavaki aṇikalaṅkaḷic ceypavar poṟkollar eṉappaṭuvar. poṉṉilē nalla maṇikaḷip patikkum iyalpaṟintu aṇikalaṉkaḷamikkum irattiṉap paṇittaṭṭārkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ peruka vāḻntārkaḷ. tirumaṇi kuyiṟṟunar eṉpār muttukkōppavarāvar caṅka kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ - tiru. ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ (1950) pa. 133
3.	The five classes of artisans are :-
	1. Manu, Kollar ........... (kollar) Black - smiths.
	2. Maya, Taher (taccar) ............................ Carpentars.
	3. Tuvashta, Kannar (kaṉṉār) Brass - founders.
	4. Silpa Sirpi (á‰ã)...................... Mason, Stonecutters, sculptors, Architects, Immage makers and Painters.
	5. Visvanna, Tattar (j£lh®)...................... Goldsmiths.
	These firve artisans were born out of the five faces of Visvakarma - (Foot Note:) Ancient Jaffna. P. 15
