The Hindu artisan of our day inherits the methods and skill he uses by direct descent of immemorial tradition” - Report on jewellery and precious stones in the French Exhibition of 1866.”	- by Mr. Maskelyne.

7. oṉpatumaṇikaḷ (navarattiṉaṅkaḷ)
aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikaḷum
tamiḻ makkaḷ ātiyil malar aṇikaḷi aṇintu vantārkaḷ. appāl kuṉṟimaṇi, mañcāṭi, kaḷaṅcik koṭṭi, miḷaku, kottamalli, nellikkāy, marutaṅkāy, kaḷimaṇ uruṇṭi, mākkalmaṇi, ceṅkalmaṇi, cippimāli, caṅkumāli pōṉṟavi kaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ tātupporuḷkaḷik kaṇṭa tum poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷ kaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷi amittu vantaṉar - appāl maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṉ aṇikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa arappā makkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē poṉ aṇikaḷiyum maṇikaḷ patitta poṉ, veḷḷi aṇikaḷiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu intiya varalāṟu kūṟum uṇmi. āṉāl tamiḻaka makkaḷ iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṉ aṇikaḷip pūṇ ṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kaṭic caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉṉar tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāṉa poṉ aṇikaḷ nilaviyiruk kalām eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku illi. kiṟittava ūḻi arumpiya kālattiliruntu tamiḻakattil poṉ, veḷḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum arumpi viṭṭaṉa eṉṟu collalām. itaṟku nām kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷum ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷum kāṭṭamuṭiyum. kiṟittava ūḻiyil nilaviya poṉ aṇikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷi nammāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭa muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷ etuvum puti poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉa rāl akaḻntu ārāyappaṭṭu avikaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷō pommikaḷō kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭu ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭiruppa tāka nām aṟiyavilli; kēḷvippaṭavum illi. āticcanallūr akaḻvārāycciyil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kiṭikkavilli.
kiṟittava ūḻikkup piṉ eḻunta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pala cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyap peṟṟu vantaṉa. ataṉāl poṉṉip paṟṟi ārāyap peṟṟatu. maṇikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ uyarvum kuṟṟamum ati merukiṭum muṟiyum paṭṭan tīrkkum vaḻikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. maṇi vakikaḷum avaṟṟip paṟṟiya ārāycciyum ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum arumpiṉa. poṉṉiviṭa maṇikaḷukku matippu uyarntatu.
maṇikaḷ eṅkellām kiṭikkum eṉpatu ārāyap peṟṟatu. periya maṇikaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil iruntellām koṇṭu varap peṟṟaṉa. vili matikka voṇṇāta maṇikaḷ pala, ulakil kiṭittaṉa. maṇikaḷ aṇikaḷil maṭṭum iṭam peṟavilli. vēṟu pala kāriyaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ teyva uruvaṅkaḷ kūṭa ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  uyarnta maṇikaḷi aṭivataṟ kāka maṉṉarkaḷ oruvarukkoruvar pōriṭṭuk koṇṭatum uṇṭu. maṉṉarkaḷum vaṇikarkaḷum uyarnta maṇikaḷukku atika vili koṭuttu vantaṉar. maṉṉarkaḷum perum vaṇikarkaḷum viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṉ aṇikaḷi teyvaṅ kaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyāka aḷittu vantaṉar.
namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil (1) vacciram (2) intira nīlam (3) marakatam (4) karkētaṉam (5) patumarākam (6) uruttirākkam (7) viṭūriyam 
(8) vipulam  (9) vimalakam (10) irācamaṇi (11) paṭikam (12) cantira kāntam (13) cukantikam (14) kōmētakam (15) caṅkam (16) makānīlam (17) puṭparākam (18) piramamaṇi (19) cōtiracam (20) cīvayakam (21) muttu (22) pavaḷam eṉṟu palavaki maṇikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟu mēṉāṭṭil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa maṇivakikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. atil tamiḻ nāṭṭār maṇikaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟip poṟukki eṭuttu avikaḷi maṭṭum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. avi navarattiṉam - oṉpatu maṇikaḷ ākum. navamaṇikaḷil iraṇṭu nīril viḷivaṉa; ēḻu nilattil viḷivaṉa. nīril viḷivaṉa muttu, pavaḷam ākiya iraṇṭē ākum. nilattil viḷivaṉa viram, marakatam, māṇikkam, puṭparākam, viṭūriyam, nīlam, kōmētakam eṉum ēḻum ākum.
ilakkiyaṅkaḷil maṇikaḷ
mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē, maṇi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ pala, tamiḻil iruntaṉa. iṉṟu avikaḷil oṉṟik kūṭak kāṇa muṭiya villi. tamiḻar kaṇṭa aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷil maṇic cōtaṉi (iratṉap parīṭci)yum oṉṟu. iṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil irattiṉap parīṭci eṉṟu oru nūl irukkiṟatu. ataṉ tamiḻākkam namakku iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattil maṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilappatikārattilum  ataṉ ciṟappurikaḷilum maṇikaḷip paṟṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. kallāṭam, piḷḷit tamiḻ pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷilum navamaṇikaḷip paṟṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
maṇikaḷiṉ nalaṉkaḷ
namatu maruttuva nūṟkaḷilum cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷilum maṇi kaḷiṉ perumiyiyum iṉṉa ilakkaṉattil piṟantōrkku iṉṉa maṇi aṇintāl nalam viḷiyum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maruttuva nūṟkaḷil maṇikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa pamaṅkaḷ piṇikaḷi nīkkum viparaṅkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivatum, poṟkalaṅkaḷil pucippatum iṉṉiṉṉa nōykaḷi akaṟṟum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷi aṇivatāl pala nōykaḷ akalvatōṭu poṉṉum maṇikaḷum kūṭi iraṭṭippāṉa nalaṉkaḷic ceykiṉṟaṉa; nōy akalkiṟatu; uṭal palam peṟukiṟatu eṉpatu viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ maṇikaḷip paṟṟi ārāyntu pala moḻikaḷil nūṟkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. maṇi kaḷip paṟṟiya āyvut tuṟiyi mēṉāṭṭār jemmālēji (Gemology) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. mēṉāṭṭār maṇikaḷ ēṉ kaṉamākavum uṟutiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa? maṇikaḷiṉūṭē pukum oḷikkatir ēṉ vēṟupāṭuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu? eṉpatiyum iṉṟu mika nuṇukkamāka āyntuḷḷārkaḷ. oḷi ārāyccit tuṟiyil ulakap perumpukaḻ īṭṭiya tamiḻnāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉar car.ci.vi. irāmaṉ maṇikaḷiṉ  oḷikaḷip paṟṟi oppaṟṟa ārāycci ceytuḷḷār.
maṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam
navamaṇikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ maṇikaḷi, tali, iṭi, kaṭi, eṉṟu mūṉṟu taramākap pirippar. talimaṇikaḷ viram, muttu, marakatam, māṇikkam eṉṉum nāṉkum ākum. iṭimaṇikaḷ nīlam, puṭparākam, viṭūriyam eṉum mūṉṟum ākum. kaṭi maṇikaḷ kōmētakam, pavaḷam eṉṉum iraṇṭum ākum. ivvakiyaṉṟi navamaṇikaḷi, makārattiṉam uparattiṉam eṉṟum pirippar.
navamaṇikaḷil tamiḻnāṭṭu muttukkaḷum intiya nāṭṭu viramum ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟavi. iṉṟu āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyiṉ maṇimuṭiyiliruntu aṇi ceyvatu intiya nāṭṭil viḷinta kōkiṉūr eṉṉum ulakap piracitti peṟṟa viramaṇiyākum. kiṟittava ūḻi eḻum kālattilē pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muttukkaḷ urōm nāṭṭu aracikaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyilum paṭṭāṭiyilum, oḷi vīciyatōṭu avarkaḷiṉ melliya uṭaliyum aṇi ceytu niṉṟaṉa. kiḷiyōpattirā eṉṟa ekiptiya araciyiṉ uḷḷattik kavarntu niṉṟatu koṟki muttēyākum. avvaraci koṟki mutti, āṭiyil piṇittum aṇikaḷil iṇittum karittuk kuṭittum vantāḷ. iṉṟum ceyappūr marakatam cekameṅkum pukaḻ parappi varukiṟatu. oru kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭup pavaḷam veḷi nāṭṭiṟkellām ceṉṟu namatu nāṭṭiṟkup perumi viḷivittu vantatu.
talimaṇikaḷ
viram (Diamond)
ulakam tōṉṟip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil, kālakkōḷāl pūmiyiṉ mītuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ vīḻntu ataṉ aṭiyil putiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap pūmiyiṉuḷ putintu kiṭanta inta maraṅkaḷ ceṟivuṟṟu nilakkariyāy māṟiyatu. itaṉōṭu mēlum mēlum maṇṇum kallum cērntu putiyuṇṭa nilakkariyip palavita māṟutalkaḷukku āḷākkiyatu. mēlē irunta poruḷkaḷ ati aḻutti vantaṉa. uḷḷirukkum veppam ati atikamākat tākkiyatu. kariya niṟam vāynta nilakkari veṇmi niṟam peṟṟatu. karikkuṉṟu virak kuṉṟāy miḷirntatu.
viññāṉa viḷakkam
viratti viññāṉikaḷ paricōtittup pārttaṉar. atu karipporuḷāl āṉatē eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ cōtaṉiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟatu. karipporuḷi urukkip pārttaṉar. atu viramāka oḷi vīciyatu. uṭaṉē ulakiṟku taṅkaḷatu kaṇṭupiṭippi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
viram amilaṅkaḷil (tirāvakaṅkaḷil) kariyātu; cātāraṇac cūṭṭiṉāl nekiḻātu; atikamāṉa cūṭēṟṟiṉālum atu iḷakātu. āṉāl taṉṉantaṉiyāṉa pirāṇavāyuviṉ cuvāliyik koṇṭu atit tākkiṉāl erintu pōkiṟatu. appoḻutu virattiṉiṉṟu kariyamila vāyu  oṉṟu tāṉ kiṭikkum. itaṉāl viramāṉatu taṉik karipporuḷē tavira vēṟaṉṟu eṉat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṭuppuk kari, nilakkari ākiyavikaḷi viṭa nalla kalappaṟṟa karip poruḷik kuṟukkiṉatu tāṉ viram eṉpatu.
marakkari, nilakkari, kārīyakam (Graphite) viram ākiyavi aṉittum karipporuḷiṉ uruvamāṟupāṭukaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟalla. ipporuḷkaḷiṉ putīkak kuṇaṅkaḷ māṟupaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭṭālum aṉittum karipporuḷākiya irācāyaṉap poruḷēyākum. eṉavē ivaṟṟiṉ irācāyaṉa kuṇaṅkaḷum amippum oṉṟāka amintuḷḷaṉa. karipporuḷiṉ tiṇmi yāṉatu nāḷaṭivil ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiviṭa maṟṟoṉṟil atika mākik koṇṭē vantu iṟutiyil uṟutiyāṉatum veṇmi vāyntatum oḷimikkatumāṉa viram mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.2
irattiṉak kaṟkaḷiṉ (maṇikaḷiṉ) amippu muṟi palavaki yākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. oru irattiṉak kallukkum maṟṟoru irattiṉak kallukkum atikamāṉa vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. virattiṉ irācayaṉa amippō cātāraṇamāṉatu. cuttamāṉa veṟuṅkarip poruḷ oṉṟik koṇṭē viram amintiruk kiṟatu. māṇikkattil iraṇṭu mūlaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cila irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pala mūlaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉavāy amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
virattiṉ taram
viram uyarntatā, kuṟṟamaṟṟatā? eṉṟu pārkkac cila cōtaṉikaḷ uṇṭu. virattiṉmītu puḷi, uppu ivikaḷi arittup pūci, pātaracattil pōṭṭu eṭuttāl nalla viram niṟam māṟātu irukkum. ceyaṟki viram niṟam māṟiviṭum.
virak kaṟkaḷiṉ ciṟappu ataṉ oḷiyiṉik kōṭac ceyyum caktiyēyākum. atāvatu virattiṉvaḻi ūṭuruvic cellum oḷi irēkiyāṉatu ēṉiya poruḷkaḷiṉ mūlam ūṭuruvic cellum poḻutu cātāraṇamākak kōṭuvatik kāṭṭilum atikamākak kōṭukiṟatu. atāvatu oḷik kiraṇam virattiṉ mēṟ paṭuvataṉ muṉ entap pakkatti nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatō antap pakkatti viṭṭu atikam vilaki atu, virattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē varum poḻutu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu aminta oḷikkōṭum caktiyāṉatu ovvoru poruḷilum ataṉ tiṇmik kēṟpak kuṟintum niṟintum kāṇappaṭum. tiṇmi niṟinta virattil itu niṟivākat tikaḻkiṟatu. inta oḷikōṭum tiṟamiyiṉ niṟiviṉāltāṉ virattiṟku oppaṟṟa oḷiyum ariya cōpiyum uyariya kāntiyum uṇṭākiṟatu. itaṉāl viram taṉiyāka oḷirntu ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kuṟṟamaṟṟa virattiṉ mītu atilum naṉṟākap paṭṭan tīrttup paḷapaḷaveṉṟu miṉṉum virattiṉ mītu oḷik kiraṇam oṉṟupaṭṭuk kōṭumpoḻutu viram takatakaveṉṟu kaṇṇip paṟippatupōl oḷi vīcukiṟatu.
cōtiṭa muṟippaṭi pārttāl, viram cukkiraṉ, ātikkat tiṟku uṭpaṭṭatu. iṭapa lakkaṉattil allatu tulālakkaṉattil piṟantavarkaḷukku viram atirṣṭam tarum maṇiyākum. cātakattil cukkiraṉ palam uḷḷavaṉāka iruntālum viratti aṇiyalām. kurava uyarvu, akaṉṟa aṟivu, taṉatu tēvi niṟivu ākiyavi viram aḷikkum teyvīkat taṉmi, nalla virattiṟku uṇṭu. atu āyuḷi nīṭikkat tuṇi ceyyum. pittiyam tīravum pāmpu kaṭi mutaliyavikaḷukku āḷākāmal kākkum taṉmiyum virat tiṟku uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṟṟamaṟṟa viramaṇi kiṭippatu aritu. ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa viraṅkaḷilum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. pukaḻ peṟṟa viramākiya kōkiṉūrilum kūṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. veṇmi, mañcaḷ, poṭi niṟa nīlam, veḷiṟiya civappu niṟa viraṅkaḷ kiṭippatu aritu.
intiya viramaṇikaḷ 
pārata nāṭṭiluḷḷa kōlkoṇṭā, paṉṉā, vacrakuram pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil viraṅkaḷ muṟkālattil kiṭittaṉa. vintiya maliccāralil virappaṭikaṅkaḷ vaḷam peṟṟiruntaṉa. 3 taṭcaṇa pīṭa pūmiyiṉ kiḻakkup pakkattilum virac curaṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiruṭṉā āṟṟup pirāntiyaṅkaḷil varalāṟṟup pukaḻ vāynta virak kaṟkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. paḻiya kālattil kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭic cārnta kōlār eṉṉum iṭattil uyarnta viraṅkaḷ kiṭittu vantaṉa. ippoḻutu intiyāvil atikamāṉa viraṅkaḷ kiṭikka villi. pūmikkaṭiyiluḷḷa virac curaṅkaṅkaḷi kaṇṭupiṭikka intiya aracāṅkam cōviyat nipuṇarkaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu perum muyaṟci ceytu varukiṟatu.
veḷi nāṭukaḷil viram
pirēcil nāṭṭil miṉācukiricuṭi meṇṭiṉāriyō apēṭ eṉṟa pakutikaḷil virac curaṅkaṅkaḷ perum aḷavil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pākiyā eṉṟa pakutiyil 54 āyiram kāraṭ viram kiṭittatu.
iṉṟu āppirikkā vira uṟpattiyil pirēcil nāṭṭit tāṇṭi viṭṭatu. 1906ām āṇṭil ulaka vira uṟpatti 90 catavikitam teṉṉāppirikkāvil iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. kimparli, ṭīpircu, rōṭēciyā, lūṭaricu virikuṭā pōṉṟa pakuti kaḷil nalla virac curaṅkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aṅku ērāḷamāṉa viraṅkaḷ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu amerikkā vilum cōviyat yūṉiyaṉilum viraṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
ulakap pukaḻ vāynta viraṅkaḷ
oru kālattil intiya viraṅkaḷ ulakap pukaḻ vāyntavi kaḷāy, varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟavikaḷāy miḷirntaṉa. 1726-ām āṇṭuvari ulakil uḷḷa virac curaṅkaṅkaḷil kōlkoṇṭā curaṅkaṅkaḷē cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa. intiya viraṅkaḷ veḷuṟiya nīla niṟattuṭaṉ uṟuti mikkaṉavāy oḷirntaṉa. intiya viraṅkaḷ vili matikkap peṟṟavikaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉa.
1. kōkiṉūr viramaṇi
intiya mokal aracar, cājakāṉiṭam ērāḷamāṉa vira maṇikaḷ iruntaṉa. turuva naṭcattirattiviṭa oḷitarum oppaṟṟa viraṅkaḷ avariṭam uḷḷaṉa. ati avar poṉṉāl ceyta oru kuttu viḷakkil patittu anta viḷakki makkamā nakariluḷḷa puṉitap paḷḷikkuk kāṇikkiyāka aḷittār. akkālattil itaṉ vili oru kōṭi rūpāykku matippiṭap paṭṭatu. ivvaracariṭamirunta viram, māṇikkam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ patitta taṅkattiṉālāṉa mayilācaṉam aṉṟu 53 kōṭi rūpāykku matippiṭap peṟṟatu. ivvaracaruṭiya karuvūlat til 1663ām āṇṭil makā mokal eṉṟa viramaṇi oṉṟu iruntatām. atu 787½ kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatu. ki.pi. 1526ām āṇṭil pēpar eṉṟa mokalāya maṉṉariṭam anta maṇi iruntatu. inta ariya periya viramaṇi 1739ām āṇṭu vari tilli mokalāya cultāṉkaḷiṉ karuvūlattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. appāl iti pāracīka vīraṉāṉanātar eṉṟa maṉṉaṉ kavarntu koṇṭu pōṉāṉ. appāl atu mīṇṭum intiyāvil irañcit ciṅ eṉṟa maṉṉariṭam vantatu. avariṭamiruntu kiḻakkintiya kampeṉiyār paṟittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. piṉ piriṭṭīcu araci vikṭōriyāviṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu, piriṭṭīcu maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam peṟṟu iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṟatu. aṉṟu 186 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyāka irunta immāmaṇi palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭṭu 1862ām āṇṭu 108-9 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatāy paṭṭan tīṭṭappeṟṟatu. iṉṟu piriṭṭīcu araci elicapet ammiyār avarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil ilaṅki varukiṟatu6  itaṉ vili pattāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ 60,00,000 rūpāyāka matippiṭappaṭṭatu. inta viram āṇkaḷ aṇintāl turatirṣṭa mum peṇkaḷ aṇintāl nalla  viḷivum uṇṭākum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. inta viram piriṭṭicu makkaḷ intiyāvi aṭimippaṭutti ati nīṇṭa kālamākac curaṇṭi vantatiṉ niṉivākap piriṭṭicu maṇimakuṭattil iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu. itu intiyāviṉ cottu. iṉṟalla eṉṟākilum nām allatu namatu varuṅkāla makkaḷ mīṇṭum atippeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu āci. intiya mātāviṉ viruppamum atutāṉ. kōkiṉūr eṉṟāl maliyiṉ oḷi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭum.
2. mokal viram 
intiyāvil āṭci purinta mokalāya maṉṉar cājakāṉ kōkiṉūr eṉṉum viramaṇiyuṭaṉ vittup pōṟṟi vanta ciṟanta vira maṇikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka perumi vāynta perum maṇi kaḷil mokal viramaṇiyum oṉṟākum. cājakāṉ  kālattiṟkup piṉ immā maṇi ālaṅkīr eṉṉum auraṅkacīp aracariṭam pōyviṭṭatu. itu uyaramākavum vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavum nuṉi ciṟuttum aṭi akaṉṟum aḻakiya vimāṉam pōl viḷaṅkiyatu. itu kōkiṉūr virattiṉiṉṟu veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu eṇṇuvārum uṇṭu. itaṉ eṭi 280 - kāraṭ iṟuti yil ivvira maṇi uruciya nāṭṭiṟkup pōyviṭṭatu. uruciyāviṉ muṉṉāḷ āṭci purinta jār cakkaravarttiyiṉāl mika arumiyāṉa maṇi eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭṭu vantatu. immaṇi ippoḻutu iṉṟuḷḷa cōviyat aracāṅkat tiṭam iruppatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
3. ārlāp viramaṇi
ārlāp allatu ṭāriyā nūr maṇi (Derya Noor) āṟṟiṉ oḷi eṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu. inta aḻakiya viramaṇi mokalāya maṉṉar pēpariṭam iruntatu. appāl atu micūril uḷḷa cirireṅka paṭṭiṉattil uḷḷa teyva uruviṉ kaṇṇil patippikkappaṭṭatām. piṉṉar ati 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil evarō tiruṭik koṇṭu pōyviṭṭaṉar. piṟaku atu eppaṭiyō oru āṅkilak kappalil uḷḷa kappittāṉ kikkup pōyiṟṟu. avaṉ ati ārlāḵp eṉṉum uruciya iḷavaracaṉukku 90,000m  pavuṇukku viṟṟu viṭṭāṉ. avviḷavaracaṉ uruciya araci kātariṉukkup paricāka aḷittu akamakiḻntāṉ. anta viramaṇi ip poḻutu cōviyat uruciyāvil irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ eṭi 199.6 meṭrik kāraṭ inta maṇi mañcaḷ niṟattuṭaṉ cōviyat uruciya makuṭak kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvē oḷi vīcik koṇṭiruntatu. itaṉ vili matippu rū. 50,00,000-kku mēl irukkum. itaṟku āmcuṭar ṭēm, lācurev eṉṟa peyarkaḷum uṇṭu. ikkal māṇpuṟa oḷivīcap paṭṭantīrkkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
4. piṭviramaṇi
piṭ allatu rīcaṇṭ eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟum aḻakiya viramaṇi 1701ām āṇṭu āntira nāṭṭil kiruṭṇā āṟṟaṅ kariyil kiṭittatu. aṉṟu atu 410 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭi yuḷḷatāy iruntatu. appoḻutu ceṉṉik kavarṉarāka irunta villiyam piṭ eṉpavar ati vilikku vāṅkiṉār. appoḻutu ataṉ matippu rū. 13,00,000m ākum. appāl avar pireñcu nāṭṭu araca kuṭumpattic cārnta ārliyaṉ ṭiyūk eṉpavarukku rū. 15,00,000-kku viṟṟu viṭṭār. ataṉpiṉ am maṇi pirañcu nāṭṭu maṉṉar kaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam peṟṟu ati alaṅkarittu vantatu. ippoḻutu atu 140.5 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭi irukkum. itaṉ peṟumati 60,00,000 ākum. itu iṉṟum pirañcu nāṭṭiluḷḷa lōvaril tēcīya cēkarippu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
5. nācak virakkal
nācak eṉṟa nalamikka viramaṇi micūr nāṭṭiṉic cērnta kōlāril kiṭittākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itu eṭi kuṟinta tāyiṉum eḻilmikkatu; oḷi ciṟantatu; kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉmaṇiyākap pōṟṟap peṟukiṟatu. itu mukkōṇa vaṭivil kaviṉuṟap paṭṭan tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉi intiyāvil āṅkila aracāṅkattiṉ piratiniti yāka irunta mārk vicu, ēṭiṅcu eṉpavar tamatu takkāṇap paṭi eṭuppiṉ pōtu kippaṟṟik koṇṭār. itaṉ eṭi 80.3 meṭrik kāraṭ ākum; vili matippu rū. 40,00,000 ākum.
6. cāṉci viramaṇi
pārata nāṭṭil uḷḷa pukaḻpeṟṟa viramaṇikaḷil cāṉci virakkallum oṉṟāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu kuṟippiṭattakka eḻilum oḷiyum vāyntatu. 1489-ām āṇṭil inta māmaṇi cārlacu-ṭi cāṉi eṉpavar kikkup pōṉatu. appāl atu elicapet araci, eṇṭiri eṭṭāmēriyā, kārṭiṉal pecāriṉ, patiṉāṉkām lūyi ākiyavarkaḷiṭam māṟi māṟic ceṉṟatu. piṉṉar pirañcup puraṭcik kālattil kāṇāmal pōyviṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉ peyaṉ aracar ṭemi ṭōv iṭamum intiya iḷavaracar oruvariṭamum kimāṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ eṭi 55 meṭrik kāraṭ. iti oru intiyar 60,00,000 rūpāy vili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉārām. iṉṟu itu irukkumiṭattit tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavilli.
7. nampikki nīla viramaṇi
vaṇṇam vāynta kiṇṇam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷil mikac ciṟantu oḷirvatu nampikki nīla vira maṇiyākum. itaṉi āṅkilattil hōp puḷū eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu, vaṇṇa muḷḷa viramaṇikaḷil mika mikap periya tum uṟuti vāyntatumākum. eṉṟālum inta arumperum nīlaniṟa viramaṇi ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭum amippi 1839- ām āṇṭilē peṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟu ataṉ varalāṟṟi naṉkuṇarnta aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ki.pi. 1642-ām āṇṭil ṭēvarṉiyar eṉpavar ārampak kālattil intiyāviṉiṉṟu tirumpi vantapoḻutu itaṉ eṭi 67 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa inta vaṭivāṉa vira maṇiyit tammōṭu koṇṭu vantār eṉṟu cila mēṉāṭ ṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. immaṇi 1668-ām āṇṭil 14ām lūyi maṉṉarukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. appāl ati 1830ām āṇṭil 44 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyākak kuṟittu ilaṇṭaṉil uḷḷa tāma hōppukku 90,000 pavuṉukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. 1911il ēlam viṭappaṭṭu vāciṅṭaṉiluḷḷa eṭvarṭ mekḷiṉ 30,00,000 
pavuṇukku vāṅkiṉār.
8. cantira viramaṇi
cantira viramaṇi eṉṉum ivvaḻakiya maṇi pāracīka vīraṉ nātarcā eṉpavarāl intiyāviṉiṉṟu cūṟiyāṭappaṭṭa koḷḷip poruḷkaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu iṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉi maṟutturip pārum uṇṭu. itu nātarcā pakivarkaḷāl koliyuṇṭu muṭintapiṉ oru āpkāṉiyap pōr vīraṉāl avariṭamiruntu kippaṟṟap paṭṭu caprā eṉpavarukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. appāl ammaṇi uruciyāviṟku viṟkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ eṭi appoḻutu 120 meṭṭirik kāraṭ āka iruntatu; vili aṉṟu 7,00,000 rūpāyāka matikkap peṟṟatu. ivvaḻakiya viramaṇi iṉṟu aikkiya cōviyat cōcaliṭ kuṭi araciṉ karuvūlattil iṭam peṟṟu oḷirkiṉṟatu.
9. cūpsi viramaṇi
cūpsi viramaṇi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum cōkara virakkal teṉ āppirikkāvil uḷḷa virac curaṅkattil 1895ām 
āṇṭil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. itu 239 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatāy iruntatu. itip paṭṭan tīṭṭi 60,00,000 rūpāykku viṟkappaṭṭatu. teṉ āppirikkāvic cērnta oru nīkki rōvartāṉ inta viramaṇiyik kaṇṭeṭuttār. avariṭa miruntu oru intiya nāṭṭu camatāṉa iḷavaraci 10,00,000 rūpāykku vilikku vāṅkik koṇṭār.
10. nicām viramaṇi
nicām virakkal kōlkoṇṭāvil vilikku kiṭitta tām. ati aitarāpāt nicām aracar vilikku vāṅkiṉār. ati avariṭamē iruntu varukiṟatu. anta viramaṇiyi alaṅkārap poruḷāka avar tam mēji mītu vittiruntār. ataṉ eṭi 340 meṭṭirikkāraṭ; vili 25,00,000 rūpāykku mēl matikkap paṭṭatu.
11. akparcā virakkal
akparcā eṉṉum mokal aracar varalāṟṟil ciṟappāṉa iṭattip peṟṟiruppatupōl avaratu peyarip peṟṟa inta viramaṇiyum intiya varalāṟṟil oru ciṟanta iṭattip peṟ ṟuḷḷatu. itu mutaṉ mutalāka 119 meṭṭirik kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa tāka iruntatu. itil cila arāpi eḻuttukkaḷ cetukkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. immaṇi 1886 āṇṭil oru tuṉi vaṭivil (Drop  - Shope) paṭṭi veṭṭiyatil 75 meṭṭirik kāraṭṭāka eṭi kuṟintu viṭṭatu. inta maṇiyi parōṭā maṉṉar keykvār 3,50,000 pavuṇukku vilikku vāṅkiṉār. iṉṟu itaṉ vili 26,000 pavuṇ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.8
12. kalliṉaṉ vira māmaṇi
kalliṉaṉ eṉṟa vira māmaṇik kal teṉ āppirikkāvil 1905ām āṇṭil peraṭarikvelcu eṉpavarāl kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. itu ulakil op paṟṟa periya māmaṇiyāka matikkappaṭṭatu. inta māmaṇiyi kaṇṭeṭuttatum 3106 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatāy irun tatu. itaṉi teṉ āppirikka ara cāṅkam vilikku vāṅki 1907ām āṇṭil iṅkilāntiṉ aracar ēḻām eṭvarṭ eṉpavarukkup paricāka aḷittatu. itaṉ vili aṉṟu 70,10,00,000 āka matikkap peṟṟatu. 1908ām āṇṭil intap periya virakkalli 9 peruntuṇṭukaḷākavum 87 ciṟu tuṇṭu kaḷākavum veṭṭiṉārkaḷ. veṭṭappaṭṭa ammāmaṇiyiṉ ciṟu pakuti piriṭṭi maṉṉariṉ ceṅkōlil iṭam peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. inta māmaṇiyiṉ peruntuṇṭiṉ eṭi 517 meṭrik kāraṭ ākum. itaṉ perum pakutikaḷ kirēṭ piriṭṭaṉi luḷḷa māmaṉṉariṉ makuṭattil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aintām jiyārj aracar paṭṭattiṟku vantatum kalliṉaṉ eṇ 1-eṉṟa viramaṇikku āppirikkāviṉ viṇmīṉ eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭap peṟṟatu. āppirikkā piriṭṭi aracāṅkattiṉ uṟuppāka iruppati eṇṇi anta āppirikkāviṉ viṇmīṉ eṉṟa periya viramaṇikkup piriṭṭaṉiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam aḷikkap paṭṭatu. itaṉit tuṇṭākkumuṉ itaṉ vili matippu veḷḷi (ṭālar) 45,23,400 eṉṟu matippiṭappaṭṭatu.
(II) muttu (Pearl)
paṇṭiyat tamiḻar kaṇṭa oṉpatu maṇikaḷil taliyāṉatu viram. aṭutta niliyil vittu matikkappaṭuvatu muttu. muṟkāla mutumakkaḷ muttukkaḷi peritum virumpi, kātaṇiyākavum mālikaḷākavum, palvēṟu vitamākavum aṇintu vantaṉar. aracikaḷ muttukkaḷi, cēlikaḷiṉ viḷimpil iṇittu aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷaṉṟi urōmar, kirēkkar, arāpiyar, ekiptiyar, cumēriyar, ittāliyar pōṉṟavarkaḷum ērāḷamāka muttukkaḷi vāṅki aṇintu vantaṉar. ulakilēyuḷḷa muttuk kaḷil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa koṟki muttē uyarntatu. immuttiṟku ulakam muḻuvatilum kirākki uṇṭu. yavaṉavarkaḷ kappalkaḷil vantu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya koṟkiyil naṅkūram pāyccip paṭṭiṉattiṟkuḷ vantu poṟ kācukaḷiyum, poṟkaṭṭikaḷiyum koṭṭik koṟki muttuk kaḷi koḷ mutal ceytu koṇṭu pōṉati avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintu veḷip paṇpāṭṭil koṟki muttukkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ati āynta varkaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vaṇika vaḷarcciyik kaṇṭu viyappuṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷākiya cintuveḷi makkaḷōṭu - nilavaḻiyāy 2000 kal toliviṟku mēṟceṉṟu vaṇikattoṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu iṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
muttu vakikaḷ
muṟkālattilē muttukkaḷ palavakippaṭṭavikaḷāy iruntaṉa. (1) vaṭṭamuttu (2) oppumuttu (3) kuṟumuttu 
(4) nimpōḷam (5) payiṭṭam (6) ampu (7) karaṭu (8) iraṭṭi 
(9) cappātti (10) cakkattu (11) kuḷirnta nīr (12) civanta nīr eṉṟu pala vakippaṭṭavikaḷāy iruntaṉa eṉat teṉ intiya cācaṉaṅ kaḷil viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivikaḷil vaṭṭamuttu eṉpatu, uruṇṭiyāṉa muttu. aṉuvaṭṭam eṉpatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉatu. oppumuttu oḷiviṭum uyarmuttu. kuṟumuttu ciṟumuttākum. karaṭu eṉpatu muraṭāṉa muttu. iraṭṭi eṉpatu irumuttukkaḷ iyaṟki yākavē iṇintu kāṇappaṭuvatu. ēṉiya muttukkaḷukku nalla viḷakkam teriyavilli.
tōṟṟam
muttukkaḷ kaṭaṟcippikaḷiṉ uṭaliṉiṉṟu eṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivikaḷik kaṭal muttu, cippi muttu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. ēṉeṉil kuṉṟimuttu, puḷiya muttu, āmaṇakku muttu, ākiyavikaḷukkum itaṟkum vēṟṟumi terivataṟkēyām. āṉāl muttu eṉṟu ciṟappāka collappaṭuvatu intak kaṭal muttutāṉ. cippi muttukkaḷ kaṭalil viḷintu kiṭantāl mīṉavarkaḷ muttu eṭukka muṉivar. itu mikak kaṣṭamāṉa toḻil. kaṭaliṉ aṭiyil pāṟikaḷil muttuc cippikaḷ kuviyal kuviyalāka viḷintu kiṭakkum. avaṟṟi muttukkuḷikkum mīṉavarkaḷ tōṇiyiṟ ceṉṟu kaṭalil mūḻkit taṅkaḷōṭu koṇṭu pōyirukkum kūṭiyil vāri vippārkaḷ. kūṭi niṟintatum, taṅkaḷip piṇittirukkum kayiṟṟi acippārkaḷ. tōṇiyil iruppavarkaḷ kayiṟṟi iḻuttuk kūṭiyi veḷiyē koṇṭu vantu atiluḷḷa muttuc cippikaḷit tōṇiyil koṭṭu vārkaḷ. muttu eṭuttatum muttuk kuḷippavarkaḷ mēlē varu vārkaḷ. kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷattiliruntu cippikaḷi vārum mīṉa varkaḷ kaṭalil vāḻum payaṅkaramāṉa cuṟā mīṉkaḷukkuc cila camayaṅ kaḷil āḷāvatum uṇṭu. cuṟā mīṉkaḷiṉ apāyaṅkaḷukkut tappa mīṉavarkaḷ pala mantiraṅkaḷum tantiraṅkaḷum kiyāṇṭu varuki ṟārkaḷ.
muttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu
muttukkaḷil aṭaṅki irukkum mūlapporuḷ cuṇṇāmpuc cattutāṉ eṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. muttuc cippikaḷ taṅkaḷ mītuḷḷa iru ōṭukaḷiyum tiṟantu koṇṭu veḷiyē cañcarikkum poḻutu ataṉuḷ ciṟumaṇ, kal pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil ceṉṟuviṭum. melliya taci kaḷuḷḷa cippikku itu oru perum vētaṉiyāy irukkum. eṉavē atit taṇikka virumpit taṉ uṭalil iruntu oruvakit tirāvakatti veḷippaṭuttum. cippiyiṉ uṭampil uḷḷa kal allatu maṇ mītu attirāvakam oṭṭik koḷḷum. ataṉāl ataṉ vētaṉi nīṅkukiṟatu. appāl atic cuṟṟik kaṭiṉamāṉa taci vaḷarum. nāḷaṭivil uḷḷirukkum kal allatumaṇ muttāka māṟukiṟatu.
muttuk kiṭikkumiṭaṅkaḷ
muttukkaḷ intiyā, cīṉā, cappāṉ, amerikkā, ilaṅki pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷil kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭu toṭṭu intiyāvil ciṟappākat teṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil muttuk kuḷikkun toḻil naṭipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. maṉṉār kuṭākkaṭalil kuṟippāka nelli māvaṭṭatti aṭuttuḷḷa kaṭalil nalla muttukkaḷ viḷikiṉṟaṉa. inta muttukkaḷukku ulakameṅkum matippu iruntu varukiṉṟatu. koṟki muttu kuvalayam aṉittilumuḷḷa ellā maṇic cantikaḷiyum eṭṭippārttavi. pāracīka vaḷikuṭāvil viḷiyum muttukkaḷiviṭa maṉṉār vaḷikuṭāvil viḷiyum muttukkaḷ matippuṭiyaṉavākum. koṟki muttukkaḷ veṇmi yum tiṇmiyum oḷiyum aḻakum vāyntaṉavākum. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇattil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ talinakaratti muttūr eṉṉum poruḷtarum kavāṭapuram eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
naṉ muttu
peritākavum uruṇṭiyākavum teḷinta nīr pōṉṟum tikaḻum muttukkaḷē naṉmuttukkaḷ eṉṟu navilappaṭum. tūyatāyum vaḻavaḻappāyum viṇ pōṉṟa oḷiyuḷḷatāyum nīroḷiyuḷḷa tāyum irukkum. muttukkaḷ ciṟanta muttukkaḷ eṉṟum, avi kaḷi aṇintāl ellā nalaṉkaḷum perukum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ
muttukkaḷ vikāramākavum aḻukkup paṭintum karaṭu muraṭākavum uppup pōlavum muṭiccu viḻuntum āmi uru piṟi vaṭivam, tēṉmeḻuku pōṉṟa puḷḷi, nīla niṟam, kaṟuppum mañcaḷum kalanta vaṇṇam vāyntum vēṟṟiṭat tuḷiyuḷḷaviyāyum iruntāl kuṟṟam uḷḷaṉavāka matikkappaṭum.
maruttuvattuṟi viḷakkam
muttu kuḷircciyum tittippum uḷḷatu. kaṇ nōy, vāta nōy, iratta pittam, kāyccal, mayakkam mutaliya nōykaḷi akaṟṟum. nīṇṭa āyuḷiyum, mēṉi miṉu miṉuppiyum nalkum. muttup papam pittam kācam kapam mutaliya ellā, pollāp piṇikaḷiyum pōkkum. vīriya viruttiyi aḷikkum. muttuc cuṇṇam kapatōcattik karuvaṟukkum. iḵtaṉṟi vikkal, akkiṉi mantam pōṉṟa aṉēka nōy akalum.
cōtaṉi
uppum eṇṇeyum kalanta vennīril, ōriravu muḻuvatum mutti ūṟavittu appāl ati nellōṭu cērttut tēyttup pārkkavum nalla oḷiyōṭu iruntāl naṉ muttu eṉṟum oḷi kuṉṟi niṉṟāl ceyaṟki muttu eṉṟum teḷika.
ippoḻutu ulakil ērāḷamāṉa ceyaṟki muttukkaḷum, ceyaṟki muṟiyil kaṭalil viḷivikkum muttukkaḷum malivāṉa viliyil eṅkum viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cappāṉ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷil mēṟkaṇṭa iruvaki muttukaḷum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
muttu mālikaḷ
muttu aṇikalaṉkaḷi muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ virumpi aṇintu vantaṉar. mēṉāṭṭu mātarkaḷ kūṭa malaṭṭut taṉmi nīṅkavum, kaṇpārvi teḷivuṟavum muttuk koluciyum muttu maṇivaṭaṅkaḷiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷir kaḷukku muttumālikaḷil mōkam atikam. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ makkaḷukku muttu māli, muttu maṇi, muttiyā, muttam perumāḷ, muttuk kiruṣṇaṉ eṉṟellām peyar cūṭṭi vantaṉar. paṇṭiyat tamiḻakattil muttumāli 5 vakippaṭṭatāy iruntatu. (1) talimaṇiyuṭiyatu (2) tuṇit tali maṇiyuṭiyatu (3) kaṇu niliyuṭiyatu (4) taraḷak kaṭṭuṭiyatu (5) ceṟi niliyuṭiyatu.
1. talimaṇivaṭam
vaṭattiṉ naṭuvē periya maṇiyi iṭam peṟa ceytu, kōttu iru pakkaṅkaḷilum otta aḷavuḷḷa ciṟiya muttumaṇi kaḷi eṭuttuk kōttavaṭam talimaṇi vaṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
2. tuṇit talimaṇivaṭam
naṭuvil oru paru muttum iru puṟaṅkaḷilum otta aḷaviṉi yuṭiya ciṟumuttu maṇiyum appāl perum maṇiyum iṭam peṟumāṟu māṟi māṟik kōtta muttu māli, tuṇit talimaṇi vaṭam eṉappaṭum.
3. kaṇu nilimaṇivaṭam
naṭuvil oru perum muttum irupuṟamum otta parumaṉuḷḷa iraṇṭiraṇṭu ciṟu muttukaḷumāka māṟi māṟik kōtta vaṭam kaṇu nilimaṇivaṭam eṉappaṭum.
4. taraḷak kaṭṭuvaṭam
otta aḷavuṭiya nittilaṅkaḷ (muttukkaḷ) kōtta vaṭam taraḷak kaṭṭu vaṭam eṉappaṭum. muttukkut taraḷam, nittilam eṉṟa peyarkaḷ uṇṭu.
5. ceṟi nili vaṭam 
naṭuvil periya muttu maṇikaḷik kōttu irupuṟamum muṟiyē ciṟuttu varumāṟu kōttavaṭam ceṟi nili vaṭam eṉappaṭum.
mēṟkūṟiya aintu vakiyāṉa taraḷa vaṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟil kōkkappaṭṭa maṇivakikaḷip poṟuttē vevvēṟu peyar kaḷip peṟukiṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka talimaṇivaṭam mutal aintu vaṭaṅkaḷum intirac cantam eṉṉum peyarōṭu iṇintu talimaṇi intirac cantam tuṇit talimaṇi intirac cantam eṉpaṉa pōl vevvēṟu peyarkaḷip peṟum. ivvāṟu aimpatu peyarkaḷiyuṭiya mālikaḷ uḷa. (1) intirac cantam 1008, (2) viyac cantam  500, (3) tēvaccantam 100, (4) arutta māli 64, (5) katirk kalāpam 54, (6) kuccam 32,  (7) naṭcattiramāli 27, (8) artta kuccam 24, (9) māṇavakam 20, (10) artta māṇavakam 10 - muttuk kaḷiyum uṭiyaṉa.
iṅkilāntil cut keṉciṅṭaṉ poruṭkāṭci niliyat til, ulakattil uḷḷa ellā muttukkaḷiyum viṭa periya āṇi muttu uḷatu. atu 3 avuṇcuk kaṉamum 4 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum uḷḷa oru perum muttu ākum. āṉāl ativiṭap periya māmuttu cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa cocimā (Zosima) eṉṟa kāṭci cāliyil irukkiṟatu. itaṉ peyar vāpel vikariṉā eṉappaṭum. itaṉ eṭi 28 kāraṭ. itu aḻakum oḷiyum vāyntatu. itutāṉ ulakilē periya oppumi illāta oḷi muttākum. itu koṟki muttu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷ
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil cemmi, veṇmi, poṉmi, karumi ākiya niṟaṅkaḷ vāynta naṉmuttukkaḷ tiṅkaḷukkuk kaḷippi uṇṭākkuvaṉavākum. eṉavē tiṅkaḷukku uriya vīṭākiya naṇṭu ōriyil (kaṭaka lakkaṉattil) piṟantavarkaḷ kaṭal muttukkaḷi aṇivatu nalam tarum eṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭuc cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṅkilēyarkaḷ kūṭap piṟanta tiṅkaḷi vittu ataṟkuriya maṇikaḷ iviyeṉṟu aṟintu anta maṇikaḷi aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. itu āṅkilamaṇi āyvu nūṟkaḷil naṉku kāṇappaṭum.
marakatam (Emerald)
oṉpatu maṇikaḷuḷ pacci niṟam vāyntatu marakata maṇi yākum. talimaṇikaḷil mūṉṟāvatu iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷa kaṇ kavar maṇiyākum. paccip pacumi vāyntatu eṉṟu paṇṭu mutal tamiḻarkaḷ pōṟṟi varum vaṇṇamākum. pacci parācattiyiṉ niṟam eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭuvatākum. pacci niṟamāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉiṉṟu uyirkaḷukku uyirūṭṭum uṇavup poruḷ kiṭikkiṉṟatu. aṉṉi parācakti taṉ makkaḷākiya uyirkaḷukku pāl curantu ūṭṭi avarkaḷik kaḷippil āḻttit tāṉum kaḷippuṟukiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu muṟkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ eṇṇam.
marakatattiṉ taṉmi
marakatam viram pōṉṟu kaṭiṉamāṉatu. eṉṟālum pacumi yāṉa oḷikkatirkaḷuṭaṉ meṉmiyāṉatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi marakatak kalliṉ mītu paṭṭāl atiṉ pacci vaṇṇa oḷi ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟamellām paravi arukiluḷḷa ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum pacci niṟamāka oḷirac ceykiṉṟatu. eṉavē aṇikalaṉkaḷil marakata maṇiyi naṭunāyamāka amittu ēṉiya maṇiyic cuṟṟilum vittu aṇikaḷ camikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. marakatattiṉ pacumiyāṉa oḷi ēṉiya maṇikaḷil pirati palippatu oru arum perum aḻakākum.
kiṭikkum iṭam 
marakatam kōmētakattiṉ iyalpukaḷi uṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ikkaṟkaḷ ceyappūr, kācumīr pakutikaḷilum parmā, ilaṅki, cōviyat yūṉiyaṉ, ekiptu, kalipōrṉiyā, maṭakācukkar, ātirēliyā pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum pūmiyiṉiṉṟu tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil kiṭikkum marakata maṇikaḷ mikap pacumi vāynta uyariya maṇikaḷāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu, intiya maṇi vaṇikarkaḷ ceyappūril kiṭikkum marakatamaṇi kaḷiyē peritum virumpi avaṟṟukku atika vili koṭuttu vāṅkukiṟārkaḷ. marakatak kaṟkaḷ aṇikaḷ amippataṟku maṭṭu maṉṟi iliṅkaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟkum vāṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. marakata maṇikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷ ciṟappuṭiyaṉavākum. namatu nāṭṭil cila kōyilkaḷil marakata iliṅkaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
maṇiccōtaṉi (irattiṉap parīṭci) eṉṟa nūlil uyarnta paccik kaṟkaḷ, marakatam kārut matam acma karppam, ariṉmaṇi eṉṟellām aḻikkappaṭum eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
nikaṇṭukaḷil marakatam, kāṭam, ullacitam, pēcalam, pittakam, muktam, pirutukam; eṉum aṟuvakik kuṇaṅkaḷ uṭiyatu eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷum kuṟṟaṅkaḷum
marakatattiṟkuk karukal, veḷḷi, kal, maṇal kīṟṟu, kuḻi yuṭimi, tār ōṭi iruttal pōṉṟavi kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ākum. aṟu vakik kuṇaṅkaḷ : atāvatu kārṭam appoḻututāṉ muḷitta aṟukam pulliṉ niṟam, ullaḷitam - vaḻavaḻappu, pēcalam - neṟpayir pōṉṟa niṟam, pittam - pacuṅkiḷiyiṉ iṟaku pōṉṟa vaṇṇam. muktam - tuḷici ili pōṉṟa niṟam. pirutukam - tāmari  ili pōṉṟa taṉmikaḷ immaṇiyiṉ ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷ ākum. im maṇiyi aṇipavarkaḷukku ellā nalamum perukum. iḵtaṉṟi kuṟṟamuḷḷa maṇikaḷi aṇintāl kēṭukaḷ viḷiyum eṉṟu maṇi nūṟkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
	ēkiyum māliyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
	 pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyum	  (cilap. ūr. vari. 184 - 185)
eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceyyuḷukku urikaṇṭa āciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkaṇṭa karuttukkaḷi araṇ ceyyum vaḻiyil viḷakka uri kūṟiyuḷḷār.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil marakatamaṇi
marakata maṇi vayiṟṟuk kaṭuppi nīkkum; mēka nōyip pōkkum; eḷitākap makappēṟu peṟap peritum tuṇiceyyum. marakata papam, marakatamaṇi pōṉṟavi, tiritōcaṅkaḷip pōkkum; uṭaṟ palamum nīṇṭa āyuḷum tarum eṉṟu maruttuva nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cōtaṉi
nalla cāti marakatattik kaṇṭaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟāl, oru kiṇṇattil pāli vittu atil marakata maṇiyip pōṭṭāl ataṉ oḷi, pāl muḻuvatum paravi pāl, pacci niṟam pōṉṟu tōṉṟum. ceyaṟki marakatam avvāṟu oḷi viṭātu. iyaṟki yāṉa marakata maṇiyik kallil tēyttāl maṇi tēyātu. ceyaṟki marakatam tēyum, uṭiyum.
marakata māmaṇi
ituvari mānilattil kiṭitta marakata maṇikaḷil tivāṉcayar iḷavaracariṭam irukkum 1350 kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa marakata maṇiyē mikap periyatākum. iḵtaṉṟip piriṭṭīcut tol poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyilum oru periya marakata māmaṇi irukkiṟatu. ivi kuṟṟam aṟṟavi; viliyuyarntavi.
pirivum kirakaṅkaḷum
marakatam vēḷāḷar iṉam. cātvīkakuṇam uṭiyatu. mayil, kāṭi pōṉṟa paṟavi iṟaku niṟam vāynta marakatam putaṉukku makiḻcci tarum. putaṉukkuriya irāciyil piṟantavarkaḷ kuṟṟamaṟṟa marakatatti aṇivatāl aṟivu vaḷarum; kirakatōcam akalum; aḻakum āyuḷum perukum.
māṇikkam (Ruby)
māṇikkam, civappu, patumarākam eṉṟu kūṟip pōṟṟap peṟum maṇiyākum; viramaṇikku aṭutta paṭiyāka vittup pōṟṟakkūṭiya uyarnta kallākum. māṇikkam nalla aḻaku vāynta cenniṟattiyuṭiyatu. itu mutalil veḷuppākat tōṉṟip piṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāl cenniṟam peṟukiṟatu. nalla cenniṟam vāynta māṇikkattiṟkuc ciṟappu atikam; māṇikkam maṇikaḷiṉ maṉṉaṉ eṉṟu maṇi nipuṇarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷ.
vakikaḷ 
maṇikaḷil māṇpu vāyntatāka matikkappaṭum māṇikkam  4 vakippaṭum. (1) cavuntikam (2) nīlakanti (3) patmarākam (4) kuruvintam.
(1) cavuntikam : itu āntira nāṭṭil atikamākak kiṭik kiṟatu. acōkat taḷir pōṉṟa vaṇṇam vāyntatu.
(2) nīlakanti : itu tumpurattil atikam kiṭikkiṟatu; nīla niṟam vāyntatu; kavarccimikkatu.
(3) patmarākam : immaṇi irattiṉa tīpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; īḻa nāṭṭil (ilaṅkiyil) ērāḷamākak kiṭikkiṟatu; nalla civappu niṟam vāyntatu.
(4) kuruvintam : kalāpurattil atikam akappaṭukiṟatu; mañcaḷ niṟam vāyntatu.
ivikaḷil, ilaṅkiyil centāmari malar vaṇṇattil kiṭikkum patmarākam eṉṉum maṇi ciṟantatu.
kiṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷ : iṉṟu pala māṇikkac curaṅkaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. parmā, cayām, intōcīṉam, roṭi ciyā, cācumīr (intiyā) āpkāṉicuttāṉ, ilaṅki pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil  ellām māṇikka maṇikaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl parmāvil mōkāk eṉṟum iṭattilum kāttē māvaṭṭattilum uyarnta māṇikka maṇikaḷ atikam kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉappirivukaḷ
māṇikkattil patumarākam antaṇar iṉam eṉṟum cavun tikam vaṇikacāti eṉṟum nīlakanti vēḷāḷar iṉam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
kaṉamāka iruttal, aḻakāṉa vaṇṇamāka iruttal, mācillātatāy viḷaṅkal, nalla centāmari malar pōṉṟa civappu niṟamākap polital, nalla māṇikka maṇiyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷākum. iti aṇipavarkaḷukku nīṇṭa vāṇāḷ, nōyaṟṟa vāḻkki, uṭaṟpalam mutaliyavi vaḷarum; maṉanōykaḷ iruntāl māṟum; eḻil perukum.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ : civappu niṟamaṟṟamaṇi, vēṟum vaṇṇam vāyn tatu, puḷḷi, tarācam (oḷi calittal), kīṟṟu, kākapatam, ākiyavi iruttal  māṇikkak kaṟkaḷiṉ māperum kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum. ivi kaḷi aṇintālum vittiruntālum keṭuti uṇṭākum. pakivarkaḷāl iṭiyūṟu uṇṭākum. vilaṅkukaḷālum nōy kaḷālum tuṉpamum tuyaramum viḷiyum eṉpar.
uyarnta maṇi : ulakil uyarnta māṇikka māmaṇi 167 kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatu. itu paṭṭi tīrkkappaṭātatu eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu. āṉāl intiyāvi āṇṭa mokalāya maṉṉar cācakāṉ avarkaḷiṉ mayilācaṉattil patippikkappeṟṟa timūr eṉṉum māṇikkak kal nalla paṭṭi tīrkkappeṟṟa kallākum. itu 361 kāraṭ  eṭiyuḷḷatu. uruciyāvil irunta kātariṉ araciyiṭam 50 kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa māṇikkam iruntatu. atu iṉṟiya cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa aracāṅkat tolporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil uḷḷatu.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil māṇikkam
māṇikka papam, caḷi, curam, tākam, mēkam, kaṇṇōy pōṉṟavikaḷi akaṟṟum; āyuḷ viruttiyi uṇṭākkum;  mēṉiyil paḷapaḷappiyum, uṭalukkuk kuḷircciyiyum tarum.
cōtaṉi : māṇikkak kaṟkaḷil nalla iṉakkaṟkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikka, kallil tēyttup pārkkalām; nīril pōṭṭuk kotikka vittup pārkkalām. nalla māṇikkamaṇi tēyātu; niṟam māṟātu. ceyaṟki māṇikkam uṭiyum tēyum, niṟam māṟum.
cōtiṭa nūlil māṇikkam : cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil ñāyiṟṟukku nalla civappu niṟam vāynta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷil atikappiriyam uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. māṇikkam ñāyiṟṟiṉ vīṭākiya ciṅka ilakkaṉattil piṟantavarkaḷukku naṉmiyi nalkum. ñāyiṟṟiṉ tōcaṅkaḷit tuṭikkum. maṇikaḷil māṇikkam maṉṉar marapic cērntatu. irajata kuṇam uṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
irattiṉa tīpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ilaṅkiyil irattiṉapuri eṉṉum ūril iṉṟum iṇiyilāta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷ kiṭittu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maṇikaḷi - ciṟappāka, kāyal paṭṭiṉam, kīḻakkari mutaliya ūriluḷḷa iculāmiya vaṇikarkaḷ vāṅki ilaṅkiyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum periya maṇi vaṇikar kaḷāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ittoḻilil nalla nipuṇattuvam peṟṟirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.

iṭi maṇikaḷ
I
nīlam (Sapphire)
nīlamaṇikku ataṉ niṟatti vittē nīlam eṉṟa peyar vikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. potuvāka viram-veḷḷi, marakatam - pacci, māṇikkam - civappu, puṭparākam - mañcaḷ, kōmētakam - mañcaḷum civappum kalanta civappu, viṭūriyam - veḷuṟiya nīlaniṟam, nīlam - nalla nīla niṟamum uṭiyaṉa. ivikaḷil māṇikkam, marakatam, puṭparākam, viram, nīlam ākiya maṇikaḷi aṟiñarkaḷ aintu maṇikaḷ (pañcarattiṉam) eṉpārkaḷ. marakatam, māṇikkam, intira nīlam, muttu, viram, ākiyavikaḷi māmaṇikaḷ (mahā rattiṉaṅkaḷ) eṉpārkaḷ.
iṉam
potuvāka viramum muttum antaṇar iṉam eṉṟum māṇikkamum pavaḷamum aracar iṉam eṉṟum puṭparākam, viṭūriyam, kōmētakam ākiyavi vaṇikar iṉam eṉṟum, nīlam, marakatam ākiyavi vēḷāḷar iṉameṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iḵtaṉṟi veḷuṟiya nīlam antaṇar cāti eṉṟum, civappu nīlam aracar cāti eṉṟum, mañcaḷ nīlam vaṇikar cāti eṉṟum, kaṟuppu nīlam uḻavar cāti eṉṟum kūṟuvar. nīlattil nāṉku iṉam pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atāvatu vēḷāḷar iṉamākiya nīlattilē nāṉku iṉappirivukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mēlum nīlamaṇiyi intira nīlam, makā nīlam eṉṟu iruvakiyāyp pirippatum uṇṭu. intira nīlam karumiyāṉatu; kaṉamāṉatu; ciṟantatu. calanīlam ilēcāṉatu, veḷuṟiyatu. maṇiyiṉ naṭuvil vāṉavil pōṉṟa oḷi iruntāl atu cuttamāṉa intira nīlam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itu kiṭippatu aritu. viliyum uyarntatu. inta nīlattip pālil pōṭṭāl pāl muḻuvatum nīlamākak kāṇappaṭum. itaṉi makā nīlam eṉpar. inta makā nīlattiyum ataṉ niṟattiyum tarattiyum koṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ itaṉuḷ nāṉku iṉappirivukaḷ kāṇpar.
kuṇam
maṇiccōtaṉi eṉṟa vaṭamoḻi nūlil kaṉam, kavarcciyāṉa oḷi, nalla pūrippu, pakkaṅkaḷil pūrippu, pullippiṭikkun taṉmi eṉṟa aintu kuṇaṅkaḷum nīlamaṇikku irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kuṟṟam
aprakam, cacarkaram, tirācam, piṉṉam, miruttikā karpam, acma karpam  ākiyavi nīlamaṇiyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum: 1. aprakattip pōṉṟa vaṇṇam nīlattiṉ mītu paṭarntiruntāl atu aprakam eṉappaṭum. iti aṇintāl celvam aḻiyum; āyuḷ kuṉṟum. (2) maṇiyil maṇal cērntiruntāl cacarkaram eṉappaṭum. itip pūṇṭāl vaṟumi vāṭṭum; ciṟumiyūṭṭum; nāṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭṭum. (3) porical iruntāl tirācam eṉpārkaḷ. itip pūṇṭāl naccuḷḷa uyiriṉaṅkaḷāl tīmi viḷiyum. (4) uṭinta maṇiyip piṉṉappaṭṭa maṇi eṉpārkaḷ. iti aṇintāl maṉiviyi iḻakka nēriṭum eṉpar. (5) nīlakkallil naṭuvē uṭpuṟa  māka maṇ iruppiṉ ati miruttikākarpam eṉpārkaḷ. iti aṇintāl avamatippu, uṭampil tōlippaṟṟiya nōykaḷ tōṉṟum eṉpar. (6) nīlamaṇiyil uḷḷē kal kāṇappaṭṭāl ati acmakarpam eṉpar. ati aṇipavarkaḷ avamatippukku āḷāvārkaḷ.
nīlam kiṭikkum iṭam
nīlamaṇikaḷ, intiyāvil kācumīrp pakutiyilum ilaṅki yil irattiṉapuriyilum cayāmil pāṭṭam pāṅkilum parmāvil mōkākkilum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa; aṉṟiyum kalipōrṉiyā maṭa kācukkar, kuviṉculāntu pōṉṟa kāṭukaḷilum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil nīlam :
nīla maṇiyip poṭi (papam) ceytu uṇṭāl cītaḷam, caṭam, ciṉikkam, pittam mutaliya piṇikaḷ akalum. pāṇṭu, mati mayakkam pōṉṟa pala nōykaḷum akalum. iti aṇintāl aṟivu oḷirum; iṉpam perukum.
	kutivalimē kampittam kūṟariya pāṇṭu
	 matimayakka mellā maruḷum - matinutalē
	 puttiyoṭu mēṉmēlum pōka cukamuṇṭām
	 cuttanaṟu nīlattāl col
eṉṟu patārttakuṇa cintāmaṇi eṉṟa maruttuva nūl nīla paṭpattiṉ kuṇattik kūṟukiṟatu.12
ulakil uyarnta maṇi 
ulakil uyarnta nīlamaṇi 916 kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷatu. itu 3½ aṅkula nīḷamum 2 aṅkula akalamum 1 aṅkula kaṉamum uḷḷatu. immaṇi aitarāpāttil uḷḷatu. itaṉ vili matippu 5,00,000 rūpāy ākum. muṉṉar itutāṉ ulakiṟ periya nīlamaṇiyāy iruntatu. ippoḻutu parmāvil 1029 kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa nīlamaṇi oṉṟu kiṭittiruppatākat terikiṟatu.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil nīlam
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil maṇikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intira nīlakkal caṉikku uriyatu. makaram, kumpam ākiya caṉikkuriya irācikaḷil piṟantavarkaḷ kuṟṟamaṟṟa intira nīlakkalli aṇin tāl caṉi tōcam nīṅkum. mēlum caṉi pakavāṉiṉ karuṇi perukum. āyuḷum, celvamum uṭal nalaṉum perukum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
II
puṭparākam (Topaz)
puṭparākam viram pōṉṟu mañcaḷ kalanta veṇmiyāṉa maṇi. mēl pākam vaṭṭamākavum maḻamaḻappākavum iruntāl ciṟanta maṇiyāka matikkappaṭum. itu potuvākap pāṟikaḷiṉ naṭuvilē uṇṭākiṟatu. puṭparākam pūcanaṭcattirampōl poli yum. poṉṉit teḷiya vittāṟpōṉṟu ilaṅkum. itaṉic cilappatikāram pūca uruviṟ polanteḷittaṉiya 13 eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. immaṇiyi aṇipavarkaḷukku pala nalam viḷiyum. vēntarāyiṉ veṟṟimāli cūṭuvar eṉṟu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṟatu.
kiṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷ
puṭparākam pirēcil nāṭṭil ūṭā, kolarāṭā, orō piriṭṭā, villārīkā, miṉācu nōvācu, miṉācu cerāyacu pōṉṟa pakutikaḷil kiṭikkiṉṟatu. ōrō piriṭṭāvil ciṟanta ārañcu vaṇṇap puṭparākaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ilaṅkiyil lēcāṉa mañcaḷ niṟa maṇikaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa iyūral maliyilum cipīriyāvilum lēcāṉa pacci veḷuṟiya nīlam vāynta puṭparākaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭakku nicīriyāvil uḷḷa takarak kaṉikaḷiyuṭiya vaṇṭal piratēcaṅkaḷil itaṉi otta, nīrāl aṟukkappaṭṭa paṭikaṅkaḷum kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
aḻakiya tōṟṟam, parumaṉ, teḷivu, kaṉam, meṉmi kōṅkilava malar pōṉṟa niṟam, nalla veṇmi ākiya ēḻu vitamāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷ nalla puṭparākattiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷāka vākaṭam eṉṟa nūl kūṟukiṟatu.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ 
oḷiyiṉmi, nācaṉi (māṇikkattil uṇṭākum veṇṇīr) karumi, mañcaḷ, mēṭupaḷḷam, kalaṅkal, karu veṇmi, veṇmi ākiya  taṉmikaḷ puṭparākattiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum.
puṭparākam uruvil periyatāyum vaḻavaḻappāyum tāmari yiṉ karṇiki pōṉṟa niṟam uṭiyatāyum iruntāl ati aṇiyalām. ati aṇintārkku ellāc celvaṅkaḷum nalamum poṅkum.
uyarnta maṇi
piriṭṭiṣ tolporuḷ kāṭciccāliyil nārvēyi liruntu koṇṭuvanta 137 - pavuṇṭu niṟiyuḷḷa puṭparākam oṉṟuṇṭu. intiya mokalāya maṉṉaṉ avuraṅkacīppiṭam 157- kāraṭ eṭiyuḷḷa 8 paṭṭiyāṉa puṭparāka maṇi oṉṟu iruntatu.
maruttuva nūlil puṭparākam
puṭparāka pacuppam kapatti nīkkum; uṭal vaḷattip perukkum; vīriya viruttiyum puttik kūrmiyum uṇṭākkum eṉpar.
cōtiṭa nūlil puṭparākam
puṭparākam viyāḻaṉukkuriyatu. mīṉa lakkiṉattil piṟanta varkaḷ iti aṇintāl viyāḻatōcam nīṅkum; nōy akalum; palam uṇṭākum eṉpar.
III
viṭūriyam (cat - eyes)
viṭūriyam paḻuppu niṟa oḷiyum veṇmiyāṉa oḷiyum koṇṭu oḷirum. itic cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar katiravaṉ oḷiyip pōṉṟu ilaṅkum; tēṉ tuḷiyiṉ uruvam pōṉṟu tikaḻum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kuṇam
mūṅkil ili, pūṉikkaṇ, mayiṟkaḻuttu ivi pōṉṟa oḷiyuḷḷa viṭūriya maṇikaḷē uyarntaṉa. immaṇi eṅku iruppiṉum  ataṉuḷ nūl iḻi pōṉṟa oḷi vīcum. itaṉi nūlōṭṭam eṉpar.
kuṟṟam
kaṟuppum cama uruvamum uḷḷatu; karaṭu muraṭāṉatu; nīr niṟam uḷḷatu. ilēcāṉatu, kuṟṟamuḷḷa maṇiyākum. itu aṇiyattakka taṉṟu; vilakkattakkatu. iti aṇintāl kēṭu cūḻum. vaṟumi mikum. nōy perukum.
cōtaṉi
viṭūriyatti urikallil urittāl  oḷi perukum. ituvē uyarnta viṭūriyattiṉ taṉmiyākum.
kiṭikkum iṭam
viṭūriyam intiyāvilum ilaṅkiyilum kiṭikkiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi pirēcil, urukuvē, iṅkilāntu, kāṭlāntu, vaṭa amerikkā, teṉ āppirikkā, ācuttirēliyā pāracīkam pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilum kiṭikkum.
maruttuva nūlil viṭūriyam
viṭūriya pacuppam cīvatātam, cilēttumam, puḷiyēppam, kātu cūli, kuṉmam, pittam pōṉṟa nōykaḷi akaṟṟum.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil viṭūriyam
viṭūriyam kētuviṟku uriyatu. kētu tōcam uḷḷavarkaḷ iti aṇintāl attōcam nīṅkum eṉpar.
iṉam
kōmētakam, viṭūriyam, puṭparākam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷum cantirakāntam, cūriyakāntam pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷum paṭika iṉattic cērntavi eṉṟu maṇiccōtaṉiyil tērnta nipuṇarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.

kaṭi maṇikaḷ
I
kōmēkatam (zircon)
kōmētakam pacuviṉ ciṟunīr niṟatti otta vaṇṇamāka iruppatāl kōmētakam eṉṟu peyar vantatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. airōppiyar kōmētakam kutiriyiṉ mūttira vaṇṇattil irukkum eṉpar. immaṇi mañcaḷum civappum kalantatu. cilap patikāra āciriyar immaṇiyi iru vēṟu ruva eṉṟu kūṟi yuḷḷār. itu kaṟpāṟikaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
(1) nalla, teḷinta pacuviṉ ciṟunīr niṟamuṭiyatu. (2) mācil lātatu, cuttamāṉatu. (3) paḷapaḷappuṭiyatu. (4) aḻuttam uḷḷatu. (5) kaṉam uṭiyatu (6) mēṭupaḷḷam illātatu, (7) koḻuppāṉatu, (8) oḷi niṟintatu ākiya eṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu, nalla kōmētaka maṇiyāka matikkappeṟum.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ
(1) maṅkalāṉatu, (2) mācuḷḷatu (pāci paṭarntiruppatu) (3) lēcāṉatu (4) mēṭupaḷḷam uḷḷatu (5) uppuk kacivuḷḷatu (6) takaṭu vaṭivam vāyntatu (7) oḷiyaṟṟiruppatu, (8) urukkuk kal pōliruppatu ākiya eṭṭum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ākum.
iyalpu : kōmētakattit taṇalil kāṭṭiṉāl āccariyap paṭattakka māṟutal eḻum. mikac cūṭākkiṉāl niṟaṅkuṉṟum; paḷapaḷappu mikum.
 maruttuva nūlil kōmētakam : kōmētakap pacuppam cāppiṭṭāl kapam nīṅkum; vātapitta tōcampōm, cīraṇacakti perukum; malakkaṭṭi akaṟṟum, valimitarum; uṭampiṟku nalla oḷi ūṭṭum.
cōtiṭa nūlil kōmētakam
irākukku ukanta maṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iti aṇintāl irākutōcam akalum. immaṇi aṇivatāl nalla uṭal valimi tarum. uṭal nalam perukum. uṭalil nalla miṉumiṉuppu uṇṭākum eṉṟu cukkira nīti eṉṟa ciṟpanūl kūṟukiṟatu
pavaḷam (Coral)
pavaḷap pūccikaḷ kaṭalil uḷḷa piṟa uyiriṉaṅkaḷāl taṅkaḷukku nēriṭum āpatti akaṟṟa oṉṟu kūṭi uṇṭākkum oru pātukāppu araṇē pavaḷamākum. āḻ kaṭalkaḷil pavaḷaṅkaḷ ceṭikaḷ pōl kāṇappaṭum. pavaḷak kāṭukaḷ kaṭalil - ciṟappāka intumāk kaṭalil uṇṭu. iḵtaṉṟi intu mattiya tarik kaṭalilum cenniṟap pavaḷak kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ācuttirēliyāviṟku vaṭakiḻakkilum mattiyatarik kaṭaṟpakutikaḷākiya morōkkō, alcīriyā, tūṉiciyā, cappāṉ, ayarlāntu, ittāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum pavaḷaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
niṟaṅkaḷ
pavaḷaṅkaḷ palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷavi. civappu, veṇmi kalanta civappu, veṇmi, kaṟuppu, nīlam pōṉṟa niṟaṅkaḷil pavaḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷ civappu niṟam vāynta pavaḷatti virumpuvar.
kuṇam
kōvik kaṉi pōṉṟa niṟam, viruttam (vaṭṭam) nīḷam, mēṭu paḷḷamiṉmi, maḻamaḻappu, vaṭuviṉmi, periyavi - pavaḷattiṉ ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟṟam
uḷḷē tuḷi, vaḷivu, kal uṭiyavi kuṟṟam uṭiya pavaḷamākum. veṇmi, cāmpal niṟam, kurūrat tōṟṟam, citivu paṭṭiruttal, tuḷiyāy iruttal, kaṉamiṉmi, tūya veṇmi, cora corappu, veḷuppu kōṇal ivi kuṟṟamākum.
maruttuva nūlil pavaḷam 
pavaḷa pacuppam curatōcam, kācam, iratta pittam, iratta cūli, iratta picāram, pirataranōy, malapantam mutaliya piṇikaḷip pōkkum. itip pala aṉupāṉa māṟṟattōṭu cāppiṭṭāl eṇṇiṟanta nōykaḷ nīṅkum.
cōtiṭa nūlil pavaḷam
cevvāykkup pavaḷam uriyatu. cevvāy uccamāṉavarkaḷum cevvāy tōcam uḷḷavarkaḷum pavaḷam aṇintāl nalamuṟu vārkaḷ eṉpar cōtiṭa vallunar.
kuṟippukaḷ
viramaṇi vaṇikattil nalla vallunar eṉap pukaḻ peṟṟa ciṅkappūr nipuṇar keṉṉat pullarṭṭaṉ viraṅkaḷiṉ taraṅkaḷ paṟṟik kūṟumpoḻutu, potuvāka viram ataṉ niṟam, teḷivu (tūymi), veṭṭu ākiyavikaḷē ataṉ tarattik kāṭṭum eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
uyarnta viram 
nērttiyāṉa iḷañcivappu niṟam uḷḷatu vili uyarntatu. atu kiṭippatu aritiṉum aritu. peritum iyalpāṉa nīla veḷḷi niṟa viraṅkaḷum vili uyarntavitām. tūya veḷḷi viraṅkaḷ aṭuttavi.
maṭṭa viram
ciṟitu mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta viramaṇikaḷ taraṅ kuṟintaṉavākum. āṉāl cila uṇmiyāṉa mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta viraṅkaḷ vili mikuntaṉa.
tūymiyik kaṇṭupiṭippatu
pūtak kaṇṇāṭi mūlam virattil uḷḷa veṭippukaḷum miṉukkamum kāṇamuṭiyum. virattiṉ veṭṭu mūlam miṉukkatti aḷaviṭa muṭiyum.
paṭṭaṅkaḷ (paṭṭikaḷ)
potuvāka oru viramaṇiyil 58 paṭṭaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. mēlē 33 kīḻē 25. antap paṭṭikaḷ cariyāṉa kōṇaṅ kaḷil illāviṭṭāl oḷi cariyāka virintu paravātu. ataṉāl oḷi kuṉṟum - atāvatu paḷapaḷappu illāmaṟ pōkum.
virattil tūymiyiviṭa niṟantāṉ mukkiyam, tūya mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta viramaṇiyiviṭa ilēcāṉa kōṭukaḷ uḷḷa oru veḷḷi viratti nāṉ peritum virumpuvēṉ eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār.1
1. 	(a) utpalaparimaḷam
2.	The Diamand - W. R. Catelle. New York. 1911
3.	The Story of the Gems - Herbert. P. White lock. London. P. 68
	“Like almost every other activity this ministers to the comfort of man and gratifies his taste for the beautiful; the search for and retrieving of diamonds from the earth has had a history in this an ancient interesting and romantic one - As far as we knew diamonds were first found in central India, thus carrying out the tradition that very many of the precious and semiprecious stones Originate in the Orient.”
4.	viramum ataṉ kuṇā kuṇaṅkaḷum - eyc. nārāyaṇarāv, pi.eṉ. appucāmi aiyar (kaṭṭuri - kalki)
5.	Diamond and Precious Stones - E. Jean (Paris - 1865) P. 67
	“The ancient diamond workings or India were scattered over quite a wide area but the distributing centre or mart was the town of Golconda, a name which has always been highly suggestive of opulence. As far back as that ancient epic, the Mahabharata, diamonds have figured largely in the life and history of the Hindoos and it was from India that they were introduced into Europe.”
6.	The Gemmologists compendium - Robert. Webster. F.G.A. London. 1937 P. 67
	“Koh - I - Nur Diamond; a historical diamond, sid to be an Indian stone. Originally sid to weigh 1911 metric carates, it now weighs 108.9 metric carats, the stone having been recut in 1862. The Koh - i - Nur is now set in the centre of the front cross pate of the crown of the Queen of England”
7.	World’s most famous Gems have link with India - K. Neelkant (Sunday Standard, The,. 11-4-65)
8.	 tiṅkaḷ - (tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭu) navarattiṉaṅkaḷ ci. rājappā, ceṉṉi, ē - 57)
9.	navarattiṉaṅkaḷ - cēviyar ṭēviṭ (tamiḻ malar 21-11-65 ciṅkappūr)
	Diamond Ruapsody (Illustrated weekly of India) Bombay 
7-2-54.
10.	navamaṇikaḷ - ṭi.e. vittiyanātaṉ. 1959.
11.	irattiṉa parīṭci - caravatimakāl veḷiyīṭu, 1958
12.	patārttakuṇacintāmaṇi.
13.	cilappatikāram - ūrcūḻ vari, 188		tiruviḷiyāṭaṟpurāṇam
14.	patārtta kuṇa cintāmaṇi
	vātapitta tōṭatti māṟṟumalak kaṭṭaṟukkum
	ōtuman tākkiṉiyi ōṭṭuṅkāṇ - mītil
	aḻaltaru kāram akaṟṟum oḷi ceyyum
	kaḻaṟukiṉṟa kōmē takam

8. pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ
tali, kātu, kaḻuttu aṇikaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ, ulōkak kālatti aṭinta poḻutu tāṉ, avarkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu, īyam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷāl uṇavup poruḷkaḷip perukkuvataṟkuriya koḻu, arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, kōṭāri, kūntālam, uḷi, vāḷ pōṉṟa karuvikaḷi mutalil uruvākkiṉar. appāl piṟa toḻil ceyvataṟkuriya cuttiyal, kuṟaṭu, paṟṟukkuṟaṭu, cāmaṇam, poṭi veṭṭi aram, kivāḷ, kuḻal, āṭutamar, nūl caṭṭam mutaliyavikaḷi uruvāk kiṉar. ataṉ piṉṉartāṉ poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyya muṟpaṭṭaṉar.
makkaḷ, vayiṟāra uṇṭu kaḷitta piṉṉartāṉ, taṅkaḷi aḻaku ceytu koḷḷa nāṭip poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytu aṇiya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. antap poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum tamiḻ makkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa malar aṇiyākavum arumpu aṇiyākavum kāy aṇiyākavumē pariṇamittaṉa.
itippaṟṟi āḻntu ārāyntu aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nila vāḻkkiyil malar aṇikaḷiyum arumpu aṇikaḷiyum kaṇṭaṉar. mulli nila vāḻkkiyilē ili, taḻi, ōli, koṭi mutaliyavikaḷi aṇiyāka aṇintuvantaṉar. maruta nila vāḻkkiyilē malar, arumpu, ili, ōli, koṭi, piñcu, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭi pōṉṟavikaḷi aṇikaḷāka aṇintu vantaṉar. piṉ, maruta nilamākkaḷē nel māli, miḷaku māli, kottamalli māli, kuṉṟimaṇi māli, marutaṅkāy māli, nellikkāy māli, māmpiñcuk kolucu, picci arumpu māli, kaṉṉap pū, cuṇṭikkāy māli, uruttirākka māli, tāmarik kāy māli pōṉṟavikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. neytal nilavāḻkkiyilē cippi cōvi, caṅku kōvañci, muttu, pavaḷam mutaliyavikaḷik kātaṇiyākavum, kiyaṇi yākavum kaḻuttaṇiyākavum taliyaṇiyākavum aṇintu vantaṉar.
makkaḷ maṇṇālum, marattālum, kallālum aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyamuṟpaṭṭaṉar. maruntup poruḷkaḷāl kūṭa uruṇṭi yāṉa maṇikaḷ ceytu mālikaḷākak kōttut kaḻuttil aṇin taṉar. elumpukaḷālum kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa kallukaḷālum mālikaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. cemmaṇṇālum, kallā lum, maruntālum, paḷiṅku pōṉṟa kallālum ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikaḷ cila eṉṉiṭam uḷḷaṉa. avi cumār 3000 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷaṉavāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āṉāl, tamiḻakattil ulōka kālattilētāṉ uyarnta nākarikam untiyatu; ariya kalikaḷ arumpiṉa. ōli, ili, kuḻi, kompu, koṭi, arumpu, pū, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭi, caṅku, cippi vaṭivākavē ulōkaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. avikaḷiṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ poṉ aṇikaḷil tikaḻumāṟu marakatam (pacci) nīlam (nīlam) viram (veḷḷi) māṇikkam (veḷuriya civappu) puṭparākam (mañcaḷ) pavaḷam (karuñcivappu) mutaliya maṇikaḷip patittu ili, malar, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭi mutaliyavikaḷiṉ vaṇṇam oḷirumpaṭi aṇi kalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇintaṉar. ellāvaṟṟiyumviṭac ciṟappu anta aṇikaḷukku ili, pū, kāy eṉṟa anta ātippeyari maṟavātu cūṭṭi viṭṭatākum. itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kalivaḷarcciyiyum kalippaṇpiyum kāṭṭuvatākum.
iṉṟu tamiḻakattil ettaṉiyō nākarikaṅkaḷ vantu muṭṭi mōti vantuḷḷaṉa. eṉṟālum tamiḻaka makaḷir aṇintu vanta naṉiciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyari acikka muṭiyavilli. paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṇṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarum uruvum tiruvum māṟā vaṇṇam iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ aṇintu vanta ātikāla aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, aṭiyil varumāṟākum :
1. taliyaṇi
tāḻampū, tāmarippū, corukuppū, cāmantip pū, aṭukku mallikippū, ili, aracili, patumam, caram pūrappāḷi, kōti, valampuri.
2. kātaṇi
tōṭu, koppu, ōli, kuḻi, ili, kuvaḷi, kontiḷa vōli, kaṉṉappū, muruku, viciṟi muruku, ciṉṉappū, valliki, cevippū, maṭal.
3. kaḻuttaṇikaḷ
kottu, koṭi, tālikkoṭi, kottamalli māli, miḷaku māli, picciyarumpu māli, nellikkāy māli, marutaṅkāy māli, cuṇṭakkāy māli, kaṭumaṇi māli, tāḻampū aṭṭiki, māṅkāy māli, mātuḷaṅkāy māli, kārippū aṭṭiki, arumpuccaram, malarccaram, kaṇṭa caram, kaṇṭamāli, kōti māli, kōvi pōṉṟaṉa.
4. puya aṇikalaṉkaḷ
kontikkāy
5.ki aṇikalaṉkaḷ.
kāppū, kontikkāyk kolucu
6. kiviral aṇikalaṉkaḷ
civantippū mōtiram, arumpu, vaṭṭappū.
7. kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ
māmpiñcuk kolucu, attikkāyk kolucu, ālaṅkāyk kolucu pōṉṟaṉa.
ivaṟṟil navamaṇikaḷum patikkappeṟṟaṉa. nākarikam vaḷara, vaḷara ovvoru uṟuppilum palvēṟu aṇikaḷ palvēṟu muṟi yil apiviruttiyuṟṟum vaḷarccipeṟṟum vantuḷḷaṉa. iḵtaṉṟi caṅkuvaḷi, pulippaltāli, pulinakattāli pōṉṟavikaḷum uṇṭu.
iṉṟiyat tamiḻaka makaḷiriviṭa muṟkāla makaḷir aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi viḷiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patitta, ariya vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟinta aṇikalaṉkaḷiyellām aṇintu vantaṉar. eṉavē paṇṭikkālat tamiḻnāṭṭup pūviyar namatu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil āyiḻiyār eṉṟu kūṟap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉar. itaṉāl peṇkaḷ aṇikalaṉ peyarāl aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. tamiḻ makaḷir muṟṟuppeṟṟa vēlip pāṭukaḷiyuṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ muṟṟiḻi eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟṟaṉa.
kōttu aṇintu koḷvati kōvi eṉṟaṉar (maṇik kōvi). toṭuttu aṇivatit toṭi eṉṟu moḻintaṉar. acintu āṭattakkati māli (āram) eṉṟaṉar (nellikkāy māli). oru taramāy oḻuṅkuṟa amintu koṇṭati caram eṉṟaṉar (muttuccaram, vaṉṉacaram). koṭikaḷi muṟukki aṇivatik koṭi eṉṟaṉar (pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam). vaṭivam paṟṟi ili, kuḻi eṉṟaṉar (aracili, poṟkuḻi). ōli pōṉṟavaṟṟi ōli eṉṟaṉar (poṉṉōli). kāppāka aṇi vatik kāppu eṉṟaṉar. (poṟkāppu). taṭiyāka aṇivatit taṭiyam eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. kiṇkiṇ eṉṟu olippatik kiṇkiṇi eṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇiyākat toṭuttu aṇivatik kaṇṇi eṉṟaṉar (poṟkaṇṇi).
ātikālattil caṅkukaḷāl mōtiraṅkaḷum vaḷikaḷum (kāppukaḷum) kuḻikaḷum (kātaṇikaḷum) aṇintu vantaṉar. piṟkālattil ulōkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyatum ulōka aṇikaḷukkum avvaṭivam paṟṟi valampuriccaṅku, poṉvaḷi, poṟkuḻi, poṟṟoṭi eṉap peyar vittuḷḷaṉar.
tali aṇikalaṉkaḷ
1. makuṭaṅkaḷ
1. kirīṭa makuṭam 2. karaṇṭa makuṭam 3. maṇi makuṭam (irattiṉa makuṭam) 4. caṭā makuṭam
talimītu aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷ taliyaṇi eṉap paṭum. makkaḷ uṭampil, talimika mukkiya uṟuppākiyiṉāl talimītu aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷum ciṟappuṭiyaṉavāka matikkappeṟṟaṉa. taliyil viliyuyarnta aṇikaḷiyē aṇivar. poṉṉum maṇiyum iṭam peṟuvataṟkuriya ciṟappāṉa iṭam - mutal iṭam taliyēyākum.
tamiḻarkaḷil, āṇ makkaḷ taliyiṉ makuṭattit tavira vēṟu taliyaṇi etuvum aṇintatākat teriyavilli. makuṭam maṉṉarkaḷtām  aṇivārkaḷēyaṉṟi piṟar aṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. paṇṭik kālat tamiḻ āṇ makkaḷ taliyil caṭāmakuṭattiyum talippāki pōṉṟavikaḷiyum tavira vēṟu poṉ aṇikaḷ etuvum aṇintatāka namakkut teriyavilli. āṉāl paṇṭik kālattil āṇ makkaḷ peṇ makkaḷippōl taliyil muṭi vittiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat terikiṟatu.
āṉāl, āṇ makkaḷ taliyaṇi maṭṭuntāṉ aṇiya villiyēyoḻiya kaḻuttil mālikaḷum kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum puyam, ki, iṭuppu, toṭi, kāl pātam mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku teri kiṟatu.
āṉāl āṇ makkaḷ taliyaṇi maṭṭuntāṉ aṇiya villiyēyoḻiya kaḻuttil mālikaḷum kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum puyam, ki, iṭuppu, toṭi, kāl pātam mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku teri kiṟatu.
makuṭam
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉiruntu tamiḻarkaḷ taliyil ētāvatu aṇintu varuvatu marapāka iruntu varukiṟatu. ātiyil taṭpaveṭpa nili, mūḷiyip pātikkāmal iruppataṟkākat taliyi, ili, tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl maṟittirukkalām. mūḷi irukkum pakutiyākiya tali pātukāppāka irukkumpaṭi atic cuṟṟi valuvāṉa maṇṭiyōṭu iyaṟkiyilē amintirukkiṟatu. anta maṇṭi ōṭum eḷitil tākkuṟāmal irukkavē mēl tōlum ataṉ mēl aṭarttiyāṉa mayirkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ ataṟku iṉṉum pātukāppu aḷippataṟkākak koṇṭi, caṭāmakuṭam, caṭāpāram, caṭā maṇṭalam mutaliyavikaḷi amittaṉar. avikaḷum ñāyiṟu veppatti īrppati eṇṇi talippāki (ciratarakam) kaṇṭaṉar. ēṉ? aḷakacūṭam, tammillam mutaliyavikaḷiyum kaṇṭaṉar. makkaḷ nākarikam eytiya piṉ, maṉṉarkaḷ makuṭam amittaṉar. anta makuṭaṅkaḷi, kalippulami vāynta ciṟpikaḷ poṉṉālum maṇikaḷālum ceytu nāluvakiyāka amittaṉar. avikaḷāvaṉa :
(1) kirīṭa makuṭam (2) karaṇṭa makuṭam (3) irattiṉa makuṭam (4) caṭāmakuṭam eṉappaṭum.
makuṭam ātiyil taliyip pātukākkum kavacamāka eḻuntu, appāl aḻaku ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟāka malarntuḷḷatu. piṉ atu teyvaṅkaḷukkum maṉṉarkaḷukkum uriya ciṟappāṉa ciṉṉaṅkaḷil oṉṟākat tikaḻntatu. namatu tamiḻ nikaṇṭil iṟivaṉukkuriya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟil, makuṭam (muṭiyum) oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.2
muṭi - atāvatu makuṭam taliyaṇiyāyt tōṉṟi appāl teyvaṅkaḷukkum pēraracarkaḷukkum uriya poruḷāyp pariṇamit tuḷḷatu. muṭiyip peritum ciṟṟaracarkaḷ aṇiyārkaḷ. pēraracar kaḷē aṇivārkaḷ. aracarkaḷiṉ perumiyik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu muṭiyuṭiyamaṉṉarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum. muṭi - atāvatu makuṭam ovvoru maṉṉarum ovvoru vitamāka aṇintu vantaṉar. itu nāṭṭiṟku nāṭu, kālattiṟkuk kālam vēṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. itu kirīṭam, maṇi makuṭam, muṭi, muli, cirōṉmaṇi eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum.
nālvaki makuṭaṅkaḷil kirīṭa makuṭamum irattiṉa makuṭamum tirumāl mutaliya pōka mūrttikaḷukkum, karaṇṭa makuṭam piḷḷiyār mutaliya kaṇaṅkaḷiṉ talimit teyvaṅkaḷukkum caṭāmakuṭam civaṉ, nāṉmukaṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷukkum uriyaṉa.
vīravallavar eḻutiya parata cāttiram mūṉṟuvita makuṭaṅ kaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. avi (1) pārcavākatam (pārcava makuṭam) (2) mataki (3) kiritti eṉappaṭum.
potuvākat teyvaṅkaḷum kantarvarkaḷ, yākarkaḷ, nākarkaḷ (paṉṉakarkaḷ) arakkarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷum pārcavamakuṭam tarippārkaḷ. eṉavē nāṭakattil avvēṭam pūṇṭu naṭippavar kaḷum pārcava makuṭatti aṇiya vēṇṭum.
kiruttik eṉṉum kirīṭatti uyarnta teyvaṅkaḷum, mataki eṉṉum makuṭatti naṭuttarat teyvaṅkaḷum aṇiyum. eṉavē inta teyvaṅkaḷiṉ vēṭampuṉipavarkaḷ ivviraṇṭu makuṭat tiyum aṇiya vēṇṭum. cātāraṇat teyva vēṭam puṉivarkaḷ pārcava makuṭatti aṇiyalām.
maṉṉarkaḷ mataki mātiriyāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷi aṇital vēṇṭum. vittiyātararkaḷ cittarkaḷ, kāraṇarkaḷ talimayirāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kēca makuṭam aṇiyalām.
amiccar, taḷapati, vaṇikak kuḻuviṉ talivarkaḷ kurukkaḷ pōṉṟōr talippāki aṇivatāl avvēṭam puṉipavar kaḷum talippāki aṇiyalām.  taḷapatikaḷum iḷavaracarkaḷum ciṟumakuṭam (artta makuṭam) aṇivar; muṉivarkaḷ caṭā makuṭam aṇivar. ivvēṭam puṉipavarkaḷum immakuṭaṅkaḷ aṇiya vēṇṭum.
maṇimakuṭaṅkaḷum, māmakuṭaṅkaḷum maṉṉarkaḷālum teyvaṅkaḷālum kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉpē tamiḻakattil aṇiyap paṭṭiruppatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. teyvaṅkaḷukkuriya aṇiyil makuṭam iṉṟiyamiyātatu. inta makuṭam ovvoru nāṭṭilum ovvoru vitamāy avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu, āṟṟal, celvam, kali mutaliyavikaḷukkēṟpa amintirukkiṟatu. tamiḻakattil cērarkaḷ, cōḻarkaḷ pallavarkaḷ mutaliya maṉṉarkaḷ vevvēṟu vitamāṉa muṭikaḷip puṉintuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nāṭṭil eḻunta teyvaṅ kaḷum vevvēṟu vitamāṉa aḻakiya makuṭaṅkaḷōṭu viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟṟaṇṇal vāyil, acantā, cikiriyā pārkūt mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷilum maturi, tañci, kāñci, māmalla puram, amarāpati, kāñci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷi lum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷiyum taliyaṇikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
tirumāl intiraṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum vēṟu pala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum aṭiparuttu nuṉiciṟutta aḻakiya vēlippāṭuḷḷa muṭikaḷip puṉintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. piṟkālattil ellāt teyvaṅkaḷum oru niyatikkuṭpaṭṭuc ciṟcila vitamāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷi aṇintiruppatāka aṟikiṉṟōm.
tamiḻakattiṉ makuṭaṅkaḷ pala ariyavēlippāṭamintaṉa vāy nalla vaḷarcciyip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. nām pallavarkaḷ kālattiliruntu iṉṟuvari, atāvatu ki.pi. 6vatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari makuṭaṅkaḷiṉ apiviruttiyi ārāyntāl pala vicayaṅkaḷi aṟiyalām. ovvoru kālamum ataṉ apiviruttiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvatāy amintuḷḷatu. ataṉuṭiya aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷiṉ eḷimi ataṉuṭiya paḻiya paṇpāṭṭiṟku oru cōtaṉiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvitat tōṟṟam ataṉ kālatti nirṇayikkattakka karuviyāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu. makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ amcaṅkaḷ aṉittum ammakuṭaṅkaḷil naṉku piratipalikkiṉṟaṉa.
1. ki)rīṭa makuṭam
ik kirīṭa makuṭam mūṅkil ilipōṉṟu tikaḻum. iḵtiṉṟi ciṟparattiṉam eṉṉum nūliṉ ilakkaṇa vitiyi aṉucarittu muṭṭi vaṭivamākavum irukkum. tāmari muki, kuṭi, āmi, vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. immakuṭam poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟu viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟirukkum. nālu pakkaṅkaḷilum makara uruvattōṭu koṭikaḷum ilikaḷum taḷir kaḷum pūkkaḷum arumpukaḷum aṇi ceyyum. muttuk kōvi kaḷum viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷum iṭam peṟṟilaṅkum. neṟṟikku mēl uṭaṉaṭiyāy makuṭattic cuṟṟi akaṉṟa oru paṭṭapantam irukkum. makuṭattiṉ maṟṟap pakutikaḷ muli pantaṅkaḷālum ceṭi koṭikaḷālum eḻil peṟṟilaṅkum. makuṭattiṉ aṭip pākam iḷam piṟiyip pōṉṟu vaḷintirukkum mutal vaki tirumāliṉ paṭimaṅkaḷilum cakkiravarttikaḷiṭattilum kāṇap paṭum.
immakuṭam 16, 18, 24 aṅkulam  eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyil amiyum. ivvāṟāka amikkappaṭum aḻakiya kirīṭa makuṭattil ucci (cikaram) mika aḻakāka ilaṅkum. ivi 3, 5, 7, eṇṇikki yuḷḷa kumiḻkaḷ pōṉṟa uṟuppukaḷuṭaṉ tikaḻum. paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ amippukku ēṟṟavāṟu eṇṇikkikaḷ kuṟintum kūṭiyum irukkum. inta kirīṭa makuṭam, muṭṭi (aṇṭam) vaṭivamākavō, tāmari malar, kuṭi, āmi pōṉṟa vaṭivamākavō amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu ciṟpa vitikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
inta makuṭaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappi vittu ati aṇintirukkum teyvam allatu aracaṉiṉ āṟṟal, perumi mutaliyaṉa teriyak kūṭum. intak kirīṭa makuṭattil ulakil uyarnta viram, māṇikkam, muttu marakatam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkum. maṉṉaṉiṉ mariyāti, māṉam, kuravam ellām inta makuṭattilē aṭaṅki irukkum.
2. karaṇṭa makuṭam
karaṇṭa makuṭam aṭiyiṉiṉṟu muṭivari paṭippaṭiyāka cuṟṟaḷavu kuṟintu koṇṭē pōy makuṭattiṉ nuṉi, mokku (arumpu) pōṉṟu kuvintu kāṇappaṭum itu 3, 4, 5 karaṇṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷatāka irukkum. maṟṟavi kirīṭamakuṭam pōṉṟu irukkum.
teḷivākak kūṟuvatāṉāl karaṇṭa makuṭam oru karaṇṭaka vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru muṭi eṉṟu kūṟalām. itu oru kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatāka irukkum. itu neṭṭi yākavō allatu kuṭṭiyākavō allatu oru pakkam kuvin tirukkāmalō aḻakāka irukkum. itu tōṟṟattil mikac ciṟi tākavē kāṇappaṭum. pala karaṇṭaṅkaḷ aṭiyum nuṉiyum oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. pāttira aṭukkukaḷip pōl oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟāka 3,5,7 varicikaḷākap poṟittiruppatu ataṉ uruvatti ciṟitāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu. intak karaṇṭa makuṭaṅkaḷ perumpālum tēvikaḷ, piḷḷiyār, iḷamurukaṉ, aṉumāṉ mutaliya teyvaṅ kaḷiṭattilum pēraracarkaḷukkuk kīḻpaṭṭa kuṟu nila maṉṉarkaḷiṭat tilum kāṇappaṭum. intak karaṇṭa makuṭa vakikaḷi vēṟu eṅkum kāṇamuṭiyātu. itu tamiḻakattiṟkē oru ciṟappāka aminta makuṭamāka eṇṇappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
intak karaṇṭa makuṭamum poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatē yākum. itilum viliyuyarnta viram marakatam māṇikkam marakata nīlam pōṉṟa palvēṟu vaṇṇak kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭirukkum. itu pārvikkuc ciṟitākak kāṇappaṭṭālum eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻum. itaṉ ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ piṟamakuṭaṅkaḷiviṭa eḷḷaḷavum kuṟintaṉavalla.
3. irattiṉa makuṭam
irattiṉa makuṭam eṉṟāl maṇikaḷ patitta aṇi makuṭam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. itu karaṇṭa makuṭatti viṭa uyaramākavum aḻakākavum irukkum. immakuṭam poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭu oṉpatu vitamāṉa maṇikaḷ aḻuttappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. itu atika māṉa maṇikaḷ iḻittuc ceyyappaṭṭatāl irattiṉa makuṭam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu. irattiṉa makuṭam civaperumāṉ pōṉṟa yōka muṟiyit taḻuvi niṟkum teyvattiṟkuriyataṉṟu. pōka muṟiyi nāṭum tirumāl, tēvikaḷ pōṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷ aṇiya lām. maṉṉarkaḷukku irattiṉa makuṭam ēṟṟatu.
makuṭattiṉ naṭuvil muṉpakkam periya viram allatu māṇikkam allatu marakatam vittu irukkalām. atu ellā vaṟṟiyumviṭa periyatāy ñāyiṟu pōṉṟu tikaḻum. itic cuṟṟic ceṭi koṭikaḷum malarkaḷum kāykaṉikaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟu iṭi iṭiyē maṇikaḷ patippikkappeṟ ṟirukkum.
inta makuṭaṅkaḷ maṉṉarkaḷiṉ celvattiyum valimiyi yum perumiyiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭuvaṉavāka irukkum. makuṭaṅ kaḷukkākap pala pōrkaḷ nikaḻntatuṇṭu. eṇṇaṟṟa uyirkaḷi oru makuṭattiṟkākap paliyiṭṭatumuṇṭu.
tamiḻakattiṟku urittāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟip pallavarkaḷiṉ maṇimakuṭaṅkaḷum eṅku ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nammāl kaṇṭupiṭikkavē muṭiyavilli. avikaḷil etuvum nammi iṟutiyāka āṇṭa āṅkilēyarkaḷiṉ tol poruṭ kāṭci cāliyilō allatu pirañcu, ulāntākkārarkaḷiṉ tol poruḷ kāṭcicāliyilō iruppatākat teriyavilli. avi kaḷik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟu akkari nammiṭam illi. āṉāl namatu varuṅkāla cantatikaḷukkāvatu avvāci varavēṇṭum eṉpatu ematu eṇṇam.
4. caṭāmakuṭam
caṭā makuṭam ulōka kālattiṟku muntiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikattil eḻunta oru taliyaṇiyākum. intac caṭā makuṭam  caṭimuṭiyāl toppi allatu oru muṭiccip pōṉṟu koṇṭi piṉṉappaṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. caṭi muṟkālat til kuṭimallam iliṅka uruvil kāṇappaṭṭatu pōl muṟukkap paṭṭa muṭiyāka allāmal piṉṉappaṭṭa muṭiyākak kāṇappaṭum; itu piṟkālattil muṟukkiya muṭiyākiviṭṭatu. ākamaṅkaḷ aṉit tilum  kuṟippāka uttara kāmikākamattilum itaṉ viḷakkam kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ivvitamāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷ pala cakkaraṅkaḷ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. avvaṇikaḷ makara kuṭam, pattirakuṭam, irattiṉakuṭam, puritaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. makarakuṭam eṉpatu muṉpakkattil aṇi ceyyappaṭavēṇṭum. eñciya ēṉiya pakutikaḷil allatu nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum puritaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē iṭam peṟalām, puritaṅkaḷum allatu makara vaṭivaṅkaḷum ceṭi, koṭi, ili, pū, kāy, uruvaṅkaḷum iṭatu pakkattil allatu valatu pakkattil maṭṭum irukkalām; mēlē irukkakkūṭātu. makarakuṭam muṉ pakkattilum pattirakuṭam allatu irattiṉakuṭam piṉ pakkattilum iṭam peṟṟirukkalām.5
caṭāmakuṭam civaperumāṉukkē uriyatu. caṭā makuṭattiṉ valatu pakkattil veḷ erukkam pū, pāmpu, kaṅkātēvi uruvaṅ kaḷum iṭatu pakkattil iḷam piṟi uruvamum kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭum. itaṉ mūlam civaperumāṉ ātikālattil atāvatu ulōka kālam arumpummuṉ atāvatu kaṟkālattil eḻunta paḻam perum teyvam eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu. arappā nākarika mum civaperumāṉ 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē - āriyarallātār kaṇṭa perunteyvam eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttu kiṟatu.
tali aṇikalaṅkaḷ (ā)

15. cuṭiki 16. cuṭṭi 17. curitam 18. cūṭiki 19. cūṭāmaṇi 20. cūṭi 21. cūṭṭu 22. cūriyappirapi 23. cūḻi 24. cūḷāmaṇi 25. cēkaram
