29. teyva utti 30. paṭṭam 31. piṟi 32. pullakam 33. polam pūntumpi 34. poṟpū 35. poṟṟāmarippū 36. poṉvāki 
37. poṉṉarimāli 38. mārāṭṭam 39. mukacaram 40. muñcam 
41. vayantakam 32. valampuri.
1. taliyaṇi vakikaḷ (ā) (makaḷir aṇikalaṉkaḷ)
1. aracili
aracili vaṭivākap poṟṟakaṭṭāl ceytu taliyil aṇiyum oru aṇikalaṉ. itu paḻaṅkālat tamiḻar aṇipōl tikaḻkiṟatu.
2. irākkuṭi
taliyiṉ  ucciyil piṉpuṟam nōkki aṇiyappaṭum tali aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. itu poṉṉāl takaṭu vaṭivākac ceyyap paṭṭu aṭiyil veḷḷittakaṭu poruttappaṭṭuk kīḻē maṭiyum vikkappaṭṭirukkum. itaṉ uṭpuṟam pukalākac ceyyappaṭṭu meḻuku vikkappaṭṭirukkum. poṉ takaṭṭiṉ mītu ariya cittira vēlippāṭukaḷ eḻilpeṟṟilaṅkum. itaṉik kottumāṉa vēliyiṟ ciṟanta poṟkollarkaḷē ceyvar. irākkuṭiyuṭaṉ piṟi eṉṉum aṇiyum iṇittē aṇivar. atuvum itip pōṉṟa vēlippāṭu ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. irākkuṭiyum, piṟiyum ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum pōl tikaḻum. itu caṭāṅkattiṟku mēl aṇiyappaṭuvatu mikap poruttamāṉatāka irukkiṟatu.
3. ilampakam
ilampakam taliyil aṇiyap peṟṟu nutalil toṅkum oru aṇi. ataṉāl itu nutalaṇiyākavum eṇṇappaṭum. itaṉi āṭal makaḷir virumpi aṇivar. avarkaḷ aṇintuḷḷa tali aṇikaḷiṉiṉṟu ilampakam neṟṟikku nērākat toṅkum. itu patakkam pōṉṟu aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkuvatōṭu aṭiyil ciṟu poṟ cataṅkikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭirukkum.
4. ili
ili eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟuc civappu veḷḷi pacci maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟum oru ciṟu tali aṇiyākum.
5. kaṭiki
pavaḷattāl ceyyappaṭṭa tali aṇikaḷil oṉṟu. kaḻuttil aṇiyum kaṭikiyāram vēṟu; taliyil aṇiyum kaṭiki vēṟu.
6. kaṉṉacaram
kaṉṉacaram tali aṇikalaṉ eṉṟum taliccāmāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum. ivvaṇikaḷ neṟṟiyic cuṟṟiyum iru kātukaḷiṉiṉṟu neṟṟikku mēl ari vaṭṭamākat tali muṭiyiṉmītu aṇivataṟ keṉap poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟa aṇikalaṉākum. muṟkālattil itil viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ aḻuttap peṟṟiruntaṉa. iṉṟu pōlik kaṟkaḷē perumpālum iṭam peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṉṉacarattiṉ oru nuṉi valatu kātiṉ mēluḷḷa muṭiyil piṇikkappaṭṭirukkum. maṟṟoru nuṉi iṭatu kātiṉ mēluḷḷa muṭiyil piṇikkappaṭṭirukkum. ivi ciṟuciṟu caturap peṭṭikaḷ pōl ceyyappeṟṟu variciyākac caṅkilit toṭar pōl piṇikkappaṭṭirukkum.  ovvoru caturattilum kaṟkaḷ patikkappeṟṟirukkum. aṭiyil ciṟu kaṟkaḷum mēlē ciṟitum peritumāṉa kuṇṭukaḷum poruttap peṟ ṟirukkum. kīḻē ovvoru caturak kaṭṭattiṉ kīḻum poṉṉāl ili pōṉṟa oru vaṭivamum naṭuvē oru maṇiyum iṭiyiṭiyē uruṇṭi vaṭivamāṉa kuṇṭukaḷum nuṉiyil cataṅki pōṉṟa ciṟu poṉ maṇikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tali naṭuvil caṅkili pōl, ucciyiliruntu neṟṟi vari oru poṉcaṅkili paṭṭipōl iṇikkappaṭṭirukkum. iti vakuppuccaram eṉpar. itilum kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum. muṉṉar kaṟkaḷiṉṟip poṉṉālē ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. cilavaṟṟil muttukkaḷum  patikkap peṟṟirukkum iv vaṇiyiṉ naṭuvil neṟṟiyiṉ miyattil mūkkiṟku nērāka mēlē oru patakkam toṅkum. itu piṟi vaṭivil irukkum. itil kaṟkaḷ patikkappeṟṟirukkum. naṭuvē oru periya kal iṭam peṟṟirukkum. kīḻē cuṟṟilum poṟ cataṅkikaḷ toṅkum. itic cintāmaṇi eṉpārumuḷar. ivvaṇikalaṉkaḷukku mēl taliyil valatu pakkattil vaṭṭamāṉa oru kal villi aṇiyappaṭum. atic cūriyappiṟi eṉpar. iṭatu pakkam piṟi vaṭivamāṉa kallaṇi aṇiyappaṭum. atic cantirappiṟi eṉpar. ivi iraṇ ṭilum civappu veḷḷi pacci niṟa maṇikaḷ pala aḻuttap peṟṟirukkum.
inta aṇikaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ ākiya kōḷkaḷiṉ  tōcaṅ kaḷi akaṟṟum eṉpatu paṇṭikkālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nampikki. iḵtaṉṟi iru paṟavikaḷ atāvatu - aṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭivil uḷḷa taliccāmāṉkaḷum uṇṭu.
taliccāmāṉkaḷil iṭatu pakkamum valatu pakkamum cerukappaṭuvatu campu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. itu oruvakic cālar aminta aṇikalaṉākum.
inta aṇikalaṉkaḷi inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattil peṇkaḷ maṇakkōlattiṉpōtu aṇintu vantaṉar. āṉāl āṭaṟkaliyil īṭupaṭṭa peṇkaḷ ivvaṇikaḷi vēta viti pōl eṇṇi nēṟṟu vari aṇintu vantaṉar. ippoḻutu iv vaṇiyi nāṭṭiyak kaliyil īṭupaṭṭa peṇkaḷiṟ palar aṇiva tilli. ceṉṉi kalāccēttirattiṉ niṟuviyār tirumati urukku maṇi aruṇṭēl tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ paṇpi maṟavātu inta aṇi kalaṉi aṇintu āṭi vantaṉar.
camīpakālam vari īḻa nāṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷ taṅkaḷ tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷil iti eḻilpeṟa aṇintu vantaṉar. itu annāṭṭu makaḷiriṉ aḻaki mēlum uyarttik kāṭṭiyatu. ciṅkaḷa mātarkaḷi ṉiṉṟum avarkaḷ taṉic ciṟappuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu uyarttik kāṭṭiyatu. iṉṟu avarka ḷiṭamiruntu ivvaṇi naḻuva ārampittu varukiṟatu.
7. kuccam
pala poṉ maṇikaḷ kōviyāy iṇitta oru taliyaṇi poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu iṭik kālattil eḻuntatu. iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
8. kuñcam
caṭiyiṉ mītu caṭāṅkam vittuk kaṭṭap peṟṟu caṭiyiṉ nuṉiyil kuñcam vittuk kaṭṭappaṭum. itu mūṉṟu pantukaḷ pōl ceyyappeṟṟu aṭiyil kuñcan toṅkum. mēlē poṉ pūṇpotiyap peṟṟirukkum.
9. koṇṭit tiruku
koṇṭit tirukukku mēlē aḻakiya pū vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa poṟṟakaṭṭiṉ aṭiyil veḷḷit takaṭu poruttap paṭṭirukkum. uḷḷē meḻuku aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum. aṭiyil tirukukaḷ irukkum. itaṉ muṭimītu vittuc cuṟṟuvatāl muṭiyi iṟukap paṟṟik koḷḷum. itu koṇṭiyil vittut tirukuva tāl koṇṭit tiruku eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu.
10. kōti
kōti eṉpatu makaḷiriṉ talik kōlattil uḷḷa oru tali aṇiyākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru pūmāli yākum. itaṉ vēlippāṭu mika aṟputamāṉatu. muttuvaṭam kōkkappeṟṟu aḻakuṟum aṇi.
11. caṭāṅkam
caṭāṅkam  eṉpatu caṭiyiṉmītu aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇikalaṉ; pāmpu pōṉṟa oru taliyaṇi. caṭāṅkattiṉ mēlē atāvatu caṭiyiṉ mēl piṭarippakkam aintu tali nākam allatu pāmpu aracaṉ (nākarācaṉ) tali irukkum. kīḻē caṭimītu ataṉuṭiya vāl toṅkuvatupōl caṭi mītirukkum. itu poṉṉāl mikka ki tērnta poṟkollarkaḷāl ceyyap peṟum. peṇkaḷiṉ nīṇṭa tali mayiri caṭiyākap piṉṉi ataṉmītu caṭāṅkam eṉṉum aṇikalaṉic coruki caṭiyiṉ nuṉiyil kuñcatti vittuk kaṭṭuvārkaḷ kuñcattiṉ mēluḷḷa pakutiyic caṭāṅkattiṉ kīḻē iṇittu viṭuvārkaḷ.
kiruṣṇaṉ nākarācaṉi aṭakki avaṉ talimītu kāli vittuk koṇṭum vālip piṭittuk koṇṭum āṭum ceyali itu piratipalippatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. kiruṣṇaṉ ciṉṉañ ciṟu paruvam uḷḷavaṉāyp pāmpiṉ paṭattiṉ mītu kāli ūṉṟik koṇṭu kaḷi naṭam puriyum  kāṭci kaṇ koḷḷāk kāṭciyākat tikaḻum. āṉāl iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum uruvam tirumāl kiruṣṇaṉākat tōṉṟuvatu pōl tīṭṭappeṟṟirukkiṟatu. tirumāliṉ vākaṉattiṉ iṟakkikaḷ naṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. avi oru yāḻiyiṉ taliyōṭu iṇintirukkum. iru pakkamum tirumāliṉ vākaṉamākiya karuṭaṉ tāṅki niṟkiṟatu.
inta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ tirumāliṉ ciṉṉam iṭam peṟṟirun tālum toṉṟu toṭṭu civaṉmītu pakti koṇṭuḷḷa civarkaḷ vēṟṟumi uṇarcciyiṉṟi tārāḷamākat taṅkaḷ illattil payaṉpaṭutti vantārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. pāmpu civaperumā ṉuṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟa uṟutiyāṉa nampikki avarkaḷukku iruntatu pōlum ēṉ? tirumālē civaperumāṉuṭiya mittuṉar tāmē. civaperumāṉukku avaciyam nērum poḻutellām avar kūṭa iruntu varuvatākap purāṇaṅkaḷ ellām kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉa vām.
12. caṭi nākar
itu irākkuṭi vittu aṇi ceyyappaṭum iṭattil ucciyil vittu aṇi ceyyappaṭum. itu irākkuṭi, corukup pū, tirukup pū, cuṭṭi pōṉṟu uḷḷē meḻuku aṭittuc ceyyap peṟṟa poṉ aṇiyākum. inta aṇiyil ēḻu taliyuḷḷa pāmpu taṉatu paṭatti akala virittu aḻakuṭaṉ iruppatupōl ceyyap peṟṟirukkum. iti iṉṟum tamiḻakattup peṇkaḷ virumpi aṇivar. ivvaṇi iṉṟum nelli māvaṭṭattil vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
13. caṭit tiruku
caṭiyil vittut tirukik koḷḷum oru poṉ aṇiyākum. itu tirukup pū, corukup pū pōṉṟa poṉ aṇi. mēlē poṟṟakaṭṭāl ceyyappeṟṟu kīḻē veḷḷittakaṭu poruttap peṟṟu uḷḷē meḻuku vikkappeṟṟirukkum. mēlē uḷḷa poṟṟakaṭṭil nalla kottumāṉa vēlikaḷ ceyyap peṟṟirukkum. itu kiṭṭattaṭṭa oru rūpāy vaṭivil irukkum.
14. cantirap pirapi
itu tiṅkaḷ vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟu maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟatu. taliyiṉ valap puṟattil cūriyap pirapiyum iṭap pakkam cantirap pirapiyum vittu aṇi ceyyappaṭum. itu cantirak kali eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. cantirak kaliyā mēkak karuṅkuḻali eṉṟu piramōttirakāṇṭam kūṟukiṟatu.5
15. cuṭiki
cuṭiki eṉpatu makaḷir talik kōlattil oṉṟu. itu poṉ aṇikalaṉākum. itaṉi neṟṟic cuṭṭi eṉpar.
16. curitakam
curitakam caṭiyil aṇiyappaṭum tirukup pūppōṉṟa vaṭṭamāṉa maṇikaḷ patitta poṉ aṇiyākum. (naṟ. 86 : 5-8)
17. cūṭiki
taliyic cuṟṟi aṇiyum  oru aḻakiya aṇikalaṉākum.
18. cūṭāmaṇi
cūṭāmaṇi aḻakiya taliyaṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu.
19. cūṭi
cūṭiyiṉ maṇi.
20. cūṭṭu
cūṭṭu makaḷir taliyil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. makaḷir taliyic cīvi koṇṭiyik kaṭṭi atil aṇiyum cuṭṭi pōṉṟa poṉ aṇiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu. 6
21. cūriyappirapi
makaḷir taliyiṉ muṉpuṟam, iṭatu pakkattil cantirap pirapiyum, valatu pakkam cūriyap pirapiyum aṇivar. itu poṉṉāṟ ceytu maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum; iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
22. cūḻi
cūḻi, makaḷir aṇiyum tali aṇikaḷil oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. āṉāl ataṟku ātāram illi. āṉāl akanāṉūṟṟil cūḻi eṉpatu yāṉikku aṇivikkappaṭum mukappaṭām  eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.7
23. cūḷāmaṇi
oṉpatu maṇikaḷ iḻittu villiyākac ceyyap peṟṟa oru tali aṇikalaṉākum. ippoḻutu ivvaṇi irattiṉac cuṭṭi eṉa aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.
24. cēkaram
cēkaram poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟa taliyil aṇiyum oru ciṟu aṇikalaṉ. itu piṟkālattatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
25. corukup pū
corukup pū poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu aḻakiya pū vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyap peṟṟatu. taliyil corukum pū vaṭiva māṉa aṇiyātalāl corukup pū eṉappaṭṭatu. itu irākkuṭi, nākar pōṉṟa aṇi.
26. talippāḷi (toyyakam)
talippāḷi, toyyakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum itu oṉpatu vitamāṉa maṇikaḷ aḻuttic ceyyap peṟṟa oru poṉ aṇiyākum. inta aṇikalaṉ tali mayirp piriviṉ naṭuvil aṇiyappaṭuvatāl itu talippāḷi eṉappaṭum. muṟkālattil itaṉip pūrap pāḷi eṉṟum pukalvar. piṟkālattil vāku cuṭṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
27. tāḷampū
tali nākar pōṉṟu mēlē poṟṟakaṭṭil ariya vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuk kīḻē veḷḷittakaṭu poruttap paṭṭa oru ciṟu aṇiyākum. uḷḷē meḻuku aṭikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. kīḻē taliyil piṇikka maṭal vikkappaṭṭirukkum. naṭuvē akaṉṟum iru puṟamum nuṉikaḷ ciṟuttuk kūrākavum irukkum. itu kiṭṭattaṭṭa ili vaṭivilē irukkum.
28. teyva utti
teyva utti cītēvi eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum. itu vaṭṭa māṉatu; irākkuṭi pōṉṟatu. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu aḻakiya cittira vēlippāṭu ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. itaṉāl itu cittira utti eṉappaṭṭatu.
29. paṭṭam
itu taliyaṇi eṉṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (peruṅ 1 : 40, 149-150) neṟṟip paṭṭam eṉpatu vēṟu aṇikala ṉākum. itu oru poṉ takaṭākum.
30 piṟi
itu piṟi vaṭivamāka poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟuc ciṟārkku aṇivikkum oru tali aṇi. itu paḻaṅkāla aṇi.9
31. pullakam
pullakam taliyiṉ piṉpuṟam piṭariyil palli vaṭivamākap poṉṉāl ceytu aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇiyākum. valatu puṟam vittu aṇiyappaṭum pullakatti vaṭa palli eṉṟum iṭatu puṟam vittu aṇiyum pullakattit teṉ palli eṉṟum aṇivar. itu mikap paḻaṅkāla aṇi. iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
32. polam pūntumpi
tamiḻ nāṭṭu vēntarkaḷ tam paṭiyil cēvi puriyum ciṟanta vīrarkaḷukku poṉṉāl ceyta tumpip pūvip paricāka aḷippārkaḷ. avarkaḷ atit taliyil cūṭik koḷvārkaḷ. tumpi malar, veṟṟiyiṉ ciṉṉamākak karutappaṭṭatu.
33. poṟpū
muṟkālattu, maṉṉarkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa pūkkaḷi aṟiñarkaḷukkut tāṉamāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. avarkaḷ  taṉiyākavō kampikaḷil kōttō taliyil aṇintu vantaṉar.11
34. poṟṟāmarippū
pāṇarkaḷ, pāṭiṉikaḷ viṟaliyar ākiyavarkaḷiṉ pāṭal kaḷik kēṭṭu makiḻnta maṉṉaṉ ivarkaḷukkup poṟṟāmarip pūvip paricilāka vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkam paṇṭit tamiḻakattil uṇṭu. avarkaḷ atit taliyil cūṭuvar. ivi atikamāka iruntāl veḷḷi nārāl toṭuttaṇivar.12
35. poṉ vāki
paṇṭiya tamiḻnāṭṭu vīrarkaḷ veṟṟikku aṭiyāḷamāka poṉṉāl ceyta vāki malarit taliyil aṇintu vantaṉar. vāki veṟṟiyiṉ ciṉṉam. vāki cūṭiṉāṉ eṉṟāl veṟṟi peṟṟāṉ eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum.
36. poṉṉarimāli
tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṉṉar pāṭiṉikaḷukkum viṟaliyarkaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ āṭal pāṭal kēṭṭuk kaḷittu poṉṉari māliyip paricaḷippār. ati avarkaḷ  taliyil cūṭuvar. miṉṉiruṅ kūntal mētakap puṉinta poṉṉaripāliyi13 eṉak kūṟappaṭuvatāl itu taliyaṇi eṉap peṟappaṭum. ikkālattil māṇikkam iḻittuc ceyyappaṭuvatu. neṟṟip paṭṭam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭu kiṟatu.
37. mārāṭṭam
mārāṭṭam muṟkālat taliyaṇiyākat teriyavilli. cilappatikārattilō cintāmaṇiyilō piṟa nūlkaḷilō kāṇappaṭavilli. itu marāṭṭa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pōnta aṇiyāka irukkalām. ampuki kaḻumiyaaṇi mārāṭṭam14 eṉṟu peruṅ kati kūṟukiṟatu.
38. mukacaram
mukacaram, eṉpatic cilar talic cāmāṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum. itu mūṉṟu pirivākap pirikkappaṭṭu oṉṟu - atāvatu oru caram ucciyiṉiṉṟu neṟṟi vari irukkum. maṟṟiru pirivukaḷum naṭu neṟṟiyiṉiṉṟu kātu vari nīṇṭu kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum. itu parata nāṭṭiyamāṭum āṭal makaḷir āvalōṭu aṇiyum aḻakiya aṇi. itaṉi āṭaṟ kaliyip payiṉṟa airōppiya  mātarkaḷum aṇintu parata nāṭṭiyam āṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvaṇi naṭu neṟṟiyi ṉiṉṟu kātuvari tali mayir neṟṟiyilō kaṇkaḷilō viḻāta paṭi kāppatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum tali mayirukku oru varampu kāṭṭuvatu pōlavum tikaḻkiṟatu. maṇappeṇkaḷ nīṇṭa kālamāka itaṉi aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar; ippoḻutu aṇiva tilli. ivvaṇiyiṉ naṭuviliruntu oru patakkam neṟṟiyil toṅkum. ati neṟṟic cuṭṭi eṉpar. iti aṇintu taliyiṉ muṉpakkam iru patikaḷil cūriyap piṟiyum cantirap piṟiyum aṇivar.
39. muñcam
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil tāymārkaḷ tam arumic ciṟār kaḷukkut taliyil vittu muṭikkum oru taliyaṇi. itu poṉ ṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu ucciyil vittu muṭikkappaṭum oru aṇi kalaṉākum.15
40. vayantakam
itu taliyaṇi. itu taliyiṉiṉṟu neṟṟiyil toṅkuva tāl nutal aṇi eṉpārum uḷar. itu āṭum makaḷir virumpi talik kōlattiṉiṉṟu neṟṟiyil tāḻumpaṭi oru vaṭamākac ceruki vikkappaṭum poṉ aṇikalamākum. itu mikunta vēlippāṭuḷḷatāy irukkum.16
41. valampuri
valampuri eṉpatu poṉṉāl valampuric caṅku vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭa oru tali aṇiyākum. itu teyva uttikku aṭuttut taliyil aṇiyappaṭum. oru talik kōla aṇi. itu mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil iruntu tamiḻakattil aṇiyappaṭṭu vanta oru poṉ aṇikalaṉākum. innaki iṉṟiya vaḻakkil illi. cilappatikārattilē, mātavi teyva uttiyōṭu valam puriyum toyyakamum, pullakam ākiya periya kūntalil aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyum toṭarntu aṇintu vantatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.17
valampuri eṉṉum inta aṇikalaṉi nanta vaṭṭap pū eṉṟum aḻippar. iḵtu iṭatu puṟattil ēntiḻiyar aṇiyum eḻil aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu.18
II  nutal aṇikalaṉkaḷ
1. tiru uṟuppu 2. tilakam  3. neṟṟic cuṭṭi 4. neṟṟip paṭṭam 5. pattik kaccu 6. pattirac curiki 7. makarap pakuvāy 8. makara valayam.
nutal (neṟṟi) makkaḷiṉ uṟuppukaḷil tali ciṟantatu; uyarntu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. nutal ciṟanta iṭamāka iruppatāl civa perumāṉ atil oru kaṇṇit tōṉṟap paṇṇiṉār. makkaḷ atil kaṇṇit tōṉṟumpaṭi ceyya muṭiyātatāl atil tilakam iṭṭaṉar. tilakam makkaḷiṉ aḻaki mikuttuk kāṭṭu vatāy uḷḷatu. atu pōl makaḷir nutal aṇikaḷip pūṇṭu tam iyaṟkiyaḻaki ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkumāṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar.
nutal aṇikaḷ kātaṇikaḷ kaḻuttaṇikaḷ, kiyaṇikaḷ pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ pōl taṉitta aṇikaḷalla. perumpālum nutal aṇikaḷ taliyaṇikaḷiṉ iṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. nutal aṇikaḷ tali aṇikaḷiṉiṉṟu nutalil toṅkuvaṉavākavē irukkum. nutalil toṅkuvatāl nutal aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa; nutalaṇikaḷ cilavāka iruppiṉum ciṟappāṉavikaḷākum. ivi paṉṉiṟa maṇikaḷ patippikkap peṟṟu aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkum. ivvaṇikaḷ mukattiṉ mēṟpakutiyil muṉ pakutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatāl mukattiṟku aḻakūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvaṇikaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu:
1. tiru uṟuppu
tiru uṟuppu poṉṉum maṇiyum iḻittuc ceyyap peṟṟa talik kōlattiṉiṉṟu toṅkum oru aḻakiya nutal aṇi. itu mika aḻaku tarum nutalaṇiyākap pōṟṟap peṟṟu vantatu.
2. tilakam
tilakam eṉṉum oru poṉ aṇi muṟkālattil neṟṟiyil toṅkumāṟu aṇiyappaṭṭiruntatu. tilakam cuṭṭi vaṭivamāy nutalil toṅkum taliyaṇiyiṉ uṟuppēyākum. tilakam kalit toki pōṉṟa paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum iṭam peṟ ṟuḷḷatu.19
3. neṟṟic cuṭṭi
neṟṟic cuṭṭi muṟkālattut toyyakam pōṉṟa oru nutal aṇikalaṉākum. itu cuṟāmīṉiṉ vāy pōṉṟa vaṭivil amikkap peṟṟu neṟṟiyil toṅkumāṟu aṇiyap peṟṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. vācavatatti neṟṟic cuṭṭiyi aṇintu aḻaku poliyat tikaḻntati peruṅkati eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu. 20
4. neṟṟippaṭṭam
neṟṟiyil aṇiyum oru poṉ takaṭṭālāṉa nutalaṇi. itu mikap paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻaka naki. vāṇutaṟ paṭṭam miṉṉi eṉṟu cintāmaṇi ciṟappittuk kūṟum aṇikalaṉ ituvākum. 21
5. pattik kaccu
itu nutalil toṅkum oru poṉ aṇi. itu paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. āṉāl peruṅkatiyil pattik kacciṉō ṭottavi piṟavum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. (peruṅ 2; 5-144) itit tavira ivvaṇiyip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyavilli.
6. pattirac curiki
pattirac curikiyum paḻaṅkāla tamiḻaka aṇiyākat teriya villi. atu piṟkālattil poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu nutalil iṭam peṟṟa pēraṇikalaṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cittirap piṇi yalum pattirac curikiyum eṉṟu peruṅ kati kūṟukiṟatu.
7. makarap pakuvāy
itu cuṟā mīṉ vaṭivamākap poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa oru nutalaṇi. ituvum tamiḻakattu paḻaṅkālattu aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ oṉṟu. eṟimakara vayala maṇitikaḻ nutaliyar eṉṟu paripāṭal kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. (pari. 10; 77).
8. makara valayam
makara valayam poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟup palavita maṇikaḷ iḻittu aḻakuṟac ceyyap peṟṟa poṉ aṇi. itu cuṟā vaṭivam vāyntatu. itiviṭa inta aṇikalaṉip paṟṟi atikam col vataṟkilli. paripāṭalil cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivamākac ceyyap peṟṟa makara valayam eṉṉum nutalaṇi aṇiyap peṟṟa aḻaku poliyum neṟṟiyiyuṭiya makaḷir eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.22
III kātaṇikalaṉkaḷ
mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiliruntē tamiḻarkaḷ kātaṇi kaḷi aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. intak kātu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ātiyil kuḻiyākavum iliyākavum ōliyākavum pūntōṭākavum iruntu piṉṉar palvēṟu vitamākap pariṇamittaṉa. eṉavē piṟ kālattil poṉ aṇikaḷ, kuḻi vaṭivamākavum ili vaṭivamāka vum, ōli vaṭivamākavum, tōṭu vaṭivamākavum eḻuntaṉa. atōṭu peyaril kūṭa māṟṟam peṟātu kuḻi eṉṟum ōli eṉṟum tōṭu eṉṟum poṉ aṇikaḷ peyar tāṅkiṉa. kātaṇikaḷ āṇ kaḷukkum peṇkaḷukkum uriya aṇiyāka eḻuntaṉa. iṉṟu āṇkaḷ kātaṇi, aṇiyum vaḻakkam niṉṟu viṭṭatu. makaḷir kūṭa, kātil tōṭu tavira piṟa aṇikaḷik kiviṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. tōṭu aṇiyum vaḻakkam kūṭa ippoḻutu maṟiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. ātiyil tōṭaṇinta tamiḻaka makaḷir piṟkālattil kātu vaṭittu, kātu, tōḷvari toṅkumāṟu peritākki atil 4,5 periya kātaṇikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. inta vaḻakkam pōrṉiyō nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṭam kūṭa iruntu vantatākat terikiṟatu. kātu vaṭikkum vaḻakkam tamiḻakattil eppoḻutu eḻuntatu eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. cilar intiyāvil mokalāyar āṭci eḻunta piṉ kātu vaṭikkum vaḻakkam eḻuntatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. piṟa enta māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiyum viṭa nelli māvaṭṭattilē peṇkaḷ mika nīḷamākak kātukaḷi vaṭittiruntatōṭu ataṟkeṉṟu pala putiya nakikaḷiyum uruvākki iruntaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭil vēḷāḷarkaḷ, paratavarkaḷ, nāṭārkaḷ, kammāḷarkaḷ, vāṇiyac ceṭṭiyārkaḷ, maṟavarkaḷ, iṭiyarkaḷ pōṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷ nīṇṭa kātukaḷip peṟṟu atil pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi, muṭiccu, iṭṭaṭikki, (ciluvaṇi), mēlīṭu pōṉṟa periya nakikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar. ati ayal nāṭṭārkaṇṭu āccariyappaṭṭu vantaṉar. iṉṟu ivvaḻakkam peritum maṟintuviṭṭatu.
kuḻi, kuṇṭalam, kaṭukkaṉ mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷi āṇ kaḷum aṇintu vantaṉar. kuḻi aṇintataṉāl civaperumāṉukkuk kuḻik kātar eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu. nēṟṟu vari, tamiḻakattu mutaliyār peṇkaḷ nīṇṭa kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa civak kurukkaḷ vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa periyakuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉkaḷi aṇintu varu kiṉṟaṉar.
inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ pātik kālamvari tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil kātu aṇikaḷ aṇivatu oru ciṟappākak karutappeṟṟatu. ovvoru tamiḻaṉ vīṭṭilum kātu kuttuvatu - atāvatu kuḻantikaḷukku mutaṉ mutalāka kātil tuvāramiṭṭu aṇikaḷ aṇivatu oru viḻāvākak karutappeṟṟatu. kuḻantiyik kōyilukkuk koṇṭu pōyk kātu kuttuvatākat teyvap peyaril poruttam paṇṇik koḷvatum uṇṭu.
muṟkālattil peṇ kuḻantikaḷukkuk kātik kutti atil pañci vittum takki vittum kātut toḷiyip peritu paṇṇiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu attoḻilil tērntavarkaḷik koṇṭu mikak kavaṉamākak kātik kuttit tuḷiyip peritākkik kātaṇikaḷ pūṇṭu vantaṉar. appāl avvaḻakkam māṟip peṇ kuḻantikaḷukkut tōṭu pōṭum vaḻakkam varavē, kammāḷarkaḷi (ācārimārkaḷi) aḻittuk kātu kutti vantaṉar. avarkaḷukkut taṭcaṇiyum tēṅkāy, paḻam, carkkari kalanta paccarici mutaliyavi celavu ceyya vēṇṭiyatiruntatu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil oru vayatil kātu kuttuvatu eṉpatu kiviṭappaṭṭu 4, 5 10 vayatukaḷil kātu kutta muṟpaṭṭaṉar. tōṭṭiṟkup patilāka ciṟu kuccikaḷik kātil aṇivatē nākarikamāka eḻuntatu. kātil pala tuvāraṅkaḷ iṭuva taṟkup patilāka oru tuvāramē iṭappaṭṭatu. atuvum ciṟu ūciyiyō muḷḷiyō koṇṭu tāymārkaḷē tuvāraṅ kaḷic ceytu koṇṭaṉar.
1. aṭṭiki, 2. iṭṭaṭikki 3. ōli : (1) poṉ ōli 
(2) māṇikka ōli 4. kaṭippiṇi (cantirapāṇi allatu vira ōli) 5. kaṭukkaṉ (1) maṇikkaṭukkaṉ (2) kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉ (3) civantippūk kaṭukkaṉ 6. kampi 7. kaṉṉappū 8. kuṇṭalam :- (1) irattiṉak kuṇṭalam (maṇik kuṇṭalam) (2) carppak kuṇṭalam (pāmpuk kuṇṭalam) (3) cimmak kuṇṭalam (ciṅkak kuṇṭalam) (4) pattirak kuṇṭalam (5) makarak kuṇṭalam (6) yāṉik kuṇṭalam 9. kuṇukku 10. kutampi : (1) nīlamaṇik kutampi (2) māṇikka maṇik kutampi 11. kuṟaṭu kātiṉ veḷip puṟamuḷḷa kuṟaṭu eṉṉum uṟuppil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉākum 12. kuḻi : (1) oṇkuḻi (2) kaṉaṅkuḻi (3) pūṅkuḻi (4) poṟkuḻi (5) makarak kuḻi 13. kuvaḷi : (1) poṟ kuvaḷi (2) maṇikkuvaḷi (3) vaṭṭak kuvaḷi 14. koppu 
15. caṉṉāva tañcam 16. ciṉṉappū 17. cevippū 18. taṭuppu 19. taṇṭaṭṭi 20. tāḷuruvi 21. tiricari 22. tōṭu : (1) pūntōṭu (2) poṟṟōṭu 
(3) maṇit tōṭu 23. navacari 24. navakaṇṭiki 25. nākapaṭam 
26. pañcacari 27. pāmpaṭam 28. pāmpaṇi 29. pukaṭi 30. pūṭi 31. makari 32. mañciki 33. maṭal 34. māttiri 35. muṭiccu 36.  muruku : (1) poṉ muruku (2) viciṟi muruku (3) maṇimuruku 37. mēlīṭu 38. valliki 
39. vāḷi
1. aṭṭiki
aṭṭiki eṉṟu oru kātaṇi iruntatākac collappaṭu kiṟatu. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ataṟku ātāram kāṇappaṭavilli. āṉāl aṭṭiki eṉṟoru kaḻuttaṇiyuṇṭu. avvaṇi inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil vaḻakkil iruntatu. ippoḻutu maṟintu varukiṟatu.
2. iṭṭaṭikki
kātu vaḷartta peṇkaḷ nīṇṭu toṅkum kātu maṭalkaḷil aṇiyum pāmpaṭam. taṇṭaṭṭi, muṭiccu, mēlīṭu ākiya nakiyōṭu cērnta aṇikalaṉākum itu. itaṉi melliya poṟṟakaṭṭāl pukalākac ceytu meḻukaṭit tiruppar. iṉṟum vayatu mutirnta paṇakkārap peṇkaḷiṉ kātukaḷil 5 nakikaḷ paḻak kulikaḷ pōṉṟu toṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
3. ōli : (1) poṉ ōli
ōli vaṭivamākac ceyyap peṟṟa kātaṇi. muṟkālattil makaḷir kātukaḷil iḷavōliyi aṇintu vantaṉar. ulōka kālattil poṉṉāl ōli vaṭivamākac ceytu atic curuṭṭi vaṭṭamākac ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar.
(2) māṇikka ōli
poṉ ōli aṇinta makaḷir piṟkālattil maṇikaḷ kiṭitta poḻutu, atil māṇikkaṅkaḷip patittu aṇintu vantaṉar. ataṉāl māṇikka ōli eṉa aḻikkap peṟṟatu.
4. kaṭippiṇi (cantirapāṇi)
muṟkāla makkaḷ mutalil kātukaḷil kaṭippu eṉṉum aṇiyiyiṭṭuk kātut toḷiyip peritākkiyapiṉ piṟa aṇi kaḷi aṇivar. vayirak kaṭippiṭṭu vār kātu tāḻap perukki eṉṟār āḻvār (nā. tiv. pira. 142 : 1) kaṭip piṇi eṉṉum aṇikkuc cantirapāṇi eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. ik kaṭippu intira nīlattiyum iṭi iṭiyē vira maṇikaḷiyum aḻuttic ceyyap peṟṟatu. itaṉit talikkaṇi eṉpārum uṇṭu.
	intira nīlattu iṭiyiṭi tiraṇṭa
	 cantira pāṇi takipeṟu kaṭippiṇi
	 aṅkātu akavayiṉ aḻakuṟa vaṇintu23
eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceyyuḷukku uri ceytu uriyāciriyar nīlak kutampi, vayirak kutampi ākiya iraṇṭiyum iru kātilum aṇintāḷ eṉṟār.
5. kaṭukkaṉ : (1) maṇik kaṭukkaṉ
kaṭukkaṉ kātiṉ kīḻppakutiyil uḷḷa tuḷiyil aṇiyum oru vāḷiyākum. itil maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟāl maṇikkaṭukkaṉ eṉappaṭum. kaṭukkaṉ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇiyum aṇikala ṉākum.
(2) kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉ
kuvaḷip pū vaṭivamākac ceyyap peṟṟatu kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉ eṉa aḻikkappaṭum. intak kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉ inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampak kālam vari vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu.
(3) civantip pūk kaṭukkaṉ
cuṟṟilum civappuk kaṟkaḷ vittu naṭuvil veḷḷik kal vittuc ceyyap peṟṟa poṉṉāl ceyta kaṭukkaṉ civantip pūkkaṭukkaṉ eṉa aḻikkap peṟṟatu. muṟkālattil vaki vaki yāka eṇṇaṟṟa kaṭukkaṉkaḷ iruntu vantaṉa.
7. kaṉṉappū
itu tōṭṭiṟku mēlē kātiṉ viḷimpil aṇiyum oru aṇikalaṉākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu mēlē iruntu variciyāka oṉṟu mūṉṟu nālu eṉa muṟiyāka civappuk kaṟkaḷ patittu aṭiyil ciṟu poṉ cataṅki toṅka viṭappaṭṭirukkum. inta aṇikalaṉkaḷi kātu vaṭittavarkaḷum, tōṭu puṉintavar kaḷum aṇivar. inta aṇiyum inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭup pakutivari iruntu maṟintu viṭṭatu.
8. kuṇṭalam (i) irattiṉak kuṇṭalam
irattiṉak kuṇṭalam maṇikaḷ vittu iḻitta poṉṉā lāṉa kātaṇi. itu makaḷir aṇiyum kātaṇiyākak karutappaṭum. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil itu pārvati tēvikkuriya aṇiyākak kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi tēvi karṉikā paraṇam allatu makara kuṇṭalamum aṇintiruppār.
(ii) carppak kuṇṭalam
carppak kuṇṭalam, pāmpu vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa poṟ kuṇṭalamākum. itu oru aḻakiya kātaṇi. civaperumāṉukku ukantatu.
(iii) cimma kuṇṭalam
cimma kuṇṭalam eṉpatu ciṅka mukam pōṉṟu poṉṉāl  ceyyappaṭṭa oru kātaṇiyākum. itic cimma pattiram allatu viyāḻa kuṇṭalam (pāmpuk kuṇṭalam) eṉpārkaḷ. itu valatu kātiṟkuriya vaṭṭamāṉa kātaṇi ituvum civaperumāṉuk kuriyatēyām.
(iv) pattirak kuṇṭalam
pattirak kuṇṭalam kātaṇikaḷil oṉṟu. itaṉ akalam 3, 4 allatu 5 aṅkulam irukkalām. itaṉ kaṉam oru yavi. itu veṇmiyallatu civappu niṟamāka irukkum. iṭatu kātil karṇiki allatu caṅkapattiram aṇivatum uṇṭu. karṇiki tāmari mokkup pōṉṟa vaṭivam vāyntirukkum. caṅkapattiram caṅkiṉāl ceyta kātu vaḷiyamākum.
(v) makarak kuṇṭalam
makarak kuṇṭalam makara mīṉ vaṭivāyc ceyyappaṭṭa kuṇṭalam, ik kuṇṭalamāṉatu viḻitta kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya cuṟāmīṉ pōṉṟu irukkum. cintāmaṇiyil makara kuṇṭalam pala iṭaṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.24
(vi) yāṉik kuṇṭalam
yāṉik kuṇṭalam yāṉi mukam pōṉṟu amikkap paṭṭatu. iru kātilum aṇiyappaṭum. 2, 4, 5 aṅkula nīḷamirukkum. vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉatu. 18 yavam irukkum. uyaram 4 aṅkulam. vāḻippū, tāmari pōṉṟu tikaḻum.
9. kuṇukku
itu kātiṉ kīḻppakutiyil aṇiyappaṭum oru vaḷiyamē yākum. muṟkālattil poṉṉāl ceyta kuṇukku aṇiyap paṭṭatu. 40 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kātu vaṭitta ēḻic ciṟu peṇkaḷ īyak kuṇukkukaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar.
10. kutampi : (1) nīla maṇik kutampi
nīla maṇikaḷ patitta poṉṉālāṉa kutampi nīla maṇik kutampi eṉappaṭum. kōvalaṉ nīla maṇik kutampiyiyum cantira pāṇi eṉum kaṭippiṇiyum aṇintiruntāṉ eṉṟu cilap patikāram kūṟukiṟatu.25
(2) māṇikkak kutampi
mukappiṟ kaṭṭiṉa māṇikka maṇiyiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē vayiraṅ kaṭṭiṉa māṇikkak kutampiyum akkāla makkaḷ aṇivar. cilappatikārattil virak kutampi oru kātilum nīlak kutampi oru kātilum kōvalaṉ aṇintāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
11. kuruṭu
kātiṉ aṭippuṟam - atāvatu kaṉṉatti oṭṭic ciṟitu nīṇṭu irukkum catip pakutiyil aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇi kuruṭu eṉappaṭum.
12. kuḻi : (1) oṇkuḻi
kuḻi vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa kuṇṭalattiṟkum kuḻi eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu. caṅkak kāla makaḷir oṇkuḻikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar. (2) kaṉaṅkuḻi : muṟkāla makaḷir kaṭippu eṉṉum aṇiyi iṭṭuk kātip peritākki appāl kaṉaṅ kuḻiyaṇintaṉar. kaṭippiku kātiṟ kaṉaṅkuḻi peyareṉṟu paripāṭal kūṟukiṟatu26 (3) pūṅkuḻi : caṅkak kāla makaḷir aṇinta kuḻikaḷil pūṅkuḻiyum oṉṟu. ivi poṉ aṇi; pūn toḻil niṟintiruntaṉa. poṉ kuḻikaḷōṭu acōkan taḷiriyum kuvaḷi malariyum aṇiyum vaḻakkam paḻaṅkāla makaḷiriṭamuṇṭu.27 
(4) poṟ kuḻi : muṟkālattil āṭavarkaḷum makaḷirum ili vaṭivamāṉa poṉ kuḻi eṉṉum aṇiyi aṇintu vantaṉar. 28 (5) makarak kuḻi : makara mīṉ (cuṟāmīṉ) vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa kuḻikku makarak kuḻi eṉṟu peyar. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu maṇikaḷ iḻikkappaṭṭirukkum. (6) poṟ kuḻi : poṟ kuḻi eṉpatu ili vaṭivamāṉa poṉṉāl ceyta kātaṇi yākum. kaṭip paṇintu kātip peritākkip piṉ poṟ kuḻiyip pūṇpar.
13. kuvaḷi : (1) poṟ kuvaḷi
kuvaḷi oru vaki nīrp pū. kuvaḷip pū vaṭivamākap poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa kātaṇikkup poṟ kuvaḷi eṉṟu peyar. āṇkaḷ kātil 2 allatu 3 kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉkaḷ aṇivar. (2) maṇik kuvaḷi : kuvaḷip pū vaṭivamākap poṉṉāṟ ceytu ataṉ iṭiyē maṇikaḷ matikkap peṟṟa kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉukku maṇik kuvaḷi eṉṟu peyar. (3) vaṭṭak kuvaḷi : vaṭṭamākac  ceyyap peṟṟa kuvaḷik kaṭukkaṉukku vaṭṭak kuvaḷi eṉṟu peyar. itaṉi iṉṟuvari nelli māvaṭṭa civak kurukkaḷ aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
14. koppu
itu kātiṉ mēṟ pakutiyil tuḷiyiṭṭu aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇikalaṉ. ivvaṇi naṭuvē taṇṭum mēlēyum kīḻēyum cuṇṭikkāy pōṉṟa iru maṇikaḷum poruttap peṟṟirukkum. anta maṇiyiṉ mēlē ciṟu kumiḻ irukkum. maṇiyiṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka aṇi aṇiyāka kōṭukaḷ tīrkkap paṭṭirukkum. mēlē irukkum kumiḻil maṇikaḷ patittiruppatum uṇṭu.
15. caṉṉāva tañcam
itu piṟkālak kātaṇi kalaṉkaḷil oṉṟākum. ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil itu kāṇappaṭavilli.
16. ciṉṉappū
ciṟu pū vaṭivamākac ceyyappeṟṟa kātaṇi ciṉṉap pū eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟatu. itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟatu. 29
17. cevippū
kātil aṇiyum maṟṟoru vaṭivamāṉa aṇikalaṉ cevippū eṉappaṭṭatu. ituvum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatākum. (nālā. tiv. pira. 500 : 4)
18. taṭuppu
kātil aṇiyum iru aṇikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uracāmal taṭuppāka aṇiyum oru vaḷiyam taṭuppu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ.
19. taṇṭaṭṭi
taṇṭaṭṭi kātu vaṭitta peṇkaḷ aṇiyum piṟkālattup poṉ aṇiyākum. itu melliya poṉ takaṭṭāl pukalākac ceytu uḷḷē arakku aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum.
20. tāḷuruvi
poṉṉāṟ ceyta kātaṇikaḷil oṉṟu tāḷuruvi. kaṭippik kātiliṭṭu atip peritākkip piṉ tāḷuruvi aṇivar. itu caṅkak kāla aṇikalaṉkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.30
21. tiricari
mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya oru aḻakiya kātaṇi. itu teyvaṅkaḷukku aṇiyum poṉ aṇiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ vēlippāṭu mika aṟputamāṉatu. itaṉ mukappil uyarnta maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum.
22. tōṭu : (a) pūntōṭu
tōṭu pūviṉ aṭippakuti. pūvaracup pūviṉ aṭip pakutiyip pārttāl poṉṉāṟ ceytu cemmaṇi patitta poṟṟōṭu pōl irukkum. pū vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟamiyāl pūntōṭu eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. (ā) poṟṟōṭu : tōṭu maṇikaḷ patikkāmal poṉṉilē ceytu aṇivatum uṇṭu. itip poṟṟōṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. (i) maṇittōṭu : maṇikaḷ patittu aḻakuṟac ceyta tōṭṭiṟku maṇittōṭu eṉṟu peyar. māṇikkaṅkaḷ cuṟṟilum patittu naṭuvil viram patippatum uṇṭu. muḻuvatum viram patittu virattōṭu eṉṟu kūṟi aṇivatum uṇṭu.
23. navacari
oṉpatu vaṭaṅkaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru kātaṇikalaṉākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. aḻakiya maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟatu.
24. nava kaṇṭiki
poṉṉāṟ ceytu paṉṉiṟa maṇikaḷ patittu aḻakuṟac ceyta māli. mukappuc ciṟuttum varavara naṭuppākam vari peruttum naṭuvil variciyāka perumaṇikaḷum viḷimpil ciṟiya maṇikaḷum naṭuvil patakkamum ataṉ kīḻē poṉ cataṅkikaḷum toṅkum. inta aṇi iṉṟum maturi mīṉāṭci ammaṉi aḻakupaṭuttukiṟatu.
25. nākappaṭam
pāmpiṉ paṭam pōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa oru kātaṇi itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu; yāḻppāṇattil uḷḷa tamiḻ makkaḷ iṉṟum aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
26. pañca cari
pañca cari aintu vaṭaṅkaḷālāṉa oru poṉ aṇi. itu teyvaṅkaḷukkuc ciṟappāka aṇiyappaṭum oru aḻakiya kātaṇi. 31
27. pāmpaṭam
kātu vaṭitta peṇkaḷ taṇṭaṭṭi, muṭiccu eṉṉum aṇi kaḷōṭu cērttu aṇiyum pāmpu pōṉṟa oru poṉ aṇiyākum. itu melliya takaṭṭāl ceytu uḷḷē meḻuku aṭikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. 32
28. pāmpaṇi
pāmpu vaṭivilāṉa oru kātaṇi.
29. pukiṭi
pukiṭi peṇkaḷ puṟac cevi itaḻil aṇiyum kātaṇikaḷil oṉṟu; pukiṭi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
30. pūṭi
itu kātiṉ mēṟ puṟattil aṇiyum oru ciṟu aṇi naṭuvē oru ciṟu kuḻal vaṭivamāṉa curi, ataṟkut tirukum uṇṭu. mēlē yum kīḻēyum nāṉku ciṟu periya kaṭuku pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ porutti ataṉ mēlē caturavaṭivum uyarē oru muṉi irukkum paṭiyākap poruttap peṟṟirukkum poṉ aṇi. itu vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
31. makari
makari cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya kātaṇi kalaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. makariki vayirak kuṇṭalam alampum - tōḷ puṭi vayaṅka eṉṟu irāmāyaṇam kūṟukiṟatu.
32. mañciki
mañciki oru poṉ kātaṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. peruṅ katiyil mañciki aṇikalaṉkaḷ vikkum peṭṭi eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu. āṉāl cintāmaṇi itu oru aṇikalaṉāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.33
33. maṭal
itu kātil aṇiyum kātaṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu; poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. maṭalaṇi peṇṇi yīṉṟa maṇi maruḷ kurumpi māṉ -  eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu. (cin. 2053)
34. māttiri
māttiri oru vaki poṉ aṇikalaṉ. kātil aṇintu koḷḷum aṇikalaṉ. kuḻiyaṇi kātiṉiṉ māttiriyum eṉṟu tiruvicippā kūṟukiṟatu. cempoṉ māttiri eṉṟu peruṅ kati kūṟukiṟatu.34
35. muṭiccu
itu kātu vaṭittavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nīṇṭa kātil aṭip pakuti yil pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi, iṭṭaṭikki mēlīṭu mutaliya kātaṇi kaḷōṭu aṇiyum oru poṉ aṇi. poṉ kampikaḷi uruṇṭi yāṉa vaṭṭa vaṭivil piṉṉi mukappu vittu kātil aṇivar. ēṉiya pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi mutaliya aṇikaḷukku meḻuku aṭittiruppatu pōl itaṟku meḻuku aṭippatilli.
36. muruku
(1) poṉ muruku: poṉ muruku vil pōṉṟu vaḷintu ari vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa piṟi pōṉṟa oru poṉ aṇi. naṭuvē peruttum iru nuṉiyum ciṟuttum irukkum. itu kātil mēṟ puṟattil aṇiyappaṭum. (2) viciṟi muruku : kātiṉ mēṟ pakutiyil iṭap peṟṟa tuḷiyil viciṟi pōl ceytu aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇi. itu pūṭikkup pakkattil aṇiyappaṭum patakkam pōl irukkum. (3) maṇi viciṟi muruku : kātil mēṟ puṟam viciṟi pōl ceyta poṉ aṇiyil maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟāl ataṉi maṇi viciṟi muruku eṉṟu aḻippar. itu inta nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ ārampak kālattilē vaḻakkil iruntu maṟintatu.
37. valliki
valliki koṭi pōṉṟu poṉ iḻiyāl piṉṉappaṭṭa oru ciṟu paḻiya kātaṇi; piṅkala nikaṇṭu, kātaṇi vakikaḷil oṉṟāka kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi inta aṇikalaṉip paṟṟi atikam terivataṟkilli.
38. vāḷi
kampi vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa kātu aṇiyākum. mutaṉ mutalākak kātaṇi aṇiyum poḻutu kampi vaṭivamākac ceyyap peṟṟa vāḷiyō allatu poṉ kampi vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa vāḷiyō aṇiyappaṭṭirukkalām.35
IV kaḻut taṇikalaṉkaḷ
aṇikalaṉkaḷil kaḻuttaṇikalaṉkaḷ ciṟappuṭiyaṉavākum. pēraṇikalaṉkaḷil perumpālāṉavi kaḻuttaṇikaḷākavē irukkum. makaḷirkku maṇamakaṉ aṇiyum maṅkala aṇi kaḻuttil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉāka irukkiṟatu. viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷik koṇṭa aṇikaḷ aṉittum  kaḻuttilē iṭam peṟṟirukkum. mēlum atika eṭiyuḷḷa perutta aṇikalaṉkaḷit tali muṭikaḷō, kātukaḷō, mūkkō, kiyō, viralō tāṅka muṭiyātu. eṉavē kaḻuttilē kaṉamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ maṭṭumalla atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇiyap peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
āti kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ naṉṟāka uṇṭapiṉ uvaki koṇṭu āṭum poḻutu avarkaḷ pū, ili, kāy, paḻam koṭṭi mutaliyavikaḷi atikamākak kaḻuttilē kōttup pōṭṭuk koṇṭē āṭiṉar. antap parampari vaḻakkam iṉṟu vari tamiḻar camūkattil nīṭittu iruntu varukiṟatu.
iṉṟum oruvari varavēṟka māli aṇivatu kaḻuttilēyē aṟiñar oruvarkkup poṉṉāṭi pōttuvatum  kaḻuttilē tāṉ. paṇṭik kālat tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ tiṟami mikka ici vallunarkkum āṭal vallunarkkum pulavarkaḷukkum viliyuyarnta poṉṉari māli, maṇivaṭam, muttumali mutaliyavaṟṟik kaḻuttilē tāṉ aṇivippatu vaḻakkam.
eṉavē kaḻuttu atikamāṉa vaṭaṅkaḷiyum māliyiyum, kōvikaḷiyum, caṅkilikaḷiyum tāṅkik koṇṭatu. iṅku nām kaṇṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷil atikamāṉa eṇṇikkikaḷiyuṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ kaḻuttaṇikalaṉkaḷēyām. kaḻuttaṇi vakikaḷ 100kku mēṟpaṭṭaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku nām 59 aṇikalaṉ kaḷi maṭṭum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm avi varumāṟu :
kaḻuttaṇikalaṉkaḷ

5. ēkavaṭam 6. ēkavalli 7. kaṇṭamāli 8. kaṇṭacaram 9. kaṇṭiki 10. kaḻaṟcikkāymāli 11. kaḷiki 12. kācumāli 13. kāṟi (poṉ  kāṟi) 14. kiṟi 15. kottamallimāli 16. kōti 17. kōvi

(u) paṉmaṇittāli  (ū) pulippaltāli (e) pulinakattāli (ai) piṉṟāli (o) maṇit tāli (ō) poṉṟāli 26. tāḻvaṭam 27. tiru 28. tumpu 29. toṭar 30. toṭiyal 31. nāṇ 32. nuṇtoṭar

47. maṇimiṭi pavaḷam 48. matāṇi 49. marutaṅkāy māli 
50. malarccaram 51. māṅkāy māli 52. miḷakumāli 53. muttu vaṭam 54. muttumāli 55. muttuvaḷḷi 56. mummaṇikkācu 57. mōkaṉa māli 58. vaṉṉacaram 59. vīraccaṅkili.
kaḻuttaṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. aṭṭiki
kaḻutti oṭṭi aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇi. itu takaṭṭuvaṭivilum caṅkili vaṭivilum uṇṭu. naṭuvē patakkam toṅkum. kal patitta aṭṭiki kal aṭṭiki eṉappaṭum.
2. arumpuccaram
poṉṉāl arumpu vaṭivil ceytu poṉ nāṇil kōkkappaṭṭa oru kaḻuttaṇikalaṉ.
3. āmittāli
itu āmi vaṭivamāṉa aṇi. iti viṇavarkaḷ aṇivar. inta aṇi tirumālukkukantatu āmittāli pūṇṭa aṉanta cayaṉ eṉṟu nālāyirat tivviya pirapantam kūṟukiṟatu. 36
4. uṭkaṭṭu
uṭkaṭṭi cātāraṇa makkaḷ ikkaṭṭu eṉṟaṉar. itu uṭkaḻuttil aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉṉaṇi. itu oru pakkam nērākavum maṟṟoru pakkam mukkōṇam pōlavum uḷḷa pala tuṇṭukaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉākum. itu irupatām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakutivari iruntu vaḻakkoḻintu pōyiṟṟu.
5. ēkavaṭam
navamaṇikaḷ iḻitta oṟṟic carattālāṉa oruvakik kaḻuttaṇiyākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya aṇikalaṉākum. iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
6. ēkavalli
muttukkaḷālāṉa oru kaḻuttaṇi itu peruṅkatiyil ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuvatuṭaṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil innaki pēcappaṭu kiṟatu. itu caṅkakkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil eṅkum kāṇappaṭa villi.
7. kaṇṭamāli
kaḻuttil aṇiyum poṉṉālāṉa oru māli. itiviṭa namakku vēṟu oru takavalum kiṭikkavilli.
8. kaṇṭacaram
(kaṇṭacaram eṉpatum kaṇṭiki eṉpatum orē aṇi) pala pūkkaḷ vaṭivamāṉa poṉ maṇikaḷ oṉṟākak kōkkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
9. kaṇṭiki
kaṇṭiki oru poṉṉāṟ ceyta kaḻuttu aṇi. itik kaṇṭacaram eṉṟum kūṟuvar. aḻakiya kaṇṭikiyōṭu muttut tōḷvaḷi cērttuk kaṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.37
10. kaḻaṟcikkāy māli
itu kaḻaṟcikkāy vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa poṉ aṇi. itu mikat toṉmiyāṉa tamiḻ makkaḷ kaṇṭa nākarikattil eḻunta aṇikalaṉākum.38
11. kaḷiki
itu caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. ilip peruṅkaḷikiyum eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu.
12. kācumāli
itu kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu untivari toṅkum oru pēraṇi kalaṉākum. aṭukkaṭukkāy poṟkācukaḷukku maṭi vittup poṉ allatu veḷḷic caraṭṭil kōttu aṇivārkaḷ. kācu kiṭikkāviṭil kācukaḷ pōṉṟu acciṭṭuk kōttu aṇi vārkaḷ. pavuṇkaḷik kōttu aṇintu koḷvatu pavuṇmāli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. itu iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. kōyil kaḷil tiru uruvaṅkaḷukku pavuṇmāli aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ.
13. kāṟi
itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟa kaḻuttaṇi. itu vaṭṭavaṭiva mākak kampipōl ceyyappaṭṭatu. muṉpuṟam iṭi iṭiyē pūkkaḷ pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ irukkum.39
14. kiṟi
itu, muṟkāla aṇikalaṉ paṭṭiyalkaḷil kāṇappaṭa villi. āṉāl itu viṇavarkaḷiṉ kaḻuttu aṇiyākak kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
15.  kottamallimāli
kottamalli vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa oru māli. 100kku mēṟpaṭṭa kotta mallikaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkum. itu aḻakiya poṉ aṇi.
16. kōti
pūvaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru poṉ aṇi. itu muṟ kālattilēyuḷḷa aṇiyākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. kōti mārpiṟ kōtiyāṉum eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu kūṟukiṟatu.41
17. kōvi
aintu allatu āṟuvaṭaṅkaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭu piṉpuṟam paṭukaṇ nōkku vāyōṭu kūṭiya mukappiyuṭiya kaḻuttaṇi; poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu (cilap 6 : 102)
18. caṅkili
mūṉṟu allatu 4 caṅkilikaḷ uḷḷa kaḻuttaṇi; poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟatu; aḻakiya mukappu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mukappil kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum.
19. cantirap piṟi
cantirappiṟi eṉpatu piṟi vaṭivil ceyta kaḻuttaṇi. cantirap piṟiyum cantirakāramum orē vitamāṉa aṇikalaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
20. carappaḷi
itu ciṟu ciṟu kuṇṭukaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭu poṉ caraṭṭil kōkkappaṭṭirukkum, iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷāka irukkum - carappaḷi aṇintu vanta peṇ eṉṟu muṟkālattil perumiyākap pēcik koḷvārkaḷ.
21. cavaṭi
kaṉamāṉa poṉ takaṭukaḷi piṟi pōl ceytu oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu iṇikkappaṭṭiruppatu. cantirakāram, carappaḷi cavaṭi aṇivatu peruṅ kuṭumpattiṟkuriya marapu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. iṉṟu inta naki vaḻakkil illi.
22. caṉṉavīram
caṉṉavīram piralampam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum. itu pūṇul māli, kirivēyakam, nākayaññacūttiram, irattiṉapantam, upak kirīvam, nitalāvali ivikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭuvatu. ivi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇintu toṅkum. itu iṉṟu teyva uruvaṅkaḷukku aṇiyappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
23. cūlam
muṟkālattil ivvita aṇi oṉṟiruntatu. āṉāl itip paṟṟi namakku viḷakkamāṉa ilakkiyac cāṉṟō piṟa cāṉṟukaḷō kiṭikkavilli.
24. cuttumaṇi
cuttumaṇi poṟṟakaṭṭāl pukalākac ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷē meḻukaṭikkappaṭṭa pala maṇikaḷiyuṭiya oru nalla vēlip pāṭaminta kaḻuttaṇi. inta cuttumaṇi nelli māvaṭṭattil iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. ovvoru maṇikkum peyar uṇṭu. itu vaṭṭamākavum nīṇṭa caturamākavum uruṇṭiyākavum catura mākavum paṭṭamākavum ceyyappaṭṭa palavitamāṉa maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ. itaṉ naṭuvē maṅkala aṇiyākiya tāliyik kōttu aṇintu vantaṉar.  kim peṇkaḷ inta aṇi kalaṉi aṇivatilli. intac cuttu maṇikaḷ nāṭṭukku nāṭu vēṟu paṭṭirukkum.
25. tāli
iṉṟu aṇintu varum tirumaṇa aṇiyaṉṟu. itil palavaki yuṇṭu.
(a) aimpaṭit tāli : itu, ciṟuvarkaḷ aṇi. tirumāliṉ aimpaṭikaḷāṉa caṅku cakkaram, kati, kaṭakam, cārṅkam eṉṉum āyuta vaṭivil aṇi ceytu ciṟuvarkaḷukku paḻaṅkālat tamiḻar aṇintu vantaṉar. itu aimpaṭittāli eṉappeṟum. 42
(ā) tālik koḻuntu : tālik koḻuntu paḻaṅkāla aṇiyākat teriyavilli. āṉāl tālik koḻuntit taṭaṅ kaḻuttil pūṇṭu, eṉṟu nālāyirat tivviyap pirapantam kūṟukiṟatu.43
(i) ciṟuneṟṟāli : cila arumpukaḷit toḻil vaṉmi kāṇa naccup pāmpiṉ pal varicipōl niralpaṭa amittatu ciṟu neṟṟāli. itu oru kaḻuttaṇi.44
(ī) paṉṉirittāli : pāmpiṉ palpōṉṟu pacum poṉ ṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa, paṟkaḷi niralākak kōtta paṉṉirittāli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu pāmpeyiṟṟaṉṉa paṉṉirit tāli eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu.45
(u) paṉmaṇittāli : paṉmaṇittāliyum  paḻaṅkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷ variciyil kāṇappaṭavilli. mummaṇikkācum paṉmaṇittāliyum eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu.46
(ū) pulippaltāli : pulippallip poṉṉāl potintu kaḻuttil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉip pulippal tāli eṉpar. muṟkālattil puliyōṭu pōrāṭi, veṉṟavīraṉ taṉ maṉivikkup paricāka inta aṇiyi aṇivittu vantāṉ.47
(e) pulinakattāli : puliyi veṉṟa vīraṉ ataṉ nakaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu vantu taṉ tuṇivikkup paricāka aḷittāṉ. avaḷ atip poṟṟakaṭṭāl potintu taṉ kaḻuttaṇiyāka aṇintu vantāḷ. ataṟkup puli nakattāli eṉap peyariṭap paṭṭatu.
(ē) poṉṟāli : poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa kaḻuttaṇi; itu maṅkala aṇi aṉṟu.
(ai) piṉṟāli : piṉṟāli, pūṭṭavum kaḻaṟṟavum vallatāy irupuṟaṅkaḷilum  tuḷiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ciṟukuṇṭukaḷ vikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. ivaṟṟip piṇikkak kokki oṉṟum uṇṭu. itik kokkuvāy eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu kokkiṉvāypōl iruppa tāl kokkuvāy eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. kōppataṟku eḷitāka iruppatāl kōkkuvāy eṉṟu tiripuṟṟu; appāl kokku vāyāka māṟiyatu. itu maṇikaḷ patitta pēraṇi. itaṉi periya mukappuṭiya muttāramākac ceytu itil navamaṇikaḷ patittup piṉpuṟam toṅkaviṭappaṭuvatāl piṉṟāli eṉap paṭṭatu. itu tirumaṇattil kaṭṭappaṭum tāliyaṉṟu.
(o) maṇittāli : maṇikaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru kaḻuttaṇi. itiviṭa atikamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ illi.
26. tāḻvaṭam
kaḻuttiṉ aṭiyil aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ kīḻ aṇiyappaṭum caraṭu pōṉṟa oru poṉ aṇikalaṉākum.
27. tiru
itu paḻaṅkālat tamiḻnāṭṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa kaḻuttaṇikaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. itu piṅkalanti nikaṇṭil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
28. tumpu
tumpu eṉpatu nāṭṭuk kōṭṭic ceṭṭip peṇkaḷ aṇiyum oruvakit tāli uru. itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟatu.
29. toṭar
itip paṟṟi atikam teriyavilli.
30. toṭiyal
toṭiyal eṉpatu poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa kaḻuttaṇi. āṉāl piṅkalanti nikaṇṭil toṭiyal oru tōḷ aṇi māli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu tōḷvari parantu kiṭappa tāl tōḷaṇiyākak kuṟippiṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉar.
31. nāṇ
ñāṇ, nāṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉi muṟkālattil poṉṉāl ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. poṉ kampiyāl tiraṭciyākap piṉṉappaṭṭa oru kaḻuttaṇi. iticcaraṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu vaḻakkil uḷḷa aṇi.
32. nuṇ toṭar
itu tamiḻar kaṇṭa paḻaṅkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. caṅkili nuṇtoṭar eṉṟu inta aṇikalaṉ cilappatikārattil ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itu paḻaṅkāla aṇikalaṉ eṉpataṟku nalla cāṉṟāka amintuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. (cilap. 6 : 99)
33. nellikkāy māli
itu paṇṭik kālat tamiḻar kaṇṭa oru toṉmiyāṉa aṇikalaṉākum. itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvaṇi iṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷiṭam vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu. āṉāl nelli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tamiḻ muculim kaḷiṭam vaḻakkil iruntu varum oru nallaṇiyākum. iti muculim peṇmaṇikaḷ viruppuṭaṉ aṇivar.
34. nērñcaṅkili
nērñcaṅkili poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa oru ciṟiya aṇi kalaṉākum. itu kaḻuttaṇi.
35. patakkam
patakkam, caraṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil kōkkappaṭṭu kaḻuttil aṇiyappaṭum oru aṇi. itu kalliḻittuk kaḻuttil muṉpuṟam toṅkum oru vaṭṭamāṉa aṇikalaṉākum.48
36. pavaḷamāli
pavaḷaṅkaḷ kōttamāli pavaḷa māli eṉappaṭṭatu. itil iṭiiṭiyē poṉmaṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. pavaḷam civappākavum veḷḷiyākavum irukkum. civappup pavaḷam ciṟappumikkatu.
37. paṉmaṇi māli
pala maṇikaḷiyum muttukkaḷiyum pavaḷaṅkaḷiyum poṉṉiyum tokuttup paṉmaṇimāli eṉa oruvaki māli ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṟkup paṉmaṇimāli eṉap peyar.49
38. pūṅkoṭi poṟkalam
itu pūṅkoṭi vaṭivāṉa poṉṉaṇi ākum. ivvaṇi peruṅkatiyil ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.50
39. poṉṉarimāli
tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ viṟaliyarkkum pāṭiṉiyarkkum avarkaḷatu tiṟaṉukkākap poṉṉari māliyip paricāka aḷittuvantaṉar.51
40. poṭṭu
poṉṉāl ceyta oruvakittāli. itu paḻiya aṇi kalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. itu irāmāyaṇattil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
41. poṟcaraṭu
poṟkampiyāl piṉṉappaṭṭuk koṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru caraṭu. itaṟku aḻakiya mukappu uṇṭu.
42. poṉñāṇ
poṉ kampiyāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru poṉ koṭiyākum. itu oru kaḻuttaṇi.
43. poṉmaṇimāli
poṉmaṇikaḷ pala kōkkap peṟṟa oru aḻakiya kaḻuttaṇi.
44. maṅkala aṇi
maṅkala aṇi eṉpatu maṇamakaṉ kaṭṭum tirumaṇac ciṉṉam.59
45. maṇivaṭam
irattiṉamaṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa oru vaṭam. inta aṇi kalaṉil mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷ kōkkappaṭṭu iruppatum uṇṭu.
46. maṇiyāram
ituvum poṉ maṇikaḷ kōttup peritākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru aḻakiya aṇikalaṉākum. itu muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷāl virumpi aṇiyappaṭuvatu.
47. maṇimiṭipavaḷam
ivvaṇikalaṉ poṉmaṇiyum pavaḷamum kalantu aṇiyap paṭum oru aṇikalaṉākum.52
48. matāṇi
kaḻuttaṇikalaṉiṉ toṅkalukku matāṇi eṉṟu peyar tiṇkatir matāṇi eṉṟu maturik kāṇṭattil kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu.
49. marutaṅkāy māli
itu mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattup poṉ aṇi. iṉṟum tamiḻaka muculimkaḷ marutaṅkāy vaṭivilāṉa inta aṇiyi aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
50. malarccaram
itu poṉṉāl taṉittaṉip pūvaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟup poṉ allatu veḷḷik kampiyāl iṇikkap peṟṟa oru caram.
51. māṅkāy māli
itu ciṟu māmpiñcu vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭa oru kaḻuttaṇi; poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṉ aṭippakuti maṭṭamāka irukkum. itu puṭic ciṟpampōl muṉpakkam tuṟuttik koṇ ṭirukkum. itu mikat toṉmiyāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu.
52. miḷaku māli
miḷaku vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa poṉ aṇi. itu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷatāy irukkum. itu kottamalli māliyip pōl mēlē vari variyāṉa kōṭukaḷ illātatāl aḻukkup piṭiyātu eṉṟu peṇkaḷ piriyappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. uḷḷē pukalāka irukkum. itu vaḻakkil uḷḷa aṇi.
53. muttuvaṭam
itu naṭuvē periya muttum iru puṟamum vara vara ciṟiya muttākac ciṟuttuk koṇṭē pōy iṟutiyil mikac ciṟu muttukkaḷ kōkkappaṭṭirukkum.
54. muttumāli
itu muttukkaḷālāṉa māli. oru kālattil nelli māvaṭṭattup peṇkaḷ piriyamāka immāliyi aṇintu vantaṉar. muttumāli eṉṟu taṅkaḷ teyvattiṟkup peyar iṭṭaṉar. muttumāli eṉṟu piḷḷikaḷukkup peyar vippa tum uṇṭu.
55. muttu vaḷḷi
muttuvaḷḷi yeṉṉum poṉṉaṇi caṅkak kālattil iruntatākat teriyavilli.55
56. mummaṇikkācu
mummaṇikkācum paṉmaṇit tāliyum eṉṟu peruṅ katiyil ivvaṇi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.56
57. mōkaṉamāli
itu aḻaki poṉ kaḻuttaṇi; piṟkālattil eḻuntatu eṉṟālum iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
58. vaṉṉacaram
pōr vīrarkaḷ aṇiyum aṇi. vīrarkaḷ pakivarkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷip piṭuṅki kaṇmaṇikaḷiyum yāṉikaḷiṉ tantaṅkaḷi liruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa muttukkaḷiyum narampukaḷil kōttup pōrkkaḷattil pūṇum oru kaḻuttaṇiyākum.
59. vīrac caṅkili
poṉṉāl peritākac ceyyappaṭṭirukkum oru kaḻuttaṇi. muṟkālattil maṉṉarkaḷ. pōr maṟavarkaḷukku avarkaḷ āṟṟiya vīrac ceyalukkup paricāka vīraccaṅkili aḷippar.
