pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ
II
tōḷ, ki, kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ
tali, kātu, kaḻuttu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷ aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ uḷḷavi. viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patippikkap peṟṟavi. ivi taliyāya aṇikaḷ eṉalām. tōḷ, ki, kiviral, iṭuppu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟuvaṉa iṭittara aṇikaḷ eṉalām. kāl, kālviral mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil aṇiyum aṇikaḷ kaṭittara aṇikaḷ eṉalām.
tōḷ aṇi
inta aṇi muḻaṅkikku mēl aṇiyappaṭum. itu vevvēṟu peyarkaḷuṭaṉ vevvēṟu nāṭukaḷil uḷḷatu. camīpa kālattil vaṅki eṉṟa aṇiyip puyattiṉ kīḻē peṇkaḷ aṇintuvantaṉar. inta aṇiyic ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ kēyūram eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itip peritum nāṭṭiyamāṭum peṇkaḷ aṇintu vantaṉar. iṉṟu pala airōppiyap peṇkaḷ kūṭa aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar. cila mēṉāṭṭup peṇkaḷ muḻaṅkikku mēlē oru poṉ vaḷiyi aṇintu ati ārmaleṭ (Armlet) eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭu atil oru ciṟu kikkuṭṭiyiyum cerukik koḷ kiṉṟaṉar. atu iṉṟiya nākarika aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟākum.
tōḷaṇikaḷ piṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷip pōl atikamāka vaḷaravilli. nām aṟinta aḷavil tōḷaṇi vakikaḷ cumār 10-kku uṭpaṭṭavikaḷē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
tōḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ
1. kēyūram (vaṅki): (a) irattiṉavaṅki (ā) pācip pantu (i) aṅkatam (ī) kaṭattirayam 2. kontik kāy 3. cutti 4. tōḷ patakkam 5. toṭi : (1) kaḻal toṭi (2) kōl toṭi (3) tōḷ valayam (calāki) 6. vāku valayam (tōḷ vaḷi)
1. kōyūram : kēyūram eṉpatu vaṅkiyēyākum. ivvaṇikalaṉ muḻaṅkikku mēlē aṇiyappaṭum. itu amippil neḷivu (muṭakku) mōtiram pōṉṟu irukkum; āṉāl peritāka irukkum. itaṉ amippu orē vitantāṉ. itil eṭṭu itaḻ tāmarip pūvi amikkalām. itaṉ muṉpuṟam kampikaḷ mītu iṉṟiya 50 kācu vaṭivil pūkkaḷ pōṉṟu amikkappaṭṭirukkum. itaṉ naṭuvil aḻakākac cataṅkikaḷ kōttut toṅkaviṭappaṭṭirukkum. inta aṇiyil muttukkaḷō maṇikaḷō kiṭiyā. itu makaḷirkku aḻaku ceyyum aṇikalaṉāka muṉṉar matikkappaṭṭu vantatu. itu cilappatikāram pōṉṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. muṉṉar kēyūrattip pōṉṟa pala vaki aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi itiṉiṉṟu ciṟ cila vēṟupāṭukaḷiyuṭiyaṉa. (a) irattiṉa vaṅki : itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ. irattiṉaṅkaḷ patittu kēyūra amippil ceyyappaṭṭa puya aṇikku irattiṉa vaṅki eṉpārkaḷ. iṉṟum kōyilkaḷil teyvaṅkaḷukku aṇivikkap peṟukiṟatu. (ā) pācippantu : pācip pantum kēyūra vaṭivil pācik kottup pōṉṟa amippuṭiyatu. itil ilikaḷum vēṟu pala uruvaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu amikkappaṭṭatip pattira pūritam eṉpar. pattira pūritattiṉ kīḻ puṟamuḷḷa kāmpu puyam muḻuvatum paraviyirukkum. itaṉ akalam 3, 4 aṅkulam irukkum. akalam uyarattil pāti irukkum. (i) aṅkatam : kēyūram pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉēyākum. peritum namatu teyvaṅkaḷukku ukantatu. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil inta aṇikalaṉkaḷ paṟṟi atikam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kēyūram puya mūlattil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ. aṅkatam (puyavalayam) tōḷ mattiyil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉ. (ī) kaṭattirayam: kēyūram pōṉṟateṉiṉum ciṟ cila vēṟupāṭukaḷ irukkum. kaṇuk kikku mēl aṇiyappaṭum aḻakiya aṇi. poṉṉāl eḻiluṟac ceyyap peṟṟatu. cilavaṟṟil iṭi iṭiyē oru cila maṇikaḷum patippikkap peṟṟirukkum. itu atikam vaḻakkil illi. nūṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē vaḻakkiḻantu pōyiṟṟu. āṉāl kōyilkaḷil allatu ciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇa lām.
2. kontikkāy
itu muṟkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari tamiḻakattil vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. pavaḷa maṇikaḷum poṉ maṇikaḷum nūlil kōttu muḻaṅkikku mēlē puyattiṟkuk kīḻē aṇiyappaṭum. itu atikamāṉa vili matippu illāta aṇiyāy iruntatāl ēḻi makkaḷiṭamum iṭam peṟṟiruntatu.
3. cuttu
cuttum inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakuti vari tamiḻakattil vaḻakkil iruntu, iṉṟu maṟintupōṉa aṇikalaṉ kaḷil oṉṟāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟu poṟkampikaḷi oṉṟāka iṇittu muṉ puṟam mukappu vittuk kīḻē mūṉṟu cataṅkikaḷ toṅkumpaṭi ceytu, aḻaku poliya aṇintu vanta poṉ aṇi. mukappil oṉṟiraṇṭu maṇikaḷum iṭam peṟṟirukkum.
4. tōḷ patakkam
tōḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ, patakkam oru aḻakiya aṇikala ṉākum. itu poṟṟakaṭṭil māṇikkaṅkaḷ aḻutti muttukkaḷ patittu aḻakuṟac ceyyap peṟṟirukkum. muṟkālattil aracikaḷ āvaluṭaṉ iti aṇintu vantaṉar. itu aḻakāka irukkum.
5. toṭi : (1) kaḻal toṭi
muṟkālat tamiḻakattil vīrarkaḷ tōḷkaḷil toṭiyaṇivatu vaḻakkam. atu nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ uṭiyatāka oḷirum. aḻaku vāyntatāka irukkum.1
(2) kōl toṭi
itaṉ ariya vēlippāṭukaḷiyuṉṉik kōl toṭi eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭatu. itu paḻaṅkālat tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷ aṇinta cīriya aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu.2
(3) tōḷ valayam (calāki)
tōḷ valayam, calāki eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭum. tōḷ valayam makaḷir aṇivatu. talivaṉip pirinta talivi tōḷ vaḷikaḷik kaḷivatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭu kiṟatu.3
intat tōḷ vaḷi yāṉi maruppiṉālum, caṅkiṉālum, veḷḷiyiṉālum kūṭac ceyyap peṟṟu makaḷir aṇintu vantaṉar. mātavi māṇikka vaḷiyoṭu, muttut tōḷvaḷiyum aṇin tiruntāḷ4 eṉṟu cilappatikāram ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟatu. aḻakiya kaṇṭikiyoṭu tōḷukku muttu vaḷiyum aṇintiruntāḷ eṉṟu uriyāciriyarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum kaḻuttu vaṭattil piṉ tālikku iraṇṭu pakkamum pakuttup pōntu vāku valayattōṭu toṭakkik kiṭappatōr aṇikalaṉ. kāmarkaṇṭiki yuṭaṉiṉamaṇi neruṅkiṉa tōḷ vaḷiyit tōḷukku aṇintu eṉṟu viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar uri nūṟ pulavarkaḷ.
6. vākuvalayam
iṉṟu makkaḷiṭam itu vaḻakkil illi. teyvaṅkaḷukku aṇivikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu akalattiṉ aḷavi iru maṭaṅkākki atil kāl paṅku kuṟittu, allatu akalattip pōl iru maṭaṅku uyaram uḷḷatāy amikkappaṭṭa poṉ aṇikalaṉākum. itil maṇikaḷum patikkap peṟṟirukkum. iti mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiliruntē perumpālum maṉṉarkaḷum teyvaṅkaḷum aṇintu vantatākat terikiṟatu. mēlum vāku valayamum tōḷ vaḷiyum orē vitamāṉa aṇikalaṉāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
vikiyāṟṟil putuppuṉal poṅki eḻuntatu. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum puṉal nīrāṭṭu viḻā koṇṭāṭiṉar. veḷḷam, peṇkaḷ aṇintirunta nakikaḷ cilavaṟṟip paṟittuc ceṉṟatu. ataṉic caṅkak kālap pulavar miyōṭak kōlaṉār, nīrāṭiya varkaḷiṭamiruntu puṉal paṟittuc ceṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷik kuṟip piṭukiṉṟār. atil vākuvalayam kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.5 itaṉ mūlam itu paḻaṅkālat tamiḻar aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. tōḷ vaḷiyamum, vāku valiyamum oṉṟāka irukkalām.
VI. mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷ
mārpil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷ mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉappaṭum. inta mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟip perumpālāṉa aṟiñarkaḷ kuṟippiṭātu viṭṭuḷḷaṉar. ciṟpaṅkaḷil mārpaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉatu naṇparum ciṟpakkali ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭiruntavarumāṉa kālañ ceṉṟa tiru. to. mu. pākarat toṇṭimāṉ avarkaḷ kiruṣṇāpuram ciṟpak karuvūlattik kaṇṭu kaḷitta poḻutu oru maṅkiyiṉ mārpil aṇintirunta aḻakiya vēlippāṭuḷḷa oru mārpaṇiyi nāṉ kuṟippiṭṭu viyantu pārāṭṭiṉēṉ. avar atu mārpaṇi aṉṟu, mārpuk kaccu; piṉṉal vēliyāl eḻilpeṟac ceyyappeṟṟa kaviṉuṟuṅkaccu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. avaratu kūṟṟip pala aṟiñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl ilakkiyaṅkaḷil maṅkiyarkaḷ mārpaṇi kalaṉkaḷip paṟṟi maṭṭumaṉṟi maṉṉarkaḷiṉ mārpaṇikalaṉ kaḷip paṟṟiyum teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum pala aṟiñarkaḷ aṟintō aṟiyāmalō mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatē illi. cilar muṟkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ mārpil aṇikalaṉ aṇintu vantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tamiḻar nākarikat tukku iḻukkeṉṟu karuti atik kuṟippiṭāmal viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. paṇṭikkālat tamiḻaka makaḷir cilar toṭiyaṇi aṇintu vantaṉar; iṭiyaṇi pūṇṭu vantaṉar.  nuṇṇiya iṭiyil eḻil oḻukum pūṅtukiliṉ mītu aṇintuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi āṭiyiṉuḷ iṭuppil alkul maṟiyumāṟu aṇinta pavaḷamēkaliyik kuṟippiṭum āciriyar, mārpaṇiyik kuṟippiṭa ēṉ piṉtaṅka vēṇṭum? mārpil āṭi yaṇiyāta kālattil mārpaṇi pūṇṭatum, kaccaṇintatum toyyil eḻutiyatum ciṟanta nākarīkamākak karutappaṭumēyoḻiya nākarikak kuṟivākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. mēlum uṇmiyi urippatu nākarikamēyoḻiya ati maṟippatu nākarikam aṉṟu. camīpattil tamiḻ ilakkiyattil aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟu tiruvaṉantapuram tiruniṟi celvi irā. nty«khŸ, v«.V., tamatu nūlil mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷ palavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟār avarukku eṉatu pārāṭṭu urittākuka.
mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷum viḷakkamum varumāṟu :-
(1) itayavācaṉi (2) nāmaravaḷi (3) nittila matāṇi (4) pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam (5) pūṇ (6) poṟkaccu
mārpaṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. itaya vācaṉi
caṅkakkāla maṉṉarkaḷ mārpil maṇippūṇ aṇintirunta ṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. kallār maṇippūṇ mārpu eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu.6 ilip perumpūṇum itayavācaṉiyum eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu. itayavācaṉi ōr mārpaṇi eṉṟu uriyāciriyarkaḷ urikkiṉṟaṉar.7
2. nāmaravaḷi
nāmaravaḷi eṉṉum mārpaṇi paḻaṅkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. āṉāl peruṅkati nāmaravaḷiyum kāmar kiviṉi eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.8 itaṉāl nāmaravaḷi piṟkāla makaḷir aṇinta mārpaṇi eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
3. nittilamatāṇi
āṇkaḷ mārpil aṇiyum oru aṇikalamākum. tirumāl mārpil aḻakuvāynta muttukkaḷālāṉa aḻakiya patakkam veṇ ṇiṟattālum vaṭṭa vaṭivālum muḻumatiyi ottiruntatu eṉṟu paripāṭal ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟatu.9
4. pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam
itu makaḷir mārpiṟ pūṇum oru aḻakiya naki eṉṟu tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākat terikiṟatu.10
5. pūṇ
maṅkiyar taṅkaḷ koṅki maṟiya mārpil pūṇ eṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭu vantaṉar. itu ili vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟiruntatu. cīvaka cintāmaṇi ilippūṇ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu kiṟatu. muṟkālattil pūṇkaḷ pūṇṭa pūviyar tam kātalari muyaṅkiyatāl avarkaḷ mārpil uḷḷa cantaṉattil pūṇ uruvam patintatu, eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.11
6. poṟkaccu
poṟkampiyāl ceytu mārpil pūṇum kaccu vaṭivamāṉa oru poṉ aṇiyākum. atu pūṅkoṭi vaṭivamāka aḻakuṟa amin tirukkum. itu pala ciṟpaṅkaḷil aḻakuṟat tikaḻkiṟatu.12
VII. ki aṇikalaṉkaḷ
ki aṇikalaṉkaḷ uṟuppukaḷil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷil oru vakic ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. ēṉiya uṟuppu aṇikalaṉkaḷip pōlaṉṟi ivi yāvarum kāṇum iyalpuṭiyaṉa. kikaḷil makaḷir palavaki vaḷikaḷi aṇintuk koṇṭu naṭakkum poḻutu āṭum poḻutu kutikkum poḻutu vaḷikaḷ kalakala veṉṟu olikkum. ataṉāl makaḷirip pulavarkaḷ oṇṭoṭi makaḷir eṉṟu urippar. mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nila vāḻkki naṭattiya poḻutu makaḷir kikaḷil koṭikaḷiyum malarkaḷiyum kāyppūkkaḷiyum muṉ kiyilum - maṇik kaṭṭiṉmītum kaṭṭi vantaṉar. appāl mutiyavar kaḷ tam makkaḷip pēy picācu vilaṅku mutaliyavikaḷiṉiṉṟu kāppataṟku mantiram ōtiya nūlkaḷik kiyil kaṭṭi vantaṉar. annūl kāppu eṉappaṭṭatu. appāl anta nūlil poṉ takaṭṭil mantiraṅkaḷ eḻuti ati maṭakkik kaṭṭi vantaṉar.  piṉ mantiram eḻutiya takaṭṭi oru poṟ kuḻāyil aṭittu aṇintu vantaṉar. intak kāppu eṉṉum nūl piṟkālattil kāppu eṉṉum oru poṉ aṇiyākat tiripuṟṟu eḻuntatu. muṟkālattil muṉkiyil caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. paḻaṅkālat til koṟkiyil caṅkaṟukkum toḻil peritum naṭipeṟṟu vantuḷḷatu. aṅkup pūmiyiṉ kīḻ iruntu akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭum aṟukkappaṭṭa caṅkut tuṇṭukaḷē itaṟku nalla cāṉṟākum. āticcanallūr paḻam perum nākarikattil tamiḻar caṅku vaḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.13 poṉvaḷikaḷum, poṟṟoṭikaḷum, poṟkaṭakaṅkaḷum, poṟkāppum, poṟ kaṅkaṇaṅkaḷumākap piṟkālattil eṇṇaṟṟa muṉki aṇikaḷ perukiṉa. iṟutiyil navamaṇikaḷ patitta pala vaḷikaḷum kāppukaḷum eḻuntaṉa.
1. āṭakam 2. āḷikkāppu 3. iṭiccari 4. kaṅkaṇam 5. kaṭakam 6. kāppu 7. kuruṭu cari 8. kik kaṭṭu 9. kic cari 10. kivaḷi 
11. kolucu 12. caṅkuvaḷi 13. cūṭakam 14. tantavaḷi 15. toṭi 
16. tōṭā 17. navarattiṉa vaḷi 18. nīlakkaṭic ceṟi 19. pariyakam 
20. pavaḷavaḷi 21. pācittā maṇi 22. pāṭlā 23. pintoṭi 
24. poṉ vaḷi 25. makaravāyp purivaḷi 26. muṟukku 27. muttuk kolucu 28. vālvaḷi 29. vaṇṭu 30. valampuri vaḷi.
1. āṭakam
āṭakam aḻakiya muṉkip poṉ vaḷiyākum. itil palvēṟu maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum. mukappil māṇikkamum pattikaḷil viramum aḻuttap peṟṟirukkum. itaṉ viḷimpukaḷil aḻakiya pū vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. itu piṟkālat til pāṭṭil allatu pāṭṭilā eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu inta aṇikalaṉum vaḻakkil illi. āṉāl irupatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ pāṭṭilā eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ nelli māvaṭṭattil kūṭa vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu.
2. āḷik kāppu
poṉṉāl vaṭṭa vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa kāppu ākum. itaṉ mukappu aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itil aḻakiya maṇikaḷ patikkappaṭṭirukkum. itu iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
3. iṭic cari
kiyil aṇiyappaṭum oru akaṉṟa kic cariyākum. itu kāppu, kolucu ākiya aṇikaḷukku iṭiyē aṇiyappaṭṭu vantatu.
4. kaṅkaṇam
kaṅkaṇam kaṭakam pōṉṟa oru aṇi. itu nīṇṭa kālattiṟku muṉpē vaḻakkil iruntu maṟintuviṭṭatu. āṉāl poṉ kaṅkaṇa aṇikkup patilāka kaṅkaṇam eṉṟu meḻukāl ceyyap peṟṟa aṇiyi irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭup pakuti vari makkaḷ aṇintu vantaṉar.14
5. kaṭakam
kaṭakam, eṉṉum aṇi kikaḷil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇintu varum oru kiyaṇiyākum. itu makara mīṉ kavviya koṭi vaṭivam uṭiyatāka iruntatu. ik kaṭakam ili vaṭivamum peṟṟiruntatu. cūṭakamum kaṭakamum oṉṟeṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉar.15
6. kāppu
poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru kiyaṇi. itu poṟṟa kaṭṭāl pukalākac ceytu uḷḷē meḻukaṭittu nīṇṭa kāla māka vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. iṉṟu illi.
7. kuruṭu cari
kuruṭu pōṉṟa poṉ aṇi ākum. itu ciṟu vaṭaṅkaḷ toṅkumpaṭiyākac ceytu kiyil aṇintuk koḷḷappaṭum oru kiyaṇiyākum.
8. kik kaṭṭu
makaḷir, poṉ maṇiyum pavaḷamum kōttuk kiyil kaṭṭik koḷḷum oru paḻaṅ kālak ki aṇi, kik kaṭṭu eṉappaṭum. kiyil kaṭṭappaṭuvatāl kik kaṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
9. kiccari
kic cariki eṉpatu kāppukku mēṟ pakutiyil kiyil aṇiyappaṭum cariki eṉṉum aṇikalaṉēyākum. itu mēlē paruttum kīḻē ciṟuttum ciṟu kuḻāy vaṭivil irukkum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉākum. irupatām  nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil veḷḷiyāl ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar.
10. kivaḷi
kivaḷi pala vakippaṭum. atu vāl vaḷi, cil vaḷi, niri vaḷi, poṉ vaḷi, valampuri vaḷi, tanta vaḷi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. cilvaḷi viṟaliyarkku aṭiyāḷamāka aṇiyap paṭṭatu.16
11. kolucu
itu kāppup pōṉṟa oru poṉ aṇikalaṉ. āṉāl itaṉ mēlē naṭuvil uyarntum, iru puṟamum tāḻntum koṭik kayiṟukaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāy cuṟṟappaṭṭatu pōla ceyyappaṭṭu irukkum. piṟkālattil meḻukāl ceyta kolucukaḷi vaḷiyal kārarkaḷ ceytu viṟṟu vantaṉar.
12. caṅku vaḷi
muṟkālat tamiḻar ērāḷamākac caṅku vaḷikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. koṟki eṉṉum pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉam paḻaṅkālattil caṅkut toḻilukkup peyar peṟṟatāy iruntatu. nakkīraṉār caṅkaṟukkum kulattic cērntavar. 17
13. cūṭakam
cūṭakam oru poṉ aṇi. itu viram patikkap peṟṟu aḻakuṟat tikaḻum kiyaṇiyākum. cūṭakamum pāṭṭilāvum oṉṟeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uṇṭu. mātavi viraṅkaḷ patitta vili yuyarnta aṇiyākiya cūṭakattōṭu pariyakam poṉ vaḷi, caṅku vaḷi, pavaḷa vaḷi mutaliyavikaḷi aṇintu aḻaku poliyat tikaḻntāḷ, eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu.
14. tantavaḷi
muṟkālat tamiḻaka makaḷir caṅku vaḷi, pavaḷa vaḷiyōṭu yāṉi maruppiṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa tanta vaḷiyiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. tanta vaḷi caṅku vaḷiyiviṭa mikap paḷapaḷappākat tikaḻntatu; viliyilumuyarntatu.
15. toṭi
paṇṭiya tamiḻakattu mātarkaḷ palvakit toṭikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. naṇṭiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa aṟuppu vēlikaḷ ceyta aḻakiya toṭikaḷit tam ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukku aṇintu vantaṉar.20
16. tōṭā
itu kiyil aṇiyum oruvakik kāppu. piṟkālattil pulavarkaḷukkum vīrarkaḷukkum maṉṉarkaḷ paricākap poṉṉāl ceyta tōṭāvi aḷittu vantaṉar.
17. navarattiṉa vaḷi
oṉpatu vaki maṇikaḷum aḻuttic ceyyappeṟṟa poṉvaḷi navarattiṉavaḷi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itaṉi muṟkāla araca makaḷir ārvamuṭaṉ aṇintu vantaṉar. iṉṟu ivvaṇi vaḻakkil illi. piṟkālattil periya vaṇika makaḷir aṇiyat talippaṭṭaṉar.
18. nīlakkaṭic ceṟi
nīlak kaṭic ceṟi eṉpatu paṇṭik kālat tamiḻaka makaḷir kiyil ceṟivāka aṇiyum oru kiyaṇi. ki aṉṉap pūttirukkum kāntaḷ malarmītu vaṇṭukaḷ iruppatu nīlakkaṭi aṇintiruppatu pōl kāṇappaṭum. itaṉik kalittoki aḻakuṟa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.21
19. pariyakam
pariyakam poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟa oru kikkāppu ākum. itaṉik kiccari eṉṟu kūṟuvār. ikkālattil vaḻakkil irunta veḷḷic cariki vakikaḷil ituvum oṉṟā allatu vēṟā eṉṟu teriyavilli. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil pariyakam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
20. pavaḷavaḷi
paḻaṅkālattup pāviyar caṅkuvaḷikaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟip pavaḷavaḷikaḷiyum pāṅkuṭaṉ aṇintaṉar. pavaḷavaḷikaḷōṭu kuvaḷip pacuttaṇṭiṉiyum āmpal taṇṭiṉiyum aṇintu vantaṉar eṉṟu caṅkak kālap pulavar palar eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar.
21. pācittāmam
itu uyarnta marakatamaṇikaḷ patittuc ceyyap peṟṟa poṉ vaḷiyākum. ik kālattil kūṭa ivvaṇiyik kuḻantikaḷukkuc celvarkaḷ aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
22. pāṭlā
kāppup pōṉṟa oru poṉki aṇi. itaṉ mukappu naṭuvil uyarntu uruṇṭum iru puṟamum ṭayamaṇṭ vaṭivil iru takaṭukaḷ iṇikkappaṭṭumirukkum.
23. pintoṭi
poṉvaḷiyiṉ mukappil marakata maṇikaḷ patittuc ceyyap peṟṟatu pintoṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉip paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ virumpi aṇintu vantaṉar.
24. poṉvaḷi
poṉvaḷi, poṟṟoṭi eṉappaṭum. ciṟu kuḻanti kaḷukkup poṟṟoṭi aṇintu vantaṉar.  iṉṟum ivvaḻakkam iruntu varukiṟatu. muṟkālattil makkaḷ poṟṟoṭi aṇintu vantaṉar.24
25. makarappakuvāy
makaramīṉ kavviya koṭi uruvattil ceyyappeṟṟa vaḷi makarap pakuvāy eṉappaṭum. mariyilik kammamoṭu makaraṅkavvi eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu.25
26. muṟukku
poṟkampikaḷi muṟukki oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟu porunta iṇittuc ceyyap peṟṟavaḷiyē muṟukku eṉappaṭṭatu.
27. muttuk kolucu
poṟkoluciṉ mēlē variciyāka cumār 70 muttukkalāka ovvoru kolucilum patippikkappeṟṟa aḻakiya kolucu muttuk kolucu eṉappaṭum.
28. vālvaḷi
itu veḷḷiyāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru kivaḷi. kikaḷil mañcaḷ, civappu, pacci vaṇṇaṅkaḷ kūṭiya kiyaṇiyōṭu veḷḷiniṟak kiyaṇiyum cērntilaṅka veḷḷiyāl ceyta vāl vaḷiyum caṅkiṉāṟ ceyta vālvaḷiyum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
29. vaṇṭu
ituvum poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa oru kiyaṇiyākum. itip paṟṟi vēṟu oṉṟum namakkut teriyavilli.
30. valampurivaḷi
valampuric caṅkiṉāṟ ceytavaḷi valampurivaḷi eṉa aḻikkappeṟum. valampuri vaḷiyi muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ mika āvalōṭu aṇivar. iṉṟu tamiḻakattil caṅkuvaḷi aṇiyum vaḻakkam maṟintupōyviṭṭatu. vaṭa intiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻakkil irukkiṟatu. paṇam paṭitta peṇkaḷ viravaḷiyōṭu caṅkuvaḷiyum aṇivar.26
valampurivaḷi aḻaku aṇiyāka maṭṭumaṉṟi oru camayac cārpuḷḷa aṇiyākavum karutappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉāl pala piṇikaḷ akalum; pillicūṉiyam pōṉṟavi aṇukā eṉṟu nampap paṭukiṟatu.
VIII. kiviral aṇikalaṉkaḷ
mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālattiliruntē tamiḻarkaḷ ki viral kaḷil palavitamāṉa aṇikaḷip pūṇṭu vantaṉar. kiviral aṇi mōtiramviral aṇi, viralāḻi, kaṇiyāḻi eṉṟellām aḻikkappaṭṭu vanta ivvaṇi poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. appāl palavita maṇikaḷ vittu aḻakuṟac ceyyap peṟṟu aṇintu varappeṟṟatu. inta mōtiram ātiyil atikamāka pū vaṭi vākavum ili vaṭivākavum iruntu piṉṉar pāmpu vaṭivamākavum, cuṟāmīṉ vāy vaṭivamākavum ceyyap peṟṟatu. ātiyil makkaḷ caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷic ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari avvaḻakkam iruntu varukiṟatu. maṉṉarkaḷ tam pōr maṟavarkaḷukku ēṉāti eṉṟa paṭṭamum  ēṉāti eṉṉum poṉ mōtiramum paricaḷittu vantaṉar. aracarkaḷ mōtiraṅkaḷil tamatu araca ciṉṉatti (ilāñcaṉatti) amittu vantaṉar. ati makkaḷ matittu naṭantu vantaṉar. airōppiyarkaḷ mōtiratti maṇa aṇiyākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. iṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ illattilum kūṭa maṇaviḻāvil tālikaṭṭal, māli māṟṟal, mōtiram māṟṟal mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷukku mōtiram aṇivatum, maṇamakaḷ maṇamakaṉukku mōtiram aṇivatum maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷiṉ aṇṇaṉ allatu tampikku mōtiram paricaḷippatum tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭṭil naṭipeṟum maṇaṅkaḷil iṉṟum naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
1. ēṉāti mōtiram 2. caṅku mōtiram 3. civantip pū mōtiram 4. ciṟutāḷ 5. ceṅkēḷ kiḷarmaṇi 6. marakatat tāḷceṟi 7. maṇi mōtiram 8. māṇikkattāḷ ceṟi 9. muṭakku mōtiram 10. muttiri mōtiram 11. mōci 12. pakuvāy mōtiram 13. poṉ mōtiram, ivi mōtira vakikaḷ.
1. ēṉāti mōtiram
maṉṉarkaḷ, tam pōr maṟavarkaḷukku ēṉāti eṉṉum paṭṭamum, ēṉāti eṉṉum mōtiramum paricaḷittu vantaṉar. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ nūṟpā uriyil nakkīrar ēṉāti mōtiram paṟṟik kūṟukiṟār.27
2. caṅku mōtiram
caṅkiṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa mōtiram caṅku mōtiram. inta mōtiram paṇṭutoṭṭē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkil iruntu varukiṟatu. iṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil atikamāka vaḻakkil irukkiṟatu.
3. civantippū mōtiram
civantippū vaṭivil maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟa mōtiram civantippū mōtiram eṉappaṭum. immōtiram iṉṟum tamiḻakat til vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟu maṇikaḷ aḻuttap peṟṟirukkum.
4. ciṟutāḷ
kāntaḷ malar pōṉṟa melliya viralil avviral, maṟiyumpaṭi aṇiyum ciṟumaṇikaḷ patitta aḻakiya mōtirattic ciṟutāḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
5. ceṅkēḷ kiḷarmaṇi
kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟuc cenniṟa māṇikka maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. cenniṟak kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟatāl ceṅkēḷ kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. cemmalar pōṉṟa viralkaḷi aṇiceytu niṟkum. immōtiram iṉṟu vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. itu toṉmiyāṉa kālattil uḷḷatu. irattiṉam kaṭṭiyatu aṭukkāḷi.28
6. marakatat tāḷ ceṟi
ciṟu vayiraṅkaḷ cūḻa naṭuvē oru periya marakatamaṇi patittuc ceyyap peṟṟa mōtiram marakat tāḷ ceṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. inta mōtiram 2000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattil vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. vāṅkuvil vayirattu marakatat tāḷceṟi eṉṟu cilap patikāram kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉic cuṭṭu viralil aṇivar. itu vaṭṭap pū, marakatak kaṭiceṟi, marakatanāyakam eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum.
7. maṇi mōtiram
maṇikaḷ patittu aḻakuṟap poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa mōtiram maṇimōtiram eṉappaṭum. 29
8. māṇikkattāḷ
māṇikkaṅkaḷ cuṟṟilum patittu naṭuvil viram ceṟitta mōtiratti māṇikkat tāḷceṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu mikap paḻaṅkālattiliruntu aṇintu varappaṭum mōtiram.
9. muṭakku mōtiram
cuṟāmīṉ vāy tiṟantāṟ pōṉṟa vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa mōtiratti muṭakku mōtiram eṉṟaṉar nammuṉṉōr. piṉṉōr neḷi mōtiram eṉṟu neṭunelvāṭiyilum, cilappatikārat tilum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu iṉṟum neḷivu eṉṉum peyaril vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
10. muttiri mōtiram
maṉṉarkaḷ taṅkaḷ muttiri (ilāñcaṉam) poṟitta mōtirattik kiviralkaḷil aṇintu vantaṉar. mukkiya cācaṉaṅkaḷil inta muttiri mōtiram patippikkappeṟumām. pāṇṭiyarkaḷ iraṇṭu mīṉ ceṅkuttāy niṟkum vaṭivil uḷḷa muttiri mōtiratti aṇintu vantaṉar.
11. mōci
mōci muṉ vaḷiyōṭu caṅkiliyāl piṇikkap paṭṭiruntatu.31
12. pakuvāy
im mōtiram virinta vāyiyuṭiya mīṉ, pāmpu vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa poṉ mōtiram. inta mīṉ vaṭivamuḷḷa uruviṉ kaṇkaḷil maṇikaḷ patittiruppatum uṇṭu.
13. poṉ mōtiram
poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa mōtiram. poṉmōtiram eṉappaṭum. itil peyar poṟittalum uṇṭu. cīvakaṉ taṉ peyar poṟitta mōtirattik kuṇamālikku aḷittāṉ eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu.32
IX.  nucuppaṇikalaṉkaḷ
muṟkālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻnta makaḷir iṭuppil pala vitamāṉa iṭi aṇikaḷ aṇintu vantaṉar. āṇkaḷ ariyil ariñāṉ pūṇṭu vantaṉar. peṇkaḷum ariyil ñāṇ pūṇpa tuṇṭu. iṉṟu tiruccirāppaḷḷi māvaṭṭattil cila peṇkaḷ ariyil veḷḷi ñāṇ pūṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. ariyil kaṭṭiya cēli eḷitil nekiḻntu viṭātirukka cēliyi anta veḷḷi ñāṇāl iṟukap piṇittuḷḷaṉar. ciṟitu kālattiṟku muṉṉar iḷam peṇkaḷ oṭṭiyāṇam eṉṉum poṉ paṭṭikiyi ariyil pūṭṭi vantaṉar. appāl avarkaḷum ati naḻuva viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. āṭal makaḷir nīṇṭakālamāka ariyil oṭṭiyāṇam aṇintu vantaṉar. ippoḻutu atuvum maṟintu varukiṟatu.
muṟkāla mātar ariyil poṉ paṭṭikikaḷiyum caṅkili kaḷiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. arippaṭṭikikaḷil 2 mutal 32 vari ēṉ 62 vaṭaṅkaḷ toṅkum. palavaki iṭiyaṇikaḷi (nucup paṇikaḷi) aṇintu vantaṉar. intap paṭṭikiyil toṅkum vaṭaṅkaḷ pavaḷa vaṭaṅkaḷākavum muttu vaṭaṅkaḷākavum poṉ maṇi vaṭaṅkaḷākavum, māṇikkam, marakatam, viram patitta maṇi vaṭaṅkaḷākavum iruntaṉa. ciṟumikaḷ ariyil arimūṭi eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum ciṟuvarkaḷ aric cataṅki eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum, arivaṭam eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum aṇintu vantaṉar. teyvaṅkaḷ kūṭa kaṭicūttiram eṉṉum nucuppaṇi aṇintu vantatākat terikiṟatu. pala aḻakiya ciṟpavaṭivaṅkaḷil inta aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
iṅku iṭiyaṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum viḷakkamum koṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷaṉ avi aṭiyil varumāṟu :
nucuppaṇikalaṉkaḷ (iṭiyaṇikalaṉkaḷ)
1. aracilip poṉ aṭar, 2. ariñāṇ 3. arimūṭi 
4. ariccataṅki 5. ēṇippaṭukāl 6. kōvi 7. kaṭi cūttiram 
8. koṭi (arikkoṭi) 9. cataṅki (ariccataṅki) 10. pāṇṭil 11. poṟṟōri 12. poṟpaṭṭi 13. ariyaṇi (I) tāmam (18 vaṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu) (II) parumam (16 vaṭaṅkoṇṭatu) (III) mēkali 
(8 vaṭaṅ koṇṭatu (a) pavaḷamēkali (ā) māṇikka mēkali) (IV) kāñci (16 vaṭaṅkoṇṭatu (V) kalāpam (18 vaṭaṅ koṇṭatu (VI) viriciki (32 vaṭaṅkoṇṭatu) (VII) maṇiyaṇi (7 vaṭaṅ koṇṭatu)
iḵtaṉṟi, attu, arippaṭṭiki, irataṉam, utara pantaṉam, kīrttimukam, maṇikkōvi, aimpaṭik kōvi, kacci pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷum iruntatākat terikiṟatu. mēkali vakikaḷil kāṇappaṭum vaṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷil kūṭutal kuṟital kūṟuvāṟum uṇṭu.
1. aracilip poṉ cuṭar
itu aracili vaṭivākap poṟṟakaṭṭāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru ariyaṇi. itu makaḷiriṉ aḻakiya vayiṟṟil eḻil taruvatā yuḷḷa nīrccuḻi pōṉṟa koppūḻiṉ kaṇ, payattāl nuḻiya varum pāmpu pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭum eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu. (peruṅ 2 : 7 22-2A)
2. ari ñāṇ
āṇkaḷ ariyil aṇiyum oru poṟkoṭi. ēḻikaḷ veḷḷikkoṭiyum aṇivar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil makaḷirum ariyil veḷḷikkoṭi iṉṟu aṇintuḷḷaṉar. itu mūṉṟu nūlāl āṉatu. itaṉ mukappil ciṅkam allatu yāḻi uruvam irukkum. itu kaṭṭuvār allatu kīrttimāṉam eṉappaṭum.
3. ari mūṭi
iti aracili vaṭivil poṉṉāl ceytu ariyil uḷḷa koṭiyil toṅkaviṭṭiruppar. itu alkuli maṟippa taṟku eṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa aṇi. veḷḷiyilum kaṇṇāṭiyilum ceytu aṇivatu muṇṭu.
4.  aric cataṅki
ciṟu kuḻantikaḷil āṇiṟkum peṇṇiṟkum inta aṇiyi poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum ceytu aṇivatuṇṭu. iṉṟum inta aṇi vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. āṇ kuḻantikaḷukku aṇivatu kuṟintu varukiṟatu.
5. ēṇippaṭukāl
ēṇippaṭukāl mēkali iṉattic cērnta oru nucuppaṇi. puṉal viḷiyāṭṭiṉ pōtu ēṇippaṭukāli iṭiyil iṟukap pūṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu paripāṭal kūṟukiṟatu. (pari. 10-11-12)
6. kōvi
kōvi poṉṉāṟ ceyta aṇiyākum itu oru paḻami vāynta aṇikalaṉākum. ikkōviyic cilappatikāra uri yāciriyar piṉṟāli eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kāmaṉ tūmaṇi ceyattaku kōvi eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu.
7. kaṭi cūttiram
kaṭi cūttiram oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟa nucuppaṇi. oṭṭi yāṇam pōṉṟu kaṭi cūttirattiṉ poṉ paṭṭikiyil muṉpuṟam ciṟu ciṟu cataṅkikaḷ toṅkum. kaṭicūttirattiṉ muṉpakkam ciṅkattali pōṉṟa mukappu irukkum. tañcik kalveṭṭu itip paṭṭiki eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. itiṉiṉṟu toṅkum pala vaḷivu kaḷik koṇṭa muttuc caraṅkaḷi ūrutāmam eṉpar. ic caraṅkaḷ iṭuppiṉiṉṟu toṭi vari 3 aṅkula nīḷantoṅkum. pallavar, paḻaṅkālac cōḻar ciṟpaṅkaḷil orē muttuccarantāṉ uḷḷatu. kal ciṟpaṅkaḷil ovvoru toṭi mītum iru muttuc caraṅkaḷum naṭuvil oru caramumāka aintu muttuccaraṅkaḷ toṅkum. itaṉik kirutarimāciyam eṉpar.
8. koṭi
poṉ iḻikaḷāl piṉṉappaṭṭup piṉṉar ciṟiya accil uḷḷē pukutti iḻuttuk kampi vaṭivākac ceyta oru iṭuppaṇi. itu veḷḷiyilum uṇṭu.
9. cataṅki
poṉṉāl pukalāyc ceytu nuṉiyil ciṟu arumpukaḷ vittu mēlē maṭi vittuḷḷa ciṟu aṇiyākum. iti 40 allatu 50  cataṅkikaḷ kayiṟṟilō allatu poṉ veḷḷik koṭiyilō kōrttu iṭuppil aṇivar. itu ciṟuvar ciṟumiyar aṇiyum aṇi.
10. pāṇṭil
mēkaliyip pōṉṟa nucuppaṇi pāṇṭil. itu mēkali yōṭu cērttu aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉākum. itu vaṭṭamākap poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṉic cuṟṟi poṉmaṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭirukkum.34
11. poṟṟōri
itu poṉṉāl melliya iḻi pōṉṟu ceyyap peṟṟa vaṭamākum. itu oru nucuppaṇi. cīvakacintāmaṇi, alkuṟ poṟṟōri miṉṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. (cin : 213. 2 : 1)
12. poṟpaṭṭi
itu ariyil aṇiyum oru poṟṟakaṭākum. nīṇṭa poṉ takaṭuvaṭivāṉatu. itu āṭi nekiḻaviṭātu ariyil piṇikkum.
13. arimaṇi (I) tāmam
itu patiṉeṭṭu muttuk kōvikaḷ toṅkum iṭi aṇi. āṭiyiṉ mītu aḻakuṟap paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷir iti aṇintu vantaṉar. iti muttut tāmamum muṭivillātōreḻil nilimēṉi eṉṟu oru viṇavap pācuram tirumāliṉ aṇiyākap pōṟṟukiṟatu.
(II) parumam : itu patiṉāṟu kōvi koṇṭa arip paṭṭiki. āṉāl itu eṉṉa maṇivaṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa kōvikaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu teriyavilli.
(III) mēkali : inta nucuppaṇi; eṭṭu pavaḷak kōvikaḷāl āṉatu. iru pavaḷakkōvi koṇṭa mēkali eṉṟum ēḻu pavaḷakkōvi mēkali eṉṟum iruvakikaḷ uṇṭu.33
(a) pavaḷamēkali
(ā) māṇikka mēkali : mēkali vakikaḷil oṉṟu. itu māṇikka vaṭaṅkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭatākum. mēkali muttu, pavaḷam mutaliyavikaḷil ceyyappaṭṭiruppatu pōl māṇikkak kaṟkaḷ patittum ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
mēkaliyi aḻakiṟkāka pavaḷa vaṭaṅkaḷik kōttuc ceyvar. āṉāl pavaḷattiviṭa māṇikkam ciṟanta maṇiyāka iruppatālum itu paḷapaḷaveṉa oḷirviṭṭōṅkuvatālum māṇikka mēkali mika aḻakuṭiyatāka matikkap paṭṭatu.
(IV) kāñci : mēkali iṉattic cērntatu. itu eṭṭu muttu vaṭaṅkaḷ kōtta nucuppaṇi. maturiyil naṭipeṟṟa putuppuṉal nīrāṭṭuviḻāvil makaḷir tam iṭuppil kāñci aṇintiruntatākap paripāṭal kūṟukiṟatu. inta aṇi iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi. namatu nāṭṭuc ciṟpaṅkaḷil inta aṇikalaṉiṉ uruvattik kāṇa muṭiyum. kāñci 16 vaṭaṅkaḷ kōtta aṇi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ciṟpikaḷ ciṟpa vitippaṭi, nūṟkaḷiṉ vitikaḷukku muraṇiṉṟic ciṟpaṅkaḷip paṭittiruppatāl inta aṇikalaṉiṉ tōṟṟatti yum atil uḷḷa muttukkōvikaṉiṉ eṇṇattiyum nām tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka aṟiya muṭiyum. iḵtaṉṟi itaṉ vaṭivatti nām naṉkaṟiya vēṟu vaḻiyilli.
(V) kalāpam : itu oṭṭiyāṇampōl iṭuppi iṟukkip piṇikkat takkatāy, paṭṭikiyākavō caṅkiliyākavō iruk kum. atiṉiṉṟu kīḻē patiṉāṟu kōvikaḷālāṉa caṅkilikaḷ toṅkum. paṇkoḷ kalāpam patiṉāṟu eṉṟu cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar iti uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟār. itu 16 muttu vaṭaṅkaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa iṭiyaṇiyākum. itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu arippaṭṭikiyum aḻakiya mukappum kīḻē aḻakuṟat toṅkum 16 muttu vaṭaṅkaḷum uṭiyatāy eḻil peṟṟilaṅkum iṭiyaṇiyākum.
	vaṭṭuṭi polinta vaṇṇak kalāpamoṭu
	 paṭṭuc cuman tacinta pāvi yalkul     (peruṅ 2; 4 - 122 - 123)
cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār kalāpam pavaḷakkōvi eṭṭiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. ittakiya mēkalikaḷi makaḷiraṉṟi tāymār tam ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukkum aṇintu vantaṉar.
(VI) viriciki : inta nucuppaṇi periyatu; ariyatu; aḻaku mikkatu. antukil mēkali acintaṉa eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceyyuḷaṭikku uri kaṇṭa uri āciriyar 
	eṇkōvi kāñci eḻukōvi mēkali
	paṇkoḷ kalāpam patiṉāṟu - kaṇkoḷ
	parumam patiṉeṭṭu muppat
	tiraṇṭu viriciki
eṉṟa nikaṇṭi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. itaṉ mūlam viriciki muppattiraṇṭu maṇik kōvikaḷiyuṭiya oru mātar nucuppaṇi eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
	piṟaṅkiya muttari  muppat tirukāḻ
	niṟaṅkiḷar pūntukil nīrmiyi ṉuṭīik	        (cilap 6 : 88)
eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceyyuḷaṭikku uriyāciriyar parumuttuk kōvi muppattiraṇṭāṟ ceyta viriciki eṉṉum kaliyi nīla niṟaṅkiḷarum pūttoḻiliyuṭiya nīlac cātar uṭiyiṉ mītu aṇintāḷ eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār. itaṉāl itu āṭi yiṉ mītu aḻakuṟa aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ eṉṟum naṉku terikiṟatu. mēkaliyum anta iṉattic cērnta viricikiyum akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, kalittoki, paripāṭal, tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭi, maṇimēkali mutaliya nūṟkaḷilellām iṭam peṟṟiruppatāl itu iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ piṟantu, tamiḻakattil iruntu varum aṇikalam eṉṟum inta mēkali vakikaḷ iraṇṭu kōvikaḷiliruntu muppattiraṇṭu kōvikaḷ variyum palvēṟu peyaruṭaṉ pavaḷam, muttu, maṇikaḷ pōṉṟa vaṭakkōvikaḷi yuṭiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa eṉṟum terikiṟatu.
(VII) maṇiyaṇi : maṇiyaṇi 7 vaṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu.
X. toṭiyaṇikalaṉkaḷ
inta aṇikalaṉ itara aṇikalaṉkaḷip pōl attuṇic ciṟappāṉataṉṟu. toṭi aṇikalaṉ perumpālum āṭikkuḷ iruppatālum kātu, ki, iṭuppu pōṉṟa uṟuppukaḷip pōl aṇikaḷ aṇivataṟku toṭi vacatiyāṉa iṭamāka illāta tālum inta aṇikalaṉ atikam ēṟṟam vāyntaṉavāka illi.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ poṟ kālamākiya caṅkak kālattil ivvaṇi iṭam peṟṟiruntatākat teriyavilli. āṉāl cilappatikārak kālattil ivvaṇi tōṉṟiviṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. toṭi aṇikalaṅkaḷāvaṉa : (1) kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi (2) kavāṉ ceṟi.
1. kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi eṉpatu toṭiyil aṇiyappaṭum oru poṉ aṇikalaṉākum. kuṟaṅkaṇi kimpuri vaṭivamāka iruntatu eṉṟu cintāmaṇi mūlam aṟikiṟōm.36 iḵtaṉṟi  makaramīṉ vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa kuṟaṅkaṇiyum akkālattil iruntatu. toṭiyik kuvi irukkumāṟu ivvaṇi ceyyappaṭ ṭiruntatu. ivvaṇi uṭiyiṉ mēl toṭiyil aṇiyap paṭṭatākak kūṟuvārum uṇṭu.
2. kavāṉ ceṟi : itip paṟṟiya viḷakkam oṉṟum teriya villi. kuṟaṅku eṉpataṟku kavāṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē kuṟaṅkuc ceṟiyum kavāṉ ceṟiyum orē vita aṇikalaṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
XI. kālaṇikalaṉkaḷ
kālaṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu kāliṉ aṭiyil atāvatu pātattiṉ mītu poruntumāṟu aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākum. intak kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ toṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa valla. mikac ciṟappāṉavi. kālaṇikalaṉkaḷ varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭiyavi. iṉṉum viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl kālaṇi kāppiyac ciṟappuṭiya aṇikalaṉākavum eḻuntuḷḷatu. ilakkiyac ceṟivu mikka cilappatikārattik kāl aṇiyākiya cilampu peṟṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi cilampu veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. maṉṉar kaḷum māperum vaṇikarkaḷum poṉṉāl cilampic ceytu ataṉuḷḷē māṇikkak kaṟkaḷum muttukaḷum iṭṭu naṭakkum poḻutu atu kaṇīr! kaṇīr eṉṟu ōci eḻumpuvatik kēṭṭu makiḻntu vantaṉar. iṉṟu kāṟcilampu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. āṉāl kic cilampu oṉṟu cila kōyilkaḷil uḷḷatu. atu veḷḷiyāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. cila cāmi āṭikaḷ kikaḷil aṇintu ūrvalaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟaṉar. muṟkālattil kāṟ cilampu kaḷil paralāka iṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷukkup patilāka ippoḻutu kic cilampiṟkuḷ kaṟkaḷ paralāka iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
āṭavarkaḷ vīrattiṉ ciṉṉamākak kālil kaḻal aṇintu vantaṉar. poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa kaḻal poṉ kaḻal eṉap paṭṭatu. maṉṉarkaḷ paki aracarkaḷi veṉṟu avarkaḷ poṉ muṭiyāl (makuṭattāl) vīrak kaḻal ceytu kālil aṇintu vantaṉar.
iḵtaṉṟik kāl cariki, kiṇkiṇi, kolucu, cavaṭi, kaṭakam, taṇṭi pōṉṟa pala vitamāṉa kālaṇikaḷ aṇintu vantaṉar. kālaṇi tamiḻakattiṟkē ora ciṟappāka aminta aṇikalaṉāka matikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
kālaṇikaḷiṉ peyar
1. ariyakam (pātacālam allatu pātacaram) 2. aravam 
3. kaḻal :  (1) vīrak kaḻal (2) koṭik kaḻal (3) polaṉ kaḻal 
(4) kaṇṭi 5. kāṟcariki 6. kāṟkolucu 7. kālvaḷi 8. kiṇkiṇi 9. cālakam (paṭṭik kolucu) 10. cilampu (kuṭiccūl) 11. kiṟumaṇi 12. ñekiḻam 13. pariyakam (kāṟcavaṭi) 14. parivaṭimpu 15. pāṭakam 
16. pucaṅkak kaṭakam 17. puṉiyāram 18. taṇṭi 19. nūpuram 20. veḷḷittaḷi.
kālaṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. ariyakam
mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālan toṭṭu tamiḻakattil aṇiyap paṭṭu  varum oru kālaṇi. itaṉip pātacālam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. cilappatikārattilē, ariyakam kālukku amivuṟa aṇintu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu.
2. aravam
pāmpu vaṭivamākac ceyyap peṟṟa oru kālaṇi ākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. iṉṟu itu vaḻakkil illi. itaṉ aḻakum amippum naṉku teriyavilli.
3. kaḻal : (a) vīrak kaḻal 
āṭavarkaḷiṉ kāl aṇiyākum. maṉṉarkaḷ paki aracar kaḷi veṉṟu avvaracarkaḷiṉ poṉ muṭikaḷāl kaḻal ceytu kālil aṇivar.
(ā) koṭik kaḻal : maṉṉarkaḷ vīrarkaḷukkum pulavar kaḷukkum nalkum koṭiyiṉ ciṉṉamākum. vīrarkaḷum pulavar kaḷum kālkaḷil koṭik kaḻalkaḷi aṇintuk koḷvatu tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻiya marapākum.
(i) polaṉ kaḻal : poṉṉāl kaḻalkaḷ ceytu kālil aṇivatum uṇṭu. itu polaṉ kaḻal eṉappaṭum. cintāmaṇiyil
	paṭiya ṉalkāy pacu maṇikaḷ vēyt
	 tōṅkum pimpoṟ ceṟikaḻaliṉāy
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.38
4. kaṇṭi
kaṇṭi pōr maṟavarkaḷ, kālil aṇiyum periya maṇikaḷ kōtta oru kālaṇi. yāṉi maṇiyum kaṇṭi maṇi eṉappaṭum. vīrarkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉ vīrak kaṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
5. kāṟcari
itu kuḻāy pōṉṟa vaṭivil takaṭṭāl ceyyappaṭṭu kālil taṇṭikku mēlē aṇiyum kālaṇiyākum. itaṉ mēṟpuṟam peritākavum kīḻp puṟam ciṟitu ciṟuttum kālukku ēṟṟa vitamāyc nāḻi pōl ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. itē pōṉṟu ciṟitākac ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ kic cariyākum.
6. kāṟkolucu
kālil aṇiyappaṭum kolucu kāṟkolucu eṉṟu kūṟap paṭum. cataṅkiyum kāṟ kolucum orē aṇikalaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itu veḷḷiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭut tamiḻakattil nēṟṟu vari vaḻakkil irunta kālaṇiyākum. ik koluciṉ kīḻp pakkam cataṅkikaḷ toṅkum. itu ariyakam pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉākum.
7. kāl vaḷi
kāl vaḷi eṉpatu taṇṭiyēyākum. taṇṭiyum kāl vaḷiyum orē vita aṇi eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uṇṭu. āṉāl taṇṭi vēṟu kālvaḷi vēṟu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu.
8. kiṇkiṇi
kiṇkiṇi kālaṇikaḷil oṉṟu. itu tavaḷiyiṉ vāy vaṭivil irukkum. eṉavē tēri vāyk kiṇkiṇi ārppa eṉṟu kalittoki kūṟukiṟatu. tavaḷivāy polañcey kiṇkiṇi eṉṟu kuṟuntoki kūṟukiṟatu. kiṇkiṇi eṉṟu olippatāl kiṇkiṇi eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu.
9. cālakam : (paṭṭik kolucu)
itu pātaṅkaḷiṉ mītu kaṇik kālukkuk kīḻē aṇiyap paṭum. itaṉip paṭṭic caram eṉṟum, paṭṭik kolucu eṉṟum pakarvar. muttukkaḷ pōl taṉit taṉiyē ceytu cālakat tilē kōkkappaṭṭu vali pōṉṟu iṇittu niṟkum ciṟu paṭṭikaḷ itil uṇṭu. ivvaṇi ippoḻutu paṭippaṭiyāy maṟintu varukiṟatu.
10. cilampu (kuṭic cūl)
cilampik kuṭiccūl eṉṟum aḻippar. cilampu nīṇṭa vaṭṭamāṉa oru kālaṇi. itu pukalākac ceyyap peṟṟu uḷḷē paralkaḷ pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. cilampiṉuḷ muttukaḷum māṇikkaṅ kaḷum muṟkālattil paralkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. inta aṇikalaṉ pērālē cilappatikāram eṉṉum peruṅkāppiyam eḻutap peṟṟatu. muttari poṉcilampu olippat tattuṟṟu eṉṟu naṟṟiṇi navilkiṟatu. nīṭāḻpu tōkki nittila varic cilampu eṉṟu paripāṭal pakarkiṟatu. cilampaṇintu naṭantāl paralkaḷ kaṇīr kaṇīr eṉṟu oli eḻuppum.
11. ciṟumaṇi
ciṟumaṇi eṉpatu cataṅkikaḷ kōttuc ceyyappaṭṭa oru kālaṇi. itu veḷḷiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu iṉṟiya vaḻakkil illi. ivvaṇi maṟintu pala āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa.
12. ñekiḻam
ñekiḻam eṉpatu oru vakic cilampu. itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. ñekiḻamum nūpuramum oruvita aṇi eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.
13. pariyakam (kāṟcavaṭi)
pariyakam poṉṉāṟ ceyta ciṟu vaṭaṅkaḷic cērttu muṟukki oru puṟattik kāl peruviral mōtirattil porutti maṟṟoru puṟattic cilampiṉ kīḻ aṇivar. itaṉip pātacālam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. pariyakamum ariyakamum oṉṟeṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. cilappatikārattil ivi vevvēṟu aṇikaḷākak kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.39
14. parivaṭimpu
parivaṭimpu paḻaṅkālat tamiḻar kaṇṭa kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟu. itu pōr maṟavarkaḷ kutiri mītēṟi ati virintu celuttuvataṟkeṉṟu kālil iṭap peṟṟa oru veḷḷik kālaṇi yākum. iti puṟanāṉūṟu kaṭumā kaṭiiya viṭupari vaṭimpiṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. itu paḻaṅkālan toṭṭu tamiḻarkaḷ aṇintu vanta oru kālaṇiyākum.
15. pāṭakam
pāṭakam cilampiṉ mēlē aṇiyappaṭum oru kālaṇi. itu akalamākak kaṉamākac ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. pariyakam nūpuram pāṭakam eṉṟu cilappatikārattil ivvaṇi ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
16. pucaṅkak kaṭakam
pāmpu vaṭivamākap poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa taṇṭi pucaṅkak kaṭakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itu paḻaṅkāla aṇiyākat teriyavilli. civaperumāṉukkukanta aṇiyāka eṇṇip piṟkālattuc civarkaḷ iti aṇintirukkalām.
17. puṉiyāram
puṉiyāram oru aḻakiya veḷḷik kālaṇiyākum. inta aṇiyum piṟkālattil eḻuntatākavē karutappaṭukiṟatu. paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil inta aṇikalaṉ iṭam peṟṟatākat teriyavilli.
18. taṇṭi
taṇṭi, kaṭṭiyākavum, pukalākavum ceytu aṇiyappaṭum. muṟkālattil itu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil veḷḷiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu vaṭṭamāṉa vaḷiyam pōṉ ṟirukkum. iru nuṉikaḷum caturamākac ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. itu paḻaṅkāla aṇi.
19. nūpuram
nūpuramum cilampum orē vita aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. pariyakam nūpuram pāṭakam cataṅki eṉṟu cilap patikāram nūpurattic ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟatu. peruṅkati, nūpuram pāṭakattōṭu cērttu aṇiyum kālaṇi eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.41
20. veḷḷit taḷi
veḷḷiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru kālaṇiyākum. itu ciṟu veḷḷik kampiyiṉāl piṉṉappaṭṭa cavaṭi pōṉṟa aṇiyākum. inta aṇi tirumālukku ukantatu. ivvaṇi viṇava camaya nūṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.42
XII. kāl viral aṇikalaṉkaḷ
kāl viralkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum mōtiram kāl viral aṇi eṉappaṭum. kāl viralkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum kālāḻikaḷip peṇ kaḷē aṇivar. āṇkaḷ kāl viralkaḷil evvita aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇivatilli. atōṭu ki viralkaḷil aṇiyum mōtiraṅ kaḷip pōl kāl viralkaḷil poṉ aṇikaḷō, maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikaḷō aṇivatilli. perumpālum kālaṇikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiliruntē makaḷirāl aṇintu varappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
potuvāka ātikālattil iruntē tamiḻarkaḷ kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ malikaḷil kuṟiñci nilamākkaḷāy vēṭṭuva vāḻkki naṭāttiya poḻutē avarkaḷiṭam aṇikalaṉkaḷ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. aṇikalaṉkaḷ ili, pū, kāy, koṭṭi, mīṉ mutaliya peyarāl iruppati muṉṉar eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉōm.
ippoḻutu kālaṇiyilum kāy, mīṉ vāy pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭutaliṉāl ak karuttu muṟṟilum mey eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
1. kāl mōtiram 2. kālāḻi 3. tāḻ ceṟi 4. nallaṇi 
5. pāmpāḻi 6. pillaṇi 7. pīli 8. makaravāy mōtiram 9. muñci 
10. meṭṭi.
XIII. kāl viral aṇikaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. kāl mōtiram
kāl peruviralil aṇiyappaṭum oru vaḷiyamē kāl mōtiramākum. itip peruviral cuṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvārkaḷ. pīli, kālāḻi iṉṟik kaliyāṇamā eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. piṟkālattil kāl mōtiram vēṟu vēṟu vaṭivantāṅki tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir kāl viralil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi.
2. kālāḻi
kālāḻi eṉpatum kāl mōtiram eṉpatum oṉṟē eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar makaravāy mōtiram, pīli, kālāḻi mutaliyavi kāl viral mōtiram eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
3. tāḻ ceṟi
tāḻ ceṟi oru kāl viral aṇi. cilappatikārattil marakatat tāḷ ceṟi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl marakatam patikkap peṟṟa kiviral aṇi eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uṇṭu. atōṭu itu kiviral aṇikaḷōṭu cērttuk kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
4. nallaṇi
nallaṇi oru kāl viral aṇi. cilappatikāram nalattaku melviral nallaṇi ceṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mūlam itu kāl viral aṇi eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. uriyāciriyarkaḷum itu kāl viral aṇi eṉṟē karutukiṉṟaṉar.
5. pāmpāḻi
pāmpu vaṭṭamākat taṉ vālic cuṟṟi vittuk koṇ ṭiruppatip pōl ceyyappaṭṭa oru kāl viral aṇiyākum. itu veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. tali uṭalōṭu oṭṭi yirukkum.
6. pillaṇi
pillaṇi iṇinta iru vaṭṭa vaṭivattiṉ mītu maṟṟoru iṇinta iru vaṭṭa vaṭivatti vittāṟ pōṉṟu āṅkila eṇṇākiya eṭṭip pōṉṟu irukkum. inta vaṭṭaṅkaḷ variciyākak koṭikaḷ cuṟṟi vikkappaṭṭatu pōl irukkum. naṭuvil cokkaṭṭāṉ kaṭṭam pōl oru caturam irukkum. inta uruvamum vaḻakkil illi.
7. pīli
pīli, kāl peruviralukku aṭutta viralil aṇiyappaṭum oru aṇikalaṉākum. itaṉ aṭiyil ellāk kāl viral aṇikaḷukkum iruppatu pōl, viralic cuṟṟumpaṭiyāka oru cempu allatu veḷḷi vaḷiyam poruttappaṭṭirukkum. vaḷiyattiṉ mītu oru takaṭu vikkappaṭṭu ataṉ mēl paḻat taṭṭil paḻaṅkaḷ vit tiruppatu pōl aṇiyaṇiyāy uruṇṭi muttukkaḷ vikkap paṭṭirukkum. pīliyil aṭukkup pīli, veṅkāya pīli eṉṟu iru vakiyuṇṭu. tālikkup pīli tuṇi eṉpatu paḻaṅ kālap paḻamoḻi.
makaravāy mōtiram, makara mīṉiṉ tiṟanta vāy pōl irukkum. itu iṉṟu veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. itu neḷi mōtiram pōṉṟatē yākum. makaravāyk kāl viral aṇi, kāl viralil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉākum. iṉṟu inta makaravāyk kāl viral aṇiyip pārppatu aritu. perumpālum kāl viral aṇikaḷ maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa.
9. muñci
muñci eṉpatip peṇkaḷ miñci eṉṟu kūṟuvar. ivvaṇi mēlē iru vaṭṭamāṉa takaṭukaḷ porutti ataṉ mītu naṭuvē ṭayamaṇṭ vaṭivamuḷḷa oru veḷḷit takaṭu poruttap paṭṭirukkum. cuṟṟilum miḷaku pōṉṟa uruṇṭi vaṭivamāṉa kāykaḷum iṭi iṭiyē kaṭuku pōṉṟa ciṟu maṇikaḷum variciyāka iṭam peṟṟirukkum.
10. meṭṭi
itu ātiyil veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. piṉ kalappu ulōkattālum ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu oru kaṉatta kampiyi vaḷiyac ceyyappaṭṭatu pōl irukkum. itu viralkaḷukku iraṇṭu mūṉṟu iṭam peṟṟirukkum. naṭakkum poḻutu, iv vaṇikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mōti, kaṇīr kaṇīr eṉṟu olikkum. inta oli cila camayap paktarkaḷukkup piṭikkavilli. ataṉāl meṭṭi kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ aṇiyak kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟi vantaṉar. āṉāl inta aṇikaḷip parattiyarkaḷ (tācikaḷ) viruppamuṭaṉ aṇintu vantaṉar. intak kāl viral aṇiyum iṉṟu vaḻakkil illi. kāṇpataṟkē mika aritāyp pōyviṭṭatu.

9. ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷ
arappāvum ātitta nallūrum
arappāvum ātitta nallūrum tamiḻar nākarikattiṟku nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷākum. arappāvip pōl ātittanallūrilum akaḻntu kaṇṭa pommikaḷiṉ mītu, namatu paḻaṅkāla aṇikalaṉ kaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku aṇikalaṉkaḷum cila kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉ attiyāyaṅkaḷil arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. arappāvil kaṇṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ ātittanallūr aṇikalaṉ kaḷi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
vētakkāla nākarikam
vētakkāla nākarikattiṟku arappā - ātitta nallūr nākarikaṅkaḷukkum aṇuvaḷavum campantamilli. āṉāl kalik kaḷañciyattil vētakāla nākarikam (ki.mu. 1500 - ki.mu. 500 vari) eṉṟa talippil ātitta nallūr nākarikattiṟkup piṟaku ki.mu. 3,000 mutal ki.mu. 1500 vari atāvatu vētakālam vari irunta aṇikaḷip paṟṟi aṟiya evvitac cāṉṟukaḷum kiṭṭavilli. akkāla aṇikaḷi vēta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil iruntutāṉ oruvāṟu ūkikka muṭiyum. akkālac ciṟpaṅkaḷō aṇikaḷō akappaṭa villi. akkālattup peṇkaḷ talikku kumpā allatu kurirā eṉṟa aṇiyum kātukaḷil vaṭṭamāṉa kammaliyum kāl, kikaḷil kāppukaḷiyum viralkaḷil mōtiraṅkaḷiyum  tōḷkaḷil kaṭakaṅkaḷiyum aṇintaṉar. āṉāl ivikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum viṭak kaḻuttil aṇiyum kācumāli pōṉṟa aṇiyiyum matittārkaḷ. mika mukkiyamāṉatākavum karutiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1
tamiḻaka āṭi aṇikaḷip paṟṟi eḻuta muṉ vanta aṟiñar vētakkāla nākarikattip paṟṟi eḻuta eṉṉa avaciyam eḻuntatu eṉṟu namakkut teriyavilli. vētak kāla āriyarkaḷ āṭu māṭu mēyttuk koṇṭu intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntavarkaḷ eṉpati naṇpar maṟantuviṭṭār pōlum. mēlum avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kōṭṭikaḷ kaṭṭavum eḻutavum aṇikalaṉkaḷ camikkavum aṟiyārkaḷ eṉpati naṇparukku niṉippūṭṭik koḷḷu kiṟōm. arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum eḻunta paṇpāṭṭi ṉiṉṟum nākarikattiṉiṉṟum paṭiyeṭuttuk koṇṭavi kaḷit tāṉ vēta nākarikam, āriya nākarikam eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar, eṉpatiyum niṉivūṭṭik koḷkiṟōm.
tirāviṭa nākarikam
tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟil ki.mu. 500 mutal ki.mu. 200 iruḷ aṭinta kālamāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kavaliyuṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. mika vaḷarcci aṭinta arappāviṉ arum perum paṇpāṭṭi aḻitta āriyarkaḷ  mīṇṭum mīṇṭum tuḷirtta tirāviṭa nākarikatti aḻittuk koṇṭē vantirukkiṟārkaḷ. āriya nākarikatti etirttum tirāviṭa nākarikattit taḻuviyum puttar perumāṉ oru perum kiḷarcciyi eḻuppi vittār. ataṉ piṉṉarē tirāviṭa nākarikam ōraḷavu pātukākkappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. 200 mutal ki.pi. 400 vari arumpiya amarāvati nākarikattil tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭak kali, tirāviṭa ōviyam, tirāviṭa icikkaruvikaḷ, tirāviṭa ciṟpam, tirāviṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi naṉku kāṇa muṭikiṟatu. amarāvati ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ yāḻ uruvatti, mutaṉ mutalāka aṟiñar āṉantak kumāracuvāmi kaṇṭār.2 avaratu kaṭṭurikaḷi ṉiṉṟē vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūli eḻutit tamiḻ ulakattiṟku oru perum naṉkoṭiyāka uvantaḷittār. amarāvati nākarikattil  kāṇappaṭum tirāviṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum tamiḻakac ciṟpaṅkaḷilum iṉṟum kāṇak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. amarāvati nākarikam iṭiccaṅkak kālattiṉ iṟutik kaṭṭamāka irukkalām, eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. amarāvatik kālattiṟkup piṉṉar kūṭa atāvatu ki.pi. 400ām āṇṭukkup piṉṉar mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ ki.pi. 700 vari nam aṇikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi aṟiya muṭiyavilli, eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ aṅkalāykkiṟārkaḷ. āṅkāṅkē oru cila ciṟpaṅkaḷil cila aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. 700ām āṇṭiliruntu ki.pi. 1600 vari tamiḻaka ciṟpaṅkaḷ tārāḷamākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷi muṟkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟu oruvāṟu muntiya attiyāyaṅkaḷil viḷakkik kūṟiviṭṭōm.
iṉi 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉiṉṟu iṉṟu vari eḻuntuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷi oruvāṟu ārāyvōm. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ kiṭṭattaṭṭa vicaya nakara cāmrācciyam vīḻntatu. ataṉ vīḻcci tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ vīḻcci eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. appāl tamiḻakattil arumpiya muculim āṭciyum ataṉ piṉṉar arumpi irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari nilaviya tirāviṭa nākarikatti uyiraṟṟatāy vīḻttiya āṅkila āṭciyiṉ ātikkamum nilaviyatu. ki.pi. 1600kkup piṉ eḻunta tamiḻaka aṇikalaṉkaḷip piṟkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷākak koṇṭu iṅku ārāyvōm.
aṇikaḷiṉ pirivukaḷ
aṇikalaṉkaḷ avaṉi muḻuvatum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ uṟpatti  nāṭṭukku nāṭu vēṟṟumiyuḷḷatāka irukkiṉṟatu. atu annāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ aṟivu, annāṭṭil kiṭikkum paṇṭaṅkaḷ, annāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppa nilikaḷiyum poṟuttatāy amintirukkiṉṟatu.
ivikaḷiyellām vittuc cilar aṇikalaṉkaḷip pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟum ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟum pirittiruk kiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu tamiḻakam campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil pēraṇikalaṉka ḷellām peṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷāyum ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷ ellām peritum āṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷāyum ciṟu kuḻanti kaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷāyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nām iṅku itaṉ muṉṉar peṇkaḷiṉ pēraṇikalaṉ kaḷākiya viliyuyarnta nakikaḷip paṟṟi ārāyntuḷḷōm. iṉi iṅku āṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyum paṟṟi ārāyvōm. mēlum kāla veḷḷattāl inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil pēraṇikalaṉkaḷiṟ pala maṟintu ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷ pōl ākiviṭṭaṉa. ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷ pala irunta iṭam teriyātu maṟintu pōyviṭṭaṉa.
aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ māṟṟam
namatu nāṭṭiṉ pēraṇikalaṉkaḷil periya māṟṟam eḻun tuḷḷatu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam tamiḻakattil airōppiya nākarikattiṉ mōtalum  makkaḷiṉ poruḷātāra niliyumēyākum. itaṉāl iṉṟu pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. aimpatu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ peṇkaḷ kātukaḷil poṟṟakaṭukaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟu uḷḷē meḻuku aṭikkap peṟṟa pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi, iṭṭaṭikki, muṭiccu pōṉṟa periya kātaṇikaḷ palavaṟṟik kātukaḷil māṭṭi vantaṉar. avi kātukaḷil paḻakkuli cāttiṉāṟ pōl toṅkik koṇṭiruntaṉa. ataṉāl peṇkaḷ ōṭavō virivāka naṭakkavō añcik kiṭantaṉar. ōṭiṉāl kātil kiṭakkum aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāti aṟuttuk koṇṭu kīḻē vīḻntu viṭum eṉṟa payam iruntatu. atē pōṉṟu kaḻuttil meḻuku allatu cempuk kaṭṭi vittup poṉṉāl ceyta cuttu maṇi, aṭṭiki, kāppu, kolucu, mōtiram, muruku, viciṟimuruku mutaliyavikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. kaḻuttil meḻuku aṭikkāta uṭkaṭṭu (okkaṭṭu) aṭṭiki, kāri, nellikkāy māli, miḷaku māli, kottumalli māli pōṉṟa nakikaḷi eṭi kuṟivākap pukal nakikaḷākac ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar.
poruḷātārattil piṉtaṅkiya iṉattavarkaḷ palarum kātil oru pāmpaṭamum oru ciṟu pukaṭi, pōliyākac ceyta viciṟimuruku pōṉṟa nakikaḷiyum kaḻuttil cuttu maṇiyum tavira kikaḷil kāppu, kolucu, kaṭakam, cariki mutaliyavikaḷiyum kālil taṇṭi, kāṟ kolucu, kāṟ cariki, pātacaram pōṉṟavikaḷiyum viralil miñci, pīli, pillaṇi, meṭṭi mutaliyavikaḷiyum veḷḷiyi ṉāl ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. ciṟu piḷḷikaḷ īyattiṉāl ceyta kuṇukkukaḷi nīḷamāka vaṭikkap peṟṟa kātukaḷil aṇintu vantaṉar. kikaḷil mōtiraṅkaḷ kūṭa veḷḷi, cempu mutaliya vili kuṟinta tātup poruṭkaḷil ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. itu, namatu nāṭṭil pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattil tali tūkkiya āṅkila āṭciyil arumpiya avala niliyākum.
peṇkaḷum - nākarikamum
tamiḻakam campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil āṇkaḷ eḷitil taṅkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvārkaḷ. paḻiya paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, kali, uṭi, aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi eḷitil viṭṭu viṭṭu putiyaṉavaṟṟi eḷitil ēṟṟuk koḷvārkaḷ. itil naṉmiyum eḻalām tīmiyum eḻalām. āṉāl potuvāka anniya nākarikattāl tīmi tāṉ viḷiyum eṉṟu namatu aṉupavattil kaṇṭuḷḷōm. airōppiya nākarikattiṉ viḷivāl āṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ aṇintu vanta aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉitti yum tuṟantu viṭṭārkaḷ. oru cila kirāmaṅkaḷil iṉṉum taṅkaḷi uyarnta iṉattavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭu mēṉāṭṭup paṭippi veṟuttu varum cila camayap paṟṟuḷḷa kuṭumpattil uḷḷa iḷiñarkaḷ kātil kaṭukkaṉum kiyil kāppum neṟṟiyil tirunīṟum kaḻuttil kavuṭum aṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷ nākarikamaṟṟavarkaḷāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷukku iṉṟu peṇkaḷ kiṭippatu kūṭa aritāki viṭṭatu. inniliyil āṇkaḷ aṭiyōṭu paḻiya aṇikalaṉkaḷit tuṟantu āṅkilam paṭittu, mēlē caṭṭiyum meyppiyum kiyil kaṭikāramum viralil oru mōtiramum aṇintu varuvatē nākarikak kōlamāka iruntu varukiṟatu. ariyil kāṟcaṭṭi pōṭuvatu uyarnta nākarikattiṉ cīriya ciṉṉamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.
āṉāl inta māṟṟam peṇkaḷiṭam atikam eḻavē illi. tamiḻakam campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil tamiḻ makkaḷ iṉṉum uṭampil kavuṇ eṉṉum toṭivari toṅkum āṭiyi aṇiya virumpavilli. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kāṟcaṭṭi aṇiyum āṇkaḷkūṭa taṅkaḷ maṉiviyō, makaḷō anta ari nirvāṇa āṭiyaṇiya viṭuvatē illi. āṉāl peṇkaḷ iṉṟu kātukaḷ vaṭippati niṟuttiviṭṭuk kātil oru ciṟu tōṭu allatu kuccu maṭṭum aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟu toṅkaṭṭam cimikki pōṭuvatik kūṭa viṭṭu viṭṭārkaḷ. taliyil aṇikaḷ aṇivatē illi. iṉṟiya tamiḻakattil uḷḷa peṇkaḷukku muṟkālat taliyaṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷkūṭa teriyātu. kālaṇikaḷ, kālviral aṇikaḷ palavum kāṭēṟi viṭṭaṉa. kiyil oru cila melliya vaḷikaḷ, kiviralil oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu mōtiram kaḻuttil oru tālic caṅkili allatu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vaṭattil oru poṟcaṅkili maṭṭuntāṉ iṉṟiya tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ aṇikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṭik kālattil tamiḻp peṇkaḷ kaḻuttil aṇintu vanta neklē eṉṟa kaḻuttaṇi kūṭa ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭavilli.
mūkkaṇi
tamiḻ nāṭṭil muṟkālattil mūkkit tuḷittu atil nakikaḷ aṇiyum vaḻakkam iruntatilli eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. nākarikak kuṟivāṉa āppirikka makkaḷ tāṉ mūkkiṉ iru pakkaṅkaḷil allatu mūkkiṉ naṭut taṇṭilum tuvāra miṭṭu elumput tuṇṭiyō allatu etāvatu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ nakaṅkaḷiyō allatu iṟakukaḷ, kuccukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyō aṇivārkaḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷirkaḷ muṟkālattil mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇivatu vaḻakkamilli. muṟkāla ōviyaṅkaḷilō paṭimaṅ kaḷilō cilikaḷilō mūkkaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭa villi eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. āṉālum, piṟkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ mūkkaṇi aṇintu vantaṉar eṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmi yākum. avarkaḷ mūkkiṉ naṭut taṇṭil tuvāramiṭṭu atil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉukku pullākku eṉṟu peyar. itu ciṟu patakkam pōl mūkkiṉ naṭuvil - mēl utaṭṭiṉmītu  toṅkum. itu aḻakāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu aṉṟiya makaḷir kūṟivantaṉar. camīpattil mēṉāṭṭil paṭittup paṭṭam peṟṟa oru tamiḻp peṇmaṇiyic cantittēṉ; avar pullākku aṇintiruntār. āṉāl mūkkiṉ naṭut taṇṭil tuvāram iṭātu mūkkut taṇṭi iṟukap piṭittuk koḷḷumāṟu ceyta pullākkāka iruntatu. inta naki ellāp peṇkaḷukkum aḻakāka irukkum eṉṟu eṉṉāl kūṟamuṭiyātu. āṉāl nāṉ pārtta cila peṇmaṇi kaḷukku aḻaku taruvatākavē amintiruntatu eṉṟu eṉṉāl tiriyamāka kūṟamuṭiyum.
mūkkiṉ iṭatu pakkam tuvāramiṭṭu atil oru ciṟu taṅkak kuccō allatu oru kal patitta kuccō mūṉṟu kal patitta kuccō paṇṭu aṇivārkaḷ. itu mūkkutti, mukkaṭṭu, nattu, pēcari eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum. cilar iṭatu nācip pakkam aṇivatum uṇṭu.
inta aṇikaḷ perumpālum tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa pārppa ṉarkaḷ, vēḷāḷar pōṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷē aṇintu vantaṉar. pārppaṉar allāta tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa cila iṉattavarkaḷ pēcari eṉṟa oru vaki  natti ciṟappāka aṇintu vantaṉar. ituvum poṉṉāl  ceyyappaṭṭu kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭirukkum. inta nakikaḷ vaṭa intiyāvilum uṇṭu. āṉāl avarkaḷ mūkkil oru periya vaḷiyam pōṉṟa nakiyiyum aṇikiṟārkaḷ. atu attuṇi aḻakuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭavilli. tamiḻnāṭṭu mūkkaṇikaḷ aḻakuṭiyaṉavāyum ciṟanta vēlippāṭukaḷi yuṭiyaṉavāyum viḷaṅkiṉa.
inta mūkkaṇikaḷiṉ ciṟappip paṟṟiyum vēlit  tiṟaṉip paṟṟiyum viyantu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṭap pārāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
inta mūkkaṇi oru ciṟṟaṇikalaṉāka matikkappaṭum. muṟkālat til tamiḻarkaḷ avvaṇikalaṉi aṇiyavilli eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar aḻuttamākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum ayalnāṭukaḷiliruntu vanta mūkkaṇikaḷ illi. mokañcatārō, arappā mutaliya nākarikaṅkaḷilum tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ mūkkaṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. mūkkaṇikaḷ muculimkaḷ mūlam tamiḻakattiṟku vantirukkalām eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ. paṇṭiya pēraṇikalaṉkaḷiyellām akaṟṟiviṭṭu oru cila ciṟṟaṇikaḷi aṇiya muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻarkaḷ piṟkālattil intap putiya anniya nākarikattil vanta mūkkaṇikaḷi eppaṭi aṇiya muṟpaṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu namakkup pērāccariyamāka irukkiṟatu. āṉāl irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ arip pakutiyik kaṭantu 10 āṇṭukaḷ muṭintatum tamiḻaka makaḷir paṭip paṭiyāka mūkkutti,  nattu pēcari, pullākkup pōṉṟa mūkkaṇikaḷ aṉittiyum vaḻakkiṉiṉṟu oḻittukkaṭṭi viṭṭārkaḷ. iṉṟikku irupattintu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ paṇakkārar vīṭṭil tirumaṇap pēccu ārampittāl virattōṭu viramūkkutti māṇikkam allatu nīlakkaṟkaḷ patitta pēcarikaḷ eṉṟa pēccē kēṭkum. iṉṟu appēccu aṭiyōṭu maṟintu viṭṭatu.4
tamiḻakattil mutaṉmutalākap peṇkaḷ irupatām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivati akaṟṟi viṭṭaṉar. perumpālum ciṟṟaṇikalaṉkaḷiyē inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil ārampittaṉar. āṇkaḷ inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mutaṟ kāl pakutiyilē taṅkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ perum pakutiyi akaṟṟi viṭṭaṉar. inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mattiyiliruntu kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum kūṭa maṟiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa.
namatu tamiḻakattil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum āṅkila nākarika mayakkil nam nāṭṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷi vilakka muṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉarē eṉṟu tamiḻaka nākarikattip pātukākka virumpum nāṭṭup paṟṟuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ varuntukiṉṟaṉar. inniliyil namatu intiya aracāṅkam taṅkak kaṭṭuppāṭṭuc caṭṭam koṇṭu vantu makkaḷ poṉ aṇikaḷ aṇivataṟkut tuṇiyum vaḻiyillāmal ceytu viṭṭatu. itaṉ palaṉāka eṇṇaṟṟa poṟ kollarkaḷ vēli yiṉṟit tavittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. nalla kivaṇṇam vāynta poṟkollarkaḷ ittoḻili maṟantu vēṟu toḻilō allatu vēṟu vaṇikamō ceytu vayiṟṟik kaḻuvi varukiṉṟaṉar. ittoḻil iṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāy maṟintu varukiṟatu.
namatu aracāṅkam, peṇkaḷ, poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivati virumpavilli. peṇkaḷ poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyum vaḻak kattik kiviṭṭu viṭṭāl ērāḷamākap poṉ aracāṅkat tiṟkuk kiṭikkum.  atik koṇṭu piṟa nāṭṭiṉariṭam tāṉ peṟṟirukkum kōṭāṉu kōṭi rūpāy matippuḷḷa kaṭaṉit tīrkkalām eṉṟu pakaṟkaṉavu kaṇṭu ēmāntu niṟkiṟatu. itōṭu mēṉāṭṭup peṇkaḷip pōl namatu peṇkaḷum aṇi kalaṉkaḷ aṇiyātiruppatē nākarikam eṉṟum namakkup pōtittu varukiṟatu.
itaṉāl namatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉittum maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē vacati vāyntavarkaḷ namatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉittiyum iyaṉṟa aḷavil cēkarippatōṭu ellā aṇi kalaṉkaḷum namatu tolporuḷ kāṭcicāliyil iṭampeṟac ceyya vēṇṭiyatum iṉṟiyamiyāta naṟpaṇiyākum. eṉṟāvatu oru nāḷ inta aṇikalaṉkaḷ mīṇṭum tali tūkkalām.
kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ
toṉṟu toṭṭu tamiḻakattil kuḻantikaḷukku avarkaḷiṉ aṉṉiyarkaḷ ārvamōṭu aṇiceytu akamakiḻntu aṇi kalaṉkaḷ pūṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. nākarikam peṟṟu aṇikalaṉkaḷi virumpāta peṇkaḷum tam kuḻantikku aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvittu aṇivittu aḻaku pārttu akamakiḻkiṉṟaṉar. kuḻantikku kātu kuttutali oru viḻāvāka naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum oru vīṭṭil kuḻanti piṟantu viṭṭāl ataṉ uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum kiviralil mōtiraṅkaḷ, kaḻuttil, caṅkili, kiyil kāppu, iṭuppil poṟkoṭi, poṟcataṅki ceytu pōṭuvatu vaḻakkil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ arippakutikku appāl tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭṭil āṇpiḷḷikaḷukkuk kātu kuttuvati niṟutti viṭṭārkaḷ. oṉṟiraṇṭu pērkaḷ peṇ kuḻantikaḷukkuk kātu kuttuvati niṟuttiṉālum tirumaṇac camayattil kātik kuttik kātaṇiyi aṇintu koḷkiṉṟārkaḷ. nāṉ aṟinta maṭṭil eṉakkut tamiḻ kaṟka ārvamūṭṭiya taṉit tamiḻp pulavar uyartiru cīrvikuṇṭam vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kūṭṭuṭaṉ kāṭu caṅkaracuppupiḷḷi avarkaḷ tam peṇ makkaḷukkuk kātu kuttavē illi. avarkaḷatu aruntavap putalvi tirumaṇam āṉa piṉṉarum kātu kuttavēyilli. iṉṟu inta ammiyārukku cumār 60 vayatu irukkalām. kātu kuttāmalē perumpālāṉa kālattik kaḻittu viṭṭaṉar.
niṟkat tamiḻarkaḷ caṅkakkālam mutal taṅkaḷ makkaḷukkup poṉ aṇikaḷip pūṭṭik kaṇ kuḷirak kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟu vantaṉar. ataṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟukaḷ tarukiṉṟaṉa.
kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattil, nalla murukaṉukku viḻā naṭantatu. atik kaṇṭukaḷikkum poruṭṭu āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum kuḻantikaḷum ērāḷamākak kuḻumi niṉṟaṉar. ciṉṉañ ciṟu kuḻanti kaḷiṉ kaḻuttil aṇintirunta aimpaṭittāli eṉṉum aṇi kalaṉi avarkaḷ vāyiṉiṉṟu cintum umiḻnīr naṉittatu. itaṉic cīttalic cāttaṉār eṉṉum perumpulavar maṇimēkali eṉṉum kāppiyattil aḻakuṟa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.5
taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceru veṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ taṉ iḷam pirāyattil ēṟu pōṉṟu pōrkkaḷam pukuntāṉ. avaṉ kaḻuttil aimpaṭittāli iruntatu. atu puṟap pāṭṭil
	tāli kaḷintaṉṟum ilaṉē
eṉṟu eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu6 ārāyattakkatu.
muṟkālattil pulippal tāli eṉṉum nakiyum pūṇṭi runtaṉar. iṉṟu kuḻantikaḷukkup puli nakattip poṟṟakaṭṭil potintu caṅkiliyil kōttu aṇikiṉṟaṉar, makaḷirum aṇi kiṉṟaṉar.7
	maṟaṅkoḷ vayappuli vāypiḷantu peṟṟa
	 māli veṇpaṟ ṟāliniri pūṭṭi
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.8 iṉṉum pala nūṟkaḷil pulippal, puli nakam pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cataṅki pōṉṟa kāl aṇikaḷic ciṟuvarkaḷ aṇintu vantatākat terikiṟatu. nallicip pulavarākiya uruttiraṅ kaṇṇa ṉār paṭṭiṉappāliyil
	poṟkāl putalvar
eṉṟu viḷakkik kūṟukiṟār.9
potuvākak kuḻantikaḷukkut taliyil ciṟu cuṭṭiyum kātil ciṟu kuṇṭalamum kaḻuttil aimpaṭit tāliyum kiyil kāppu, viralil mōtiram pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷum iṭuppil poṉñāṉum aric cataṅkiyum kālil taṇṭi cataṅki pōṉṟa aṇikaḷum aṇivippārkaḷ. peṇ kuḻantiyāṉāl ariyil ari mūṭi eṉṉum poṉ aṇiyum aṇivippārkaḷ.
kāppip pōl kuḻantikaḷukku aimpaṭit tāli pātukāppiṟkāka eḻunta aṇikalaṉākum. aimpaṭit tāli kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya tirumāliṉ caṅku, cakkaram, vil, vāḷ, kati iṉṉō raṉṉa aintu paṭik kalaṉkaḷiyum ciṟiya vaṭivil ceytu kōttuk kaḻuttil aṇiyappaṭum oru paḻaṅkāla aṇi. itu kuḻantikaḷukku nōy varātiruppataṟkāka kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.
āṇkaḷ āti kālattil peṇkaḷip pōl pēraṇikaḷi atikamāka aṇivatilli. kātil kaṭukkaṉ allatu kuṇṭalam kaṭukkaṉ pōṉṟa ētāvatu oru aṇikalaṉiyum kaḻuttil oṉṟiraṇṭu mālikaḷiyum kiyil kāppu, kolucu, vīrac caṅkili, ki viralkaḷil mōtiram, puyattil vākuvalayam kālil taṇṭi, vīrac caṅkili pōṉṟa oṉṟiraṇṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. atuvum perumpālum aracarkaḷē aṇintu vantaṉar. aracarkaḷiṉ cakāptam tamiḻakattil muṭiyavum anta aṇikalaṉ kaḷum aṭiyōṭu maṟintu viṭṭaṉa.
perumpālum āṇkaḷ kātil oru ciṟu kaṭukkaṉ maṭṭum aṇiyat talippaṭṭaṉar. kaḻuttil oru cila paṇakkāra vāliparkaḷ oru ciṟu poṟ caṅkili allatu poṟṟakaṭṭāl potinta urutti rākkam (kavuṭu) allatu poṉ maṇikaḷ iṭi iṭiyē kōtta tuḷaci maṇi, kikaḷil iru poṟ kāppu, allatu oru kiyil oru caṅkili, viralkaḷil 4 allatu 5 mōtiraṅkaḷ iṭuppil poṉ koṭi (ariñāṇ) maṭṭum aṇintu vantaṉar.
muṟkālan toṭṭu āṇkaḷ atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇiya virumpavilli. āṉāl illatti alaṅkarikka akamuṭi yāḷē aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu poṉ kāykkum maram eṉa viḷaṅka vēṇṭumeṉa āṇkaḷ ācippaṭṭaṉar. paṇṭu āṇkaḷ kuṟiñci nila vāḻkkiyilum mulli nila vāḻkkiyilum puliyōṭu pōrāṭi veṟṟi peṟṟatum ataṉ pal ataṉ nakam mutaliyavi kaḷik koṇṭu vantu tam kātalikkō maṉivikkō aḷittu ati avarkaḷ aṇintiruppatik kaṇṭukaḷittu vantaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. āṇkaḷ poruḷ tēṭa veḷiyūrukkum, veḷi nāṭu kaḷukkum cella nērntatu. avarkaḷ kal uṭikkavum maṇ cumakkavum vēṇṭiya nili eḻuntatu. vīrarkaḷ pakivarkaḷōṭu pōriṭuvatu avaciyamākat tōṉṟiyatu. in niliyil avarkaḷ viliyuyarnta kaṟkaḷ patitta poṉ aṇikaḷiyellām aṇintuk koḷḷa muṭiyātiruntatu. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭilē iruntu vantatāl avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇiyum vāyppu atikam eḻuntatu.
āṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ
ātikālattil aracarkaḷē atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. atiyoṭṭi amiccarkaḷum periya nilak kiḻārkaḷum, periya vaṇikarkaḷum taṅkaḷ uyarniliyi ulakiṟkuk kāṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. āti kālattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi āḻntu ārāyntu pārttāl āṇkaḷ atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷi - pēraṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭirun tārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟavataṟkilli. avarkaḷ viliyuyarnta palvēṟu vaṇṇappaṭṭāṭikaḷi aṇintu vantārkaḷ eṉṟum col vataṟkilli. paṇṭutoṭṭup parutti āṭikaḷi - atuvum nāṉkumuḻa āṭiyi ariyil kaṭṭiyum tōḷil iraṇṭu muḻat tuṇṭiyumē aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ eppoḻutum āṭamparam aṟṟa āṭikaḷiyē aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
paṇṭutoṭṭu iṉṟuvari tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟinta aḷavuḷḷa āṭikaḷiyē aṇintu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭamuṭiyum.
	uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppatu nāṉkumuḻam
eṉṟa pāṭṭē tamiḻaṉiṉ āṭamparamaṟṟa vāḻviṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum. muṟkāla aracarkaḷkūṭa ariyil nāṉku muḻa āṭi yum mēlē oru nīḷamāṉa cālviyum aṇintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟē aṟikiṟōm. avarkaḷ kirēkkarkaḷōṭum, urōmarkaḷōṭum neruṅkip paḻaki vaṇika uṟavu koṇṭirunta kālattilum piṟarip pōla caṭṭikaḷ aṇintu koḷvati virumpavilli. kuḷirāṉa nāṭṭukkē iṟukkamāṉa uṭikaḷum nīṇṭa caṭṭikaḷum tēvi. veppanāṭṭiṟku avi tēviyilli eṉṟum eṇṇi vantaṉar. atōṭu caṭṭikaḷ aṇivati iḻivākavum karutivantaṉar. caṭṭikaḷ aṇipavarkaḷi kañcuka mākkaḷ, caṭṭik kārarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟi avarkaḷik kuṟivākavē matittu vantaṉar.
makuṭam
muṟkāla maṉṉarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantālum peṇkaḷip pōl pēraṇikaḷi aṇiyavilli. maṉṉarkaḷ taliyil makuṭam aṇintu vantaṉar. maṟavarkaḷ talippāki aṇintu vantaṉar. muṉivarkaḷ caṭāpāran tāṅki vantaṉar. maṉṉarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ patavikkāka makuṭam tāṅkuvatu iṉṟi yamiyātatāka iruntatu. makuṭaṅkaḷiṉ vakikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟamum aḷavum poṉṉiṉ eṭiyum maṇikaḷiṉ peyar kaḷum eṭiyum aṇiceyya vēṇṭiya muṟiyum muṭicūṭa vēṇṭiya taṉmiyum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. vīrakkaḻal
maṉṉarkaḷ kālil tam vīrattiṟku aṟikuṟiyākavē kaḻal pūṇṭiruppārkaḷ. itip pōrukkup pōkum poḻutu kaṇṭippāka aṇintu celvar. pakivarkaḷi veṉṟa vēntaṉ tōlviyuṟṟa aracaṉiṉ makuṭattiluḷḷa poṉṉi urukkik kaḻal eṉṉum aṇiceytu tām avarkaḷi veṉṟataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka kālil aṇintukoḷvāṉ. itaṟku vīrakkaḻal eṉṟu peyar.
2. vīrak kaṇṭi
itukālil aṇintu koḷḷum oru poṉ aṇikala ṉākum. ikkaṇṭi periya maṇikaḷ kōtta aṇikalaṉākum. vīrarkaḷ pōril pakivarkaḷi viraṭṭiyaṭittu veṟṟi koṇṭa taṟkāka aṇivikkappaṭum aṇi.
3. cataṅki
itukālil aṇintu koḷḷum oru aṇiyākum. itu muṟkālattil poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟatu. piṟkālattil veḷḷi, pittaḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟatu.
4. ariyaṇi ariñāṇ
ariñāṇ aracarkaḷum amiccarkaḷum celvarkaḷum ariyil tarikkum poṉṉālāṉa tiraṭciyāṉa oru koṭiyākum. intap poṟkoṭi pūṇum vaḻakkam iṉṟum pala paṇakkārar kaḷiṭam iruntu varukiṟatu. itaṉ mukappil ciṅkat talivippatu ciṟappu. itaṉik kaṭi cūttiram eṉpārkaḷ ciṟpikaḷ.
5. pavaḷa vaṭam
pavaḷa vaṭamum āṇkaḷ ariyil aṇiyum aṇikaḷil oṉ ṟākum. iṭi iṭiyē poṉ maṇikaḷum kōkkap paṭṭirukkum. iti maṉṉarkaḷ viruppamuṭaṉ aṇivar.
6. toṭi
toṭiyaṇi eṉpatu karu muttukkaḷāl kōkkappaṭṭu ari yil aṇiyum ariya iṭiyaṇiyākum. iti āṇkaḷ aṇivar.
7. kaṅkaṇam
kaṅkaṇam kiyil aṇintu koḷḷum kolucu pōṉṟa oru vitap poṉ aṇiyākum. itu ātiyil makkaḷ pūṇum uṟutikkuc cāṉṟāka eḻuntatu.
8. vīravaḷi
itu kiyil aṇintu koḷḷum oru poṉ aṇiyākum. vīrarkaḷ veṟṟi peṟa naṭattiyap pōrukkāka avarkaḷukku aṇi vikkappaṭum oru vaḷi.
9. kaṭakam
kāppi yotta oru aṇikalaṉ makaravāy uṭiyatu; māṇikkam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ aḻuttic ceyyap peṟṟatu; itu ik kāla vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. kaviṉ peṟa itic ceyta kikku kaṭakam pōṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
10. mōtiram
ki viralkaḷil naṭuviral nīṅkalāka ēṉiya nāṉku viral kaḷilum poṉṉāl ceyta mōtiraṅkaḷ aṇivar. atil pala vēṟu maṇikaḷum patikkap peṟṟirukkum.
11. kolucu
kolucu kāppu pōṉṟu kiyil aṇiyum oru poṉ aṇikalaṉākum. ituvum iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
12. kāppu
itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru ki aṇiyākum. itu ātiyil kaṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭatu. appāl pukalākac ceyyap paṭṭatu. mukappil kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟum.
13. patakkam
ituvum tōḷaṇikaḷil oṉṟākum. itu poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. vākuvalayamum patakkam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. āṉāl iraṇṭum vevvēṟāṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ.
14. vākuvalayam
aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīrattiṟku aṟikuṟiyāka puyattil vāku valayam eṉṉum poṉ aṇiyip puṉintu pōrukkup pōvārkaḷ. itaṉi aṅkatam eṉpar. tōḷ vaḷiyi kēyūram (vaṅki) eṉpar.
15. kaḻuttaṇi vaṉṉacaram
vīrarkaḷ pōril pakivarkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷip paṟittu yāṉi yiṉ tantaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu eṭukkum muttukkaḷōṭu narampil kōttu kaḻuttil aṇintu pōrukkuc celvar.
16. poṉ māli
poṉṉāl ceyta muttu vaṭam. iti aracarkaḷ eppoḻutum kaḻuttil pūṇṭu koṇṭiruppatu vaḻakkam.
17. muttu vaṭam
muttu vaṭam eṉpatu uyarnta muttukkaḷit tuḷiyiṭṭu nūlil allatu kampiyil kōttu  māliyākak kaḻuttil celvarkaḷum aracarkaḷum aṇivatākum.
18. kaṭukkaṉ
itu kātil pōṭum oru poṉ vāḷiyākum. itu vaṭṭa mākavō allatu nīṇṭu vaḷintō irukkum. itaṉ iṭiyil kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟirukkum. itu muṟkālattil peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum aṇiyum aṇikalaṉāka iruntatu.
19. kuṇṭalam
itu oru kātaṇi. kātaṇi pala vakippaṭum. mīṉ vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru poṉ aṇi makarak kuṇṭalam eṉappaṭum. vāḻippū, tāmari mokku, pāmpu vaṭivamāka ellām kuṇṭalam irukkum.
peṇkaḷ pēraṇikaḷ palavaṟṟi aṇintiruntaṉar. iṉṟu pēraṇikaḷ aṉittum maṟintu makaḷir ciṟṟaṇikaḷ ciṟappuṭaṉ aṇintu koḷḷum nili eḻuntuḷḷatu. ippoḻutu pala ciṟṟaṇikaḷum maṟintu cila ciṟṟaṇikaḷ peyar aḷavilēyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṇikaḷ aṇivatilē peṇkaḷukku ōraḷavu veṟuppu eḻumāṟu cūḻniliyum nākarikamum avarkaḷi neruṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
cila āṇṭukaḷukku veḷi nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu āṅkilam paṭittu uyarnta paṭṭam peṟṟa peṇkaḷ cilaric cantittēṉ. avarkaḷil oruvar mūkkil pullākkum kātil ciṟu virat tōṭum aṇintiruntār. avaratu pullākkup peṇkaḷ palarik kavarntatu. palarum pullākkup pōṭa muṉintaṉar. maṟṟoru maṅki kaḻuttil paṭṭan tīṭṭiya aḻakiya pavaḷatti mēlum kīḻum poṟ pūvil potintiruppatu pōl ceytu poṉ caṅkilikaḷ kōttu kaḻuttil cumār 10 periya pavaḷaṅkaḷi aṇintiruntār. avaratu pavaḷa vaṭam pala peṇkaḷukkuk kavarcciyaḷittatu.
itaṉ mūlam paḻan tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭil paṟṟuḷḷa eṉṉip pōṉṟavarkaḷukkup paṭitta peṇkaḷ niṉittāl namatu paḻiya paṇpāṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷum mīṇṭum namatu nāṭṭil tuḷirkkac ceytu viṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu uṟutiyāṉa nampikki eḻuntuḷḷatu.
kuṟippu
1.	kalikkaḷañciyam tokuti 1. pak. 358
2. 	Ornament - Annadacoomaraswamy. (Bulletin)
3.	yāḻnūl - vipulāṉantar
4	The bands of lattice - work and the edges of the jewel are formed of the finest filaments and globules of gold, soldered together with marvellous skill and accuracy. The Drawing is the full size of Pecharf, but the work is so minute that I have added enlarged sections of the inner and outer edges to explin the construction. The outer edges, where it meets the fastening is finished by a parrot minutely ohased, and four rubies emblish this wonderful nose ring. The goldsmiths who do this fine built up work are a special class; they are found only in villages as their customers are generally agriculturists, their fellow workmen in the large towns look upon them as low castes.” - The Journal of Indian Art and Industry. Vol. V. “The Art industries of the Madras Presidency - by E.B. Havel. P. 5
5.	maṇimēkali 3 : 137	
cevvāyk kutali meypeṟā matali	
cintupu ciṉṉī rimpaṭi naṉippa	
vaṟṟaṅ kāvāc cuṟṟuṭip pūntukil
6.	puṟappāṭṭu
7.	cilappatikāram 17 : 27 - 28	
maṟaṅkoḷ vayappuli vāypiḷantu peṟṟa	
māli veṇ paṟṟāli niripūṭṭi
8.	paṭṭiṉappāli

10. cācaṉac cāṉṟukaḷ
namatu tamiḻakattil toṉṟu toṭṭu, varalāṟu variyum vaḻakku illi. paḻam poruḷkaḷip pātukāttu vikkum paṇpu kuṉṟiviṭṭatu. eṉavē iṉṟu namatu camayam, varalāṟu, moḻi, kali, eḻuttu, paṭimam, ōviyam, aṇikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, āṭi, ici, kūttu, maruttuvam, vāṉanūl, kali, paṇpāṭu pōṉṟavikaḷip paṟṟi ārāya muṉvarupavarkaḷ perum allalukku āṭpaṭṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉṟiya tamiḻarkaḷ ceyta tavap payaṉāka aḻiyātu niṟkum kōyilkaḷum avikaḷil uḷḷa cilikaḷum cempup paṭimaṅkaḷum ōviyaṅkaḷum aṇikalaṉ kaḷum icik karuvikaḷum kalveṭṭu, cep pēṭukaḷum aḻiyātu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷi āyntu nūl vaṭiva mākkuvatil nām iṉṟu atikamāṉa akkari eṭuttuk koḷḷavilli. avi aṉittum ārāyappaṭumāyiṉ namatu varalāṟu, moḻi, kali, eḻuttu, nākarikam, toṉmi mutaliyavikaḷip paṟṟiya putiya uṇmikaḷ veḷivarum.
iṉṟu, aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟi ārāya muṉvarupavar paṇṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷik kaṇṇāl pārkkavē muṭivatilli. oru cila aṇikalaṉkaḷ namatu tirukkōyilkaḷil irukkiṉṟaṉa. perumpālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷik kāṇavē muṭiyavilli. āṉāl muṟkālattil irunta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum avi enta uṟuppukaḷil aṇiyat takkaṉa eṉṟum, eṉṉa poruḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa? evvita maṇikaḷ aḻuttap peṟṟaṉa? eṉṟum namatu caṅkak kāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷum uriyāciriyarkaḷum viḷakkam tantuḷḷārkaḷ. namatu kalveṭṭukaḷilum ceppēṭukaḷilum, kōyilkaḷukku aṇikalaṉkaḷik kāṇikkiyāka aḷitta maṉṉar peyarum aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum ataṉ eṭi mutaliya vivaraṅkaḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl anta aṇikaḷ enta āṇṭil uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa? enta nāṭṭiṉ amippi yuṭiyaṉa? enta pāṇiyil ceyyappaṭṭaṉa? eṉpati nām eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.
atōṭu kōyilil uḷḷa ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum, aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kal veṭṭukaḷilum ceppēṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum paṭimaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum aṇikalaṉ kaḷil pala paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭum kālaṅkaḷil uḷḷa aṇi kalaṉkaḷāka illi, ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭum paḻaṅkāla naki kaḷākum. mēlum avi viliyuyarnta pacum poṉṉāl nalla kuṟṟa maṟṟa maṇikaḷ iḻittuc ceyyap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷākum. akkāla makkaḷ kiṭittaṟkariya periya maṇikaḷi vilikku vāṅkit teyvat tirumēṉikaḷiṟ pūṭṭi makiḻntaṉar eṉpataṟku namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē namatu kal veṭṭukaḷilum ceppēṭukaḷilum kāṇak kiṭikkum aṇikalaṉ kaḷi iṅku ārāyvōm.
inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu, maṉita nākarikattiṉ poṟkāla māka irukkiṟatu - ēṉ nākarikattiṉ uccak kaṭṭamāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. makkaḷiṉ aṟivum āyvum nākarikamum viṇ muṭṭa uyarntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu, moḻi, eḻuttu paṇpāṭu, kali, nākarikam mutaliyavi āḻntu aṟivup pāṅkāka ārāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṟiñarkaḷ aṟiviyal āyvu muṟiyil kūrntu nōkkic cintittup pārttup pala putiya uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar.
āṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ, tol kuṭimakkaḷ; avarkaḷ moḻi, kali, varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam mikap paḻami vāyntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭālum aṟiviyal pāṅkāka avikaḷip paṟṟi āyntu iṉṉum muṭivu kaṭṭavilli. cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭimakkaḷiṉ toṉmikkum tiṇmikkum paṇpiṟkum aṉpiṟkum nākarikattiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāy, oru peruṅkaruvūlamāy iruppiṉum accīriya karuvūlam aṟiviyal āyvu eṉṉum oḷiyiṉ vaḻiyil tamiḻarkaḷ ceṉṟu pōtiya āyvukaḷ naṭatta muṉvaravilli. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil tāṉ tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭimakkaḷiṉ taliyāṉa makkaḷāṉa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ moḻi, eḻuttu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, varalāṟu mutaliyavikaḷil, etik kūṟa muṟpaṭṭālum tamiḻ nāṭṭilē tamiḻarkaḷ pōl vāḻum oru cilar eṭuttataṟkellām maṟuppuk kūṟiyum tāḻttiyum iḻittum kūṟuvatiyē taṅkaḷ āyvākat taṅkaḷ toḻilākak koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. mēlum tamiḻarkaḷ piṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭukaḷāl nākarikattāl ciṟappuṟṟu vāḻpavarkaḷ eṉṟa taṅkaḷ kūṟṟi nilināṭṭa muyalkiṉṟaṉar. namatu nākarikam eppaṭi paḻami yāṉatu? evvāṟu āriya nākarikattiṟku muntiyatu? eṉpataṟku āṇṭum, ātāramum kēṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmiyilē namatu paṇpāṭṭiṉ ciṟappāṉa kālatti nilināṭṭa ātāram uṇṭu; āṉāl pōtiya ātāram ituvari tēṭappaṭavilli; iṉṉum kiṭikkavilli. atōṭu kiṭittuḷḷa ātāraṅkaḷ paṟṟi nammavarkaḷ iṉṉum cariyāka aṟintu koḷḷavum illi. eṉavē tamiḻp pakivarkaḷ tamiḻ maṇṇilē tali nimirntu niṉṟu koṇṭu naṭakka iṭam ēṟpaṭṭu varukiṟatu. ākavē tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ etiyum āyntu takka cāṉṟu kāṭṭi nilināṭṭa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta kālattil vāḻntu varukiṟārkaḷ eṉpati iṅkuk kōṭiṭṭukkāṭṭi niṉippūṭṭik koḷ kiṉṟēṉ.
tamiḻ nāṭṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷukku iṉṉum pōtiya puti poruḷ ārāyccic cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭavilli. arappā, mokañca tārō, arikkaṉ mēṭu, āticcanallūr pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil oru cila paḻaṅkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl avi pōtumāṉaviyalla eṉpatu namatu uṟutiyāṉa karuttākum. namakkup pōtiya ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa. ellāvaṟṟiyum viṭa nam tirukkōyilkaḷil tikaḻum cilikaḷum cempup paṭimaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉmītu aṇivittu varum pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷākum. iviyaṉṟi intat tiru uruvaṅkaḷ mītu aṇivikka maṉṉarkaḷum aracikaḷum vaḷḷalkaḷum piṟarum vaḻaṅkiya aṇi kalaṉkaḷum uḷa. ivikaḷi avarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku aḷitta viparaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum, aṇikalaṉ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum, vaḻaṅkiya kālaṅkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiyataṟkuriya avaciyam mutaliyavi kaḷiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. inta cācaṉaṅkaḷil namatu nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷum piṟa nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷum makkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum kōyilkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. intac cācaṉaṅkaḷ, kāṭṭilum mēṭṭilum kiṇaṟu, kuḷam pōṉṟa nīr nilikaḷilum ēṉ? - pūmik kuḷḷum putintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷi yellām cēkarikka vēṇṭum. cēkarikkappaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷ pala paṭikkappaṭāmalum ārāyappaṭāmalum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi aṉittum paṭittu nūl vaṭivil veḷivarac ceyya vēṇṭum. iṉṟu nūl vaṭivam peṟṟa cācaṉaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil namatu aṇi kalaṉkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. antap pakuti kaḷil cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟōm.
cōḻarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ
tañcāvūril kōyil koṇṭirukkum periya nāyakikkum peru uṭiyārukkum mā-irācarāca cōḻaṉ  (Raja Raja the Great) eṉṟa civa pāta cēkaraṉ eṉṉum māmaṉṉaṉ nava maṇikaḷālāṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum poṉ veḷḷi pōṉṟa uyarnta tātup poruḷkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷiyum viḷakkukaḷ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷiyum ceyvittu civapāta cēkaraṉ eṉṟa taṉ peyarip poṟittu, kāḷaṅkaḷ, tiruppaḷḷit toṅkal, tavaḷac cattiram, tirukkoṟṟakkuṭi, īccōppiki, veṇcāmarakki, kāḷañci mutaliyavikaḷiyum aḷittuḷḷāṉ. mēlum nal aṇi kalaṉkaḷāṉa cirimuṭi, vīrappaṭṭam, tiru utarap pantaṉam, tiruvaṭik kāri, tiruppaṭṭiki, capta cari, pañca cari, tirukkutampi, tōṭu, irāca varttam, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, tāli maṇivaṭam, ciricantam mutaliya uyariya poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum vaṭukavāḷi, ēkavali, muttiṉ cūṭakam, tirukkālvaṭam mutaliya muttu aṇikalaṉkaḷum kaṇṭanāṇ, pullikik kaṇṭa nāṇ, pāca māli, māṇikkattiṉ tāli, ciri vākuvalayam, patakkam, irattiṉa vaḷiyam, irattiṉa kaṭakam, irattiṉa mōtiram, navarattiṉa mōtiram, piruṣṭa kaṇṭiki mutaliya maṇikaḷālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷum poṉṉā lum, veḷḷiyālum ceyta oṭṭu vaṭṭil, kalacam, kuṭam, taṭṭam, kuṟumaṭal, kiṭāram mutaliya parikalaṅkaḷum nipantamāka aḷikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
irācarāca cōḻaṉ tirumakaḷ mātēvaṭikaḷār tiruviyāṟṟu olōka mātēvīcura muṭiyārkku aḷitta nivantaṅkaḷip paṟṟit tiruviyāṟṟuk kalveṭṭuk kūṟuvatāvatu :- ciri irācarāca tēvarkku āṭiyaruḷap paṉmānam peṟṟa poṉṉil yāṇṭu 25-vatu nāḷ 164il cātti aruḷak kuṭutta paṭṭam oṉṟu niṟi kuṭiñi kallāl poṉ nāṟpat teṇ kaḻañcē mukkāl mañcāṭi yum mēṟpaṭi paṭṭam oṉṟu mēṟpaṭiyil niṟi nāṟpat teṇ kaḻañcē mukkālē mañcāṭiyum it tēvarkku yāṇṭu 27-vatu.... ceytiṭṭa aḻakiya tēvar tirumuṭi oṉṟu, poṉ eḻupattu muk kaḻañcē mukkālum itil ēṟiṉa muttu nāṉūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟu iraṇṭu cavar yōkam nūṟṟu oru pattāṟum pottip patiṉālum, taruppu nūṟṟeṇpattu iraṇṭum uḷpaṭa niṟi eṇpattu eṇ kaḻañcari. mēṟpaṭi cāttiyaruḷum tirumāli oṉṟiṉuḷ poṉ pattoṉpatiṉ kaḻañcēy mañcāṭiyum kuṉṟiyum itaṉuḷ kaṭṭiṉa muttu aympattu ēḻum potti oṉpatum taruppu irupattintum cavaru yōkam aṟupattu mūṉṟu uḷpaṭat tirumāli oṉṟum mēṟpaṭiyār cāttiyaruḷum celva viṭaṅkaṉ ciṭukku iraṇṭiṉāl muttup patiṉeṭṭiṉāl niṟi kaḻañcēy mañcāṭiyum ivi kōtta nūliṉāl eṇ kaḻañcē mañcāṭiyum kuṉṟiṉ muttu uḻuttu nāliṉāl niṉṟa nālu mañcāṭiyum kaḻiñcum cavaru yōkam ēḻum taruppu eṭṭum uḷpaṭa niṉṟa mū...... y ēḻu mañcāṭi mēṟpaṭiyār cāttiyaruḷum vaṭam oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu muppattu nālilum niṟi nāṟ kaḻiñcēy mañcāṭiyum tāḷimpam iraṇṭum paṭukaṇ oṉṟum kokkuvāy oṉṟum uḷpaṭa poṉ eṭṭum mañcāṭi mēṟpaṭiyār cātti aruḷum vaṭam oṉṟiṉuḷ mu..... iraṇṭiṉāl pavaḷam iraṇṭum irācāttuvam oṉṟum nīlam - mēṟpaṭiyār cārttiyaruḷum vaṭam oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu irupat tāṟiṉāl niṟimuk kaḻañcēy arikkālum tāḷimpam iraṇṭum paṭukaṇ oṉṟum uṭpaṭa poṉ mukkālē kuṉṟiyum, pavaḷam iraṇṭum irācāvattuvam iraṇṭum uḷpaṭa vaṭam oṉṟum mēṟpaṭiyār cārtti aruḷum tiricaram oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu nūṟṟiruppat tēḻiṉāl niṟi ēḻu kaḻañcēy mañcāṭiyum mūṉ ṟoṉṟāka aṭukki viḷakkiṉa tāḷimpam iraṇṭum paṭukaṇ oṉṟum kokkuvāy oṉṟum niṟi muk kaḻañcariyē nālu mañcāṭiyum kuṉṟi pavaḻam āṟum irācavattam āṟum uḷpaṭa tiricaram oṉṟu mēṟpaṭiyār cātti aruḷum pañca cari oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu irunūṟṟeṇpat tiraṇṭiṉāl niṟi pattoṉpatiṉ kaḻiñcum añcoṉṟāka aṭukki viḷakkiṉa tāḷimpam iraṇṭum paṭukaṇ oṉṟum kokkuvāy oṉṟum iṭik kaṭṭu iraṇṭum uṭpaṭap poṉ arik kaḻiñcēy mukkālēy mañcāṭiyum itaṉil kōtta pavaḷam pattum nīlam pattum cavaru yōkam pattum uḷpaṭa pañca cari oṉṟum mēṟpaṭiyār cāttiyaruḷum kaḷāpam oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu nūṟṟirupattiraṇṭiṉāl niṟi eṇ kaḻiñ cēy oṉpatu mañcāṭiyum kuṉṟiyum nāleṉṟāka aṭukki viḷakkiṉa tāḷimpam iraṇṭum paṭukaṇ oṉṟum kokkuvāy oṉṟum iṭik kaṭṭu iraṇṭum uḷpaṭap poṉ muk kaḻañcēy kālum pavaḷam eṭṭum uḷpaṭak kaḷāpam oṉṟum mēṟpaṭiyār cārtti yaruḷum muttiṉ vaḷiyal nālum muttiṉ kāl kāṟi iraṇṭum uḷpaṭap poṉ muppattu mukkaḻañ cariyē mūṉṟu mañcāṭiyum kuṉṟiyum ivaṟṟuḷ vaḷiyal oṉṟuḷ muttu irunūṟ ṟeḻupatum mēṟpaṭi oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu irunūṟṟup pattoṉpatum mēṟpaṭi oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu irunūṟṟaṟupatum tirukkāl kāṟi oṉṟiṉuḷ muttu nūṟṟu nāṟpattēḻum āka uru āṟiṉuḷ muttu āyirattu munnūṟṟup patiṉmūṉṟu piḷḷiyār kaṇapatiyār tiruvāparaṇam tirumuṭi oṉṟum paṭṭam oṉṟum poṟ pū nālum........ oṉṟum āka niṟi mukkaḻañcēy mukkālē...... eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.2
itil mātēvaṭikaḷār olōka mātēvīc carattup paṭṭam kutampi, nīlattiṉ uḻuttōraṇi, tiruccaṉṉi vaṭam kaṭṭi, tirumuṭi, tirumāli, celva viṭaṅkaṉ, ciṭukku, vaṭam, iṭukku, vaḷiyal, tiricaram, kaḷāpam, tāḷiṉpam muttiṉ  vaḷiyal, muttiṉ kāl kāṟi, muttiṉ paṭṭiki, kōḷaki, tāḷ, kūṭṭuk kampi, ōraṇi, pañca cari, ciricantam mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷ māṉiyamāka aḷittuḷḷa viparam naṉku tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iḵtaṉṟi maṟṟōr tiruvāvaṭu tuṟik kalveṭṭil pala aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi aṭiyil varumāṟākum :
uṭiyār, ciri irācēntira cōḻa tēvar nam pirāṭṭiyār tiri lōkkiya mātēviyār taṅkaḷ ācciyār irāmaṉ apimāṉa toṅkiyār tiruvā vaṭu tuṟi uṭiyārkku amutu ceytaruḷa veḷḷit taḷiki oṉṟu niṟi kuṭiñik kallāl eḻupatiṉ kaḻañcum mēṟpaṭiyārē uṭiyārkkuk koṭutta poṟ koḷki oṉṟu kuṭiñik kallāl niṟi cem poṉ nāṉūṟṟu oru pattu muk kaḷañcariyum irācātirāca tēvar koṭutta poṉ irupattaṟu kaḷañcum periya vēḷattu alōyaṉ aṇukki yiṭṭa poṉ nūṟṟu muppattu eṇ kaḻañcum, eṇ kaḻañcum mēṟpaṭi vēḷattu..... iṭṭa poṉ muppattaṟu kaḻañcē mukkālum uḷpaṭa ceyta poṟ pū irupat tēḻum ceṅkaḻu nīrp pū muppattāṟum māntaḷir oṉṟum picca tēvar kaṇṭa kāṟi oṉṟum poṟki īccōppi oṉṟum muṉpu kal veṭṭiṉa koḷkiyilum āka ittēvar aṭiyāṉ tiru nāvukkariyaṉ picciyāl vanta kuṭiñik kallāl māṉap poṉ aṟu nūṟṟu eḻupatiṉ kaḻañcē mukkālē mūṉṟu mañcāṭiyum, muṉpuḷḷa poṉ uṭiyār cāttum.......paṭṭam oṉṟum poṟ pū irupat toṉṟum ivaṉ cāttum muttuc cari nāliṉāl poṉṉum cantiracēkaratēvartirupahivēkamoṉṟumtiruvaṭiniliiraṇṭiṉālpōntapoṉṉumuḷḷiṭṭatirumēṉikaḷcāttumtiruvāparaṇaṅkaḷālumpoṟkiīccōppiyālumākakkuṭiñikkallālirācarācaṉmāṭikkīḻoṉpatumāṟipoṉnāṉūṟṟuaṟupattorukaḻañcēoṉpatumañcāṭiyumākappoṉāyirattuorunūṟṟunāṟpattirukaḷañcēēḻumañcāṭi.....3e‹ṟukūWmkalveṭLppakutiyāYmivikiḷaaṟiyalā«.
namatu maṉṉarkaḷ makiḻntu mātēvarkku aḷitta māṉiyaṅ kaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu arakku, ceppāṇi, caraṭu mutaliyavikaḷi nīkkip poṉṉi maṭṭum taṉiyāka niṟiyeṭuttum; avikaḷil maṇikaḷ aḻuttap peṟṟiruntāl avi ivvaḷavu eṭiyuṭiyaṉa iṉṉiṉṉa taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu urittu, itaṉviyittadkāReṉṟu«kuṟippiṭa¥peṟṟirukkiṟatu. maṇikaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu porivu, muṟivu, kākapintu iratta pintueṉwita‹kuṟṟaṅkaḷu«kuṇa§kaḷu«kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷad. akkālattil oruvita aṇikalaṉukku cōṉakac ciṭukkiṉ kūṭu eṉṟu peyar kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cōṉakaṉ eṉṟa patam muṟkālattil kirēkkar, arēpiyar pōṉṟa varkaḷik kuṟippiṭuvatākum. eṉavē 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cōḻa nāṭṭil cōṉakart toṭarpu atikam iruntatākat terikiṟatu.
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	S.I.I.Vo. II, ii PP. 203-217 also on 69 of 1888.
2.	itu tiruviyāṟṟuk kalveṭṭu. aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattiluḷḷa vittuvāṉ tiru. vi. cuntarēcavāṇṭiyār avarkaḷāl tiruviyāṟu oru lōka mā tēvīc curattiṉ artta maṇṭapac cuvaril irācarāca cōḻaṉ makaḷ mātēvaṭikaḷār aḷitta nipantaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. ikkalveṭṭu iṉṟuvari teṉ intiyak kalveṭṭut tokutiyil veḷiyiṭap peṟātatākum.
3.	tiruvāvaṭutuṟik kalveṭṭu vittuvāṉ vi. cuntarēca vāṇṭiyār (tiruviyāṟu) avarkaḷ  paṭieṭuttutirukkōyileṉṉumtiṅkaḷveḷiyīṭṭilmāli4,malar8il(1962mēâṅkaḷil)âUvāvaṭutuṟikkašveṭṭuvulāṟue‹ṉumtilappiltḷiyiṭṭaka£ṭuriyākum.m) flveṭṭukaḷilfcu,fṇam,bpāṉMkiyeṇayaṅkaḷipgṟṟiecciṉārkkiṉiyarÓvakacintāmaṇicriyilFṟippiṭLḷḷār.fhz« eṉpatu oru poṟkācu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. kāṇam poṉ ivikaḷukkuc camamāṉa matippuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ tiruccentūr kalveṭṭil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. kāṇam poṉ ivi oru kācil pattil oru paṅkākum.
