11. ciṟpa nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ
namatu  muṟkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷil mikat toṉmiyāṉavi kaḷi ippoḻutu kāṇpatē aritu. makkaḷ iṉṟu paḻaṅkālap pāṇikaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇiya virumpuvatē illi. aṇiya virumpāviṭṭālum, avikaḷip pātukāttu vittirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṭac colvataṟkilli. āṉāl airōppiyarkaḷ ulakil atikamākap putumiyi virumpupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ āṭikaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷum illaṅkaḷum taṭṭu muṭṭuc cāmāṉkaḷum ovvoru nūṟṟāṇṭilum putup putu māṟṟam aṭintu vantuḷḷaṉa. nām āṇṭāṇṭu tōṟum peṟṟu vanta airōppiya nākarikam ippoḻutu nāḷukku nāḷ māṟṟam aṭintu varukiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum mēṉāṭṭār paḻami yāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyō, āṭikaḷiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷiyō, kōppikaḷiyō, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyō aḻittu viṭuvatilli; pattiramākap pātukāttu varukiṟārkaḷ. ammakkaḷ taṅkaḷ illattiṟku varum viruntiṉarkaḷukku, itu eṅkaḷ koḷḷup pēraṉār upayōkitta nāṟkāli, itu eṅkaḷ pāṭṭi aṇinta aṇikalaṉ, atu eṅkaḷ aṉṉi eḻutiya peṉcil, itu eṉatu māmiyār eḻutiya ūṟṟuppēṉā eṉṟellām eṭuttuk kāṭṭi makiḻkiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭṭil attakiya paḻam poruḷkaḷip pātukāttu vippatē kiṭiyātu. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka eṉatu vīṭṭil eṉ maṉivi eṉatu tāyār upayōkitta aṇikalaṉkaḷiyō, āṭikaḷiyō, veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷiyō pātukāttu vikkavilli, vikka virumpuvatumilli. avikaḷi viṟṟuviṭṭup putiya paṇṭaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvatē vaḻakkamāyviṭṭatu. ataṉāl namatunāṭṭil paḻiya pāttiraṅ kaḷiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷiyō paḻiya icikkaruvikaḷiyō paḻiya viḷakkukaḷiyō allatu paḻiya paṇṭaṅkaḷiyō kāṇamuṭivatilli. namatu varalāṟṟup paḻam perumiyik kūṟa muṉvarum eḻuttāḷarkaḷ evvita ātāramum iṉṟi allaṟpaṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ upayōkitta poṟkācukaḷil oṉṟu kūṭa iṉṟu evarum pattirappaṭutti vikka villi. ēṉ, maturiyil āṇṭa muculim maṉṉar kācukaḷiyum, nāyakkamaṉṉar kācukaḷiyum, āṅkilak kampeṉi āṭci veḷi yiṭṭa poṟkācukaḷiyum nām pātukāttu vikkat tavaṟi viṭṭōm. namatu aṭimic cakāptattil, camūkam peṟṟa iḻi niliyum, poruḷātāra vīḻcciyumē itaṟku mukkiya kāraṇaṅ kaḷākum. iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil oru cila paḻamiyāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kiṭippatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl avikaḷum paḻamiyāṉa kālattil ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷākat teriyavilli. avi paḻiya peyarkaḷip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl uruvattil perum māṟutal peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē paḻiya peyarkaḷuṭaṉ ippoḻutu putitākac ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟu tiṭṭamāka colla muṭiyum.
namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pala paḻiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi nām kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta putup peyarkaḷāyum, uruvaṅkaḷāyum uḷḷaṉa. in niliyil namatu paḻiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa uruvatti eppaṭip pārppatu?
āṉāl namatu ciṟpaṅkaḷil paḻamperum aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi uṟutiyākat tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻiya aṇi kalaṉkaḷiṉ uruvamēyākum. āṉāl avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi aṟivatu mikavum aritu. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil vaṭamoḻiyil peyarkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl ivi tamiḻaka aṇikaḷ alla eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uṇṭu. namatu kāḷiyi vaṭamoḻiyil kuri tēvi eṉṟum, murukaṉi kantaṉ eṉṟum tirumāli mahā viṣṇu eṉṟum civaperumāṉi caṅkaraṉ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatik koṇṭu ivi āriyarkaḷiṉ teyvamāki viṭumā? namatu kik kāppi ata kaṭakam eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāl atu āriyarkaḷ aṇi kalaṉākiviṭa muṭiyumā? namatu nāṭṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷukku vaṭa moḻip peyar cūṭṭiyatāl avi tamiḻaka aṇikalaṉkaḷ alla veṉṟu kūṟiviṭamuṭiyātu. namatu teyva uruvaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉittum tamiḻaka aṇikalaṉkaḷēyākum. oṉṟiraṇṭu ayalaka aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭiṉum avikaḷi nām eḷitil aṟiya muṭiyum. namatu ilakkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷip pōlaṉṟi namatu ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil iruppati aṟintu cilar aiyam uṟukiṉṟaṉar, uṇmiyil ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum teyvaṅkaḷ tamiḻar kaṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷākum. anta ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum muṟikaḷum tamiḻar muṟikaḷēyākum. intap paṭimaṅkaḷi uruvākkum ciṟpi kaḷum tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. āṉāl nam muṉṉōrkaḷ ekkāraṇatti ṉālō tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpak kaliyi vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuti vittuḷḷārkaḷ. ati vittu intac ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ nam nūlkaḷ allaveṉṟō aṅkuk kāṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷ nam aṇikalaṉkaḷ allaveṉṟō karutuvatu tavaṟu.
nam ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil palavitamāṉa mālikaḷum pala vitamāṉa vaḷikaḷum kāppukaḷum iṭiyaṇikaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kaṭakam, kāppu, kolucu, āṭakam, pariyakam, pācittāmaṇi, pavaḷam, palavaḷi, cūṭakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷellām oruvar orē camayattil kiyil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷā? paṭimaṅkaḷil pala aṇikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil kaṭakam etu? āṭakam etu? cūṭakam etu? pariyakam etu? pācittāmaṇi etu eṉṟu aṟivatu? eṉa viṉavukiṉṟārkaḷ. itu nalla kēḷviyēyākum. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum peyarkaḷip paṭittu vittuk koṇṭu cilikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭippatu ciramantāṉ. iti virumpupavarkaḷ tapatikaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu eḷitil aṟiya muṭiyum. nāṉ cila tapatikaḷiṭam cila aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kūṟi anta aṇikalaṉ eppaṭi irukkum eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷ cuvarkaḷilum tāḷ kaḷilum peṉcilik koṇṭu aḻakāka varintu kāṭṭiviṭ ṭārkaḷ. aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṟiya aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟi ārāynta  aṟiñarkaḷiṉ ottuḻippum ciṟpikaḷiṉ ātaravum iruntāl eḷitil ellā aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum aṟintu viṭa muṭiyum. ārāya muṉvantuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṟivuk kuṟivāl cila tavaṟukaḷum eḻum eṉpati nāṉ iṅkuk kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli. in niliyil namatu tamiḻakattiṉ karuvūlamākak karutap peṟum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkum aṇikalaṉ viḷakkam namatu ārāyccikku mika iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. avi aṭiyil varumāṟu:
cilparattiṉam eṉṉum cīriya nūliṉ patiṉāṟām atti yāyattil aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu :
talimītu mēl nōkki kūrmiyākak kāṇappaṭum aṇikalaṉē kirīṭam (makuṭam) ākum. namatu nāṭṭup paṭimaṅkaḷil ellām carva cātāraṇamāka itip pārkkalām. makuṭaṅkaḷil pala vitam uṇṭu. kirīṭa makuṭam eṉpatu oru vaki. tirumāl uruvaṅkaḷil taliyil kēcāntattil uṣṇīca paṭṭam oṉṟu taliyic cuṟṟiṉavāṟāka oru aṅkula aḷavil amikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. kirīṭattil aṭippuṟattil taliyic cuṟṟit talip pāki pōl aṅkula akalam uḷḷatāy paṭṭiyāka irukkum pākamē uṣṇīca paṭṭam eṉappaṭum. itaṉ mēl makuṭattuṭaṉ oḷi vīcum kōṭīratti amikka vēṇṭum. kōṭīram, makuṭam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ muṭiyiṉ (kirīṭattiṉ) uṭ pakutiyik kuṟippaṉa vām. makuṭam eṉpatu kirīṭattiṉ aṭippīṭattiyum kōṭīram eṉpatu makuṭattiṉ mēṟ pakutiyiyum uṇarttuvaṉavākum.1 intak kōṭīrattiṉ aḷavu ellā pakkaṅkaḷilum eṭṭaṅkulamē amiyum.
tirumālukku amikkappaṭum kirīṭam uṣṇīcattiṉ mēl viḷaṅkum makuṭattuṭaṉ kūṭiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum. ataṉ aḷavu patiṉāṟaṅkulam, patiṉeṭṭaṅkulam, irupattu nāṉku aṅkulam eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyil amiyum.
ivvāṟu amikkappaṭum kirīṭattiṉ cikaraṅkaḷi (ucci nuṉi) aḻakuṟa amikka vēṇṭum. mūṉṟu, aintu allatu ēḻu eṇṇikkiyuṭiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ amip pukku ēṟṟavāṟu ivveṇṇikki iṭam peṟum. intak kīriṭam aṇṭam (muṭṭi) pōṉṟatākavō tāmari malar pōṉṟa tākavō kuṭi pōṉṟatākavō āmi pōṉṟatākavō avaravar viruppampōl amikkappaṭalām.
kēcāntattiliruntu makuṭāntam vari neṟṟiyil paṭṭam amikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. mukattiṉ akalam evvaḷavō, avvaḷavē makuṭattiṉ akalamum irukka vēṇṭum. makuṭattiṉ aṭippuṟattiṉ akalattil ēḻu eṭṭu allatu oṉpatil oru paṅku kuṟintatāka ataṉ nuṉiyiṉ akalam irukkum. atil nāṉku pūrimaṅkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum. pūrimaṅkaḷiṉ uyaram paṉṉiraṇṭu aṅkulam. kirīṭa makuṭam allatu karaṇṭa makuṭam camikkalām. karaṇṭa makuṭattil aṭiyiṉiṉṟu muṭivari paṭippaṭiyāka akalam kuṟintu koṇṭē pōkum. makuṭattiṉ muṭi muṇṭaka mokkup pōṉṟatāka amikka vēṇṭum. karaṇṭa makuṭam mūṉṟu, nāṉku allatu aintu karaṇṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
pattira kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ 3, 4 allatu 5 aṅkulam irukkalām. ivaṟṟiṉ curuḷkaḷiṉ kaṉam oru yavam irukka vēṇṭum. avi veṇmi allatu cenniṟamāka irukkalām.
makarak kuḻi - makaramīṉ vaṭivam pōl uḷḷatu. ciṅka karṉam eṉum kuḻi ciṅkamukam pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷatu. yāṉik kuḻi yāṉi muka vaṭivil uḷḷatu. kuḻikaḷ pala vakiyāy iruppatu pōl kuṇṭalattilum palavakikaḷuṇṭu. kuṇṭalam, vaṭṭam, tāmarip pū, tāmari arumpu pōṉṟa vaṭivilum irukkalām. upakrīvam eṉṉum uḷkaḻuttaṇiyum aṭṭikiyum mēṟkaḻutti aṇiceyyum palavitamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇivikkalām. uruttirākkamaṇimāli, tuḷaci maṇimāli, tāmarikkāy maṇi māli poṉmaṇimāli pala aḻakiya cittira vēlippāṭu kaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum. uruttirākkamaṇi, tuḷaci maṇimāli poṟ ṟakaṭukaḷāl  potiyap peṟṟu veḷḷik kampiyil kōkkap peṟ ṟirukkum. itōṭu poṉ aṭṭikiyum takkavāṟu aṇivikka vēṇṭum.
muṉ kiyil kaṭakam valayam ivaṟṟi aṇivikka vēṇṭum. (muṉ kiyil aṇiyum vaḷikaḷ kaṭayam  valayam eṉa iruvakip paṭum. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu ciṟu viraliṉ cuṟṟaḷavu irukkalām. ivvaḷi uruṇṭiyākavō taṭṭiyākavō irukkalām. vaḷi pala vaki maṇikaḷ patittuc cittira vēlippāṭukaḷ uḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
kiyiṉ mēṟpuṟattil - atāvatu puyattil kēyūram (vaṅki) eṉum tōḷvaḷiyi aṇivikka vēṇṭum. eṭṭitaḻt tāmarip pūvi itil eḻiluṟa amikkalām. palavaki maṇikaḷi oḷi tikaḻumāṟu itil porutta vēṇṭum. tāmari malarukkup patil pācik kottup pōṉṟa amippiyum uṇṭākkalām. eḻiloḻukum ilikaḷ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ itil iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum. itip pattira pūritam eṉpar. ivvāṟāṉa kēyūratti puyattiṉ mattiyil aṇivikka vēṇṭum. ippattira pūritattiṉ kaṉam kēyūrattiṉ kaṉam pōṉṟatām. ituvē pākuvalayam (puyattil aṇiyum vaḷi) eṉappaṭum.
kiviralkaḷiṉ naṭuviralit tavira ēṉiya nāṉku viral kaḷilum mōtiram aṇivikka vēṇṭum. mōtiram vaṭṭa vaṭiva mākap palavakiyāṉa maṇikaḷ aḻutti vicittiramāṉa vēlip pāṭukaḷ ceytu aḻaku niṟintatāy tiru uruvaṅkaḷukkut takkatāy amikka vēṇṭum.
mārpil koṅkikaḷukku eṭṭu aṅkulat tolivil uracu cūttirattiṉiṉṟu toṅkiyavāṟāka caṉṉavīram eṉṉum aṇi tikaḻa vēṇṭum. itu pūṇūl pōl amiyum. āṉāl iru tōḷkaḷi ṉiṉṟum toṅkum. (itu kikaḷiṉ mēl cellāmal uṭampukku maṭṭum aḷavuṭaṉ nirumikkappaṭṭu aṇiyum poṉmayamāṉa caṭṭiyākum. uṭampiṉ mēṟpakutiyil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum oṉṟu cērttuk kaṭṭiṉāṟ pōlat tōṉṟuvatum valatu puṟam pūṇūl toṅkukiṟa vakiyil vayiṟṟil iṭatu puṟam toṅkum muttuccaram pōṉṟatumāṉa oru aṇiyē caṉṉa vīramākum.)
kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu koṅkikaḷiṉ naṭuvil toṅkumāṟu kirivēyakāram eṉṟa aṭṭiki vaṭivamāṉa māliyi aṇi vikka vēṇṭum. itu palavaki maṇikaḷ aḻuttip poṉṉāl paḷapaḷaveṉa oḷivīcumāṟu ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. kaḻuttiliruntu utarapantam vari toṅkumāṟu akkamāli (uruttirākkamāli) aṇivikkappaṭavēṇṭum. allatu tāmari maṇimāli allatu tuḷacimaṇimāli mutaliyavikaḷi aṇivikkalām. iti hruṉmāli eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṉi kantamāli eṉṟa maṟṟoru vakiyāṉa aṇiyiyum aṇivikka vēṇṭum. palavaki malarkaḷālāṉa immāli tōḷiṉ mēṟpuṟam toṭaṅkip puyattiṉ mēlēyē irupuṟamum toṅkiyavāṟu irukka vēṇṭum.)
ariyil (kaṭicūttiram) ariñāṇ mūṉṟu nūlkaḷ uḷḷatāy irukka vēṇṭum.
kirutrumāciyam eṉṉum aṇi kaṭicantikku mēṟpuṟattil mēṭarattiṉ kīḻ aṇivikkappaṭavēṇṭum. muṉpuṟam mattiyil ciṅkam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mukam pōṉṟa paṭṭam iṭiyil cuṟṟiya vāṟu amikkappaṭum mattiyil mukam pōṉṟa pakutitāṉ kirutru māciyam. itiliruntu toṭiyil muttucaraṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭa vēṇṭum.
pātaṅkaḷ iraṇṭilum kaṇukkālukkuk kīḻē cālakam (paṭṭicaram) aṇivikkavēṇṭum. itu pātacaram eṉṟum kūṟap paṭum cila iṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭik kolucu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itil cālakaṅkaḷi oru cūttirattil kōttuk kaṭṭa vēṇṭum. muttukkaḷ pōl taṉit taṉiyākap paṇṇappeṟṟu cālakacūttirattil kōkkappaṭṭu valipōl iṇintu niṟkum ciṟu paṭṭikaḷ kūṭiyatu pātacaram.
cakaḷātikāram : cakaḷātikāram eṉṉum ciṟpa nūl āṇ kaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟa talippil kūṟuvatāvatu :- cōma kanta cukācaṉa mūrttiyiṉ iṭatu kiyil kaṭakam aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṭatu kātil caṅkapattiram (caṅku vaḷiyum) valatu kātil makarak kuṇṭalam allatu cimmak kuṇṭalam aṇiya vēṇṭum. iṭuppil mukam pōṉṟa vēlip pāṭuḷḷa ariñāṇum kiyil mūṉṟu kaṅkaṇaṅkaḷum koṭip pū vēli ceyta kēyūramum tōḷ vaḷiyum aṅkatam (vāku valayam) utara pantaṉam (vayiṟṟup pakkam aṇiyum aṇikalaṉum) aṇintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ūrutāmam eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum māli pōṉṟa pātacaramum tuṭi vari yil toṅkum. kaṉṉa pūram eṉṉum kātaṇiyum āram, āraciri vatcam (māliyil toṅkum māṇikkamaṇi) maṇi aṭṭiki nitalāñcalamum aṇintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
āṭa vallāṉukkuk kaḻutti oṭṭi irukkum upakkirīvam eṉṉum aṭṭiki aṇintirukka vēṇṭum.
kaṅkātara mūrttikku uḷḷaṅ kiyil aṭippākattil kaṅkaṇam (vīra caṅkiliyum) neṟṟiyil paṭṭamum aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.2
māṉacāram : māṉacāram eṉṉum ciṟpa nūlil, taliyil kirīṭamakuṭam, maṇi makuṭam, karaṇṭa makuṭam, cirattāra makuṭam mutaliyavikaḷum cirac cūḷam eṉṉum uccan taliyil aṇiyum akaḷa cūṭamum aṇiya vēṇṭum eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
āparaṇaṅkaḷ pattira kalpam, cittira kalpam, irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam eṉa nāṉku vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēvarkaḷukku ellāk kalpamum uriyaṉa. maṉṉarkaḷukku cittira kalpam, irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam ākiya mūṉṟum uriyaṉa. atirācaṉ, narēntiraṉ ākiya aracarkaḷukku irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam ākiya iraṇṭum ēṉiya maṉṉarkaḷukku miccira kalpamum uriyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
pattira kalpam : ilikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya koṭikaḷip pōl alaṅkarittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ.
cittira kalpam : vicittiramāṉa ellā maṇikaḷālum tāmari malarkaḷālum alaṅkarikkap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉākum.
irattiṉa kalpam : malarkaḷālum maṇikaḷālum eḻil peṟa aḻaku ceyyap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉ.
miccira kalpam : ilikaḷālum taḷirkaḷālum maṇikaḷā lum aḻaku ceyyap peṟṟu oḷirum aṇikalaṉ.
mālikaḷ, upakkirīvam, kēyūra kaṭakaṅkaḷ, cupūrimam, miccira kalpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
vāku valayam (tōḷ valayam), tāmam (māli) eṉṟa aṇikaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pirakōṣṭattil (maṇikkaṭṭukkum muḻaṅ kikkum naṭuvil) valayam eṉṟa aṇiyum maṇi pantattil (maṇik kaṭṭil) kalāpam eṉṟa aṇiyum, utarapantam, taṉa cūttirakam (itu utara pantattiṉ mēliruntu kuṟukku neṭukkāka mēlē ceṉṟu koṅkikaḷi maṟippatu), kēyūra kaṭakattōṭu iṇinta pāku mālā valampam (kanta mālā valampam) eṉṟa tōḷiṉiṉṟu toṅkum aṇiyum, pūrimamum (taḻi, kuḻi pōṉṟa aṇiyum) kātil makarak kuṇṭalam (mīṉ vaṭivamāṉa kātaṇiyum), tirumālukku vaṉa māliyum (kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu nuṉikkāl vari toṅkum aṇikalaṉum) iṭuppil paṭṭiki, kaḻuttil mālikaḷ, maṇikaḷ patitta kaṅkaṇaṅkaḷ, taliyaṇikaḷ, paṭṭikiyil uḷḷa kiṇkiṇi vaḷiyaṅkaḷ, cuvaraṇa tāṭakam eṉṟa kātaṇikaḷ, taliyiṉ ucciyil uḷḷa cūṭāmaṇi, neṟṟiyil aṇiyum pāla paṭṭam, irupattēḻu maṇikaḷ koṇṭa upakkirīvamāṉa naṭcattira māli, pāti aḷavu toṅkum artta āram, upakaṇṭam eṉum kaḻutti yoṭṭiya māli, kēyūram (tōḷvaḷi), maṇi pantaṉam (maṇikaṭṭiṉ aṇi) ariñāṇ, nīvi (iṭiyil aṇiyum calaṉam eṉṟa acintāṭum mukappuṭaṉ kūṭiya iṭip paṭṭikiyum) mōtiraṅkaḷ, pātacaraṅkaḷ mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷum iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum.3
ciricāracuvatīya cittirakarmacāttiram : innūlil cōma kanta cukācaṉa mūrttiyiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟum poḻutu avaratu iṭatu kātil karṇi (tāmari naṭuvil irukkum kāy pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa tōṭupōṉṟa naki) allatu caṅkapattiram (caṅkiṉā lāṉa kātaṇi) valatu kātil makarak kuṇṭalam (cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivamuḷḷa kuṇṭalam) kēcari kuṇṭalam (ciṅkak kuṇṭalam) viyāḻak kuṇṭalam (pāmpu pōṉṟa kuṇṭalam) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṭuppil ariñāṇ allatu kaṭṭuvār irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum ariñāṇ mukappil ciṅkam allatu yāḷi pōṉṟu amikkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟi kēyūram (tōḷvaḷi), aṅkatam, vāku valayam (tōḷaṉi), kaṭakattirayam (mūṉṟu vaḷiyal), muntiriki (mōtiram), utarapantam (iṭuppil kaṭṭum vār) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kēyūram tōḷ mūlattil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ; aṅkatam tōḷ mattiyil pūṇum aṇikalaṉ. kaṭakattirayam, tōḷvaḷi, pirakōṣṭavaḷiyam, maṇipanta kaṭakam ākiyavikaḷākum. utara pantam vayiṟṟil iḻuttukkaṭṭum vār. pattiram ilikaḷiṉ vaṭiva muḷḷa aṇi; itu vāril toṅkaviṭappaṭum. ūrutāmam - tuṭi yil toṅkaviṭappaṭum kayiṟu, allatu kuñcam; pātacālam - pātacaram ākum. kirivēyām - kaḻuttiliruntu mārpuvari toṅkum kācumāli, kāri, aṭṭiki pōṉṟa uḷkaḻuttaṇikaḷ. irattiṉa panta mupakīrivam - maṇikaḷ iḻitta uḷkaḻuttaṇi mutaliyavi iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.4
cakaḷātikāram : cakaḷātikāram eṉṉum nūlil peṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatāvatu :
tēviyiṉ kātil makarakkuṇṭalamum tōḷil caṉṉa vīramum (kuṟukkumāliyum) kaḻuttil palavitamāṉa mālikaḷum, puyattil tōḷvaḷiyum tikaḻavēṇṭum. tēvikkup pāmpu mukattōṭu kūṭiya ariñāṇum iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. 5
ciri cāracuvatīya cittirakarma cāttiram : ciri cāracu vatīya cittira karma cāttiram eṉṉum nūl cōmakanta mūrtta vaṭivil irukkum umātēvikkuk kal iḻitta ōli allatu poṉ ōliyum callari (olikkum vaḷi), atavalayam (kivaḷi), pūrimam (tōḷiṟku mēlē aṇiyum aṇi), aṅkuliyakam (mōtiram) mutaliya aṇikaḷ aṇiyap peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
koṟṟavikku (makicācuramarttaṉikku) kaṉṉatāmā valampaṉam - kātil kayiṟu pōlat toṅkum māṭṭi, cimikki, ṭōlak pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.6
kuṟippu
1.	cilparattiṉam - kat. 11 pak. 245-271.	
cirikāciyapacilpacāttiram 56. pak. 212-215.
2.	cakaḷātikāram (akattiyar) pak. 64-65.
3.	māṉacāram pākam II at 50. pak. 34-40.
4.	ciricāracuvatīya cittirakarmacāttiram at. 14 pak. 154-157.
5.	cakaḷātikāram - (akattiyar) pak. 67-68.
6.	a) ciricāracuvatīya cittirakarmacāttiram at. 14 pak. 153-155.	 
ā)  Indian Images Part I B. C. Battacharya M.A., F.R.S.S.G.S.	
i)   Elements of Hindu Iconography - Gopinatha Rao	
ī)	    Principles of Indian Silpa Sastra - Prof. Phanindra Nath Bose, M.A. 		      (Lohore)

12. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ
tirāviṭarkaḷ iṉṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē aṇikalaṉkaḷ palavaṟṟic ceytu aṇiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭār kaḷ. arappā nākarikattil arumpiya poṉ aṇikaḷum, maṇikaḷ patitta kal aṇikaḷum namakku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāyt tikaḻ kiṉṟaṉa.
tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ mūtta - mutuperum nākarikattik kaṇṭa mutuperuṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarikatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭa āticca nallūril akaḻntu eṭutta aṇikaḷum, aṅkuk kaṇṭa pommi kaḷil kāṇappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷum ariya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷāy aṟiñarkaḷāl matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āticcanallūr  nākarikatti viḷakkik kūṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ pōl, namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷukkuriya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa.
1. tolkāppiyam
tolkāppiyam iṉṟu kāṇakkiṭakkum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil paḻamiyāṉatu. atil tamiḻarkaḷ cumār mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar aṇintirunta aṇilaṉkaḷip paṟṟic cila kuṟippukaḷ, uḷḷaṉa. atikamāṉa kuṟippukaḷ illāviṭiṉum mukkiyamāṉa oṉṟiraṇṭu kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. atāvatu,
kātoṉṟu kaḷital (tol. poruḷ 1205) eṉṟu oru cūttirattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkuk kātil aṇinta kuḻiyi nīkkutal eṉpatē poruḷākum. eṉavē tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil kuḻi arumpiviṭṭatu. civaperu māṉ kuḻi aṇipavarātaliṉ avarukku kuḻikkātar eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu eṉṟu nām ellōrum naṉkaṟivōm. maṟṟōr cūttirattil
aṇintavi tiruttal - (tol - poruḷ - 1206) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkup poruḷ kaṇṭa uriyāciriyar kaḷ aṇintirukkum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ alaṅkōlattic carip paṭuttik koḷḷutal eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi iṉṉum, iḻiyaṇintu, iḻiyē - (tol. poruḷ. 1096, 1038) eṉṟu maṟṟīriṭattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟku aḻakiya nalla vēlippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ eṉṟu uri kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
ilakkaṇa nūlātaliṉ tolkāppiyattil mika viḷakkamākap pala aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.
2. cilappatikāram
cilappatikāram aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. innūl cēranāṭṭu iḷavaracar oruvarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. inta iḷavaracar iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ eṉṉum tuṟavi; eṉṟālum aracar, vaṇikar mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkic ciṟappi naṉkaṟintavar. itu oru nāṭakak kāppiyam eṉṟu matikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu camaṇac cārpuṭiya nūlākum.
aṇikalaṉkaḷ
cilampu eṉpatu peṇkaḷ kālil aṇiyum oru aṇikala ṉākum. tamiḻ aṉṉikku āciriyar camarppikkum oru ciṟanta kāl aṇikalaṉ pōṉṟu ipperum kāppiyatti  eḻutiyuḷḷār. tamiḻ nūlil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatu in nūl oṉṟēyākum. mēlum āciriyar aracakumārarāy iruntami yāl araca kuṭumpattār aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyellām naṉkaṟintirukkiṟār eṉṟu naṉkaṟikiṟōm. kaṇṇakiyiṉ kāṟcilam piṉuḷ viliyuyarnta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷum, pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉivi yiṉ kāṟcilampiṉuḷ vili kuṟinta muttukkaḷum pōṭappaṭ ṭiruntatē nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
kālam
ipperuṅkāppiyam tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil arumpiyatu eṉṟu aṟiñar vi.ār. ār. tīṭcitar kūṟukiṉṟār.
nūl
innūlil cōḻanāṭu, pāṇṭiyanāṭu, cēra nāṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟu tamiḻnāṭukaḷum piṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṇika vaḷarcciyum vaṇikarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil niyāyam vaḻaṅkum muṟiyum, pāṇṭiyarkaḷ nīti pēṇum tiṟaṉum naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. cēra maṉṉaṉ tuṟaviyāy iruntum annāṭṭil pattiṉit teyvam vittu vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyamum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. ēṉ? ciṅkaḷa nāṭṭil kūṭa pattiṉit teyva vaḻipāṭu eḻum avaciyam eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟu tamiḻnāṭukaḷilum, civam, camaṇam, puttam eṉṟa mūṉṟu camayaṅ kaḷum iruntanili eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ celvac cerukkum ataṉāl eḻunta parattiyar vāḻvu, āṭaṟkali, icikkali, aṇikalakkali mutaliyavi ellām ciṟappākac cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
innūl camaṇa kāppiyam eṉṟālum itil vaṇikar vāḻvu, parattiyar vāḻvu nāṭṭiyam, ici, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavi kaḷip pōṟṟi varuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. makaḷir uṭali aḻakupaṭuttum kali, nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ naṉi ciṟanta vāḻkkinili ellām tiṟampaṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
caṅkam maruviya nūṟkaḷil perum nūlō, paḻam perum kāppiyamō namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. eṉavē caṅkak kālattiṟku muntiya aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟi atikamāka aṟiya muṭiyavilli. eṉṟālum kaṭic caṅkak kālattil pāṇṭiyanāṭu poṉṉum maṇiyum peṟṟuc ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatākat terikiṟatu. irāmāyaṇak kālattilē - pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tam talinakarākiya kapāṭapuratti liruntu āṭci celutti vantaṉar. talinakariṉ kōṭṭivāyil poṉṉum maṇiyum muttum patikkap peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu vāṉmīki vaṭamoḻi irāmāyaṇattil kuṟippiṭuvatu namatu caṅkak kāla vāḻviṟku oru eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.
cilappatikāramum aṇikalaṉkaḷum
cilappatikārattil nam aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟi mikac ciṟappāka eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
matimuka maṅki nallāḷ māmalarkkaṇṇi mātavi, intira viḻāviṉ iṟutiyil taṉ illatti eytiṉāḷ. uṭaṉē taṉ māntaḷir mēṉiyi merukiṭṭa poṟpāvi eṉṟu eṇṇumpaṭi oppaṉi ceytu koṇṭu ūṭal koṇṭa kōvalaṉ muṉ vantāḷ. avaḷ aḻakiṉik kaṇṭu avaṉ makiḻntāṉ. avaḷ evvāṟu taṉṉi aṇi ceyvittu eḻilōviyamāka - aṟputamāṉa poṟpāviyākak kāṭci aḷittāḷ eṉpati iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟappāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atu aṭiyil varumāṟu :
mātavi maṇantarum neyyi talimayirukku ūṭṭi pattut tuvariṉālum aintu viriyiṉālum muppattiraṇṭu vaki ōmālikiyiṉālum ūṟik kāyccappeṟṟa naṉṉīrilē kārmēkam pōṉṟu kaṟuttuk kaviṉpeṟa nīṇṭu vaḷarntirukkum kūntalik kaḻuviṉāḷ. naṟumaṇam nalkum pukiyūṭṭi īram ularttiṉāḷ. kūntali aḻaku peṟa vārip piṉṉik kaṭṭiṉāḷ. vācaṉik kuḻampi meykku ūṭṭiṉāḷ; koṅkiyil ceṅkiyiṉāl toyyil eḻutiṉāḷ. iṭiyil poṟcariki puṉiyappaṭṭa paṭṭāṭiyip puṉintāḷ.
cempañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭiya civanta pātaṅkaḷiṉ melliya viralkaḷukku makaravāy mōtiram, pīli, kāl viral mōtiram mutaliya kālviral aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. piṉ taṉ kālkaḷil pātacālam, pāṭakam, cilampu (kuṭiccūl) cataṅki, kiṇkiṇi, kāṟacari ākiyavikaḷip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. melliya iṭi yil aṇinta nīlavaṇṇappaṭṭāṭi mītu muppattu iraṇṭu parumuttuk kōvikaḷālāṉa viriciki eṉṉum nucuppaṇiyi māṭṭiṉāḷ. māṇikkavaḷiyuṭaṉ nīṅkāmal poṟṟoṭarāl piṇitta muttuvaḷiyit tōḷil pūṇṭu mukappil kaṭṭiya māṇikkattōṭu pattikaḷil viraṅkaḷ aḻuttappeṟṟa ōviya vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟinta cūṭakam, cempoṉvaḷi navarattiṉa vaḷi caṅkuvaḷi, pavaḷavaḷi eṉṉum muṉ kivaḷikaḷi poruttamuṟap pūṭṭiṉāḷ. vāḷip pakuvāy vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram (neḷivu), ceṅkēḻ kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram (māṇikka maṇimōtiram) viramarakat tāḷceṟi mōtiram (viram cūḻnta marakatam patikka mōtiram ākiya kiviral aṇikaḷi kāntaḷ malar pōl nīṇṭu civanta aḻakiya viralkaḷ maṟiyumpaṭi pūṇṭāḷ. vīraccaṅkili, nērñcaṅkili, ñāṇ, carappaḷi muttumāli, piṉṟāli mutaliya kaḻuttaṇikaḷik kaviṉuṟak kaḻuttil aṇintu koṇ ṭāḷ. intira nīlattiṭiyē eḻiluṟap patitta virattālāṉa nīlak kutampi, cantirapāṇi mutaliya kātaṇikaḷi, kātil aṇintāḷ. cītēvi valampuri, toyyakam, pullakam, cūḷāmaṇi, poṉṉari māli kōti mutaliya taliyaṇikaḷi aḻakiya kūntalil poruttiṉāḷ. toṭiyil kuṟaṅkucceṟi eṉum aṇikalaṉip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. ivvāṟu mātavi tāṉ kaṟṟa eḻiṟkaliyiṉ ilakkaṇam eḷḷaḷavum vaḻuvātu aṇikalaṉ aṉittiyum aṇintu āṭiyip puṉintu aḻaku ceytu koṇṭāḷ. ūṭal koṇṭa kōvalaṉ uḷḷattil uvaki ūṭṭi iṉpa mūṭṭip paḷḷiyaṟi yil pāṅkuṭaṉ iruntāḷ.2
iḵtaṉṟi maṟṟōr iṭattum mātavi eṉum pūṅkoṭi mulliyum mallikiyum oḻinta tāḷik kuvaḷi mutaliya pala pūkkaḷ viritta taṉ paḷḷiyaṟiyil aḻakiya alkul taṉṉiṭattil tuyil ceyyappaṭṭu ataṉmītu pavaḷak kōvikaḷāl aṇi ceyyum mēkaliyi aṇintāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.3
vēṉiṟ kātiyil kōvalaṉ kaṭaṟkariyiṉiṉṟu kōpat tōṭu pōyviṭṭatāl mātavi taṉ vīṭu pōntu ēḻāvatu māṭi yiṉ nilāmuṟṟattil cantaṉamum iḷavēṉiṟ kālattiṟkup poruttamāṉa veṇmuttārattiyum pūṇṭāḷ eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.4
cilappatikārattiṉ vaḻakkuri kātiyil kaṇṇakiyiṉ cilampum pāṇṭimātēviyiṉ cilampum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇakiyiṉ cilampiṉuḷ māṇikka maṇikaḷum (māṇikkapparalkaḷum) kōpperuntēviyiṉ kālcilampiṉuḷ muttukkaḷum pōṭappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṟatu. cilampiṉuḷ muttup paralkaḷō allatu māṇikkap paralkaḷō pōṭuvatu naṭakkumpoḻutu avi kaṇīr ! kaṇīr! eṉṟa iṉṉoliyik kiḷampuvataṟkēyām. māṇikka maṇi ōrvitamāṉa iṉṉoliyi yum muttukkaḷ vēṟuvitamāṉa iṉṉoliyiyum kiḷappum.5 kaḻuttil tāliyum6 maṅkala aṇiyum (tāli) iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.7
3. maṇimēkali
itu aimperuṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil cilappatikārattiṟku aṭutta paṭiyāka matikkappaṭum ariya nūl. maturik kūlavaṇikaṉ cāttaṉār eḻutiyatu. putta camayac cārpuṭiyatu. itu ki.pi. 2ām nuṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil eḻutappeṟṟatu eṉpar. itil aṇi kalaṉkaḷ cilappatikāram pōl atikam kāṇappaṭāviṭiṉum orucila mukkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
innūlil cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭamuritta kātiyil kiyil kaṭakam8 kāppu mutaliyavikaḷum, ciṟikkōṭṭam aṟak kōṭṭa mākkiya kātiyil kiyil koṭiyum aṇintatu kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.9 kaccinakar pukkakātiyil urukkut taṭṭārum, poṟ paṇikaḷ ceyvōrum piṟarum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar.10 malar vaṉam pukunta kātiyil caṅkiṉāṟ ceyta kātaṇikaḷ kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum poṟṟōṭum,11 viḻāvaṟi kātiyil muttu māliyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.12 aṉṟi aimpaṭit tāliyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
cilappatikārattil mātavi aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉittum kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu pōl maṇimēkaliyil katippōkku avaḷ āṭamparamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintāḷ eṉpatik kāṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka illi. mēlum āciriyar kūlavaṇikaṉ cāttaṉārum tamatu kāppiyam tamiḻ aṉṉikku oru aṇikalaṉāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattil itaṉi mēkali eṉṉum peyaril iyaṟṟiyatu tamiḻ makkaḷukkuk kaliyiṉ mītuḷḷa ārvattiṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu.
4. cīvakacintāmaṇi
cīvakacintāmaṇi aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kāppiya nūṟkaḷil mutalil cilappatikāramum, iraṇṭāvatu maṇimēkali yum, mūṉṟāvatu cīvakacintāmaṇiyum piṟantaṉa. itu nantāp perumi vāynta cintāmaṇi eṉṟu pulavarkaḷāl pōṟṟappeṟum nūl. itaṉi iyaṟṟiyavar tiruttakka tēvar. ivar araca marapiṉar. ki.pi. 1200ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ vāḻntavarāka matikkappaṭukiṟār. paṇṭiya pulavarkaḷ tamiḻ aṉṉikku āṟu uyarnta aṇikaḷi camarppittuḷḷaṉar. avikaḷil itu mūṉṟāvatāka vittu eṇṇappeṟum. āṟu aṇikalaṉkaḷ muṟiyē cilampu, mēkali, cintāmaṇi, vaḷi, kuṇṭalam, ceṅkōl eṉum āṟākum. ivvāṟu aṇikaḷiyum pulavarkaḷ āṟu nūlāka ākkit tamiḻ aṉṉiyiṭam camarppittuḷḷaṉar. ataṉi oru piṟkālap pulavar.
	kātoḷirum kuṇṭalamum kikkuvaḷi
		yāpatiyum karuṇi mārpil
	 mītoḷir cintāmaṇiyum melliṭiyil
		mēkaliyum cilampār iṉpap
	 pōtoḷir pūntāḷiṇiyum poṉmuṭicū
		ṭāmaṇiyum poliyaccūṭṭi
	 nītiyoḷir ceṅkōlāyt tirukkuṟaḷit
		tāṅkutamiḻ nīṭu vāḻka.
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu aimperuṅkāppiyattiṉ perumiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu. tamiḻaṉṉikku aṇikalaṉkaḷ mītuḷḷa ārvam pulaṉākiṟatu. tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aṉṉiyi makiḻvikka aṇi ceytu pūṭṭi vaṇaṅkum marapu itaṉāl veḷiyākiṟatu.
cintāmaṇiyil caccantaṉ maṉivi viciyi kaṇavaṉ kāṭēṟiyati aṟintu poṉṉaṇikaḷik kaḻaṟṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟku urikaṇṭa uriyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar poṉṉarimāliyi13yiyum neṟṟi māliyi yum talikkōlamākiya muttum pulampam mutaliyavikaḷik kuḻali ṉiṉṟu akaṟṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. tōḷiṉ muttit tuṟantu vaḷiyi uṭittu viralāḻiyi vilakkiṉāḷ eṉṟu āciriyar maṟṟoru pāṭṭil kūṟiyuḷḷār. innūlil kēmacariyārilam pakattil āciriyar kēmacari kuṇṭalam acintu oḷivīca iṭiyil tukiliṉuḷḷē mēkali oḷirntatu eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.14 kantaruvattiyār ilampakattil kantaruvattikku avaḷ tanti ariya maṇikaḷ patitta aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ vippataṟku vira maṇikaḷ iḻittuc ceyta vaṇṇap peṭṭikaḷ ainūṟum poṉṉummaṇiyum ārnta yavaṉanāṭṭil ceyta peṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭāyiramum aḷittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.15 ilakkaṇi yārilampakattil kiṇkiṇi uṭi (mēkali) poṟcilampu ākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum16 maṟṟōriṭattil poṟṟōṭu kaṭippu (a) kuḻi, (ā) cuṟavukkuḻi mutaliya aṇikaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mēlum palaviṭaṅkaḷil nutal paṭṭam17 makaramīṉ mutti umiḻvatu pōṉṟu ceyyap peṟṟa tōḷaṇi18 maṅkiyar koṅki mītu maṟṟoru aṇiyum ilivaṭivākiya pūṇ eṉṉum aṇiyum muṉ kiyil oru aṇiyum maṇikkaṭakam ilik kaṭakam pōṉṟa aṇi kalaṉkaḷum19 taliyaṇiyākiya muttuttāmamum20, nutal aṇi yākiya ilampakamum21, kātaṇikaḷākiya makara kuṇṭalam, tōṭu, kuḻi, poṟṟōṭu, maruppiṉālāyatōṭu, kuṇṭalam, kaṭippu mutaliyavikaḷip peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum aṇintu vantaṉar.22 tōḷil calāki eṉṉum tōḷ aṇiyum23 ēṉāti pōṉṟa mōtiraṅkaḷum24 poṟṟōri eṉṉum iṭiyaṇiyum, kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi eṉṉum toṭiyaṇiyum, kālil aṇiyum kaḻalkaḷ ākiyavikaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
cintāmaṇi, cilappatikāra kālattiṟku 800 āṇṭukaḷukkup piṟpaṭṭa nūl. itil cilappatikāra aṇikaḷiyaṉṟi piṟa aṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. ataṉāl ikkālattil putiya aṇikaḷ tōṉṟavilli eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cintāmaṇi eḻum kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar. avar kaḷiṭamiruntu aṇikaḷ vikkum maṇikaḷ patitta peṭṭikaḷ pala vāṅkiṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. peṭṭikaḷiṉ matippum, eṇṇikki yum pārkkumpoḻutu akkālattil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntirukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
innūlil toyyakam (pūrappāḷi), ilampakam, paṭṭam, makarakkuṇṭalam, tōṭu (poṟṟōṭu), maruppiṉāl ceytatōṭu, kuḻi, cuṟavukkuḻi, vaḷinta kuḻi, cempoṉṉōli, cen niṟakkuḻi, kaṭippu, calāki, kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi, ariñāṇ, ēṉāti mōtiram, caṭakam, kaṭippu, kaḻal, kāñci, cilampu, cūṭṭu, cūṭakam, cūḷāmaṇi, talippāḷi, toṭi, tōḷvaḷi, neṟṟippaṭṭam, pariyakam, pavaḷamāli, piṟi, pūṇ, poṟkaṇṇi, poṟṟōri, poṉñāṇ, poṉṉari māli, poṉṉirit tāli, poṉ ṉōṭi, makarakkuṇṭalam, mañciki, maṇi mōtiram, maṇivaṭam, muttumāli, mēkali, mōtiram, vaḷi pōṉṟa pala aṇi kalaṉkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cintāmaṇi cilappatikārattiṟkup pintiya kālattil piṟanta ilakkiyam. itil piṟkāla aṇikalaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu. maṇimēkaliyiviṭa atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
5. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi kaṭiccaṅka nūṟkaḷil oṉṟu. pattup pāṭṭil mutalāvatāka vittup pōṟṟappaṭuvatu. itu murukak kaṭavuḷ mītu pāṭappeṟṟa oru ariya nūl. itaṉip pāṭiyavar caṅkak kālap perumpulavar nakkīrar. innūl ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil allatu ataṟkuc ciṟitu muṉpiṉ pāṭappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉ eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
innūlil makkaḷiṉ āṭi, aṇikalaṉ ākiyavikaḷum murukaṉ āṭum vēlaṉ allatu paṭimattāṉ eṉṟa kuṟiñcit teyvattiṉ tōṟṟamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñci nilak kuṉṟak kaṭavuḷi, kuṟamakaḷir eṭutta viḻāk koṇṭāṭum muṟiyum murukaṉi ētti vaḻipaṭum neṟiyum anneṟiyāl vaḻipāṭṭāl peṟum pēṟum vivaramāka viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kuṟamakaḷir talimayirik kōti eṇṇey taṭavi ataṉ mītu veṭcippūkkaḷi, viṭupūvākat tūvi ataṟku naṭuvē kuvaḷip pūviṉ veḷḷitaḻkaḷik kiḷḷiviṭṭu appāl taliyiṉmītu aṇikalaṉkaḷic cūṭuvar. taliyil makaravaṭivāṉa neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum, cītēvi, valampuric caṅku pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum aṇintu muṟṟa muṭinta koṇṭiyil periya ceṇmukap pūvic ceruki mēlē marutam pūviyiṭṭuk kaḻuttilē kaḻumalap pūvi māliyākak kaṭṭi aṇintu kātukaḷil piṇṭit taḷirkaḷi māṭṭi mārpil aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu cantaṉam pūci ataṉ īram ularumuṉ vēṅkip pūviṉ tātiṉip parappi viḷā marattiṉ taḷiri oruvar mītu oruvar teṟittuk koḷvar eṉa in nūlil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.29
iḵtaṉṟi kāñci30 mēkali cataṅki (kiṇkiṇi)31 vīra kaṇṭi32 muṭi (makuṭam)33, poṉmakarak kuṇṭalam34, poṉṉari māli35, neṟṟippaṭṭam, vīramaṇi, vīrakaṇṭi, toṭi (vāku valayam)36 kaḻal, kuḻi, poṉ aṇi, māṟṟuyarnta maṇikaḷ pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
6. porunar āṟṟuppaṭi
porunarāṟṟuppaṭi pattuppāṭṭil iraṇṭāvatu vittu eṇṇappaṭum ariya nūlākum. itu kaṭic caṅkappāṭṭu kaḷil oṉṟu. cōḻap pēraracaṉ karikāṟcōḻaṉi muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār eṉpavar pukaḻntu pāṭiya nūl. itu 248 aṭikaḷi yuṭiyatu.
innūlil kātaṇiyākiya kuḻi37 palvēṟu muttu vaṭaṅkaḷ, maṇi mālikaḷ, iṭiyil ilaṅkum nucup paṇikaḷ, kaḻuttil ilaṅkum poṉṉarimālikaḷ38 mutaliyavi kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
ataṉṟiyum makkaḷ taliyilum kātilum kaḻuttilum kiyilum iṭuppilum kālkaḷilum aṇiyum poṉ aṇikaḷum pavaḷa mālikaḷum muttu vaṭaṅkaḷum piṟa maṇikaḷ patittu oḷiviṭum poṉmaṇi aṇikaḷum ilikaḷ pōṉṟum kulikaḷ pōṉṟum kuḻikaḷ pōṉṟum malarkaḷ pōṉṟum mīṉkaḷ pōṉṟum pāmpukaḷ pōṉṟum naṇṭukaḷ pōṉṟum ceyyap peṟṟa aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum vaḷikaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
7. perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi
innūl uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, toṇṭi māṉ iḷantiriyaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiyatu. itu pattuppāṭṭil 4-vatāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. 500 aṭikaḷiyuṭiyatu.
innūlil, uṇavu niṟinta nalla vīṭukaḷilē vaḷinta poṉ aṇikaḷip pūṇṭa makaḷir vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ muttuvaṭam pūṇṭa tam iṭiyilē mika nuṇmiyāṉa tukili aṇintirun taṉar. attukil koṉṟi malar malarnta melliya kompilē paṉittuḷi tavaḻvatu pōl acikiṉṟatu. makaḷir maliyilē kaḷi koṇṭu kalāpam virittāṭum poṉmayilip pōl kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya kālkaḷilē poṟ cilampukaḷ olik kiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ uyarnta māḷikiyilē pantāṭi iḷittaṉar. muttup pōṉṟa veṇmaṇal parappiya iṭattilē kiyil puṉinta ciṟiya vaḷikaḷ olikkumpaṭi kaḻaṅkukaḷi ēnti āṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattiṉ perumi eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭukiṟatu.39 kātu kaḷil kuḻikaḷ ūcalāṭiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.40
eṉavē aṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻnta nakar pala aṭukkukaḷi yuṭiya māṭi vīṭu niṟinta paṭṭiṉam. poṉ kaḻaṅkukaḷ vittu āṭum aḷavil celvap perukkuṭiyatu eṉṟellām terikiṟatu.
8. maturikkāñci
maturikkāñci kaṭic caṅka nūṟkaḷil oṉṟu. pattuppāṭṭu eṉṉum nūṟṟokutiyil āṟāvatāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pattuppāṭṭil ituvē kūṭutalāṉa aṭikaḷi (782) uṭiyatu. itaṉ āciriyar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār. itaṉ pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvaṉ.
nūliṉ aṭakkam
iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇkaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakap paṇpum, maturi māmaṉṉaṉ māṇpum makkaḷiṉ aṟivu, āṟṟal, ākkam, āṭi, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavikaḷ palavum aḻakāka eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. innūl pōr purivatil oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉākat tikaḻnta pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āṟṟiya pōrip pukaḻntu pāṭuvatāl iṅku avaṉuṭiya allatu araci yuṭiya allatu makkaḷuṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟap paṭavilli. eṉṟālum ōraḷavu akkāla makaḷir aṇinta tali yaṇikaḷ41 mārpaṇikaḷ42 cilampukaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.
poṟṟāmarip pūvip pūṇṭu naṉmi nalkum nāṉāvita nakikaḷi aḷḷik koṭukkum aracaṉē! kuṭṭa nāṭṭiṉar palariyum veṉṟu niṟkum kōvē eṉṟu ippāṭṭu aracaṉ aṇintuḷḷa vaḷikaḷiyum43 mālikaḷiyum ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟip pala pēraṇikalaṉkaḷiyum 44, poṉṉāl ceyta tumpip pūkkaḷip pōrmaṟavarkaḷukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻaṅkiya peṟṟiyiyum45 makarak kuḻiyiyum vīrakkaḻalkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.46, 47.
9. neṭunal vāṭi
itu pattup pāṭṭiṉuḷ 7vatu iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷa tākum. maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉ nakkīraṉār taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiyatu.
innūlil nīṇṭa makarakkuḻi tāṅki niṉṟa kātukaḷum ciṟiya iṭattiyuṭiya tāḷuruvi aḻuttiya kātukaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.48
ōriṭattu makaḷir iṉṟum aṇintuvarum neḷivu eṉṉum muṭakku mōtiramum49, valampurivaḷi50 neṭiya tāli nāṇ, kaṭukkaṉ, kaḻuttaṇikaḷ, kālviral aṇikaḷ pōṉṟavi kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟṟōriṭattup paṉittuḷi eṉa oḷi ciṟanta maṇikaḷ oḷirum mēkaliyi aḻakiya iḷamaṅkiyar tam nuṇṇiya iṭiyil aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10. paṭṭiṉappāli
paṭṭiṉappāli pattuppāṭṭil oṉpatāvatu iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷatu. pāṭiyavar kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār. ivar karikāṟcōḻaṉ mītu 301 aṭikaḷ koṇṭa paṭṭiṉappāli eṉṉum ciṟanta pāmāliyic cūṭṭiyuḷḷār.
innūlil vīrakkaḻal 51, 52 ciṟuvarkaḷ aṇinta toṭi paṟṟi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iḵtaṉṟi celvamikka illaṅkaḷilē akaṉṟa muṉṟil, oḷi nutalum maṭanōkkum poruntiya arammi yaṉṉa aḻakiya aṇaṅkukaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintu nellit tiṉṉa varum kōḻikaḷi veruṭṭataṟku eṟinta makarakkuḻi (makaramīṉ vaṭivāyc ceyyappaṭṭa kātaṇikaḷ) kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.53 pavaḷam, muttu (koṟki muttu) marakatamaṇi, poṟkāl putalvar, poṟṟoṭi cāmpūnatam pōṉṟa pataṅkaḷ pala kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
11. malipaṭukaṭām
malipaṭukaṭām eṉṉum kūttar āṟṟuppaṭi, pāṭṭut tokutiyil pattāvatu iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉip pāṭiya pulavar kucikaṉār ākum. pāṭal peṟṟavaṉ palkuṉṟak kōṭṭattuc ceṅkaṇmāttuvēḷ naṉṉaṉ makaṉ naṉṉaṉ āvāṉ. paricu vēṇṭil naṉṉaṉpāl cella, kūttari tūṇṭiyataṉāl itaṟkuk kūttarāṟṟuppaṭi eṉṉum peyar vantatu.
innūlil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavilli. eṉiṉum pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ maṇi, poṉvaḷi poṟṟāmarippū pōṉṟa oru cila aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaḷi, poṉmaṇi pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ malināṭuṭiya talivaṉāṉatāl iṅku atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavilli eṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ.53
12. paripāṭal
paripāṭal eṭṭut tokikaḷil oṉṟu. itu kaṭiccaṅka nūl. eḻupatu pāṭalkaḷil kāla veḷḷattāl aḻintatu pōka eñciyuḷḷaṉa 22 pāṭalkaḷākum. ivi nallantuvaṉār, nalvaḻuti yār nalleḻiniyār, nallaḻiciyār, nappaṇṇaṉār, nallaccutaṉār, kīrantiyār, kaṭuvaṉ iḷaveyiṉaṉār, kuṉṟam pūtaṉār pōṉṟa pala pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭal tokuppēyākum. mēlum innūl tirumāl, murukavēḷ, kāṭukiḻāḷ, viki, maturi mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷiṉ peyarum āṟu, nakarp pōṉṟavikaḷiṉ perumi yum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
itu akaccuvip poruḷip paṟṟiyē peritum kūṟum nūl. eṉṟālum āṅkāṅkē aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippu kaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
innūlil makaḷir tam taliyil talikkōlam eṉṟa aṇi kalaṉkaḷi aḻakuṟa aṇintu vantatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum vaḷi, āḻi, vākuvalayam, mēkali, kāñci, toyyakam pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talikkōlattil palvēṟu aṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭiruppatuṭaṉ ciṟārkkaṇiyum muñcakam eṉṉum taliyaṇiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.54 makaramīṉ vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa nutalaṇi55, kātil pūṇum kuḻikaḷ, kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ, kaṭippu56, pavaḷavaḷi57, mēkali, kāñci ākiya iṭiyaṇikaḷ, kivaḷi, mōtiram, toyyakam, vākuvalayam mutaliya aṇikaḷum mēlāṭiyi iṟukap piṇikkum mēkaliyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tōḷil tōḷvaḷiyum toṭiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar. tōḷ aṇikalaṉākat toṭi kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. iḷam maṅkikku iṉpa uṇarcci poṅki eḻukiṟatu. avaḷatu tōḷ pūrittut toṭiyi aḻuntap paṇṇukiṟatu eṉpatip paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.
kikaḷil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷāka viḷaṅkum palavakik kaṭakam, vaḷikāppu, kolucu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kiviralkaḷil pūṇum maṇikaḷ patitta mōtiraṅkaḷum  iṭuppiṉ puṟattē aṇiyum eṭṭu vaṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya kāñci eṉṟa nucuppaṇi, pavaḷamaṇikaḷ kōtta ēḻu vaṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya mēkali eṉṉum nucuppaṇiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. nīrāṭum poḻutu makaḷir mēkaliyi āṭiyiṉ mītu iṟukap piṇittuk koḷvatu uṇṭeṉṟum terikiṟatu. aḻakiya pūṅkoṭikaḷ ōṭippoṉ ṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa cilampiṉ paralkaḷuḷ muttukkaḷ pōṭṭuc ceyyappeṟṟiruntaṉa. itu nittila variccilampu eṉappaṭum. mēlum kātilum kuvaḷi kuṇṭalam, kaṭippu, ōli eṉṉum palavakik kātaṇikaḷ aṇintiruntatākat terikiṟatu.
ipparipāṭal mūlam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pāvi nallār kiṟittava ūḻi arumpumuṉṉarē eṇṇilā vakippaṭṭa poṉ aṇikaḷiyum maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟa aṇikaḷiyum pēraṇi kaḷiyum ciṟṟaṇikaḷiyum aṇintu iṉpamuṭaṉ vāḻntārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
13. kalittoki
kalittoki, kaṟṟār ēttum kali eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum toki nūl.
kaṭiccaṅkak kālattiṉ iṟutiyil tamiḻaka makaḷir atika māṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpataṟku in nūl nalla cāṉṟāka uḷḷatu. maṉṉarkaḷum vaṇikarkaḷum nilak kiḻārum vīrarum pala aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭuvantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu innūliṉ mūlam naṉkaṟikiṉṟōm.
kālil poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa vāypiḷanta poṟ cataṅkiyum ariyil poṉmaṇikaḷiyuṭiya vaṭamum ataṉ mēl pavaḷavaṭamum kiyil naṇṭiṉkaṇ pōl arumpu vēlikaḷ ceyta kōṟṟoḻil avirkiṉṟa toṭiyum, iṭiyil, veṭṭāta vāḷum maḻuvum toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa iṭattiyuṭiya pūṇum, mārpil karumuttu maṇiyum cērnta muttuvaṭamum ataṉ mēl nīla maṇikaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇṭār maruḷum maṇimālikaḷum vēṟu pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇipeṟa aṇintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
palavitap poṉṉaṇi60 taliyaṇi61 ciṟār aṇiyum piṟi62, vayantakam63, tōḷaṇi - patakkam ciṟuvar aṇikaḷum64, vāku valayam kātaṇi - kuṇṭalam, kiyaṇi - kaṅkaṇam piṟavaḷikaḷum65 mōtiraṅ kaḷum66 kiṇkiṇiyum67 vīravaḷi, ariyaṇi - ariñāṇa, kāla aṇi - vīrakkaḻal vīrakkaṇṭi, kālviral aṇi mutaliya palavita aṇikalaṉkaḷum pūṇṭiruntatāka innūliṉiṉṟu aṟiyalām.
ovvoru uṟuppilum aṇikaḷ palvēṟu vitaṅkaḷāy iruntaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kātaṇiyil makarak kuṇṭalam carppak kuṇṭalam, tōṭu, ōli eṉṟum kaḻuttaṇiyil kāri, māli, vaṭam pōṉṟa palavita aṇikalaṉkaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟu kalit tokiyil kāṇkiṉṟōm.68
14. naṟṟiṇi
naṟṟiṇi, kaṭic caṅkak kālattu eṭṭuttoki nūl kaḷāṉa puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, kalit toki, paripāṭal, akanāṉūṟu, kuṟuntoki ākiyavaṟṟōṭu cērttu eṇṇappaṭuvatu.
naṟṟiṇiyilum tamiḻ makkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ cila peyarkaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. talikkōlattil oṉṟāṉa curitam kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. itu poṉṉāl ākiya tirukuppūp pōṉṟatu. ivvaṇi curitakam eṉappaṭum.69
kātukaḷil aṇiyappaṭum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa kuḻikaḷil kaṉaṅkuḻiyum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.70 iḵtaṉṟi cimikki pōṉṟu kuḻiyil ūcal koḷuvappaṭṭa oṇ kuḻiyum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.71 vīrattiṉ aṟikuṟiyāka vīra maṟavarkaḷ aṇiyum tōḷ toṭiyākiya kōlkoṭiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.72 kikaḷil caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷum73 piṟa vaḷikaḷum74 makaḷir tam melliya viral kaḷil mōci eṉṉum mōtiramum75 aṇintu vantaṉar.
makaḷir putalvarkku maṇikaḷ kōtta kurumpi pōṉṟa kiṇkiṇi eṉṉum kāl aṇiyi iṭṭu aḻaku pārttatum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. 76
15. akanāṉūṟu
akaccuvip poruḷip paṟṟi caṅkap pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya ceyyuḷkaḷil akavaṟpākkaḷāl āṉavikaḷip piritteṭuttup paṉṉiraṇṭu aṭikaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa pākkaḷi neṭuntoki eṉṟa peyarāl tokuttaṉar. ituvē akanāṉūṟu eṉappaṭum.
innūlil cila iṭaṅkaḷil  aṇikalaṉkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilampu76, vīravaḷi, vīrakkaḻal77, poṉvaḷi, caṅkuvaḷi, kiṇkiṇi (cataṅki)78, kuṭaccūl (oru vakic cilampu)79, ceñcūṭṭukkuḻi, kōṟṟāṭi (aḻakiya vaḷi), kōlvaḷi80, cuḻalkoṭi81, polantār (poṉṉari māli), polaṅkalam (poṉṉaṇi), maṇiceymaṇṭi (maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṟkalaṉ) pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ pala kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.82.
caṅkak kālattilē tamiḻakattil poṉṉum maṇikaḷum aṇikaḷum poṟkaṭṭilum poṉ vaṭṭilum piṟavum ērāḷamāka iruntuvantaṉa eṉpataṟku innūl nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭut tarukiṉṟatu.
16. puṟanāṉūṟu
eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷil oṉṟākum. innūl tolkāp piyattiṟku muṉtōṉṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷiyum, piṉṉar tōṉṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷatu. itu tamiḻar nākarikattiyuṇarttum perum nūlākum.
tamiḻarkaḷ mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē muṉ kiyil vaḷikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.83 vēntarkaḷ kālil vīrakkaḻal aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.84 makaḷir iṭiyil poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa pala kācukaḷ kōtta mēkaliyum85 taliyil poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇṇiyum pūṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vaḷinta kuḻikaḷi maṅkiyar kātiṟ pūṭṭiyiruntaṉar eṉpatu naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.86 nāñcil malittalivaṉ oruvaṉ muttu vaṭaṅkaḷip pūṇṭiruntatōṭu poṉmāliyum, poṟpūvum cūṭiyiruntāṉ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm.87, 88 iḵtaṉṟip pala viṭaṅ kaḷil poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇ mutaliyavi kaḷip pūṇṭiruntāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila maṉṉarkaḷ allatu vīrarkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ, avarkaḷ paricāka aḷitta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
17. peruṅkati
peruṅkati piṟkālattil eḻuntatu. eṉṟālum caṅka nūl pōl pōṟṟap peṟuvatu. itu koṅkuvēḷir eṉṟa pulavarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. ivvāciriyar camaṇa camayattavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
innūlil patumāvati maṇakkōlam pūṇṭāḷ eṉpati āciriyar aḻakāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avaḷ viramaṇi kaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa yavaṉa nāṭṭu aṇikalap pēḻiyil89 aḻakiya poṉkoṭi, kaḷiki eṉṉum kaḻuttaṇi, poṉṉāṟ ceyta poṭṭu,  poṉpaṭṭam, poṉṉōli (kātaṇi) aivaki vaṇṇam oḷirumāṟu ceyta maṇikaḷ patitta mēkali (iṭi yaṇi)90, poṉvaḷi, poṟkaṭakam, cūṭakam, pāṭakam, nūpuram mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷi eḻiluṟap pūṇṭāḷ eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.91
utayaṇaṉ, kātaliyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyiṉāl uyir tuṟakka ettaṉittāṉ. avaṉatu tōḻarkaḷ avaṉatu kātaliyiṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷik kāṭṭi avaṉ ceyalit taṭuttaṉar. āciriyar avaḷatu aṇikalaṉkaḷāṉa poṉṉarimāli, poṉpaṭṭam92, tilakam, kuḻi93, muttumāli, kaḷiki, cītēvi, tōḷaṇiyākiya makaram, kiyaṇiyākiya kaṭakam, muppattiraṇṭu vaṭṭak kācukaḷ kōttamālikaḷ toṅkum aḻakiya nucuppaṇiyākiya viri
ciki94, tavaḷivāyk kiṇkiṇi mutaliyavikaḷi eḻilpeṟa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.95
iḵtaṉṟi toyyakam, pullakam (teṉpalli, vaṭa palli eṉṉum taliyaṇi)  kaṭikiyāram (nāḻiki kāṭṭum karuvikaḷ pōl ceyyappeṟṟa māli) poṉ mālikaḷ, mēkali, kāñci, iratap palkāḻ, makarak kuṇṭalam, pavaḷavaṭam96, muttumāli, poṉkaḻaṅku vaṭivāyc ceyyappeṟṟa māli, nellikkāy māli, kāviti, ēṉāti, eṭṭi mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷukkuriya aṟikuṟi yāka aracaṉ aḷitta poṟpaṭṭam, ēkāvali mutaliya aṇikaḷ pala eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.97
mēlum aracilip poṉ aṭar98, itayavācaṉi99, ēka valli100, kaḻaṟcikkāymāli101, kaḷiki102, ciṟuneṟṟāli103, tilakam104, toṭi105, neṟṟikkaṭṭi106, paṉṉirittāli107, paṇ maṇittāli108, pāṇṭil109, pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam110, pūṇ111, maṇivaṭam112, māttiri113, mārāṭṭam114, muttavaḷḷi115, muttu māli116, mummaṇikkācu117-118 pōṉṟa pala aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. cilappatikāram
2.	cilap : pukār - kaṭalāṭu 82 - 108	
alattaka mūṭṭiya vañceñ cīṟaṭi	
nalattaku melvira ṉallaṇi ceṟīip	
pariyaka nūpuram pāṭakam cataṅki	
ariyakaṅ kāluk kamivuṟa aṇintu	
kuṟaṅku ceṟitiraḷ kuṟaṅkiṉiṟ ceṟittup	
piṟaṅkiya muttari muppat tirukāḻ	
niṟaṅkiḷar pūntuki ṉīrmiyi ṉuḻīik	
kāmar kaṇṭiki taṉṉoṭu piṉṉiya	
tūmaṇit tōḷvaḷi tōḷuk kaṇintu	
mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiraṅ kaṭṭiya	
cittirac cūṭakañ cempoṟ kivaḷi	
pariyakam vālvaḷi pavaḷap palvaḷi	
arimayir muṉkik kamivuṟa vaṇintu	
vāḷip pakuvāy vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram	
kēḻkiḷar ceṅkēḻ kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram	
vāṅkuvil vayirattu marakatat tāḷceṟi	
kāntaṇ melviral karappa vaṇintu	
caṅkili nuṇṭoṭar pūṇñāṇ puṉiviṉi	
aṅkaḻut takavayi ṉāramō ṭaṇintu	
kayiṟkaṭi yoḻukiya kāmar tūmaṇi	
ceyattaku kōviyiṟ ciṟupuṟa maṟittāṅ	
kintira nīlat tiṭiyiṭi tiraṇṭa	
cantira pāṇi takipeṟu kaṭippiṇi	
aṅkā takavayi ṉaḻakuṟa vaṇintu	
teyva vuttiyoṭu ceḻunīr valampuri	
toyyakam pullakam toṭarnta talikkaṇi	
miyī rōtikku māṇpuṟa vaṇintu

paṭaṅkaḷ
1. itu, tañcip peruuṭiyār kōyilil uḷḷa periya nāyaki ammiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimam. itu 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭatu. ivvuruvamum itaṉmītu kāṇappaṭum āṭiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷum paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷiṉ āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷukku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. kātaṇi, kaḻuttaṇi, tōḷaṇi, kiyaṇi, kālaṇi pōṉṟavikaḷ tulāmparamāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
2. inta uruvam kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cēranāṭṭup peṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyil vanta nāyarkula naṅkiyiṉ niḻal uruvappaṭam. ippaṭattil ivaḷ paṇṭiya tamiḻp peṇkaḷiṉ pāṇiyil āṭi aṇintu aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭu aḻakuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟāḷ.
3. inta niḻaṟpaṭam 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tiru nelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa vēḷāḷar kulap peṇṇiṉ tōṟṟatti eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu. ivaḷ kātil aṇintuḷḷa pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi, muṭiccu, iṭṭaṭikki, mēliṭu ākiya aṇi kaḷum, mūkkaṇiyum kaḻuttaṇiyum akkālattil vēḷāḷar kula peṇ makkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷukku ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāy ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
4. kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa nāṭār kula naṅkiyiṉ uruvappaṭam. ippeṇmaṇi 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiluḷḷa tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷukkuriya pala aṇi kaḷi aṇintuḷḷāḷ. ivaḷ kātu vaṭittum iravikki aṇintum pūṭi, karṉappū pōṉṟa pala kātaṇikaḷiyum uṭkaṭṭu, cuttumaṇi, cuṇṭikkāy māli pōṉṟa pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷiyum aṇintavaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ. ivaḷatu tōṟṟam oru paṇakkārap peṇṇiṉ tōṟṟam pōl irukkiṟatu.
5. ippaṭam tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa oru ēḻi nāṭār kula maṅkiyiṉ paṭam. ippeṇmaṇi 19-ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷavar. ivar oru cila kātaṇi, kaḻuttaṇi, tōḷaṇi, kiyaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟār. ivaratu iṭuppil uḷḷa ciṟu kuḻanti kātu vaṭikkap peṟṟu īyakkuṇukku aṇintuḷḷatu. kaḻuttil pāci māliyum, kiyil veḷḷik kāppukaḷum pūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
6. inta niḻaṟpaṭam irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattil uḷḷa nāṭār kula maṅkiyuṭiyatu. ivaratu kātil viciṟimurukum, pāmpaṭamum, kaḻuttil cuttumaṇi, kāṟi pōṉṟa aṇikaḷiyum kiyil kiccari, kaṭakam, kāppu pōṉṟa aṇikaḷiyum, kiviralkaḷil mōtiraṅkaḷum aṇin tuḷḷār.
7. ippeṇ maturi māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa akampaṭiyarkula āraṇaṅku. ivaratu kātilum kaḻuttilum pala aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōḷiṉ kīḻē vaṅkiyum, kontikkāy kolucum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
8. (1) irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa maṟavar kula maṅki. (2) ātittirāviṭa arivi (3) kōvi māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa kavuṇṭarkulak kāriki (4) kōvi māvaṭṭattil muṉṉēṟṟam peṟāta iṉattic cērnta iḷamaṅki. ivaḷ kaḻuttaṇi, kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṟāḷ.
9. intap paṭam acentā ōviyam. itu tirāviṭa nāṭṭu eḻil oḻukum oru iḷamaṅkiyiṉ tōṟṟatti eṭuppuṭaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu. taliyaṇikaḷum kātaṇikaḷum kaḻuttaṇi kaḷum kiyaṇikaḷum paḻaṅkālattavi eṉṟālum iṉṟiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiviṭa aḻakuṟap polikiṉṟaṉa. inta ōviyam ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭiyatu.
10. cintuveḷiyil kiṭitta oru veṇkalap paṭimam. itu ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭiṟku muṉṉuḷḷatu. ippaṭimam oru nāṭṭiya māṭum naṅkiyiṉ uruvamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. ivaḷatu tōḷi ṉiṉṟu maṇikkaṭṭu vari, kiyil pala vaḷikaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāy aṇiyappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa.
11. mokañcatārōvil kiṭitta aṇikalaṉkaḷil iṅku kaḻuttil aṇiyum mālikaḷ aintu uḷḷaṉa. ivi cintuveḷit tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ aṇinta poṉ aṇikaḷ. ivikaḷil maṇikaḷ pala patikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi viyattakuvēlippāṭukaḷ koṇṭavi. ivikaḷil oṉṟu 240 - poṉmaṇikaḷum oṉṟu 70. poṉmaṇikaḷum, oṉṟu 27- poṉmaṇikaḷum, oṉṟu 6 caraṅkaḷum, oṉṟu mūṉṟu caraṅkaḷum uṭiyaṉa. ivikaḷil iṭi iṭiyē viliyuyarnta kaṟkaḷum, maṇikaḷum, patakkaṅkaḷum kōrkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu aṭṭiki pōṉṟuḷḷatu. kīḻē kāṇappaṭuvatu caṅkili pōṉṟa kaḻuttaṇi.
12. itu, arappāvil akaḻntu kaṇṭa ariya ki aṇi. itu, maṇikkaṭṭu pakkam aṇiyum oruvakikkāppu. mēlē kūrmiyāṉa muṭkaḷ pōl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uṭpuṟam irutayavaṭivilum veḷippuṟattōṟṟam cūriyaṉ taṉ oḷikkiraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇap paṭuvatu pōlum uḷḷa intak kiyaṇi kōvi māvaṭṭattil uḷḷaṉa. tiruttam peṟāta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ kikaḷil iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa.
13. nākar.
14. (a) mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa iru kaḻuttaṇikaḷum (mālikaḷum), ataṉ utirip pākaṅkaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(ā) maturi mīṉāṭci - cuntarēcuvarar kōyilil uḷḷa mīṉāṭci tēvikkuriya taliyaṇi. itu muttukkaḷālāṉa poṉ aṇi.
(i) ituvum aṅkuḷḷa maṟṟōr taliyaṇi.
(ī) ituvum mīṉāṭciyammaṉ kōyilil uḷḷa oru kaḻuttaṇiyum (navakaṇṭikiyum), patakkamumākum.
15. itu, maturi mīṉāṭci ammaṉiṉ taliyaṇikaḷil oṉṟu. itu viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patitta poṉ aṇi.
16. (1) talic cāmāṉ 1 (a) nilavu piṟi 1 (ā) cūriyap piṟi (2) māṭṭiyum tōṭum cimikkiyum (3) campum neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum (4) campum neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum. (5) campu (6) kirttā (7) neṟṟiccuṭṭi (8) cimikki
17. (1) caṭāṅkam (caṭi aṇi) (2) kuñcam (3) irākkuṭi (4) cantira villi.
18. kātaṇikaḷ 
(1) kuṇukku (nērppārviyum, pakkappārviyum (2) makara kuṇṭalam (3) kuṇukku (maturi maṟavar maṅkiyarkaḷ aṇintu varum kātaṇi (nērppārviyum pakkap pārviyum)
19. kirttā (nērppārviyum, pakkap pārviyum (2) kuṇukku (nērppārviyum, pakkappārviyum)
20. (1) koppu (2) pūvāḷi (3) cūri (muṉ pārvi) pakkappārvi, (4) taṇṭaṭṭi (muṉpārvi) pakkappārvi (maturi maṟavar kulap peṇkaḷ aṇintu varuvatu) (5) tār kuṇukku (pakkat tōṟṟamum, nēr mukat tōṟṟamum.)
21. (1) pullākku (2) pēcari (3) nattu (palvēṟu tōṟṟam) (4) nākapātam (nērppārviyum, pakkappārviyum)
22. (1, 2, 3) makara kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ (4,5,6) carppa kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ (7) irattiṉa kuṇṭalam (8) poṉ ōli (9) vāḷi (10) poṟ kuṇṭalam (11) tōṭu (12, 13) kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ.
23. kaḻuttaṇikaḷ : 1 mutal 8 variyuḷḷa uruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil palvēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ aṇiyum tālikaḷ (9) cuttu maṇiyum tāliyum.
24. (1) kāy vaṭivāṉa poṉ maṇikaḷ kōtta kaḻuttaṇi 
(2) uṭkaṭṭup pōṉṟu iru aricikaḷi oṉṟāka iṇittuk kaḻuttic cuṟṟiyaṇiyum oru aṇi. (3) kaḻuttic cuṟṟiyaṇiyum mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷālāṉa ciṟu arici kōtta oru kaḻuttaṇi.
25. (1) nellikkāy māli (2) picciyarumpu māli (3) kottamalli māli.
26. cuttumaṇi. itil kōrkkappaṭṭa ovvoru maṇikkum taṉittaṉip peyarkaḷ uṇṭu. cuttumaṇiyiṉ naṭuvil tāli iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu. itu civaliṅka vaṭivil amintirukkiṟatu. cuttu maṇi nūlil allatu poṉ koṭiyil kōrkkappaṭṭirukkum. iru puṟamum iru tāyittukkaḷiṉ uḷḷē mantirat takaṭukaḷ vikkap peṟṟirukkum. tāyittukkaḷi aṭuttup pala ciṉṉaṅkaḷ uḷḷa maṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (1) peruñcīraka vitiyiṉ oru kottu (2) poṭṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mantirat taṉmikaḷ uḷḷa vaṭṭamāṉa 4 maṇikaḷ (3) kāykaḷ (4) pū allatu paḻam (5) tuḷaci māṭam (6) cantaṉak kumpā allatu pāttiram (7) arakkuk - kulīcam - itu kōyiliṉ koṭumuṭiyiṉ oru uruva amippu (8) vātumik koṭṭikaḷ 6, (9) aṉṉācip paḻam (10) mulāmpaḻam (11) cātik kāy, (12) kattarikkāy (13) uṇavu uṇṇum pāttiram, (14) paṉṉīrcempum (15) tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyāta tīṅkaṉik kottu, (16) miḷakāykaḷ, (17) campaṅkikaḷ 2 (18) aṭiyāḷam kāṇamuṭiyāta paḻakkuli, (19) kottamallikkāyk kottu, (20) vilva ili, (21) māṅkāykaḷ 2, (22) vāḻippaḻaṅkaḷ, (23) tēṅkāy, (24) tāmari muki, (25) peyar kūṟamuṭiyāta ili, (26) peyar kūṟa muṭiyāta paḻam.
27. (1, 2, 3) kaḻuttiyoṭṭiyaṇiyum aṭṭiki pōṉṟa poṉ kaḻuttaṇi (4) veḷḷik kāppukaḷ.
28. maṇikaḷ patitta patakkaṅkaḷum, poṉmaṇi māliyum.
29. puya aṇi :  (1) irattiṉa vaṅki (a) irattiṉa vaṅkiyiṉ pakkattōṟṟam (2) poṉ vaṅki (3) pācippantu. itu āṇum peṇṇum aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ.
30. muṉkiyaṇi : (1) kiccari
31. kālaṇikaḷ : (1) kālcari - pū vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyap paṭṭa veḷḷi aṇi.
32. (1) veḷḷikkāppu (nērppārviyum, pakkap pārviyum) (2, 2 a) kāppiṉ nērppārviyum pakkap pārviyum (3) kāppu (4, ē) kāppiṉ palavakit tōṟṟam.
33. (kīḻ varici) (1) kāppu (2) kolucu (3) kaṭakam
(mēl varici) (4) muṭakku mōtiram (neḷivu) itu vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. (5) muṭakku mōtiram (pakkap pārvi) (6) oṟṟikkal patitta mōtiram (7) civantippū mōtiram.
34. nucuppaṇikaḷ : (1) mēkali (2) kāñci (3) kalāpam (4) tāmam (5) viriciki ( oru pakkat tōṟṟam)
35.  (1, 2) taṇṭikaḷ (veḷḷiyaṇikaḷ) (3, 3 a) taṇṭiyiṉ pakkap pārviyum nērppārviyum (vivacāyikaḷiṉ aṇi)
36. (1, 2, 3, 4) taṇṭiyiṉ palvēṟu tōṟṟaṅkaḷ.
37. (1) vēlaṅkāyk kolucu (2) attikkāyk kolucu (3, 4, 4a, 5) pātacaraṅkaḷ (caṅkili), piṟkālakāl aṇikaḷ
38. pātac cilampu
39. kāl viral aṇi
(1, 2) muñci (3) calaṅki meṭṭi (4) meṭṭi (5) pillaṇi (6) viralaṇi (7, 7a, 7ā) mīṉ meṭṭiyiṉ palavakit tōṟṟam. ivi iṭatu kāliṉ 2vatu, 3vatu viralkaḷil aṇipavi. 3vatu viralil aṇiyum mīṉ ciṉṉam civaṉiṉ oru aṭiyāḷam.
40. (1, 1a) kāl viralaṇi (nērp pārviyum pakkap pārviyum (2) pīliyiṉ pakkappārvi (mēlēyuḷḷa vaḷiyam viralil māṭṭuvataṟkuriyatu) (2a) pīliyiṉ mēṟpuṟat tōṟṟam (3, 3a) calaṅki miñciyiṉ pakkappārviyum, mēṟpārviyum.



1.	Ornament by Dr. Ananda K. Coomaraswamy in the Art bulletin Vol. XXI. P. 375-382. Published by the College art association of America and edited by the Johns Hapkins university.
